# Constitution of Zimbabwe (2013) — Kalanga translation

This is a translation of the original 2013 text of theConstitution of Zimbabwe. It does not include amendments made by Acts of Parliament in 2017 and 2021; the validity of those Acts is still the subject of litigation in the Constitutional Court.

Nlayo Nkulu Wezimbabwe Mbaakanyo (No. 20)

2013

ZIMBABWE

TJISUNGO TJENLAYO Tjinodzima nebakanya butshwa Nlayo Nkulu weZimbabwe TJAKALONGWA nePhalamente neNtungamili weHango weZimbabwe 1 5

N’holo Mfutshwanana

Tjisungo tjeNlayo itjetji tjingalingwa seTjisungo tjeNlayo tjebakanyiwa butshwa (No. 20) kweNlayo Nkulu weZimbabwe, mugolela 2013. 2

Tjeneso MuTjisungo tjeNlayo itjetji— “Nlayo Nkulu uyapo” kobviwa Nlayo Nkulu weZimbabwe wakatangisa hinga kubvila nsi wa18 mumwedzi waTjabewhomba, mugole la1980, nebakanyiwa kawo butshwa kubvila ipapo; “Nlayo Nkulu ntshwa” kobviwa Nlayo Nkulu weZimbabwe sebambiwa kwawo muNdolongoloso.




Dzimiwa kweNlayo Nkulu uyapo nentha yebayapo kweNlayo Nkulu ntshwa

(1) Titobela ndimana (2), Nlayo Nkulu uyapo unodzimiwa kukohinga Nlayo Nkulu ntshwa.

(2) Kudza kuwhisisike kwakayi—

(a)

Nlayo Nkulu ntshwa unotshambidzigwa “muhhuba ledusa patjena”sekwakuno tjenesewa muNdongoloso yebutanhatu yawhoyo Nlayo Nkulu, kubva kuti, iwhoyo nsi Tjisungo tjeNlayo kwatjinokwagwa muGazethi kundilana nendima 51(5) yeNlayo Nkulu uyapo; kakale Printed by the Government Printer, Harare. 1

DISTRIBUTED BY VERITAS e-mail: veritas@mango.zw; website: www.veritaszim.net Veritas makes every effort to ensure the provision of reliable information, but cannot take legal responsibility for information supplied.

(b) Kubvila iwhoyo nsi wetshambidzigwa kweNlayo Nkulu ntshwa sekwakabigwa mundimana (a), Nlayo Nkulu uyapo unoba uhinga kunoti panhambo dzinotjenesewa muNdongoloso yebutanhatu muNlayo Nkulu ntshwa.

NDONGOLOSO (NDIMA 2) NLAYO NKULU WEZIMBABWE _______________ MISIWA KWENDIMA Tjiwalilo DANHO 1 Matama Ebamba Ndima

## Section 32 — Zwinangwa zwinootobegwa neButungamili gwehango nemakubungano ose

kose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente. Tungamila kunandulamo. Bhatana, kunyalala negadzikana muHango. Susumiwa kwendulamilo nensununguko dzakazinda. Nlayo wedlidzana negalisana nedzimwe hango. Pfumbila kwehango. Piwa masimba nehhugwa kwemihingo. Bayapo kwezodliwa. Milenje. Lizana kwebetjilume nebetjikadzi. Migwa kwemitunhu kwakalizana. Bana. Batjakula. Banhu bakwegula. Banhu bakakubadzika. Mihha dzeNgwa yeLusunguko. Zwakalingisana nen’hingo nebahingi. Dzibiligwa kwen’ha. Ndobolo. Zwidiyo. Bugalo. Jidzo lebutjilo bubuya. Jidzo linopiwa banohaya. Jidzo lezwenlayo. Ntha dzemizano nezwifalayidza.

Ndima

Ndulamilo yetjila. Ndulamilo yesununguka kwenhu. Ndulamilo dzebanhu bakasungwa nebakahhaliligwa. Ndulamilo yebhakhwa nezila inabunhu. Ndulamilo yedzibililika kwenhu. Lusununguko mukudziyidziwa kene mukayikanyiwa, mukubhakhwa nezila isina bunhu kene inolengudza kene langiwa kunojitisa ntuzi. Lusununguko gwesabhakhwa bulanda. Lusununguko gwesamanikidziwa hinga kene hingisiwa kusingalambike. Lizana nesahalugwa. Ndulamilo yesumbikala. Lusununguko gwekubungana nehanganyila Lusununguko gwebhudila patjena neyendesa phethishini. Lusununguko gwenkumbulo. Lusununguko gwezwilayidza nelusununguko gweyendisiwa kwendebo. Swikilila luzibo. Masimba edziKhuta. Dzikhuta. Zwimila kwedzikhuta. Ndongoloso dzinotungamila hinga kwedzikhuta. Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu. Simba leKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu. Khuta iPehhugwi. Simba leKhuta iPehhugwi. Khuta Whulu. Simba leKhuta Whulu. Khuta yeBahingi. Khuta yeTungamigwa kweMakubungano akasiyana siyana neGalisana kwebanhu. Dzimwe khuta nembahhe. Masimba edziKhuta muna zweNlayo Nkulu. Bigwa kwebakabhatila majaji Bigwa kwemajaji edzikhuta dzemitunhu nebamwe banohinga mudzikhuta. Bahingi bedziKhuta abazofanila hingila khuta dzinopinda in’ompela. Bigwa kwebahingi bedziKhuta kwakafanila kulakidzile tjimo tjetjaba. Tuna pakugadziwa mubugalo. Lubaka gweba mubugalo kwemajaji . Dusiwa kwemajaji mubugalo. Tjimo tjehinga nelubaka gweba mubugalo kwebahingi bedzikhuta. LUBABI 3 Khomishini Inolinga Nekwen’hingo Wedzikhuta

## Section 345

Sayendedzana kwezwakakwagwa muNlayo Nkulu. NDONGOLOSO YEKUTANGA: Fulegi yeHango, Lumbo gweHango, Lutjwayo gweHango neNtolo weHango. NDONGOLOSO YEBUBILI: Miganhu yeNdulamilo muLubaka gweMbatsha yeTjaba. NDONGOLOSO YEBUTATU: Tuna neBvuma. NDONGOLOSO YEBUNA: Fanilo dzebanoVota. NDONGOLOSO YEBUHANU: Zila yebhakhwa kweMilayo itjasusumiwa muPhalamente nedzimwe ndebo dzePhalamente. NDONGOLOSO YEBUTANHATU: Tangisa hinga kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu nezila dzefuluka kunlayo nkulukugwi.

TJIWALILO _______________ Iswi banhu beZimbabwe, Takabhatana pakusiyana kwenjemulo dzelusununguko, dzendulamilo nelizana, nebuhali gwedu gwelamba tongewa nebahhi, halugwa neganda, nezila dzose dzekuligwa netshambidzigwa. Tikudza kakale tilumbidza betjilume nebetjikadzi banatjilopa bakazwipila butjilo gwabo mulubaka gwengwa yePapadzukanemungwa yelusununguko gwehango. Tilemekedza whola dzedu nebana bevu bakahinga zwakasima kuti kube nekundilambeli muhango yedu, bebona hakika kwesimba lezwitonga, naka, butjena nezwipa nlandu kweButungamili nesimba lenlayo, Timusidzila zwipila kwedu kwemisa nedzibilila ndulamilo nesununguko dzakadzika, Tiwamutjila fumwa yezwiwanikwa zwehango yedu,Tihanhila halabvuka kwemabvilo nemilenje yedu, Takazwipila kunda gose bulemo ndezwinoti pinganyidza pakubhudilila, Tikudza lusununguko, lizana, kunyalala, ndulamilo, whisisana, bhudilila negalabgwe takalingilila zwimbeli nkumbulo un’ompela. Tiwamutjila bukulu gweNdzimu, mangwana yedu imumaboko awe, Tozwipila tigalamoyo muNlayo Nkulu takalonga meya mubakanya tjaba tjakabhatana, tjinalebeswa tjinolezi bhudilila kwehango, tjakabambiwa nebunhu, lizana, sununguka, lebeswa, nebunhu gwehinga nemasimba, Kakale tikumbila tungamigwa netjidziwa neNdzimu, towamutjila Nlayo Nkulu, tizwipila kuwuli setshinde neNlayo wehango yedu yatinoda.

DANHO 1 MATAMA EBAMBA 1

Hango Zimbabwe ihango yakabhatana, yakazwimila kakale inozwitonga.


Bukulu gweNlayo Nkulu

(1) Iwhoyu Nlayo Nkulu ndiwo nlayo nkulu pamilayo yose yeZimbabwe kakale kene upi umwe nlayo, thama, tjila kene zwibhata kusingandilane nawo akutohinga.

(2) Zwinolebgwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu zwakafanila tebegwa nenhu wose, kene makubungano ose, kose neButungamili gweHango neButungamili gose, nenhabi dzenlayo nenhabi dzahulumente mumbihho dzose, zwakafanila hhadzisiwa nayibo. 3 Mabambo ebunhu nezwilemela

(1) Zimbabwe yakabambiwa nekuzwitebamisa kutebegwa bunhu nezwilemela zwinotebela—

(a) masimba eNlayo Nkulu;

(b) lawugwa nenlayo;

(c) mabambo endulamilo nensununguko dzebanhu dzakazinda;

(d) siyana kwemilenje, kwelunamato nebulemela gwemabvilo muhango;

(e) wamutjila kuti umwe nemwe nhu unozwagwa enatjilemela nebukulu gwemwe nemwe;

(f) wamutjila lizana kwebanhu bose;

(g) lizana kwebetjilume nebetjikadzi;

(h) tungamila kunandulamo;

(i) newamutjigwa nelemekedzewa kwengwa yelusununguko.

(2) Zila dzetungamila kunandulamo, dzinohanganya Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose, dzohanganyila—

(a)

tjimo tjezwepolitiki tjakasununguka kakale tjina makubungano ezwepolitiki manjinji;

(b) zila dzeyetiwa kwehalulo dzakalinga—

(i) vota kwebanhu bose bawhola nelizana kwemavoti;

(ii) halulo dzakasununguka, dzinakulizana kakale dzinotiwa nelubaka gwakatagwa;

(iii) nemigwa kwebahaluli kwakalizana.

(c)

swiswaniwa kwemasimba kunambumbiko hule kwehalulo;

(d) lemekedza ndulamilo dzemakubungano ezwepolitiki;

(e)

tebegwa kwezila yepalalanyiwa kwemasimba;

(f)

lemekedzewa kwebanhu beZimbabwe, banobe beli ibo banopa batungamili masimba otungamila;

(g) bhuda paatjena, ndulamo, zwipa-nlandu nehanya;

(h) susumiwa kwebhatana, kunyalala negadzikana muHango, kulemekedzewa siyana kwendimi, mipanga yemabvilo nemilenje;

(i)

wamutjigwa kwendulamilo—

(i) dzemakubungano emilenje, enjudzi, endimi neebugalimoyo;

(ii) dzebanhu bakakubadzika;

(iii) dzebetjikadzi, batjikula, batjakula nebana;

(iv) dzemihha dzengwa yelusununguko;

(j) kobewa kwezwiwanikwa zwehango kwakalizana, kuhanganyisila hango;

(k) lemekedza ndulamilo dzapiwa;

(l) nekobewa newagwa kwemasimba ahulumente nemihingo iye. 4

Fulegi yeHango, Lumbo gweHango, Ntolo weHango neLutjwayo

Hango yeZimbabwe inafulegi yeHango, Lumbo gweHango, Lutjwayo neNtolo weHango, kunolebgwa muNdongoloso yeKutanga. 5

Mbihho dzahulumente Mbihho dzahulumente weZimbabwe ndiyedzi—

(a)

hulumente wehango yose;

(b) makhansili ematunhu neematoropo;

(c)


nemakhansili emitunhu, kubviwa—

(i) makhansili ematoropo, kene nayipi zila yedan’wa kwawo, akafanila mila kakale atungamile zwinhu zwakalingisana nebanhu bamumatoropo;

(ii) nemakhansili emakanyi, kene nayipi zila yedan’wa kwawo, akafanila mila kakale atungamile zwinhu zwakalingisana nebanhu bakumakanyi mumitunhu sekwakapalalanyiwa matunhu.

Ndimi

(1) Ndimi dzinotebela, Chewa, ChiBarwe, English, Kalanga, Koisan, Nambya, Ndau, Ndebele, Shangani, Shona, Sign Language, Sotho, Tonga, Tswana, Venda neXhosa, ndidzo ndimi dzeZimbabwe dzinowamutjigwa kumunlayo.

(2)Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingasa dzimwe ndimi kuti dzibendimi dzinowamutjigwa kumunlayo kakale tjingasa ndimi dzinohingisiwa mukukwala.

(3) Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose dzakafanila—

(a)

banelebeswa lekuti dzose ndimi dzimunlayo dzobhakhwa nekulizana;

(b) nelingisisa ndimi dzinoyemugwa nebanhu bakakunhiwa nekunotiwa kene nezuwisana kwahulumente.

(4) Butungamili gwehango gwakafanila tshambidzila kakale gukudze hingisiwa kwendimi dzose dzinohingisiwa muZimbabwe, kuhanganyisila sign language, kakale gwakafanila yeta kuti pfumbila kwendimi idzedzi kutubugwe. 7

Susuma zibgwa kweNlayo Nkulu netjaba

Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila susuma kuti Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu uzibgwe netjaba ngeyeta kunotobela—

(a) wuntolikila mundimi dzose dzinohingisiwa kumunlayo newuyendesa kuntha dzose;

(b) haka kuti Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu udiyiwe muzwikwele kakale ube

muzwidiyo zwinodiyiwa banohinga dzibilila hango, banohingila Hulumente nebanohingila makubungano ose anohingila banhu;

(c) nekulumidza kuti banhu bose nemakubangano ose, kuhanganyila makubungano ebanhu, luzibo nezibgwa kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu mutjaba tjose. DANHO 2 Zwinangwa Zwehango 8

Zwinangwa zwinotungamila Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzahulumente

(1) Zwinangwa zwakakwagwa muDanho ileli zotungamila Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzahulumente mukubakanya nelonga milayo nendongoloso dzinowhobhatsha bambiwa, nesusumisa kwetjaba tjakazwimila, tjina butjilo gunotjilika, tjinandulamo, tjakasununguka, tjinolezi hanha nobhudilila kunabutjilo gunakuhhadzisika.

(2) Kwakafanila lingwa zwinangwa zwimuDanho ileli hapa kutjenesewa mihingo yakafanila tiwa neButungamili gweHango kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu wehango kene imwe milayo. 9

Tungamila kunandulamo

(1) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tshambidzila hinga kwendongoloso nenlayo unowhobakanya libilidza, bunyambi, zwipa nlandu, bapatjena, bunhu netambunuka mubhakhwa kwedzimari mumakubungano nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose nemumakubungano etjaba wose, kwakalingwa—

(a)

gadziwa muzwigalo zwehingila tjaba kwakafanila tiwa nezila ipatjena kwakalingwa fanilo;

(b) kwakafanila kube nenhambo dzinotogwa dzedusila patjena, gwisa nepedza gose bupitipiti nebanohingisa masimba nezila isakatambunuka belimuzwigalo zwepolitiki nebamuzwigalo zwehingila tjaba.

(2) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila bona kuti ose makubungano nenhabi dzahulumente, kwazo maKhomishini nemabunga akabumbiwa kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu, dzobhatshiwa nezose zwinohakika kuti dzihinge nezila mbuya inobaka, yakalizanila, inalebeswa nendibilidzo. 10 Bhatana, kunyalala negadzikana muHango Butungamili gweHango, nenhu wose, banhu benlayo, nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose, bakafanela milidza bhatana, kunyalala negadzikana muhango. 11 Susumiwa kwendulamilo nensununguko dzakazinda Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola dzose nhambo kuti gudzibilile ndulamilo nensununguko dzakazinda dzakakwagwa muDanho 4 nemilidza tikala nehadzisika kwadzo.

12 Nlayo wedlidzana negalisana nedzimwe hango

(1) Nlayo wedlidzana negalisana nedzimwe hango weZimbabwe wakafanila ube muzila dzinotebela—

(a)

susuma nedzibilila kwakalulamila tjaba tjeZimbabwe;

(b) tebamila nlayo wakalinga hango dzose;

(c)

galisana nekunyalala nedzimwe hango;

(d) nelulamisa sawililana nedzimwe hango nezila dzelunyalalo.

(2) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila milidza milenje, zwefumwa newililana munazwetonga nebhatana kwemitunhu nehango yose yeAfrica, kakale gwakafanila ngina mumakubungano ehango dzose nee Afrika, anomila lunyalalo, nelulama neyendila mbeli kwentunhu, kwe Afrika nebunhu. 13 Pfumbila kweHango

(1) Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente, kwakafanila gwisa kuti kube nekupfumbila kwakalizana, kakale kwakafanila tola nhambo—

(a)

dzesusumisa zwitila kwebanhu nezwihinga;

(b) dzesunda pfumbila kwezwelima nepfuwa, zwetengesa, zwebakanya mari, zwethekinologi nezwesayensi;

(c)

dzesunda pfumbila kwemabhizinesi anobakanya zwinhu nemabhizinesi etengesa zwinhu kudze banhu beZimbabwe bawane simba neluzibo;

(d)

nedzehhisiwa kwepfumbila kwakalizana mumitunhu yakasiyana yeZimbabwe, kwakalingwa kwazo lizanisiwa kwepfumbila kwemakanyi nekwematoropo.

(2) Nhambo dzinolebgwa nekwadzo mundima iyeyi dzakafanila hanganyila banhu muluka nemuhinga mano nendongoloso dzepfumbila dzakalingisana nabo.

(3) Nhambo dzinolebgwa mundima iyeyi dzakafanila dzibilila kakale dzikudze ndulamilo yebanhu, kwazo kwazo betjikadzi kuti baswikile mikoba yebhudilila kwakalizana nebetjilume.

(4)Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila bona kuti banhu mumitunhu banotjidzika nezwiwanikwa zwimumitunhu yabo. 14 Piwa masimba nehhugwa kwemihingo

(1) Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzahulumente, mumatanhilo ose, kwakafanila gwisa bhatsha kakale netola nhambo dzinopa simba kubanhu bose bakaleyewa, mabunga ose akaleyewa nenjudzi dzose dzakaleyewa muZimbabwe nezila yakafanila, inabutjena, inakulizana nemilikila pehhugwi.

(2) Mulubaka gose, Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzaHulumente mumatanhilo ose kwakafanila banelebeswa lekuti nhambo dzakafanila kakale dzakakwana dzotogwa kuti kuhhugwe mihingo yebanhu bose beZimbabwe, kwazo kwazo betjikadzi nebatjakula. 15 Bayapo kwezodliwa Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila—

(a)

kulumbidza banhu kuti balime kakale babige zodliwa zwakakwana;

(b) wana ntha dzinokwana dzebiga zodliwa;

(c)

nekulumbidza nesusuma dla zodliwa zwinandlo wakakwana guhingisa zila yediya banhu bose nedzimwe zila dzakafanila.

16 Milenje

(1) Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzaHulumente mumatanilo ose, kwakafanila susuma kakale kulondolodze milenje nemabvilo inokudza lulemekedzo, butjilo bubuya nebulizani gwebanhu beZimbabwe.

(2) Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzaHulumente mumatamhilo ose, nebanhu bose beZimbabwe, bakafanila gwisa kuti balondolodze kakale badzibilile tshinde lemabvilo leZimbabwe.

(3) Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzaHulumente mumatanilo ose, kwakafanila tola nhambo dzinopa gudzo nelulemekedzo kunhabi dzose dzemabvilo. 17 Lizana kwebetjikadzi nebetjilume

(1) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila susuma lizana kwebetjilume nebetjikadzi kwakapelela mutjaba tjeZimbabwe, kwazo kwazo—

(a)

Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila susuma bhatika kwakapelela kwebetjikadzi mumbihho dzose dzetjaba tjeZimbabwe nezila yakalizana nebetjilume.

(b) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola nhambo dzose, dzinohanganyila nhambo dzenlayo, dzinohakika kuti—

(i) bose betjilume nebetjikadzi bakamiligwa kwakalizana mumakubungano ose nemunhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose; kakale

(ii) betjikadzi babe lubabi gwakalizana negwebetjilume mumaKhomishini ose nemudzimwe nhabi dzahulumente dzinohalugwa nedzinobigwa kene dzakabambiwa kusi kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, kene tjipi Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente; kakale

(c)

Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose, kwakafanila tola nhambo dzinobhatika dzinota kuti betjikadzi baswikilile zwiwanikwa, kuhanganyila, hango, nezila yakalizana nebetjilume.

(2) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola nhambo dzinowamutjilika dzetambunudza luhalawulo nehayika kwelizana pakati kwebetjilume nebetjikadzi kwakabva mundongoloso nemazwibhato akawolobegwa makulukugwi. 18 Migwa kwemitunhu kwakalizanila

(1)Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila susuma migwa kwakalizanila kwemitunhu yose yeZimbabwe mumakubungano ose eZimbabwe nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose.

(2) Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose, kwakafanila tola nhambo dzakafanila dzebona kuti mitunhu yose inoswikilila zwiwanikwa zwinopfumbidza mitunhu kwakalizanila.

2013

Bana

(1)Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila wamutjila ndongoloso nenhambo dzinota kuti zose zwinoyendilana nebana, nezwinoyemugwa nebana zotolegwa pehhugwi.

(2) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila wamutjila ndongoloso dzakatambunuka nenhambo, kuyendilana nezwiwanika zwiyapo, dzekuti kube nelebeswa lekuti bana—

(a)

bawane hanha mun’ha neludo gwebazwadzi, kene lelewa kwakafanila hapa asitjipo mun’ha wabo;

(b) bawane bugalo nezodliwa zwinobaka mbili, jidzo lebuwongelo nejidzo linopiwa banohaya;

(c)

badzibiligwe mukusabhakwa zwibuyanana, letjedzegwa nedzidziwa nezila dzakasiyana siyana; kakale

(d) baswikilile zwidiyo zwakatambunuka nediyika.

(3) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola nhambo dzenlayo dzakatambunuka nedzimwe nhambo—

(a)

dzedzibilila bana mukuhingisiwa sebalanda; kakale

(b) kuti kube nelebeswa lekuti bana abatomanikidziwa kene bvumigwa hinga kene pa jidzo—

(i) kusakabalizanila makole abo ezwagwa; kene

(ii) linolonga butjilo gwebana, zwidiyo zwabo, mimbili kene minkumbulo yabo kene meya dzabo, pfumbidza bunhu kene galisana, mumboli. 20

Batjakula

(1) Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose, kwakafanila tola nhambo dzakatambunuka, kuhanganyila ndongoloso dzelebeswa, kuti kube nelebeswa lekuti batjakula, kubviwa banhu banamakole anobvila pana aligumi nekuhanu kunoti kuna alimakumi matatu nekuhanu—

(a)

baswikilile zwidiyo zwakatambunuka nediyika;

(b) banamikoba yezuwisana neyemigwa neyebhatika muna zwepolitiki, zwegalisana, zwebufumi gwehango nezwimwe zwakalingisana nebutjilo;

(c)

banowana mikoba yewana mihingo nedzimwe zila dzinopa masimba ezwiwanila fumwa;

(d) banowana mikoba yezwifalayidza kakale beswikilila ntha dzezwifalayidza; kakale

(e) bakadzibiligwa mumilenje yakayipa, hingisiwa kusakatambunuka nedzidziwa nezila dzakasiyana siyana.

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingata kuti kube nendongoloso in’ompela kene njinji dzebatjakula.

(3) Nhambo nendongoloso dzinobviwa mundimana (1) na (2) dzakafanila hanganyisila, satola lubihho munazwepolitiki neba netjimo tjetjaba. 21

Banhu bakwegula

(1) Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose, kwakafanila tola nhambo dzakatambunuka, kuhanganyisila nhambo dzenlayo, kuti kutubule wana lemekedzewa, bhatshiwa nedzibiligwa kwebanhu bakula nekuti batubule bhatika munazwebutjilo mutjaba tjaabo.

(2) Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose, kwakafanila gwisa nemasimba ose, kuhingisiwa zwiwanikwa zwiyapo—

(a)

kulumbidza banhu bakula kuti babhatike munazwakalingisana netjaba;

(b) hhulila mikoba, pa zodliwa newongewa, negalisana kwebanhu bakula bamutjimo tjehaka jidzo;

(c) bamba ndongoloso dzinopa banhu bakula mikoba yebhatika muna zwinopfumbidza, zwakalizanila, zwabanotubula yeta kakale zwinondidzana nezwipo nenjemulo dzabo;

(d) nekudza makubungano anopa jidzo nemilidza mamo ebutjilo gwebanhu bakwegula. 22 Banhu bakakubadzika

(1) Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose, kwafanila lemekedza ndulamilo dzebanhu bakakubadzika mumbili kene nkumbulo, kwazo ndulamilo yebhakhwa nezila yakatambunuka inabunhu.

(2) Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose kwafanila, kuhingisa zwiwanikwa zwiyapo, tjidza banhu bakakubadzika mumbili kene nkumbulo kuti babhudilile muna kwabanotubula yeta kakale kuteledziwe tshambidzigwa kwabo.

(3) kwazo kwazo, Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose, kwakafanila—

(a)

bamba ndongoloso dzetjidza banhu bakakubadzika mumbili kene nkumbulo, kwazo kwazo ndongoloso dzemihingo dzinoyendilana nematatabuko abo nekunowamutjilika kubali kene kunowamutjilika kubamilili babo benlayo;

(b) linga zwinohakika ntja mubanhu bakakubadzika kene nayipi zila, setjimwe tjezwinhu zwipehhugwi mukubambiwa kwendongoloso dzepfumbila;

(c)

kulumbidzila hingisiwa nebambiwa kwenhambo dzelebelesana dzakafanila banhu bakakubadzika mumbili kene nkumbulo; kakale

(d) kudza makubungano anopa jidzo nemilidza mamo ebutjilo gwebanhu bakakubadzika kene nayipi zila.

(4) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola nhambo dzakatambunuka kutila kuti kube nelebeswa lekuti banhu bakakubadzika banoswikilila nhanga nezwibakwa zwinoswikiligwa netjaba. 23 Mihha dzengwa yelusununguko

(1) Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose kwakafanila pa lulemekedzo gwakafanila, lumbidza nezibgwaa kwemihha dzengwa yelusununguko, kubviwa—

(a)

ibabo bakagwa muNgwa yeLusununguko;

(b) bakatjidza bagwi muNgwa yeLusununguko;

(c)

nayibabo bakasiwa mumajele, bakahhaliligwa kene bakatshambidzigwa nentha yepolitiki mulubaka gwengwa yelusunguko.

(2) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola nhambo dzakatambunuka, kuhanganyisila nhambo dzenlayo, kuti kubhakhwe zwibuyanana kakale kulemekedziwe mihha dzengwa yelusununguko.

24 Hinga nezwakalingisana nebahingi

(1) Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose, kwakafanila wamutjila ndongoloso dzakatambunuka nenhambo dzakatambunuka, kuhingisa zwiwanikwa zwiyapo, kudza kutubule pa banhu bose mikoba yehinga mihingo yabanozwihalila iyo, kudza bazwitjilise ibo nemiha yabo.

(2) Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose, kwakafanila gwisa kuti—

(a)

banhu bose bahinge;

(b) kudusiwe zwinhu zwingahhalilila mwaza hinga kwebanhu kene zwingabahhalilila kuti babhatike munazwingabatjidza pazwiwanila mari;

(c)

kube netungamigwa munazwebumihha nepfumbidziwa kwendongoloso dzezwebumihha nezwidiyo, kuhanganyisila ndongoloso nezwidiyo zwebanhu bakakubadzika;

(d) nehingisiwa kwenhambo dzinonga ludo gwen’ha dzinota kuti betjikadzi bawane nkoba wehinga bakahanha. 25 Dzibiligwa kwen’ha Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose, kwakafanila dzibilila nebhata zwibuyanana n’ha kakale kwazo kwazo kwakafanila gwisa, kuhingisa zwiwanikwa zwiyapo, kuti kuwamutjille nhambo—

(a)

dzebhata zwibuyanana netjidza bome, nabotate nebamwe banhu mun’ha banolela bana; kose

(b) nedzedzibilila hunguludziwa mumiha. 26 Ndobolo Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola nhambo dzakatambunuka kuti kube nelebeswa lekuti—

(a)

akuna ndobolo inotikala kusina zwipila nebvumilana kwebanohaka ngina mundobolo;

(b)

akutogadziwabgwe kuti kowhobe nebana mundobolo;

(c)

kunakulizana kwendulamilo nezwakafanila yetiwa nemwe nemwe mundobolo nepa paladziwa kwayo; kakale

(d) pakupaladziwa kwendobolo, nentha yelufu kene lambana, kunadzibiligwa kwebana nebazwadzi. 27 Zwidiyo

(1) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola nhambo dzose dzakatambunuka dzesusuma kuti—

(a)

bana bose bawane zwidiyo zwekutanga zwisingalipigwe;

(b) nezwidiyo zwapehhugwi

(2) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola nhambo dzinoyeta kuti kube nelebeswa lekuti basikana banopiwa mikoba yewana zwidiyo sebayisana mumatanhilo ose.

28 Bugalo Butungamili gweHango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanilo ose, kwakafanila tola nhambo dzenlayo nedzimwe, kuhingisa zwiwanikwa zwiyapo, kuti nhu wose awane bugalo gwakafanila. 29 Jidzo lebutjilo bubuya

(1) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola nhambo dzose dzakatambunuka dzinowhota kuti kube nelebeswa lekuti banhu bose banowana jidzo lezwebuwongelo lakafanila muhango yose yeZimbabwe.

(2) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola dzose nhambo dzakatambunuka dzinota kuti kube nelebeswa lekuti akuna nhu unolambigwa lapiwa netjinyolotjo mutjiwongelo tjipi natjipi.

(3) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola dzose nhambo dzedzibilila hhala kwedzingwele mubanhu guhingisa zwiwanikwa zwiyapo, kuhanganyila zwidiyo nendongoloso dzezibisa banhu nekwedzingwele. 30 Jidzo linopiwa banohaya Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola dzose nhambo dzakatambunuka, guhingisa zwiwanikwa zwiyapo, kuti gupe jidzo lakapelela kunabanohaya. 31 Jidzo lezwenlayo Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola dzose nhambo dzakatambunuka, guhingisa zwiwanikwa zwiyapo, kuti gubhatshe banhu basungigwa tjinyila tjaba nenlandu webudzudzu hapa behaka bhatsho besina mari yozwihakila bamilili benlayo babo. 32 Ntha dzemizano nezwifalayidza Butungamili gweHango kwakafanila tola dzose nhambo dzakatambunuka dzinomilidza mizano nefalayidzana, kuhanganyila bakanya panotigwa mizano nefalayidzana, nebanhu bose. 33 Londosedza luzibo gwemabvilo Butungamili gwehango kwakafanila tola nhambo dzelondolodza, dzedzibilila nesusumisa zila dzeluzibo, kuhanganyila luzibo gwemiti yelapa nebutjilo gwemhuka nemiti, kunozibgwa netjaba kose nebanhu. 34 Wamutjigwa netshambidzigwa kwemilayo yehango dzose Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila ba nelebeswa lekuti kose kwasungwa, nezwibvumilano zwakasiyana siyana zwehango dzose, Zimbabwe iliyimwe yadzedzo hango, kosiwa munlayo weZimbabwe. DANHO 3 Kuba Nhu 35 Ba nhu weZimbabwe

(1) Tjaba tjoba banhu beZimbabwe ngezwagwa, ngeludzi kene ngekwalisa.

(2) Banhu bose beZimbabwe bakalizanila wana ndulamilo, zwabanobvumigwa nezwimwe zwinhu zwabanowamutjidziwa kuba nhu weZimbabwe, kakale bana nlandu wethama mihingo nezwabakalingiligwa kuti bazwithame ngekuba nhu weZimbabwe.

(3) Banhu bose beZimbabwe bakalizanila wana ndulamilo nezwimwe zwinhu zwabanowamutjidziwa pehhugwi kwezwimwe zwabakapiwa ngenlayo, zwinotobela—

(a)

dzibiligwa neButungamili gweHango poni naponi kwabayapo;

(b) wana maphasiphoti nedzimwe whalo dzengina mudzimwe hango;

(c)

newana whalo dzezwagwa nezwithupha zwinopiwa ngehango.

(4) Banhu beZimbabwe banamilandu, pehhugwi kweyimwe yabanotilikiwa nenlayo, inotobela—

(a)

yekuti bazwipile kakale babe nekugagwabgwe muhango yeZimbabwe;

(b) yetebamila Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu nelemekedza zila nemakubungano ose akabumbigwa pehhugwi kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu;

(c)

yelemekedza fulegi yehango nelumbo gwehango; kakale

(d) yekuti nemasimba abo ose, badzibilile Zimbabwe nezwimila kwayo. 36 Ba nhu ngezwagwa

(1) Tjaba tjobe banhu beZimbabwe ngezwagwa hapa tjakazwaligwa muZimbabwe kakale, bakati bezwagwa—

(a)

mayi kene tatayi bakabeli nhu weZimbabwe; kene

(b) batategulu kene kukuyi bakabeli nhu weZimbabwe ngezwagwa kene ngeludzi.

(2) Tjaba tjakazwaligwa kuhhe kweZimbabwe banhu beZimbabwe ngezwagwa hapa kulikuti pakuzwagwa, umwe webazwadzi, waka elinhu weZimbabwe kakale—

(a)

egala muZimbabwe; kene

(b) elikuhhe kweZimbabwe ehingila hango kene ehingila gubungano lehango dzose.

(3) Mwana unowan’wa muZimbabwe, unamakole apasi kwe aligumi nemahanu kene unolingika se unamakole apasi kwe aligumi nemahanu, esingazibgwe kuti ndewayipi hango nebazwadzi babe besingazibgwe, unobe nhu weZimbabwe ngezwagwa. 37 Ba nhu ngeludzi Hapa kwakalingwa ndima 36(2), tjaba tjakazwaligwa kuhhe kweZimbabwe ba banhu beZimbabwe ngeludzi hapa kulikuti nelubaka bezwagwa—

(a)

umwe webazwadzi kene batategulu kene kukuyi waka elinhu weZimbabwe ngezwagwa kene ngeludzi; kene

(b)

umwe webazwadzi babo waka elinhu weZimbabwe ngekwalisa;

kakale zwagwa kukwe kwaka kwagwa muZimbabwe nezila yakatambunuka inondidzana nenlayo wekwalisa zwagwa. 38 Ba nhu ngekwalisa

(1) Kene wupi nhu waba mundobolo nenhu weZimbabwe kwemakole asipasi kwemahanu, kusanhu kwaswika kene kwatjipinda nsi wetangisa hinga, kakale egutsha mamo anolebgwa ngeTjisungo tjeNlayo

tjePhalamente, unandulamilo yekwagwa senhu weZimbabwe hapa akwala lukwalo gwekumbila kuba nhu weZimbabwe.

(2) Kene wupi nhu watjigala muZimbabwe kumunlayo kwemakole asipasi kwegumi, kusanhu kwaswika kene kwatjipinda nsi wetangisa hinga, kakale egutsha mamo anolebgwa neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente, unandulamilo yekwagwa senhu weZimbabwe hapa akwala lukwalo gwekumbila kuba nhu weZimbabwe.

(3) Mwana usinhu weZimbabwe, koga watogwa akalegwa nenhu weZimbabwe, kusanhu kwaswika kene kwatjipinda nsi wetangisa hinga, unandulamilo yekwagwa senhu weZimbabwe hapa akwala lukwalo gwekumbila kuba nhu weZimbabwe. 39 Dzimiwa kweba nhu

(1) Ba nhu weZimbabwe ngekwalisa kungadzimiwa hapa—

(a) nhu iwhoyo akawana kuba nhu ngebupitipiti, ngeleba manyepwa kene ngesumbika lebeswa linobhatika; kene

(b) hapa nhu iwhoyo wakabhatika mutengeselana kusimunlayo netjita kene akalebeleka netjita kene akabhatika mukutjidza tjita mungwa iyeyo, Zimbabwe yakabe ilimuyili bo.

(2) Ba nhu weZimbabwe ngezwagwa kungadzimiwa hapa—

(a)

nhu iwhoyo akawana kuba nhu ngebupitipiti, ngeleba manyepwa kene nesumbikiwa kwelebeswa linobhatika ngewumwe nhu; kene

(b) hapa, kulebgwa nekwenhu unobviwa mundima 36(3), kungazibgwa hango yakanyi kwabo kene mabvilo awe, kakale kulayidza kuti waka eli nhu weyimwe hango.

(3) Ba nhu weZimbabwe akuzofanila dzimiwa kutebegwa ndima iyeyi hapa nhu iwhoyo ewhosala esitjina kanyi. 40 Gala unakuba nhu nekene kuna ndobolo kene ndobolo yapalala Ba nhu weZimbabwe akutopela nentha yendobolo kene palala kwendobolo. 41 Bunga lakalingisana neNgina Muhango nekuba Nhu Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila yeta kuti kubumbiwe Bunga lakalingisana neNgina Muhango nekuba Nhu, lina nghalitulo nebamwe basipasi kwebabili, bakabigwa neNtungamili weHango, kuti lilingisane—

(a)

nepa nedzima kuba nhu ngekwalisa;

(b) nebvumila banhu, basi banhu beZimbabwe, kuti bagale kakale bahinge muZimbabwe, nebakanya milayo nemamo anolawula gala nehinga kwebanhu ibabo;

(c)

nethama imwe mihingo ingabe ingabvumigwa kene ingatshambidzigwa neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kuti itiwe neBunga ilelo.

42 Masimba ePhalamente muna zwekuba nhu Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingabamba mamo, anondidzana neDanho ileli—

(a)

endongoloso dzekuba nhu weZimbabwe ngekwalisa;

(b) ezwilambila ba nhu weZimbabwe;

(c)

ezila dzedzimiwa kweba nhu weZimbabwe ngekwalisiwa;

(d) ebgwiliziwa kwekuba nhu weZimbabwe;

(e)

elamba kuba nhu wehango mbili kwakalingwa ba nhu ngeludzi kene ngekwalisiwa; kakale

(f) eyeta kuti Danho ileli lihingisiwe. 43 Ndilambeli nemusidzigwa kwekuba nhu kwakatongobeyapo

(1) Wose nhu wakabe elinhu weZimbabwe, kusanhu kwaswika hhuba ledusigwa patjena, unondila mbeli elinhu weZimbabwe hule kwensi iwhoyo.

(2) Wose nhu wakazwaligwa muZimbabwe, kusanhu kwaswika hhuba ledusigwa patjena, unhu weZimbabwe ngezwagwa hapa—

(a)

bubili gwabo kene un’ompela webazwadzi babe wakabe elinhu wehango yakaba nhengo yeHango dzikubuRwa gweAfrika Dzinodlidzana (Southern African Development Community) yakabumbiwa netjibvumilwano tjakatiwa kuWindhoek muhango yeNamibia nsi wa17 Ndla mugole la1992; kakale

(b) wakabe egala muZimbabwe nehhuba ledusigwa patjena. DANHO 4 Tshambidzigwa Kwendulamilo

LUBABI 1 Hingisiwa Netjenesiwa Kwedanho 4 44 Nlandu wezwisapasi kwendulamilo dzebanhu nensununguko dzakazinda Butungamili gweHango nenhu wose, nebanhu benlayo, nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose, kwakafanila zwisapasi, dzibilila, kudza nehhadzisa ndulamilo nensununguko dzinolebgwa muDanho ileli. 45 Hingisiwa kweDanho 4

(1) Ileli Danho lobhatanidza Butungamili gwehango neButungamili gose, nhabi nemakubungano enlayo nenhabi dzose dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose.

(2) Ileli Danho lobhatanidza tjaba nebanhu bezwemilayo nezila yakabalizanila, kwakalingisisiwa tjimo tjendulamilo kene tjelusununguko gunobviwa kose nen’hingo unotilikiwa neDanho.

(3) Banhu bezwemilayo netjaba bana ndulamilo nensununguko dzinolebgwa muDanho ileli, kunoswika nhambo dzekutii idzedzo ndulamilo nensununguko dziswike kubali. 46

Tjenesewa kweDanho 4

(1) Hapa kutjenesewa Danho ileli, khuta, lubahhe, n’hanagno kene bunga—

(a)

kwakafanila hingisa nezila yakapelela ndulamilo nensununguko dzinobviwa muDanho ileli;

(b) kwakafanila kudza bunhu nemilenje inowalila zwitonga kwetjaba, kutebegwa butjena, ndulamo, bhakhwa nezila inabunhu, lizana nesununguka, kwazo kwazo nebunhu nemilenje inobviwa mundima 3;

(c) kwakafanila linga nlayo wehango dzose nayiko kose kwakasungwa nezwibvumilano zwakasiyana siyana zwehango dzose, Zimbabwe iliyimwe yayidzedzo hango;

(d)

kwakafanila tobela kose kunolebgwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, kwazo kwazo zila nezwinangwa zwinolebgwa muDanho 2; kakale

(e)

kunga hingisa nlayo wedzimwe hango unoyendidzana nenlayo weZimbabwe;

pehhugwi kwelingisiswa kwe amwe mamo ose anotobegwa, hapa kutjenesiwa Nlayo Nkulu.

(2) Hapa kutjenesewa tshambidzilo, kakale hapa kubumbiwa nlayo nenlayo wemabvilo, imwe neyimwe khuta, lubahhe, n’hangano kene bunga, kwakafanila milidza kakale kutungamigwe nemeya nezwinangwa zweDanho ileli. 47 Danho 4 halitodzibilila bayapo kwedzimwe ndulamilo Danho ileli alitodzibila bepo kwedzimwe ndulamilo nensununguko dzingalemekedziwa kene bvumigwa nenlayo, nezila yekuti dzondilana neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu. LUBABI 2 Ndulamilo Nensununguko Dzakazinda 48 Ndulamilo yetjila

(1) Wose nhu unandulamilo yetjila.

(2) Nlayo ungabvumila ntongo webulayiwa kuti utilikiwe kunhu koga unobe awanika enanlandu webulaya nezila inabuhongola, kakale—

(a)

nlayo wakafanila bvumila khuta kuti ihale tilika kene satilika nhu ntongo iwhoyo;

(b)

ntongo whoyo ungatilikiwa koga hapa kutobegwa tjisungo tjahhisiwa nekhuta inamatatabuko akalizanila tonga nlandu umumamo akajalo;

(c)

ntongo iwhoyo awuzofanela tilikiwa nhu—

(i) waka enamakole apasi kwe alimakumi mabili anagole nelubaka kutikala jinyo; kene

(ii) unamakole anopinda alimakumi alikunotondeka;

(d) ntongo iwhoyo awuzofanila tilikiwa kene tiwa munhu wetjikadzi;

(e)

nenhu watilikiwa ntongo iwhoyo unandulamilo yekumbila mazwibhatigwa kene umwe ntongo Ntungamili weHango.

(3) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila dzibilila butjilo gwebana basanhu bakazwagwa, kakale itjetjo Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjakafanila leba kuti mimba ingadusiwa koga kutobegwa nawhoyo nlayo. 49 Ndulamilo yesununguka kwenhu

(1) wose nhu unandulamilo yesununguka, inohanganyila ndulamilo—

(a)

yesahhaliligwa esanhu asekesewa; kakale

(b) yesalambigwa nsununguko yabo mwaza kene kusimunlayo.

(2) Akuna nhu angasiwa mujele nentha yekon’wa hhadzisa tjibvumilano koga.

50 Ndulamilo dzebanhu bakasungwa nebakahhaliligwa

(1) Wose nhu wasungwa—

(a)

wakafanila zibisiwa nelubaka esungwa kuti wasungigwani;

(b) wakafanila bvumigwa, tjinyolotjo—

(i) Nendleko dzeHango kuti akunhane nenkadzi kene nlume uwe, kene hama kene milili wenlayo kene umwe nhu waanozwihalila;

(ii) Nedzidze ndleko kuti abonane neMilili uwe kene unohinga zwebuwongelo kakale azibisiwe nekwendulamilo iyeyo tjinyolotjo;

(c)

wakafanila bhakwa nebunhu kakale kuhloniphiwe bunhu gugwe;

(d) wakafanila kuti asunungugwe kusina miganhu kene kuna miganhu inowhisisika, akamilila tilikwa nlandu kene sekesiwa kuhhe kwekuti apa kuna mabaka akasima anota kukafanila kuti andile mbeli akasungwa kakale;

(e)

abvumigwe kuti agwise sabe mulanyo kwesungwa kukwe iye nekukwe mbeli kwekhuta kakale asunungugwe tjinyolotjo apa kuwanika kuti wasungwa nezila isi munlayo.

(2) Wose nhu wasungwa kene wahhaliligwa—

(a)

Kudza andisiwe kukhuta; kene

(b) Nentha yekuthi watilikwa nlandu; Kakale usasunungugwa wakafanila ndisiwa kukhutha nekulibilidza kunokhoneka kakale kene muna tjipi tjimo kusanhu kwapinda ma awa elimakumi mana anahhanalume hule kwesungwa kukwe kene kwehhaliligwa kukwe kunda nekuti kuyiko ndikupi, kusineni kuti lubaka igogo gopela kuli Nghibelo, Tshipi kene hhuba lenyaluluka kwetjaba.

(3) Wose nhu usandisiwa kukhutha muma awa elimakumi mana sekunolebwa mundimana (2) wakafanila kuti asunungugwe tjinyolotjo kuhhe kwekuti hhaliligwa kukwe kwapamhidzigwa nekhutha inasimba lakajalo.

(4) Wose nhu wasungwa kene wahhaliligwa ena nlandu waanotilikwa unandulamilo—

(a)

yenyala asingalebe tjimwe;

(b) yezibisiwa tjinyolotjo;

(i) ndulamilo iye yenyalala asingalebe tjimwe; kakale

(ii) tjinootikala apa enganyalala kene apa esanyalala;

(c)

kuti asimanikidziwe zwilebulule kene abvume nlandu; kakale

(d) pakuma kukwe kukhutha kwekutanga hule kwesungwa, kuti atilikwe nlandu kene azibisiwe kuti ngeni akafanila ndila mbeli akahhaliligwa, kene asunungugwe.

(5) Wose nhu wakahhaliligwa, kuhanganyila nsungwa wakatongewa, unandulamilo—

(a)

yezibisiwa tjinyolotjo mabaka ehhaliligwa kukwe;

(b) nendleko dzidze, abonane kwakasitila neMilili uwe wenlayo waanozwihalila; kakale azibisiwe nekwendulamilo iyeyi tjinyolotjo;

(c)

kuti akunhane, kakale akumbiwe—

(i) nenkwadzi kene nlume uwe kene nhu waanodana naye;

(ii) hama;

(iii) ndhiyi webugalimoyo waanozwihalila;

(iv) milili wemilayo waanozwihalila;

(v) n’hingi wezwebuwongelo waanozwihalila; kakale

(vi) kunda nemiganhu inosiwa kudza jele nedzimwe ntha dzehhaliligwa dzihinge zwibuyanana, akunhane kakale alebesane nemwe nemwe nhu waanohala;

(d) Ntha dzehhaliligwa dzinondidzana netjilemela nebunhu, kuhanganyila ntha yezwitambunudza, ntha yegala inolipiwa nahulumente, tjin’umbana tjendila kuhango, tjena kukwe, zodliwa zwinobaka mbili, whalo dzabala dzakafanila nelapiwa; kakale

(e)

abvumigwe kuti agwise sabe mulanyo kwesungwa kukwe iye nekukwe mbeli kwekhuta kakale asunungugwe tjinyolotjo apa kuwanika kuti wasungwa nezila isi munlayo.

(6) Wose nhu unohhaliligwa etilikwa nlandu kakale usasekesiwa nelubaka gwakafanila wakafanila kuti asunungugwe kusina miganhu kene kuna miganhu inowhisisia kudza kuti hule kwekube asunungugwa—

(a)

abepo apa esekisiwa;

(b) kuti asitjinyidzile busupi gwakafanila piwa pakusekisiwa; kakale

(c)

atothama umwe nlandu kakale sekisiwa kusanhu kwatangisa.

(7) Apa kuna kumbulila kunobhatika kwekuti nhu wakahhaliligwa kusi munlayo kene kusina zila yekuti kuwhakale kuti nhu wahhaliligwa uponi, kene yani zwe nhu anganda kuKhutha ipehhugwi ehaka ndawulo—

(a)

yehabeascorpus, kubva kuti nlayo unoti nhu wahhaliligwa adusiwe, kene ahhisiwe mukhuta kudze kutjenhesiwe kuti hhaliligwa kukwe kumunlayo, kene kuhakika kuti kuzibgwe kuti nhu wakahhaliligwa wahhaliligwa poni; kene

(b) wanika kuti hhaliligwa kwenhu iwhoyo akutamunlayo neyeta kuti nhu iwhoyo wakahhaliligwa adusiwe nekulibilidza; neKhutha Whulu ingadusa ndawulo inondidzana netjimo itjetjo.

(8) Sungiwa kene hhaliligwa kunoleyana nendima iyeyi, kene satebegwa kwemamo anolebgwa munayeyi ndima, akutapo munlayo.

(9) Wose nhu wasungwa kene wahhaliligwa nezila isimunlayo unandulamilo yelipiwa nenhu wathama kuti asungiwe kene ahhaliligwe, koga nlayo ungadzibilila banhu banotebela kuti basilipisiwe kutebegwa ndima iyeyi—

(a)

nhu unohingila zwemilayo ethama kose ikoko etebela nlayo kakale enabugalibgwe kuti tjaanothama tjakatambunuka;

(b) kene upi nhu unohingila tjaba wathama kose ikoko nezila yakatambunuka kakale enabugalibgwe kuti tjaanothama tjakatambunuka kakale eziba tjaanothama kene esingathame mwaza. 51 Ndulamilo yebhakhwa nezila inabunhu Umwe nemwe nhu wakafanila lemekedzewa mubutjilo gugwe gupatjena kene gwakasumbikala, kakale unandulamilo yekuti iyeyo zila yetjila kukwe ilemekedzewe kakale idzibiligwe. 52 Ndulamilo yowahhika kwenhu Umwe nemwe nhu unandulamilo yelemekedzewa mbili kene nkumbulo, inohanganyisila ndulamilo—

(a)

2013

yesununguka muhunguludziwa kose kunobva mutjaba kene ntome;

(b) kutobegwa kumwe kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, yeyeta zwisungo zwezwalana;

(c)

yesahingisiwa muhakuludzo yezwelapa kene yezwesayensi, kene togwa kene hingisiwa kwenyama dzabo dzembili, besazibisiwa bekabvuma.

53 Lusununguko mukudziyidziwa kene mukayikanyiwa, mukubhakhwa nezila isina bunhu kene inolengudza kene langiwa kunojitisa ntuzi Hakuna nhu wakafanila kayikanyiwa kene bhakhwa nezila inodziyidza mbili kene nkumbulo, isina bunhu kene inolengudza kene langiwa kunodusa ntuzi. 54 Lusununguko gwesabhakhwa bulanda Hakuna nhu wakafanila kuti abhakhwe bulanda. 55 Lusununguko gwesamanikidziwa hinga kene hingisiwa kusingalambike Hakuna nhu wakafanila manikidziwa kene hingisiwa kusingalambike. 56 Lizana nesahalugwa

(1) Banhu bose bakalizana mbeli kwenlayo kakale bana ndulamilo yedzibiligwa netjidziwa kwakalizana, nenlayo.

(2) Betjikadzi nebetjilume bana ndulamilo yebhakhwa kunolizana, kuhanganyisila ndulamilo yewana mikoba yakalizana munazwepolitiki, munazwefumwa yehango, muna zwemilenje nezwegalisana kwebanhu.

(3) Wose nhu undulamilo yesabhakhwa nezila inohalula nentha yekuti ebva kunayipi hango, ganda lile, mbala uwe, ludzi gugwe, kwaakazwaligwa, bunhu gugwe, lulimi gugwe, tjimo tjebutjilo gugwe, bugalimoyo gugwe, ludo gwezwepolitiki, zwaanokumbula, mabvilo, milenje, bukadzi nebulume, tjimo tjendobolo, makole ezwagwa, zwisenga, kubadzika kene tjimo tjefumwa kene tjegalisana, kene kuti bakazwaligwa mundobolo kene kuhhe kwendobolo.

(4) Kutebegwa ndimana (3) nhu unobhakhwa nezila inakuhalula hapa—

(a)

abhakhwa nezila bamwe banhu yabasingabhakhwe nawo, adzibisiwa bamwe banhu besingadzibisiwe kene abhakhwa nezila bamwe yabasingabhakhwe nayo; kene

(b) bamwe banhu bewamutjidziwa kene bepiwa jidzo laasingawane.

(5) Halugwa neyimwe yezila dzalebgwa mundimana (3) akuzotambunuka, kuhhe kwekuti kwawanika kuti halugwa ikoko kwakatambunuka, kowhisisika kakale kotjeneseka mutjaba tjinozwitonga kutebegwa bhudila patjena, ndulamo, bhakhwa nezila inabunhu, lizana nesununguka.

(6) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola nhambo dzenlayo nedzimwe nhambo dzakatambunuka kuti gususume lizana kwebanhu nedzibilila kene kudza banhu kene banhu bakasiyana siyana bakatshambidzigwa nentha yehalugwa, kakale—

(a)

nhambo dzakajalo dzakafanila tolegwa tambunudza zwimo zwakafanila tambunudziwa;

(b) akuna nhambo dzakajalo dzingayi adzizotambunuka kwakalingwa ndimana (3).

57 Ndulamilo yesumbikika Nhu wose una ndulamilo yesumbikika, inohanganyila ndulamilo yekuti—

(a)

nzi wabo, zwibakwa zwabo kene nhundu dzabo kusingin’we besabvuma;

(b) mibili yabo, nzi wabo, zwibakwa zwabo, kene nhundu dzabo kusibvukudziwe besabvuma;

(c)

zwabanazo zwisintshariwe;

(d) zuwa kwabo kwakasumbikala kunginiligwe; kene

(e)

jimo tjebutjilo gwabo kudusigwe patjena.

58 Lusununguko gwekubungana nehanganyila

(1) Wose nhu unandulamilo yelusununguko gwekubungana nehanganyila, nendulamilo yesakubungana kene sahanganyila nebamwe.

(2) Akuna nhu ungamanikidziwa kuti abenhengo yegubungano kene kuti ayende kun’hangano kene kwakakubunganiwa. 59 Lusununguko gwebhudila patjena neyendesa phethishini Wose nhu unandulamilo yebhudila patjena neyendesa maphethishini, koga ndulamilo idzedzi dzakafanila hingisiwa nekunyalala. 60 Lusununguko gwenkumbulo

(1) Wose nhu unandulamilo yelusununguko gwenkumbulo, kunohanganyila—

(a)

lusununguko gwenkumbulo, mbono, lunamato kene bugalimoyo;

(b) nelusununguko gwethama nesumikila nezwilayidzila nkumbulo wabo, mbono, wabo lunamato kene bugalimoyo gwabo, palenje kene pakasumbikala kakale eliyega kene enabamwe.

(2) Hakuna nhu ungamanikidziwa kuti atune kwakaleyana nelunamato kene bugalimoyo gugwe kene kuti atune nezila yakaleyana nelunamato kene bugalimoyo gugwe.

(3) Bazwadzi nebanolela bana batukununu banandulamilo yehala, kuyendilana nebugalimoyo gwabo, bunhu nelunamato gwekudza bana babo, koga nezila isingatshambidzile ndulamilo dzinopiwa bana pasi kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, kuhanganyila ndulamilo dzezwidiyo, dzewongeka, dzedzibiligwa nedzebutjilo bubuya.

(4) Kene lipi gubungano lelunamato lingahhula nhabi panodiyiwa lunamato igogo nekene lutabi igogo guwana bhatsho lemari kuButungamili gweHango. 61

Lusununguko gwezwilayidza nelusununguko gweyendisiwa kwendebo

(1) Wose nhu una ndulamilo yelusununguko gwezwilayidza, inohanganyila—

(a)

lusununguko gwehaka, gwewamutjila, negweyendesa mikumbulo negumwe luzibo;

(b) lusununguko gwezwilayidza nemizano nehakuludzo yesayensi nebunyambi;

(c)

nelusununguko muzwidiyo.

(2) Wose nhu unalusununguko gwezweyendesewa kwendebo, gunohanganyila dzibilila dusila patjena kunobva ndebo dzentoli wendebo.

(3) Makhampani ano andamisa ne anoyendisa mibiko nedzimwe zila mumhepo analusununguko gwebambiwa, koga etobela ndongoloso dzepa ma layisensi kwehango dzinobe—

(a)

dzihakika kwazo kuti dzilawule hingisiwa kwemhepo nedzimwe zila dzeandamisa mumhepo; kakale

(b) dzakazwimila dzisingalawugwe nekunohakiwa nahulumenete, kene nezwepolitiki kene nezwemabhizinesi.

(4) Dzose khampani dzeyendisa mibiko dzinolawugwa nahulumente dzakafanila—

(a)

kuti dzisununguke kudza dzibakanye, dzakazwimila, tjinowholebgwa mundebo dzawo dzino andamisiwa nedzimwe dzinoyendisiwa nedzimwe zila;

(b) kuti dzisitole lubihho dzibe pakati napakati; kakale

(c)

kuti dzipe nkoba wakafanila wetugwa kwemikumbulo inokakabadzana nemikumbulo inopesana.

(5) Lusununguko gwezwilayidza nelusununguko gwezwedzindebo akutohanganyisa—

(a)

kulumbidza hunguludza;

(b) susuma lubengo kene ndebo dzelubengo;

(c)

kubadziwa kwakayipa kwetjimo tjenhu kene bunhu gugwe; kene

(d) nginiligwa kwezwiphiri zwenhu kwakayipa kene kusakafanila. 62 Swikilila luzibo

(1) Wose nhu nenghali weZimbabwe, kuhanganyila banhu benlayo nezwedzindebo muZimbabwe, unandulamilo yeswikilila kene gupi luzibo gwakabhakhwa neHango kene nalipi gubungano kene lutabi gwahulumente mumatanhilo ose, apa luzibo igogo guhakigwa kuti kuse patjena zila yehingigwa kwebanhu nahulumente.

(2) Wose nhu, kuhanganyila nezwedzindebo muZimbabwe, unandulamilo yeswikilila kene gupi luzibo gwakabhakhwa kene naani, kuhanganyila neHango, apa luzibo igogo guhakigwa susuma kene dzibilila ndulamilo.

(3) Wose nhu unandulamilo yetambunudza luzibo, kene dzima luzibo gusi lebeswa, gusakapelela kene gunopesa, gwakabhakhwa neHango kene nalipi gubungano kene lutabi gwahulumente mumatanhilo ose, gunoyendidzana nenhu iwhoyo.

(4) Nlayo wakafanila tshambidzigwa kuti upe masimba kundulamilo iyeyi, koga ungasabvumila swikilika kwematama kuyendidzana nedzibilila, dzibilika kwetjaba kene sadusa patjena zwebumhihha, munhambo dzekuti kwakatambunuka, kowhakala, kwakalizanyila, kakale kwakalulwama mutjaba tjakasununguka, tibva nekwemamo etjenesedzo, ndulamo dzenlayo, lemekedzeka kwenhu, lizana nesununguka. 63 Lulimi nemilenje Wose nhu unandulamilo—

(a) yehingisa lulimi gwaada;

(b) nebhatika mubutjilo gwemilenje yaanoda; koga akuna nhu unohingisa ndulamilo idzedzo ungakuthama nezila yakapeyama isingandilane neDanho ileli.

64 Lusununguko gwebumhihha negwen’hingo Wose nhu unandulamilo yehala nethama bumhihha, tengeselana kene hinga, koga tiwa kwebumhihha, tengeselana kene hinga kube munlayo. 65 Ndulamilo dzehinga

(1) Wose nhu unandulamilo yehingisiwa nezila dzakahalabvuka kakale dzakatambunuka nelipiwa nlipo unoyendilana nen’hingo watiwa.

(2) Kuhhe kwebanohinga dzibiligwa kwehango, wose nhu unandulamilo yethama nehanganyila mumakubungano anomila bahingi nemakubungano ebahingi kene ebanohingigwa abanozwihalila, nebhatika munazwinotiwa nayiwo makubungano zwimunlayo.

(3) Kuhhe kwebanohinga dzibiligwa kwehango, wose n’hingi unandulamilo yehanganyila mulayidza sagutsikana mun’hingo, nendulamilo yen’un’una nemilikila sabhakhwa zwakatambunuka mumihingo, gala, bhuda mun’hingo kene zwimwe zwiyeto zwinondilana nayikoku, koga nlayo ungaganula idzedzo ndulamilo kutigwa kuti kube nekunyalala.

(4) Wose n’hingi unandulamilo yemamo en’hingo mabuya, anogutsha.

(5) Kuhhe kwebanohinga dzibiligwa kwehango, wose n’hingi, unohingigwa, gubungano linomila bahingi negubungano lebahingi kene banohingigwa lina ndulamilo—

(a)

yelebelekela nhu wose;

(b)

yebakanya;

(c)

neyethama nengina mumabunga emakubungano akajalo.

(6) Banhu betjikadzi nebetjilume bana ndulamilo yelipiwa kwakalizana pakuthana mihingo inolizana.

(7) Bahingi betjikadzi bana ndulamilo yewana nlipo yakakwana kwemimwedzi mitatu kene inopinda mitatu besikun’hingo bakalindila tshibama kene batjitshibama. 66 Lusununguko gwebugalo neyenda

(1) Wose nhuweZimbabwe—

(a)

unandulamilo yengina muZimbabwe;

(b)

unakudzibililika mukutakhwa muZimbabwe;

(c)

unandulamilo yewana maphasiphoti nedzimwe whalo dzengina mudzimwe hango.

(2) Wose nhu weZimbabwe nemwe nemwe nhu umuZimbabwe kumunlayo unandulamilo—

(a)

yeyenda akahalabvuka muZimbabwe;

(b)

yegala kene ntha yipi yeZimbabwe;

(c)

neyebhuda muZimbabwe.

67 Ndulamilo dzezwepolitiki

(1) Wose nhu weZimbabwe unandulamilo—

(a)

yehalulo yakahalabvuka, yakatambunuka, yehala milili wetjaba kutobegwa eNlayo nkulu iwhoyu kene neyimwe milayo;

(b) nehala kwakahalabvuka ndima dzakalingisana netjimo tjetongewa kwehango.

(2) Nentha yawhoyu Nlayo Nkulu, wose nhu unabunhu gweZimbabwe unandulamilo—

(a) yebamba, yengina nebhatika munazwinotiwa negubungano lepolitiki kene seda kukwe;

(b) yelebelekela kene mila gubungano lezwepolitiki akahalabvuka kakale nezila inakunyalala;

(c)

yebhatika munazwepolitiki nezila inakunyalala;

(d) neyebhatika, aliyega kene bali banjinji, mumihangano kene mumakubungano kene munatjipi tjimo, nezila inakunyalala, yen’un’una kene bvuma ndongoloso dzaHulumente kene zwepolitiki kene tjipi tjimo.

(3) Kutobegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, nhu wose weZimbabwe unamakole aligumi nehhanakadzi kene anopinda ipapo unandulamilo—

(a)

yevota muhalulo dzose nemareferendamu anotungamigwa nawhoyu Nlayo Nkulu kene upi zwawo nlayo, kakale akute ikoku akazwisumbika kakale

(b) yengina muhalulo kuti ahaluligwe hingila tjaba kakale, hapa ahalugwa, agale mubugalo igogo.

(4) Kutila kuti kube netjimo tjezwepolitiki tjinakunyalala kakale tjina makubungano ezwepolitiki manjinji, kwakafanila kuti kube neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjinowhopa makubungano ezwepolitiki mari. 68 Ndulamilo yebhakhwa nezila inandulamo

(1) Wose nhu unandulamilo yebhakhwa zwibuyanana kumunlayo, kunakulibilidza, kunogutshikanya, kunowhakala, kwakalizanila, kusinga halule kakale kunandongoloso nenzinda dzakalulwama.

(2) Nhu unoba ndulamilo iye, lusununguko, tjaanoda kene tjaakafanila wana tjimunlayo kusabhakhwa nezila yakatambunuka, unandulamilo yepiwa nekulibilidza lukwalo gunolayidza kuti nentha yeni esabhakhwa nezila yakatambunuka.

(3) Ttjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila pa masimba ndulamilo idzedzi, kakale tjakafanila—

(a)

yeta kuti khutha yenlayo ilinge mazwibhato ebanhu bakabhata kene, hapa kuhakika, alingiwe nelubahhe gwenlayo gwakazwimila kakale gusingahalawule;

(b) pa nlandu Butungamili gweHango kuti ipe masimba kundulamilo dzimundimana (1) na (2);

(c)

nesusuma kuti kube nebhakhwa kwebanhu kunogutsha.

69 Ndulamilo yesekewa kwakatambunuka

(1) Wose nhu watilikiwanlandu unandulamilo yesekewa kwakatambunuka patjena mulubaka gunowhisisika, mukhutha yakazwimila kakale isingahalawule.

(2) Pakubakanyiwa kwendulamilo dzebahingi bahulumente nemihingo yakabafanila, wose nhu unandulamilo yesekewa kwakatambunuka, netjinyolotjo kakale kupatjena mulubaka gunowhisisik, amukhuta yakazwimila kakale isingahalawule, lubahhe kene umwe n’hangano wakabumbiwa nenlayo.

(3) Wose nhu unandulamilo yeswikilila dzikhutha, kene dzimwe mbahhe kene n’hangano wakabumbiwa nenlayo kuti utambunudze sawhisisana kungabe kulipo.

(4) Wose nhu unandulamilo, yehala kakale amigwe nemilili wenlayo kene munayipi zwayo khutha, lubahhe kene n’hangano, ezwilipila. 70 Ndulamilo dzebanhu batilikiwa milandu

(1) Nhu watilikiwa nlandu unandulamilo dzinotobela—

(a)

kuti elingwe senhu usina nlandu adzine anowan’wa nlandu;

(b) kuti azibisiwe nekulibilidza nlandu uwe, etjenesegwa nezila yekuti unotubula zwihandulila;

(c) kuti apiwe lubaka gwakakwana nezwinhu zwinowhowuntjidza pa ebakanyila zwimila;

(d) kuti ahale milili wenlayo kakale, ezwilipila, kuti amigwe nemilili wenlayo iwhoyo;

(e)

kuti amigwe nemilili wenlayo wahhisiwa neHango kakale elipiwa neHango, hapa kungatikala kuti asitongwe kwakatambunuka;

(f)

kuti azibisiwe nekulibilidza nekwendulamilo dzalebgwa mutjikamu (d) na (e);

(g) kuti abeyapo pakutongwa kukwe;

(h) kuti ahhise kakale alambe busupi gwahhisiwa;

(i)

kuti alambe akanyalala kakale asihandule mibhuzo nekwenlandu wawatilikiwa kene asimanikidziwe kuti ahhise busupi gunowhota kuti awan’we nlandu;

(j)

kuti ndongoloso dzetongwa kukwe dzilebgwe nelulimi gwaanowhisisa.

(k) asiwan’we nlandu netjaakathama kene tjaakatjuluka tjaka tjisi jinyo lubaka esekesiwa;

(l)

asiwan’we nlandu netjaakathama kene tjaakatjuluka tjisingatjabagwe sejinyo;

(m) asisekegwe jinyo kwakalingwa tjaakathama kene tjaakatjuluka tjaakatigwa mazwibhatigwa kene awan’wa asina nlandu kene awan’wa nlandu nebusupi guyapo;

(n) kuti apiwe ntongo upasi kwentongo wakalizanila jinyo ilelo hapa kulikuti ntongo wakalizanila jinyo ilelo wakapamidziwa kene wakajitisiwa pakati kwelubaka kutikala nlandu nelubaka gwetongwa.

(2) Kunoba ndima iyeyi ihaka kuti nhu apiwe luzibo kene atjenesegwe—

(a) wakafanila tjenesegwa nelulimi gwaanowhisisa; kakale

(b) hapa nhu iwhoyo esingatubule kwala kene bala, whalo dziyapo wakafanila dzitjenesegwa nezila inowhota kuti awhisise.

(3) Musekesiwa kwenlandu busupi gwawan’wa nezila isingandilane nekwaka kwagwa muDanho ileli aguzofanila hingisiwa hapa kulikuti hingisiwa kwago kungata kuti seko lisitambunuke, kene gungatjinya hingisiwa kwendulamo kene kunohanhisa tjaba.

(4) Wose nhu wasekesiwanlandu una ndulamilo yelipa kumunlayo nepiwa lukwalo gunolakidza zwakatikala, mulubakanyana kwatjipela seko.

(5) Wose nhu watjisekewa ekawan’wa enanlandu una ndulamilo, koga ingaganhugwa kutebegwa nlayo, —

(a)

yeyendisanlandu kuti ulingwe kakale nekhuta ipehhugwi kuna yaseka; kene

(b) yekumbila kuti khutha ipehhugwi kuna yaseka ilingisise wan’wa nlandu kukwe nentongo uwe. 71 Ndulamilo dzenhundu

(1) Mundima iyeyi— “zwawunowamutjidziwa mupentjeni” kobviwa pentjeni, mari inolipiwa kene imwe zwayo mari inolipiwa—

(a) umwe newumwe nhu ibva muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente;

(b) kubokewa hinga kwenhu newayehingigwa;

(c) nentha yegwala kene kubala kwenhu; kene

(d) nentha yekuti nhu waswika makole esiya n’hingo kene gwala kene kumwe bokakale inohanganyisila mari ipasi kwepentjeni iyeyo nebgwiliziwa mari yawake ulipa yakalingisana nepentjeni iyeyo; “nhundu” kobviwa nhundu kene yakatini nekene yipi ndulamilo kene njemulo munhundu.

(2) Kwakalingwa ndima 72, wose nhu unandulamilo, kene poni kweZimbabwe, yewana, yebhata, yegala, yehingisa, yepa umwe, yetengesa kene laha nhundu dzose, aliyega kene ehingidzana nebamwe.

(3) Kwakalingwa ndima iyeyi nendima 72, akuna nhu ungamanikidziwa tolegwa nhundu dzidze kuhhe kwepanohhadzisiwa mamo anotebela—

(a)

tolegwa ikoku kulimunlayo unohingisiwa;

(b) tolegwa ikoku kwakafanila tiwa nentha yekumwe kwekunotobela—

(i) nentha yedzibilila, dzibililika kwetjaba, gadzikana kwetjaba, bunhu gwetjaba, wongeka kwetjaba kene ndongoloso dzemadoropo nemakanyi; kene

(ii) kutigwa kuti kuhingisiwe nhundu idzedzo kene dzimwe nhundu kuti kutiwe tjinhu tjinowhobe jidzo mutjaba tjose;

(c)

nlayo unohaka kuti banamasimba otola nhundu—

(i) bape nhu wose, unamatatabuko kene ndulamilo munhundu unobhatikana netogwa kwenhundu idzedzo, tjizibiso tjinowhisisika tjohaka tola nhundu;

(ii) balipe nlipo wetogwa wakalizanila newakakwana kusanhu kukaswika lubaka gwetogwa kwenhundu kene kwapinda lubaka gunowhisisika nhundu dzatogwa; kakale

(iii) hapa togwa kwenhundu kwalambiwa, wakafanila kumbila khuta inaluzibo kusanhu kukatogwa nhundu, kene makumi matatu emahhuba esanhu ekaswika kuti khuta ikupe masimba otola nhundu;

(d)

nlayo unopa masimba nhu unanhundu dzatogwa kusimunlayo kuti akumbile khuta inaluzibo kudze abgwiliziwe nhundu dzidze nekulibilidza; kakale

(e)

nlayo unopa masimba nhu unohaka nhundu dzidze kuti akumbile mukhuta inaluzibo kudze kuzibgwe—

(i) bayapo, tjimo nekuti njemulo dzabo ndedzemalini munhundu idzedzo;

(ii) kuti togwa kwenhundu kumunlayo kene akutamunlayo; kakale

(iii) mari yabakafanila lipiwa; kakale kuti bakumbile khuta ibabhatshe balipiwe mari dzabakafanela piwa nekulibilidza.

(4) hapa nhu apiwa kene enandulamilo yewana pentjini, nlayo unodzima ndulamilo iyeyo unohingisiwa, kutobegwa ndimana (3), senlayo unopa masimba etola nhundu. 72

Ndulamilo dzewana hango yelima nepfuwa

(1) Mundima iyeyi— “hango yelima nepfuwa” kobviwa hango inohingisiwa kene ingahingisiwa lima nepfuwa, kubviwa zwilimo zwengadi, miti kene pfuyiwa kwedzimuka, kuhanganisila—

(a) pfuwa nezwalisa zwipfuwo, mhuka dzehango, whuku, mhuka kene nyutji; kene

(b) fulo lezwipfuwo kene lemhuka;

koga ayitohanganyila hango yebaka mizi yegala kene hango imunghanu dzematoropo kene dzematoropo matukununu amunlayo wakalingisana nema kwematoropo kene nemunlayo wakalingisana nelingwa kwemavu;

“hango” kobviwa kose kwakabhatana nemavu kene kwakakula mumavu; “lubabi gwehango yelima nepfuwa” kobviwa lubabi gwehango yelima nepfuwa gwakakwalisiwa muLukwalo gwe‘Didzi’ sehango imile yoga.

(2) Hapa hango yelima nepfuwa, kene yipi zwayo ndulamilo kene njemulo muhango yakajalo, ihakika kuti ihingisiwe netjaba kene ihingisiwe tjinowhobhatsha tjaba, kunohanganyila—

(a)

nghalo yelima nepfuwa kene imwe mihingo zwayo;

(b)

gadziwa kutshwa, miti, dzibiligwa kwezwiwan’wa zwehango kene hingisiwa kwemhuka kene zwimwe zwiwan’wa zwehango; kene

(c)

gadziwa kwebanhu bakafulusiwa nentha yehingisiwa kwehango kwalebgwa mutjikamu (a) kene (b);

hango, ndulamilo kene njemulo kungaba kwaHulumente nedusigwa patjena nekwagwa muGazethi kulebgwa kuti hango iyeyo ndiyipi, ndulamilo kene njemulo yehango, ndulamilo kene njemulo kumumasimba aHulumente kakale unobamweni nensi wedusigwa patjena.

(3) Hapa hango, ndulamilo kene njemulo yehango, inowanikala kuti ihingisiwe kunolebgwa mundimana (2)—

(a)

kutebegwa ndima 295 (1) na (2), hakuna mbhayilo unolipigwa togwa kwehango, kuhhe kwen’hingo wakatiwa mukupfumbidza hango iyeyo kusanhu kwaswika lubaka gwetogwa;

(b)

hakuna nhu ungakwalila khutha mibhuzo nekwe mbhayilo, kuhhe kwen’hingo wakatiwa mukupfumbidza hango iyeyo kusanhu kwaswika lubaka gwetogwa, kakale akuna khuta ingada wamutja mubhuzo yakajalo; kakale

(c)

togwa kwehango akungatongo lambiwa kene n’un’uniwa kuyi kwatiwa nezila inalubihho isakatambunuka yakaleyana nendima 56

(4) Yose hango yelima nepfuwa—

(a)

hakalebgwa muNdongoloso yeGunotendeka yeNlayo Nkulu wakapinda; kene

(b)

kusenhu kukaswika nsi wehinga, yakalebgwa mundima 16B(2) (a)(ii) kene

(iii) muNlayo Nkulu wakapinda;

inondilambeli iliyaHulumente, kakale akuna mbhayilo unolipigwa togwa kwehango kuhhe kwen’hingo wakatiwa mukupfumbidza hango iyeyo kusanhu kwaswika lubaka gwetogwa.

(5) Hule kwetogwa kumunlayo kwehango yelima nepfuwa kutebegwa ndimana (2), nhu unohinga wakalingisana nekwalisiwa kwebeni behango wakafanila, nekulibilidza, tambumudza mudziwhalo edusa zina lewayelimweni wayeyohango ekwala kuti hango iyeyo yabe yahulumente.

(6) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjephalemente tjingata kuti kubenlandu hapa nhu, esingatebele nlayo, etola kene egala muhango yelima nepfuwa inolebgwa mundima iyeyi kene imwe hango imumaboko aHulumente.

(7) Kwakalingwa nekwetogwa kwehango kumunlayo kugadziwa butshwa banhu kutebegwa ndongoloso yekobewa butshwa kwehango, mamo anotebela akafanila lemekedzewa kwazo kwazo—

(a)

mulubaka banhu beZimbabwe betjipasi kweButungamili gweMakhuwa bakafulusiwa muhango dzabo nezila dzisakatambunuka kakale bakafulusiwa muzwiwan’wa zwabo nezila isakatambunuka besingalipiwe;

(b)

banhu bakayenda kungwa kuti bawane hango yabo nekuzwitonga, ikoku ndiko kwakalezi Zwitonga kweZimbabwe mugole la1980;

(c)

banhu beZimbabwe batjifanila ba nendulamilo batole hango dzabo;

kakale nekwakatambunuka—

(i) Butungamili gweMakhuwa gunanlandu welipa mbhayilo wehango yelima nepfuwa yakatogwa kumunlayo kugadziwa banhu butshwa, kuhinga mari inobva mukwama tjakatigwa ikoku; kakale

(ii) hapa Butungamili gwemakhuwa kukon’wa lipa mbhayilo guhingisa mari inobva mukwama tjakatigwa ikoku, Hulumente weZimabwe haana nlandu welipa mbhayilo yehango yelima nepfuwa yakatogwa kumunlayo kugadziwa banhu butshwa.

(8) Iyeyi ndima inohinga isingatole lubihho kunlandu weButungamili gwakeguli gweMakhiwa kuti ipe nlipo wehango inolebgwa mundima iyeyi yakatolegwa gadziwa butshwa kwebanhu. 73 Ndulamilo dzezwehango

(1) Wose nhu unandulamilo—

(a)

yegala muhango isingakubadze mamo etjila kukwe kene butjilo gugwe;

(b) neyedzibilila hango yabanogala kudze ibe jidzo mutjaba tjinotjila ngwenu kene mulubaka gunohha, kuhingisiwa nhambo dzenlayo dzakatambunuka nedzimwe nhambo dzino—

(i) dzibila siwa kwehambgwa nekubadziga kwehango;

(ii) susuma tjengeta hango;

(iii) nepfumbidza nehingisa zwiwan’wa zwehango nezila isingakubadze hango kakale dzimusidzila pfumbila kwezwefumwa negalisana kwebanhu.

(2) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola nhambo dzenlayo dzakatambunuka nedzimwe nhambo, guhingisa zwiwanika zwiyapo, kuti gutshambidzile zibisiwa kwebanhu nekwendulamilo dzinolebgwa munayeyi ndima.

Lusununguko gwesatakhwa kusakatambunuka

Akuna nhu ungadusiwa munzi uwe, kene ungapaladzigwa nzi uwe, kusina ndawulo yekhutha inojalo hule kwelingiwa kwemamo ose angabvumila ikoku. 75

Ndulamilo yewana zwidiyo

(1) Wose nhu ugele kumunlayo muZimbabwe unandulamilo—

(a)

yewana zwidiyo zwekutanga zwinolipiwa neHango, kuhanganisila nezwidiyo zwekutanga zwebawhola;

(b) neyezwidiyo zwambeli, zwakafanila tiwa kuti zwibepo kakale zwisikililike, Butungamili gweHango guhingisa nhambo dzenlayo dzakatambunuka nedzimwe nhambo.

(2) Wose nhu unandulamilo yethama, netubula kukwe nemasimba awe, baka kwele tjimutjimo tjakatambunuka tjakalizanila, besingaleyane nezwinolebgwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu.

(3) Nlayo ungata kuti zwikwele zwinobviwa mundimana (2) zwikwalisiwe nehhaligwa kwezwikwele zwisimutjimo tjinohakika kene zwisimutjimo tjakalizanila kwagwa.

(4) Butungamili gweHango yakafanila hingisa nlayo kene tola dzimwe nhambo, dzinoyendilana netjimo tjematatabuko ehango, kuti nlayo wathalusiwa mundimana (1) ubhudilile hinga. 76

Ndulamilo yelapiwa newongewa

(1) Wose nhu unabunhu gweZimbabwe nenghali watjose weZimbabwe unandulamilo yelapiwa newongewa, kose nelapiwa newongewa kwakalingisana newana kene sawana bana.

(2) Wose nhu unabugwele gusingapele unandulamilo yelapiwa newongewa kwakalingisana nayigogo bugwele.

(3) Akuna nhu unolambigwa lapiwa kene wongewa nekulibilidza apa eswika mutjiwongelo.

(4) Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila hingisa nlayo kene tola dzimwe nhambo, dzinoyendilana netjimo tjematatabuko ehango, kuti kubhudilile hinga kwenlayo wathalusiwa munayeyi ndima. 77 Ndulamilo yewana Zodliwa nevula Wose nhu unandulamilo—

(a)

yewana vula mbuyanana, inon’wika;

(b) newana zodliwa zwakawunlizanila; neButungamili gweHango gwaakafanila hingisa nlayo kene tola dzimwe nhambo, dzinoyendilana netjimo tjematatabuko ehango, kuti kubhudilile wamutjilika kwendulamilo iyeyi. 78 Ndulamilo dzendobolo

(1) Wose nhu waswika makole aligumi nehhanakadzi (18) unandulamilo yeba nen’ha.

(2) Akuna nhu unoswundidzigwa bhika kene tola nkadzi asingahake.

(3) Banhu betjikadzi boga kene betjilume boga abatobvumigwa tolana (mbgwende). LUBABI 3 Tjenesiwa Kwedzimwe Ndulamilo 79 Hingisiwa kwe Lubabi 3

(1) Lubabi igogu gotjenesa nekwe ndulamilo nensununguko kutila kuti kuwhakale, kubepatjena hinga kwendulamilo nensununguko idzedzi kubanhu.

(2) Lubabi igogu aguzofanila togwa segunoghanula ndulamilo kene nsununguko dzalebgwa mulubabi 2. 80 Ndulamilo dzebetjikadzi

(1) Wose nhu wetjikadzi unatjilemela tjihhele tjenhu tjinolizana netjenhu wetjilume kakale ikoku kohanganyisila wana mikoba inolizana yebhatika munazwepolitiki, zwefumwa yehango nezwetjaba.

(2) Betjikadzi banandulamilo dzakalizana nedzebetjilume mundebo dzetjengeta nelela bana, koga tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjingatambunudza kuti ndulamilo idzedzi banodzipiwa tjini.

(3) Milayo yose, mbvilo nendongoloso dzemilenje, kunohhalilila ndulamilo dzebetjikadzi dzinolebgwa neNlayo nkulu iwhoyu, kobon’wa sekusingahinge. 81 Ndulamilo dzebana

(1) Mwana wose, kubva kuti wose mbisana nensikana unamakole apasi kwe aligumi nehhanakadzi, unandulamilo—

(a)

yebhakhwa kwakalizana panlayo, kuhanganyisila ndulamilo yewhiligwa;

(b)

yepiwa zina nentupo;

(c)

hapa kulimwana waka—

(i) zwaligwa muZimbabwe, kene

(ii) wakazwaligwa kuhhe kweZimbabwe koga enabunhu gweZimbabwe nelukata; unandulamilo yewana lukwalo gwezwagwa;

(d) yelegwa mun’ha, kene legwa nebazwadzi, kene bhakhwa zwakanaka hapa adusiwa pan’ha;

(e) adzibiligwe kayikanyiwa munazwefumwa yehango nethubayiwa, pahingisiwa zwakalema kwebana, nesabhakhwa zwibuyanana, lahiligwa hango kene kupi zwako kayikanyiwa;

(f)

yewana zwidiyo, lapiwa kwakafanila, zodliwa zwinobaka mbili nebugalo;

(g) asinginisiwe pazwinhu zwinondilana nengwa kene mungwa dzemathumo nethobolo kene gwisa kweludzini zwako;

(h) asimanikidziwe bhatika munazwepolitiki, kakale

(i)

asihhaliligwe kuhhe kwekuti kuli iyo yoga zila yasala, apa engahhaliligwa—

(i) ahhaliligwe kwelubaka gufupi gwakafanila;

(ii) asigadziwe nebamwe bakahhaliligwa banamakole anopinda gumi nehhanakadzi; kakale

(iii) abhakhwe nezila, agadziwe nezila, dzakalizanila makole awe.

(2) Zwinodiwa nebana zwakafanila tungamila mundebo dzose dzinoyendilana nekwemwana.

(3) Bana banandulamilo yedzibiligwa kwakafanila nedzikhutha dzenlayo, kwazo kwazo neKhutha Whulu sentjengeti wabo. 82

Ndulamilo dzebakakwegula Banhu banamakole anopinda makumi alikunotendeka bana ndulamilo—

(a) yewana legwa kwakatambunuka nejidzo kumiha yabo neHango;

(b) yewana jidzo lezwebuwongelo nelapiwa muango, kakale

(c) yewamutjila jidzo lemari sezila yetjila negala zwibuyanana; kakale

Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola nhambo dzenlayo dzakatambunuka nedzimwe nhambo, guhingisa zwiwanika zwiyapo, kuti gutshambidzile zibisiwa kwebanhu nekwendulamilo dzinolebgwa munayeyi ndima. 83 Ndulamilo dzebanhu bakakubadzika Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola dzose nhambo dzakafanila, kuyendilana nezwiwanikwa zwiyapo, kuti ibe nelebeswa lekuti banhu bakakubadzika banohingisa mikumbulo nemibili yabo nekuhhele, kuhanganyisila nhambo—

(a)

dzeyeta kuti bazwimile;

(b) dzeyeta kuti bagale nemiha yabo kakale babhatike muzwinhu zwinotikala mutjaba, zwebumihha kene zwezwifalayidza;

(c)

dzebadzibilila mukayikanyiwa kose nesabhakhwa zwibuyanana

(d) dzeyeta kuti baswikilile lapiwa dzingwele, zweminkumbulo nedzimwe zila dzelapiwa;

(e)

dzebatila zwinhu zwinowhota kuti zwidiya kwabo kube kulelulelu; kakale

(f)

kuti kube nezwidiyo zwinolipigwa nehango pabanozwihaka.

84 Ndulamilo dzemhihha dzengwa yelusununguko

(1) Mihha dzengwa yelusununguko, kubva kuti—

(a)

ibabo bakagwa muNgwa yeLusununguko;

(b) ibabo bakatjidza bagwi muNgwa yeLusununguko;

(c)

n a y ib ab o b a k a n g i n i s i w a m u m a j e l e , b a k a h h a l i l i g w a k e n e bakatshambidzigwa nentha yegwigwa kwehango mulubaka gwengwa yelusunguko;

banandulamilo yelemekedziwa nezwabakata kuti Zimbabwe isununguke, kakale bakafanila wana mbhayilo inonga dzipentjeni nezwebuwongelo zwinohakiwa nebanhu bose.

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila pa bakagwila ngwa yelusununguko masimba ewana kwakabafanila pasi kwendimana (1). LUBABI 4 Hingisiwa Kwendulamilo Nensununguko Dzebanhu Dzakazinda 85 Tobegwa kwendulamilo nensununguko dzebanhu dzakazinda

(1) Umwe webanhu banotobela—

(a)

bose banhu banhu bamilila zwido zwabo;

(b) bose banhu bakamila bamwe bekongwa zwimila;

(c)

bose banhu bakamilila umwe kene zwido zwegubungano zwebamwe banhu;

(d) bose banhu bakamilila ludo gwetjaba;

(e)

imwe neyimwe gubungano yakamilila zwido zwebanhu bayo;

banobvumigwa yenda kukhuta, benobika kuti ndulamilo kene nsununguko yakazinda imuDanho ileli, yatjinyiwa, kene ingatjinyiwa, nekhuta ingapa sununguka kwemoyo, kuhanganyila lusununguko kose nepa mbhayilo.

(2) Kuti nhu watjinya nlayo akutowuhhalililayenda kukhuta enowana whisisa kutebegwa ndimana (1).

(3) Milayo yekhuta dzose yakafanila pa ndongoloso inowhotobegwa mulubaka gwehaka whisisa kutobegwa ndimana (1), kakale milayo iyeyo yakafanila thama kuti —

(a)

ndulamilo yebinga khuta kutobegwa ndimana (1) inotikala nekuhhele;

(b) mamo ezila dzendongoloso, kuhanganyisila kuti dzotanga lini, atolingiwa kwazo;

(c)

khuta kene itobela milayo yendulamo ayitotshambidzigwa nendongoloso dzisazotambunuka; kakale

(d)

nhu unabumhihha engakumbigwa nekhuta, abon’we senkwinya wekhuta.

(4) Hayika kwemilayo inobviwa mundimana (3) ayitoganula ndulamilo yetanga n’hingo pasi kwendimana (1) kakale kuti ndebo isekesewe nekhuta. LUBABI 5 Panoma Ndulamilo Nensununguko Dzakazinda 86 Panoma ndulamilo nensununguko

(1) Ndulamilo nensununguko dzakazinda dzimuDanho ileli dzakafanila tobegwa kwakatambunuka kakale kutobegwa ndulamilo nensununguko dzebamwe banhu.

(2) Ndulamilo nensununguko dzakazinda dzimuDanho ileli dzingaganhugwa koga kutobegwa nlayo, munhambo dzekuti ganhugwa ikoko kwakatambunuka, kwakafanila kakale kowhisisika kutjaba tjinozwitonga kwakalingwa tjenesedzo, ndulamo, lemekedzewa kwenhu, lizana nelunsununguko kuhanganyisila—

(a)

tjimo tjendulamilo nelusununguko tjakalingiligwa;

(b) kuti nghanhu idzedzo ndedzeni, kwazo kwazo kuti koyendidzana yenjemulo yedzibilila, dzibilika kwetjaba, mazwibhato etjaba,bunhu gwetjaba, lapiwa newongewa kwetjaba, ndongoloso yemitunhu nematoropo kene gutsikana kwetjaba;

(c)

tjimo nebikulu gwenghanu;

(d)

hanha kwewumwe nhu ngentha yendulamilo nensununguko akutokubadza ndulamilo nensununguko dzebamwe;

(e)

yendidzana kweganhula nekuti koganhuligwani, kwazo kwazo kuti kene kopamhidzila tshambilidza ndulamilo kene nsununguko idzedzo sekunonga kunoyi adzitohakika kuti n’hingo ubhudilile.

(f)

kuti kene kuna dzimwe zila dzepundusa n’hingo wenghanu dzisingatshambidzile kwazo bhudililo.

(3) Akuna nlayo ungaganula ndulamilo dzinotebela dzimuDanho ileli, kakale akuna nhu ungayitjinya—

(a)

ndulamilo yebutjilo, kuhhe kwekwatjenesewa mundima 48;

(b)

ndulamilo yobhakhwa kunabunhu;

(c)

ndulamilo yesakayikanyiwa kene dzidziwa, bhakhwa nezila isina bunhu, meya mbi kene langiwa;

(d) ndulamilo yesabhakhwa bulanda

(e)

ndulamilo yesekesewa kwakatambunuka;

(f)

ndulamilo yewana ndawulo yehabeas corpusse sekwakunolebgwa mundima 50(7)(a).

87 Panoma ndulamilo mulubaka gwembatsha yetjaba

(1) Pehhugwi kwemiganhu inobvumigwa mundima 86, ndulamilo nensunguko dzakadzika dziyapo muDanho ileli dzingapamha dzika ganhugwa nenlayo wakwagwa unopa nhambo dzelingisana nemamo angaba yapo mulubaka gwembatsha yetjaba, koga kwakalizanila tjinobvumigwa nendima iyeyi neNdongoloso yeBubili.

(2) Nlayo wakakwagwa unolebgwa nekwawo mundimana (1) nedzimwe nhambo dzenlayo dzatogwa kutobegwa kwenlayo iwhoyo wakafanila kwagwa muGazethi.

(3) Kene yipi nghanu nlayo wakakwagwa unolebgwa nekwawo mundimana

(1) wayinotilika pehhugwi kwendulamilo kene nsununguko yakadzika iyapo muDanho ileli ayizofanila pinda tjinohakiwa nembatsha iyeyo.

(4) Akuna nlayo unobvuma longa tjimo tjembatsha, kakale akuna nlayo kene nhambo inotugwa nentha yetjimo itjetjo, kunga—

(a)

dzibilila kene bvumila kene pa simba ledzibililo, Butungamili gwehango kene gupi lutabi kene bunga laHulumente mumbihho dzose, kene upi nhu pakalingisana netjiyeto tjisimunlayo; kene

(b) Ganhula kene dzipi ndulamilo dzinolebgwa nekwadzo mundima 83(3), kene pa simba kene bvumila kene ipi yadzedzo ndulamilo kuti itshambidzigwe. DANHO 5 Butungamili LUBABI 1 Simba Lebutungamili 88 Simba leButungamili

(1) Simba leButungamili lobva mubanhu beZimbabwe kakale lakafanila hingisiwa nezila inondidzana neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu.

(2) Simba leButungamili gweZimbabwe ligele muNtungamili weHango unolihingisa ebvumigwa neKhabhinethi, kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu. LUBABI 2 Ntungamili Wehango Nebatebedzeli Bentungamili 89 Ntungamili weHango Ntungamili weHango ndiyeN’holo weHango naHulumente kakale ndiyeNlawuli Nkulu weMasole.

90 Mihingo yeNtungamili weHango

(1) Ntungamili weHango wakafanila milidza, dzibilila, tobela nelemekedza Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu senalyo upehhugwi weHango kakale wakafanila bona kuti Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu neyimwe yose milayo inotobegwa nezila yakatambunuka.

(2) Ntungamili weHango wakafanila—

(a)

milidza bhatana, nelunyalalo muhango kuyetila butjilo bubuya gwebanhu bose beZimbabwe

(b) milidza nelemekedza mikumbulo nebunhu gwengwa yelusununguko yeZimbabwe;

(c)

yeta kuti kudzibiligwe ndulamilo nensununguko dzebanhu dzakadzika simba lenlayo; kakale

(d) lemekedzwa siyana kwebanhu nemitunhu yeZimbabwe. 91

Fanilo dzehalugwa seNtungamili weHango neNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango

(1) Nhu unofanila halugwa seNtungamili weHango kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango hapa—

(a)

unhu weZimbabwe nezwagwa kene nelukata;

(b) watjiswikisa makole elimakumi mana ezwagwa;

(c)

wagala unogala muZimbabwe; kakale

(d) wakakwalisiwa vota.

(2) Nhu unolambigwa halugwa seNtungamili weHango kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango apa akatjitongo gala mutulo tjeNtungamili weHango pasi kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kwelubaka gweba mutulo kunoswika kabili. 92 Halugwa kweNtungamili weHango nebaTebedzeli beNtungamili weHango

(1) Halulo yeNtungamili weHango nebaTebedzeli beNtungamili weHango babili yakafanila tikala mulubaka gwakatjenesewa mundima 15.

(2) Wose nhu wangina muhalulo yeNtungamili weHango wakafanisa thanga banhu babili banongina muhalulo naye baTebedzeli, kakale wakafanila thanga umwe pakati kwabo kuti unowobe Ntebedzeli, wekutanga umwe unowobe Ntebedzeli, webubili.

(3) Ntungamili nentebedzeli, dzeNtungamili weHango banohalugwa nebanhu bakakwalisa bamulukwalo gwevota Zimbabwe yose, kakale nezila yehalugwa yakatjenesewa muNlayo weHalulo.

(4) Ndulamilo dzekwalisa mulukwalo gwevota nevota pahalugwa kweNtungamili neNtebedzeli, dzeNtungamili weHango dzakakwagwa mundongoloso yebuna.

(5) Halulo yeNtungamili weHango neNtebedzeli, beNtungamili weHango yakafanila tikala lubaka ngun’ompela nehalugwa kwebaMilili bemitunhu muPhalamente, makhansila nebamwe batungamili bemitunhu. 93 Kakabadza halulo yeNtungamili weHango

(1) Sekwakabigiwa mundima iyeyi, unobe aliwebamwe bamuhalulo unandulamilo yen’un’unila bulebeswa netambunuka kwehalulo yeNtungamili weHango kene Ntebedzeli waNtungamili weHango nelonga tjikumbilo kene kwalila kuKhuta

yeNlayo Nkulu wehango kusanhu kwapinda mahhuba aligunotondeka (7) hule kwezibisiwa kwetjimo tjehalulo kuti watola bugalo ndiyani.

(2) Halulo yeNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango ingakakabadziwa koga hapa kulingwa kuti hana zwakalingiligwa kuti nhu abe izo kudza ahalugwe .

(3) Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu wehango yakafanila lingisisa nesuka mawhi etjikumbilo kene lukwaloitebela ndimana (1) kusanhu kwapinda mahhuba aligumi nemana kwasiwa itjetjo tjikumbilo kene lukwalo,whi latugwa nekhuta ndilo linootebegwa.

(4) Mukulingisisa phetishini kene lukwalo, kutebegwa ndima (1), Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu wehango—

(a)

ingazibisa tjaba kuti wakunda muhalulo ndiyani;

(b) ingalamba wamutjila halulo, ndizo halulo ndzwa yakafanila tiwa kusanhu kukapinda mahhuba alimakumi matanhatu (60) hule kwetjisungo tjeKhuta yeNlyo Nkulu wehango; kene

(c)

dusa ndawulo layinobona libuya kakale lakatambunuka.

(5) Hapa, phethishini kene lukwalo kutebegwa ndimana (1)—

(a)

Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu wehango isendeka ntome halulo yeNtungamili weHango, halulo yeBatebedzeli beNtungamili weHango babili (2) inobva lahwa;

(b) Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu wehango isendeka ntome halulo yeNtebedzeli kene Batebedzeli beNtungamili weHango babili (2), kohakika kuti Ntungamili weHango abige nhu kene banhu bakafanila nekulibilidza, kubva, kuti abeNtebedzeli kene babeBatebedzeli beNtungamili weHango. 94

Gala mubugalo kweNtungamili weHango nebaTebedzeli beNtungamili weHango

(1) Banhu bahalugwa seNtungamili weHango nebahalugwa seBatebedzeli beNtungamili weHango banogala mutulo hapa, batjituna, zwituno zweNtungamili weHango nezweNtebedzeliweNtungamili weHango betebela zila yetuna setjenesiwa kwayo muNdongoloso yebuTatu (3), mbeli kweNtongi Nkulu weNlayo kene Ntongi Nkulu unowuntobela mubugalo uyapo—

(a)

nehhuba lebuhhanalume (9) kwakazibisiwa tjaba kuti bakunda muhalulo; kene

(b) hapa kwaba nekukakabadzana kun’un’uniwa nekwetambunuka nebapatjena kwehalulo, kusanhu kwapinda maawa ali makumi mana anahhanakadzi (48 hours) Khuta yeNlyo Nkulu wehango yazibisa tjaba zina lewakunda muhalulo.

(2) Ntungamili weHango ugele mutulo unondilambeli elimutulo kudza kutola tulo Ntungamili weHango wahalugwa sekwakunolembgwa mundimana (1).

(3) Batebedzeli weNtungamili weHango unobe Ntungamili weHango patjinyika, dita mutulo kene dusiwa mutulo kweNtungamili weHango unotola bugalo hapa, atjituna nezila yakatambunuka nekulibilidza kusanhu kwapinda maawa ali makumi mana anahhakakadzi (48 hours) kwabikiwa kuti akutjina ugele tulo tjeNtungamili weHango, dunho leNtungamili weHango nezila yakathalusiwa muNdongoloso yebuTatu (3), mbeli kweNtongi Nkulu weNlayo kene Ntongi Nkulu unowuntobela mubugalo uyapo.

2013

Lubaka gweba mubugalo kweNtungamili weHango nebaTebedzeli beNtungamili weHango

(1)Lubaka gwebamutulo kweNtungamili weHango kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango gotangisa nsi ehhodzewa kakale egala tulo nezila yathalusiwa muNdima 94(1)(a) kene 94(3)

(2) Lubaka gwebamutulo kweNtungamili weHango kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango gondilambeli—

(a)

edza ejita mutulo kene edusiwa mubugalo; kene

(b) kudza kuti hule kwehalulo, kukalebgwa kuti ndiye wakunda muhalulo kene kukalebgwa kuti umwe Ntungamili weHango ntshwa wakunda muhalulo, kakale, kuhhe kwayikoku kwalebgwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, lubaka gwabo gweba mubugalo makole mahanu kakale kwakalizana nelubaka goba muPhalamente. 96

Siya n’hingo kweNtungamili weHango kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango

(1) Ntungamili weHango angajita mutulo hule kwekwalila lukwalo Tjilebeleki gunoleba kuti wehaka jita mutulo, tjakafanila budza tjaba nejita mutulo kweNtungamili weHango nekulibilidza kusanhu kwapinda ma awa elimakumi mabili nemana (24 hours).

(2) Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango angasiya bugalo gugwe nekwala lukwalo gunoleba kuti unosiya n’hingo gunonda kuNtungamili weHango, wakafanila zibisa hango nayikoko nekulibilidza akutikale kusipinde hhuba lin’ompela asabazibisa. 97 Dusiwa kweNtungamili weHango kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango mutulo

(1) Senethi nePhalamente, netjibvumilano tjatiwa negwamu lenhengo dzeSenethi nePhalamente, ingatubula bvumilana kuti Ntungamili weHango kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango engadusiwa kene aangadusiwe mutulo tjitje edusigwa—

(a)

mazwibhato akayipa;

(b) kon’wa tobela, lemekedza nemila Nlayo nkulu iwhoyu;

(c)

tjuluka Nlayo nkulu iwhoyu mwaza; kene

(d) kon’wa ta n’hingo wetulo itjetjo nentha yegwala mumbili kene munkumbulo kwakafanila lingisisiwa akutobegwa danho ileli.

(2) Hapa kwatjisiwa tjibvumilano kwakalingwa ndimana (1), Khomithi yeMilayo iMile yakafanila biga imwe khomithi yeSenethi nePhalamente inanhengo dzilihhanalume inolakidza ma kwedzepolitiki muPhalamente, kuti ite hakuludzo nekwedusiwa kweNtungamili weHango kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili, sayikoko kunoobe kulipo.

(3) Hapa—

(a)

khomithi yabigwa seleba kwendimana (2) ingati Ntungamili weHango kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili adusiwe mutulo; kakale

(b) Senethi neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente, netjibvumilano tjin’ompela tjatiwa nembabi mbili munhatu dzenhengo dziyapo, kungabvumilana kuti Ntungamili kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango wakafanila dusiwa mutulo tjitje, Ntungamili weHango kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango unojita mutulo.

98 Lutabilo gweNtungamili weHango

(1) Etjimutulo, Ntungamili weHango atosekesewa mukhutha yenlayo esekesegwa zwinhu zwaakathama kene dzaasakathama koga akafanila zwithama

(2) Ndongoloso dzesekesa dzingatiwa kuNtungamili weHango wabva mutulo dzitigwa zwinhu zwaakathama nezwaasakatha koga akafanila thama esanhu akabe Ntungamili weHango kene atjibe Ntungamili weHango.

(3) Ndila mbeli kwema prescription kwakalingwa zwikwelete zweNtungamili weHango zwahha esanhu kene abe mutulo tjeButungamili komisiwa apa etjimutulo tjeButungamili.
