(4) Munadzose ndongoloso dzinohhisiwa nekweNtungamili weHango wabva mutulo nekwezwinhu zwaakathama kene zwaasakathama koga akafanila thama etjinamatatabuko seNtungamili weHango, unokhona zwimila nesa patjena kuti tjinhu tjaakata kene tjaakaletja thama kwaka kuli kwebugalimoyo gwakalulwama. 99 Mihingo yeBatebedzeli beNtungamili weHango Batebedzeli beNtungamili weHango banobhatsha Ntungamili weHango muyeta mihingo iye, kakale banothama imwe mihingo inohanganyisila tungamila Nhabi dzose kene Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente, yabangapiwa kuti bathame neNtungamili weHango. 100 Milili weNtungamili weHango

(1) Apa Ntungamili weHango esipo muZimbabwe kene esngatubule yeta mihingo iye nentha yegwala kene kumwe zwako, mihingo iyeyo yakafanila togwa kakale iyetiwe—

(a)

neNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango wekutanga;

(b) hapa kutikala kuti Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango wekutanga atotubula yeta mihingo iyeyo, yakafanila tiwa neNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango webubili; kene

(c)

hapa kusina Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango ungata mihingo iyeyi, itiwe naMinisita—

(i) wakabigwa neNtungamili weHango kuti unowhohinga iwhoyo n’hingo;

(ii) wahagwa neKhabhinethi, hapa kusina Minisita wabigwa neNtungamili weHango setjenesiwa mundimana (i).

(2) Kuhhe kwekuti kwabe netjibvumilano tjatiwa nenhango njinji dzelubahhe gweMaMinisita, nhu unota mihingo yeNtungamili weHango setjenesiwa mundimana

(i) azofanila hingisa simba leNtungamili weHango—

(a)

kuti atume masole

(b) athame zwibvumilano nedzimwe hango;

(c)

kuti abige kene alambe bigwa kweNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, Minisita kene Ntobedzeli waMinisita; kene

(d) longewa kene bwiliziwa mihingo kweNtebedzeli weNtungamili, Minisita kene Ntebedzeli weMinisita, kuhanganyila, kwakalingiwa Ntebedzeli weNtungamili kene Minisita, tungamigwa kwetjisungo tjenlayo kene lutabi, kene paladza n’hingo.

101 Lukata palubaka gwelufu, siya n’hingo kene haya matatabuko kweNtungamili weHango kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili hango

(1) Kukatikala kuti Ntungamili atjinyike, asiye n’hingo kene ekadusiwa mubugalo—

(a)

Ntebedzeli weNtungamili wekutanga unongina mubugalo seNtungamili kudze kupela lubaka gweNtungamili waka elipo;

(b) Ntebedzeli weNtungamili webubili unongina mubugalo gweNtebedzeli weNtungamili wekutanga kudze kupele lubaka gweNtebedzeli weNtungamili wakelipo; kakale

(c)

Ekagala mutulo seNtungamili, waka eliNtebedzeli weNtungamili kwakafanila abige nhu wakafanila kuti abe Ntebedzeli weNtungamili webubili kudze kupele lubaka gweNtungamili wakelipo.

(2) Kukatikala kuti Ntebedzeli weNtungamili wekutanga atjinyike, kene ekadusiwa mubugalo—

(a)

Ntebedzeli weNtungamili webubili unongina mubugalo seNtebedzeli weNtungamili wekutanga kudze kupele lubaka gweNtebedzeli weNtungamili wayeliwokutanga; kakale

(b)

Ntungamili kwakafanila kuti esitole lubaka biga nhu wakafanila kuti abe Ntebedzeli weNtungamili webubili kudze kupele lubaka gweNtebedzeli weNtungamili wayeliwokutanga.

102 Bhadagwa kweNtungamili weHango nebaTebedzeli beNtungamili weHango

(1) Ntungamili weHango neNtebedzeli beNtungamili weHango bakafanila wana nlipo, mbhayilo, mapentjeni nekumwe sekwagwa pasi kwetjisungo tjenlayo

(2) Mari inobhadagwa nemibhayilo yeNtungamili neNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango kwakafanila idusiwe muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente.

(3) Nhu watjiletja iba Ntungamili weHango kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango kwakafanila apiwe—

(a) pentjeni inolizana nemari inobhadagwa Ntungamili weHango umutulo kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili wehango, noyendelana kwakhona; kakale

(b) mbhayilo nekumwe bengakuwana sekwagwa pasi kwetjisungo tjenlayo. 103 Ntungamili weHango nebaTebedzeli beNtungamili weHango nebakatongo gala zwitulo izwezo abazofanila gala zwimwe zwitulo kene hinga Ntungamili neNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, newatongobe Ntungamili weHango kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango akuzomela bagale tulo mutjaba patjena kene besingabongwe kene behingile umwe nhu betjigele patulo kene bewana pentjeni yaHango sebakatongobe Ntungamili kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango. LUBABI 3 Maminisita, Batobedzeli Bemaminisita Nekhabhinethi 104 Gadziwa kwemaMinisita neBatobedzeli bemaMinisita

(1) Ntungamili weHango unogadza MaMinisita ekowapa mihingo, kuhanganila tungamila tjisungo tjenlayo kene gumwe negumwe lutabi, koga Ntungamili weHango engaletja zibisa bamwe n’hinga kukwe.

(2) Ntungamili weHango engagadza Ntebedzeli weMinisita kuti etjidze Minisita hinga.

(3) MaMinisita neBatebedzeli bemaMinisita banogadziwa bevila mumaSenetha kene baMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente, koga bahanhu, banotogwa mubanhu banabimihha nodiyika besipo muPhalamente.

(4) Ntungamili weHango unohingisa zila dzeHango nelizanisa bunhu lume nebunhu kadzi kwanogadza MaMinisita nebaTebedzeli bemaMinisita.

(5) MaMinisita nebaTebedzeli bemaMinisita basibamilili bemitunhu muPhalamente bengagala bekalebeleka koga abangavote muSenethi kene muPhalamente.

(6) Minisita kene Ntebedzeli weMinisita kwakafanila kuti atunhe sendongoloso yobutatu inazwitunho nosiyana kwazo nedzinhabi mbeli kweNtungamili esenhu ekatanga gadziwa mutulo. 105 Khabhinethi

(1) Kuna Khabhinethi, inohanganyila Ntungamili weHango, seN’holo weKhabhinethi, baTebedzeli beNtungamili weHango nemaMinisita iyawo Ntungamili weHango aangahalula kuti abe muKhabhinethi.

(2) Mihangano yeKhabhinethi inotungamigwa neNtungamili weHango kene, hapa esipo, neNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango kene, hapa besipo, naMinisita unobviwa mundima 100(1)(c). 106 Zwibhata kwebaTebedzeli beNtungamili weHango, MaMinisita neBatobedzeli beMaMinisita

(1) Wose Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, wose Minisita neNtobedzeli weMinisita wose wakafanila tobela Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu.

(2) Batebedze beNtungamili weHango, MaMinisita neBatobedzeli beMaMinisita habazofanila, lubaka betjimubugalo,—

(a)

(b)

(c)

kuti babhate bugalo gwetjaba kene hinga n’hingo unambhayilo; kuti bazwibhate nezila yakaleyana nebugalo gwabo, kene zwisa mutjimo tjingata kukaba nesandilana kwen’hingo nezila yetjila kwabo; kene kuti bahingise bugalo gwabo, kene luzibo gwabanago, bazwifumise kene wamutjidza umwe nhu kusakatambunuka.

(3) Tjusungo tjeNlayo tjephalamente tjakafanila bamba zila yezwibhata kweBatebedzeli beNtungamili weHango, maMinisita neBatobedzeli bemaMinisita. 107 Zwidusa patjenakwebaTebedzeli beNtungamili weHango, MaMinisita neBatobedzeli beMaMinisita

(1) Kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, wose Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, Minisita wose neNtobedzeli waMinisita wose, umwe nemwe kene bose bananlandu webona kuti n’hingo weNtungamili weHango unotikala nezila yakatambunuka.

(2) Wose Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, Minisita wose neNtobedzeli waMinisita wose wakafanila bayapo mun’hangano wePhalamente newemakhomithi ephalamente kudze ahandule mibhuzo yakalingisana naye nehinga kukwe. 108 Lubaka gweba mubugalo kweMaMinisita neBatobedzeli beMaMinisita

(1) Bugalo gwaMinisita kene gweNtobedzeli waMinisita gosaba nenhu—

(a)

hapa Ntungamili weHango ewundusa mubugalo;

(b)

hapa esiya bugalo hule kwekwalila Ntungamili weHango lukwalo;

(c)

hule kwegala mubugalo kweNtungamili weHango tshwa.

2013

(2) Kutebegwa ndimana (4), Minisita kene gweNtobedzeli waMinisita waka eliMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente pakubigwa kukwe mubugalo gwebaMinisita Ntobedzeli waMinisita unosiya bugalo igogo hule kwesabaMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente.

(3) Kutebegwa ndimana (4), Minisita kene gweNtobedzeli waMinisita waka eliMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente pakubigwa kukwe mubugalo gwebaMinisita kene Ntobedzeli waMinisita unosiya bugalo igogo hule kweba yapo kwezwimo zwinoota kuti bugalo gugwe gwaagale seMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente gusitjina nhu ugugele.

(4) Kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, n’hingo wepladziwa kwePhalamente, maMinisita neBatobedzeli bemaMinisita banondila mbeli belimubugalo gwabo kudza kunoti Ntungamili weHango tshwa egala mubugalo hule kwehalulo yebanhu bose. 109 Voti yesagutshikana nen’hingo waHulumente

(1) Senethi neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente, netjibvumilano tjin’ompela tjatiwa nembabi mbili munhatu dzebamilili bose benga bvumilana tula voti yesagutshikana nen’hingo waHulumente.

(2) Tjikumbilo tjebvumilana toboka dzingatogwa koga hapa kulikuti—

(a)

kwaba nemahhuba asipasi kwegunotendeka kwazibisiwa Tjilebeleki nekwenhambo idzedzo; kakale

(b) lukwalo gwezibisa nekwenhambo idzedzo gwatshambidzigwa nebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente basipasi kwe gwamu.

(3) Lukwalo gwenhambo dzetoboka—

(a)

gwakafanela lebgwa negwako mun’hangano wakahanganyila Senethi neLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente kusanhu kwapinda mahhuba ali makumi mabili anahhuba kule kwekuti Tjilebeleki tjawana lukwalo gwenhambo dzetoboka; kakale

(b)

kwakafanila kuti kuvotegwe mumihangano iligunotendeka inotebelelana hule kwelebgwa nekwako; kuhhe kwayikoko koba kwakona.

(4) Hapa Phalamente yatula voti yesagutshikana nen’hingo waHulumente, Ntungamili weHango wakafanila kuti mumahhuba aligumi nemana (14) hule kwevoti iyeyo—

(a)

dusa wose MaMinisita neBatobedzeli beMaMinisita mubugalo, kuhhe kwekuti bagala batjisiya bugalo netha yatjetjo tjibvumilwano, negadza banhu mubugalo gwabo; kene

(b) paladza Phalamente, nedana halulo yebanhu bose mumahhuba alimakumi alihhanalume (90).

(5) Hapa Ntungamili weHango esingatobele ndimana (4) mumahhuba aligumi lina manahule kwetobokewa kwehulumente, Phalamente inoba yapalala LUBABI 4 Mihingo Yebutungamili 110 Mihingo yeNtungamili weHango neKhabhinethi, yeButungamili

(1) Ntungamili weHango unamasimba anolebgwa nawhoyu Nlayo Nkulu neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kene umwe nlayo, kuhanganyila banemasimba ethama mihingo inotiwa neNtungamili weHango.

(2) Kutobegwa iwhoyu Nlayo nkulu wehango, Ntungamili weHango wakafanila—

(a)

tambunudza netshambidzila Milayo;

(b) yendisa Nlayo utjasusumiwa kuKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu wehango kuti kuzuwisaniwe kakale kupiwaniwe mano kwakalingisisiwa nekuti umutjimo tjenlayo Nkulu wehango;

(c)

dana baMilili bemitunhu, Senethi kene phalamente kun’hangano welinga nekwezwinotikala;

(d) hhodza kene biga banhu muzwitulo kwakafanila tiwa neNtungamili weHango epiwa masimba neNlayo Nkulu wehango kene umwenlayo;

(e)

dana halulo kumuNlayo Nkulu wehango;

(f)

dana nezila imunlayo mareferendamu akalingisana kene nayipi ndebo zwayo;

(g) tuma Masole;

(h) pa mibhayilo negudzo;

(i)

sa Bamilili behango kudzimwe hango, batumwa;

(j)

newhamutjila nebhata zubanana bamilili bekudzimwe hango.

(3) Kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu, Khabhinethi ina nlandu we—

(a)

samuzila hinga kwaHulumente;

(b) bhata n’hingo waHulumente muPhalamenete;

(c)

bakanya, bambiwa nethama kuti nlayo wehango utebegwe;

(d)

thama ne nethama kuti ndongoloso dzehango dzitebegwe

(e)

pa mano Ntungamili weHango.

(4) Kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu wehango, Ntungamili weHango angapedza ndebo kene hingisa matama abva pandebo, zwibvumilano nebvumilana nedzimwe hango neButungamili gwadzo nemakubungano edzimwe hango.

(5) Nkumbulo weNtungamili weHango wakafanila kwagwa pasi seleba kwenlayo hapa uwamutjigwa.

(6) Mukuthama mihingo yeButungamili, Ntugamili wehango wakafanila hinga sepiwa kukwe mano neKhabhinethi, kuhhe kwekuti abe etebela ndimana (2) ipehhugwi. 111 Ngwa nelunyalalo

(1) Ntungamili weHango unasimba letshambidzila kuti kubenengwa netshambidzila lunyalalo, nepa mano Senethi nebaMilili bemitunhu kusanhu kwapinda mahhuba aligunotendeka (7 sitting days) ahingisa lelosimba.

(2) Senethi nebaMilili bemiTunhu, tjibvumilwano tjabhudza pakakubungana banopinda mbabi mbili-munhatu (two-thirds) yegubungano lose lePhalamente, bangabvumilana kuti tshambidzigwa kuti kube nengwa kwakatiwa neNtungamili weHango kulambiwe.

(3) Hapa Phalamente yati tshambidzigwa kuti kube nengwa kulambiwe, Ntungamili weHango wakafanila tola nhambo dzebhuda mungwa, elingisisa kuti tjaba tjeZimbabwe tjakadzibilika. 112 Masimba ewhilangoni

(1) Ntungamili weHango, hule kwelebelesana neKhabhinethi, angahingisa masimba emawhilangoni, kubva kuti, anga—

(a)

whilangoni nhu wakatongewa kene wakawanika nlandu wetjuluka nlayo;

(b)

jitisa ntongo wakapiwa nhu ntjinyi;

(c)

handulila ntongo wakapiwa nhu ntjinyi kuti ubenlelu; kene

2013

(d) dzima ntongo kwelubaka kene lipa nlipo wose kene lubabi gwenlipo wentongo kene nlandu wakatilikiwa nhu; kakale angahingisa masimba awe akasanghanu pakuwhila ngoni,thubulila, handuligwa kene dzima ntongo.

(2) Hapa nhu ungali weZimbabwe atongewa kuyimwe hango yatilako nlandu, Ntungamili weHango angatshambidzila kuti ntongo watilikiwa awutobon’wa sentongo kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu wehango kene imwe milayo yeZimbabwe.

(3) Whigwangoni kene thubuligwa ntongo kene handuligwa kene dzimiwa kwentongo kwakafanila kwagwa muGazethi. 113 Zwimo zwembatsha yetjaba

(1) Ntungamili weHango engabikila banhu kuti kunatjimbo tjembhatsha yetjaba tjiyapo muhango yose kene ku imwe ntha yehango yeZimbabwe netumila matama kuGazethi.

(2) Tshambidzigwa kwetjimo tjembatsha akutjahinga hule kwemahhuba aligumi nekuna kutangisa nehhuba lekwagwa nedusiwa kwembiko muGazethi kuhhe kwekuti, kusanhu kukapela mahhuba iyawa, tshambidzilo iyeyi ibvumiwe nemhabi mbili munhatu dzenhengo dzePhalamente mun’hangano weSenethi nePhalamente.

(3) Hapa Phalamente ingapaladziwa mulubaka gwemahhuba aligumi nekuna kwatjitshambidzigwa kuti kunatjimo tjembatsha muhango, tshambidzilo iyeyo inoma hule kwemahhuba alimakumi mabili nelin’ompela, kubvila kuhhuba lekwagwa nedusiwa kwembiko muGazethi, kuhhe kwekuti munayigogo lubaka tshambidzilo iyeyi inobvumiwa nenhengo njinji dzePhalamente dzwa mun’hangano weSenethi nePhalamente.

(4) Tshambidzilo yetjimo tjembatsha yehango yabvumiwa kusi kwendimana (2) kene (3) inohinga kwemimwedzi mitatu kubvila kuhhuba lekwagwa nedusiwa kwembiko muGazethi, kuhhe kwekuti yadusiwa kene isingatjahinga kusi kwendima iyeyi.

(5) Hapa tshambidzilo yetjimo tjembatsha yehango isabvumiwa hule kwelingisisiwa nePhalamente, kene hapa kungatikala kuti ayitolingiwa nePhalamente nelubaka gwalebwa mundima iyeyi, Ntungamili weHango wakafanila kuti, mumahhuba alikunotondeka, aduse tshambidzilo iyeyo nedusa mbiko muGazethi.

(6) Hapa, netjibvumilano tjadusiwa nenhengo dzabe dzilipo mun’hangano weSenethi nebamilili bemitunhu muPhalamente, Phalamente ingati tshambidzilo yetjimo tjembatsha yehango—

(a) yakafanila ndila mbeli kwelubaka gusingapinde mimwedzi mitatu, Ntungamili weHango wakafanila yendisilambeli tshambidzilo iyeyi nekulibilidza nedusa mbiko muGazethi.

(b) yakafanila dusiwa kene kuti ihinge mu imwe ntha, Ntungamili weHango wakafanila kuti amise tshambidzilo iyeyi kene alebe kuti tshambidzilo iyeyi inohinga munayeyo ntha nekulibilidza nedusa mbikomuGazethi.

(7) Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu wehango, hapa kunanhu unaludo nako, ingahakisisa bulebeswa gwe—

(a)

tshambidzilo yetjimo tjembatsha yehango;

(b) ndisigwa mbeli kwetshambidzilo yetjimo tjembatsha yehango.

(8) Khutha yenlayo kene yipi zwayo ingahakisisa bulebeswa gwenlayo watshambidzigwa, kene tjimwe tjiyeto tjatiwa, hapa kunatshambidzilo yetjimo tjembatsha yehango. LUBABI 5 Milili Nkulu Wenlayo 114 Milili Nkulu wenlayo

(1) Kuna Milili Nkulu wenlayo wakabigwa neNtungamili weHango.

(2) Nhu wabigwa seMilili Nkulu wenlayo unogala mutulo atjituna mbeli kweNtungamili weHango, kene nhu wapiwa simba neNtungamili weHango, etuna zwipila mun’hingo nemutulo itjetjo selondesedzwa muNdongoloso Yebutatu.

(3) Nhu engabigwa seMilili Nkulu wenlayo hapa kungatikala kuti abigiwe sejaji weKhutha Whulu.

(4) Mihingo yeMilili Nkulu wenlayo ndiyeyi—

(a)

kuti abe iye unopa hulumente mano nenzwenlayo;

(b) kuti amile Hulumente mundongoloso dzezwemilayo??

(c)

kuti akwale milayo akamila hulumente;

(d) kuti asusume, adzibilile kakale amilidze bhakhwa kwenlayo kakale amile tjaba, kakale

(e)

kuti athame imwe mihingo ingapiwa Milili Nkulu wenlayo neTjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente; kakale Milili Nkulu wenlayo angazwtila mihingo iyeyo kene atume bamwe bahingi banowuntjidza, banoohinga sekwaanobabudza.

(5) Milili Nkulu wenlayo—

(a)

angayenda kumihangano yeKhabhinethi, koga aatovota;

(b) angagala kakale alebeleke muSenethi nePhalamente, koga aatovota; kakale

(c)

angagala mukhutha yenlayo, senkwinya wekhutha yenlayo mundongoloso dzose hapa hulumente asingabhatike munayizo.

115 Dusiwa mubugalo kweMilili Nkulu wenlayo Ntungamili engadusa Milili Nkulu wenlayo mubugalo. DANHO 6 Lubahhe Gwemilayo LUBABI 1 Masimba Elubahhe Gwemilayo 116 Lubahhe Gwemilayo Lubahhe gweMilayo muZimbabwe gohanganyila Phalamente neNtungamili weHango ehinga etebela Danho ileli. 117 Tjimo nebupabi gwemasimba eLubahhe gwemilayo

(1) Masimba eLubahhe gwemilayo muZimbabwe anobva mubanhu kakale akatilikiwa muNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kakale anohingisiwa neLubahhe gwemilayokutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu.

(2) Masimba elubahhe gwemilayo anopa Lubahhe gweMilayo masimba—

(a)

ebakanya Nlayo Nkulu kunoyendidzana nendima 328;

(b) ebaka milayo yelunyalalo, gadzikana netungamila kubuya muZimbabwe; kakale

(c)

pa masimba emilayo limwe bunga kene butungamili kuyendidzana nendima 134. LUBABI 2 Phalamente

118 Phalamente Phalamente inobumbgwa neSenethi nebaMilili bemitunhu muPhalamente. 119 N’hingo wePhalamente

(1) Phalamente kwakafanila dzibilila Nlayo Nkulu nosusumisa Butungamili gwakatambunuka muZimbabwe.

(2) Phalamente inamasimba oyeta kuti zwimo zweNlayo Nkulu dzomilidziwa nokuti Hango nenhabi dzose nedzinohingidzana naHulumente dzihinge pasi kweNlayo Nkulu nemuludo gwetjaba.

(3) Nentha yekunowangwa mundimana (2), dzose nhabi nedzinohingidzana neHango naHulumente dzozwitjenesa muPhalamente. LUBABI 3 Senethi 120 Ma kweSenethi

(1) Senethi inamaSenetha alimakumi anahananlume, kukoti—

(a)

(b)

(c)

(d)

batanhatu banohalugwa muzwitjaba zweZimbabwe, nohingisa ndongoloso inondidzana nekumundimana (2); Makumi matanhatu ndiboHe, kukoti babili banohalugwa neLubahhe gwaboHe bezwiTjaba, kusizwiTjaba zwematoropo, mukati kweZimbabawe. Ntungamili neNtebedzeli weNtungamili muLubahhe GwaboHe muHango; kakale Babili banohalugwa nenzila imuNlayo weHalulo kuti bamilili banhu bakakubadzika.

(2) Halulo yemaSenetha yakafanila tiwa nenzila imuNlayo weHalulo, wakafanila kuti maSenetha ahalugwa pasi kwegubungano yolizanisa bose semundimana (1)(a).

(a)

kwakadzika mumavoti atigwa bakamilila halugwa bakamila gubungano dzotonga muzwiTjaba kuti babe bamilili bemitunhu muPhalamete

(b)

mukwala, bamilili bebetjilume nebetjikadzi banonobedzana, betjikadzi ndibo banokwagwa pehhugwi.

121 Fanila nesafanila halugwa seSenetha

(1) Nhu unafanilo dzohalugwa seSenetha sekwakwaka kwagwa mundima 120(1)(a) kene (d) nekuti—

(a) wakakwalisa vota; kakale

(b) kwakamele ebe watjiswikisa makumi mana emakole ozwagwa; engasafanila sekumundimana (4) kene (5).

(2) Nhu unafanilo dzehagwa abe Senetha aliHe kunobviwa mundima 120(1)(b) hapa—

(a)

(b)

ali He; kakale wakakwalisa semvoti;

kuhhe kwekuti ewanika esakafanila kutebegwa ndimana(4) kene (5).

(3) Nhu unafanilo dzehalugwa seSenetha sekunowangwa mundima 120(1)

(d) kene hapa elinhu wakakubadzika sekwakunolebgwa muNlayo weHalulo, kuhhe kwekuti ewanika esakafanila kutebegwa ndimana(4) kene (5).

(4) Nhu hatofanila hagwa abe Senetha hapa—

(a)

awanika esakafanila kutebegwa Ndongoloso yeBuna yekwalisa semvoti; kene

(b) mumakole ali mahanu kusanhu kwatiwa halulo, akasiya bugalo gwebaSenetha kene Milili wentunhu muPhalamente seleba kwendima 129(1)(i) nentha yewan’wa nlandu.

(5) Nhu hatofanila hagwa muhalulo yeSenethi inotiwa hapa kusina nhu umubugalo gweSenethi igogo hapa eliMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente. 122 Ntungamili weSenethi

(1) Hapa kubungana kwekutanga kwapinda halulo yebanhu bose kakale kusanhu kwabhakhwa dzimwe ndebo, Senethi yakafanila hala nghali webugalo unoba Ntngamili weSenethi.

(2) Gose lubaka kwakunoba kusina nhu ugele mubugalo gweNtungamili weSenethi, Senethi yakafanila nekulibilidza hala nhu unowhogala mubugalo igogo.

(3) Nhu unofanila hagwa abe Ntungamili weSenethi hapa aliSenetha kene akatongoba Senetha kene efanila hagwa seSenetha.

(4) Halulo yegala mubugalo gweNtungamili weSenethi yakafanila lawugwaneNkwalili wePhalamente elawugwa neKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe, kuhingisiwa mabhokisi evotela seleba kweNdawulo Dzimile, kakale mbhudilo yehalulo yakafanila zibisiwabanhu.

(5) Esanhu atangisa n’hingo uwe, Ntungamili weSenethi wakafanila tuna zwitunho zwekuzwipila negala mubugalo nezila yakatjenesiwa muNdongoloso yebuTatu (3) mbeli kweNtongi Nkulu weNlayo kene Ntongi Nkulu unowuntobela mubugalo uyapo.

(6) Senetha unohagwa kuti abe Ntungamili weSenethi hatjabvumigwa kuti abeSenetha, kwakafanila kuti kuhagwe umwe unobaSenetha kutebegwa zila yakatjenesiwa muNlayo weHalulo.

(7) Ntungamili weSenethi angajita mubugalo hule kwezibisa Senethi nenlomo kene, nekwalila Nkwalili wePhalamemente, hapa Senethi isingahangane,

(8) Ntungamili weSenethi wakafanila jita mubugalo—

(a)

hhuba Senethi ihangana kwekutanga hule kwehalulo yebabanhu bose;

(b)

hapa egala gumwe bugalo gwehingila tjaba kene awana n’hingo kuwumwe nhu;

(c)

hapa eba Milili weNtunhu muPhalamente kene Tjilebeleki;

(d)

hapa eba Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, Minisita kene Ntobedzeli waMinisita;

(e)

hapa kungatikala zwinhu zwinoota kuti asiye mubugalo gugwe, hapa wayeliSenetha; kene

(f)

2013

hapa kwatiwa tjibvumilano nembabi mbili munhatu dzenhengo dziyapo muSenethi kuti adusiwe mubugalo.

123 Ntobedzeli weNtungamili weSenethi

(1) Nekulibilidza hule kwehalula Ntungamili weSenethi hule kwehalulo yebanhu bose, Senethi yakafanila hala umwe wenhengo dzayo kuti abe Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weSenethi.

(2) Lubaka gose hapa kusina ugele mubugalo gweNtebedzeli weNtungamili wesenethi, Senethi yakafanila hala nhu unotola bugalo igogo nekulibilidza.

(3) Halulo yeNtebedzeli weNtungamili weSenethi yakafanila lawugwa neNkwalili wePhalamente elawugwa neKhomishini yeZimbabwe yeHalulo, kuhingisiwa mabhokisi evotela seleba kweNdawulo Dzimile, kakale mbhudilo yehalulo yakafanila zibisiwabanhu.

(4) Asanhu ekatangisa n’hingo, Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weSenethi wakafanila tuna mbeli kweNtongi Nkulu kene jaji nkulu unowuntobela kuti unozwipila mun’hingo nebugalo gugwe setjenesiwa muNdongoloso yebuTatu.

(5) Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weSenethi angajita mubugalo nezwilebela nenlomo muSenethi kene, hapa Senethi isakahangana, akwale lukwalo gunonda kuNtungamili weSenethi kene, hapaNtungamili weSenethi esipo, kuNkwalili wePhalamente.

(6) Ntebedzeli weNtungamili wesenethi wakafanila siya bugalo gugwe—

(a)

hapa esitjiSenetha;

(b) hapa ebaTjilebeleki;

(c)

hapa eba Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, Minisita kene Ntobedzeli waMinisita;

(d) hapa kwabvumigwaniwa mugubungano leSenethi banhu bepinda mbabi mbili-munhatu (two-thirds) yegubungano lose kuti adusiwe mutulo. LUBABI 4 LUBAHHE GWEBAMILILI BEMITUNHU MUPHALAMENTE 124 Ma kweLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente

(1) Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhumuPhalamente guna banhu—

(a)

bali makulu mabili ana gumi (210) bakahalugwa nevota banobva kumitunhu ili makulu mabili ana gumi (210) seganhugwa kweZimbabwe; kakale

(b) bamwe kakale betjikadzi belimakumi matanhatu, belikutanhatu banobe bebva kulimwe nelimwe dunhu seganhugwa kweZimbabwe, bakahalugwa nezila yemila makubungano ezwepolitiki amuPhalamente, kwelubaka gweLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente neSenethi.

(2) Halugwa kwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente kwakafanila tiwa nezila kutebegwa Nlayo weHalulo.

(3) Mfanilo dzekwalisa semvoti nevota muhalulo yebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente dzakathalusiwa muNdongoloso yebuna.

125 Fanila nesafanila halugwa sebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente

(1) Nhu wakafanila halugwa seMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente hapa—

(a)

akakwalisa semvoti; kakale

(b)

enamakole anopinda makumi mabili analin’ompela (21); kuhhe kwekuti asakafanila kutebegwa ndimana (2) kene (3).

(2) Nhu azofanila halugwa Milili weNtunhu muPhalamente hapa—

(a)

Hazofanila kutebegwa Ndongoloso yeBuna yekwalisa semvoti; kene

(b)

mumakole alimahanu (5) kusanhu kaba nehalulo, wajita mutulo muSenethi kene Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kutebegwa ndimana 129(1)(i) hule kwekuti awan’wa nlandu.

(3) Nhu hazofanila hagwa pahalulo yeMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente inotiwa nentha yesabapo kweMilili wentunhu whoyo, hapa eliMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente. 126 Tjilebeleki tjeLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente

(1) Pa kubungana kwekutanga kwapinda halulo, kakale kusanhu kwabhakhwa dzimwe ndebo, Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente gwakafanila hala nghali webugalo unoba Tjilebeleki.

(2) Gose lubaka kwakunoba kusina nhu ugele mubugalo gweTjilebeleki, Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente gwakafanila nekulibilidza hala nhu unowhogala mubugalo igogo.

(3) Nhu unofanila hagwa abe Tjilebeleki hapa aliMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente kene akatongoba Milili weNtunhu muPhalamente kene efanila hagwa seMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente.

(4) Halulo yegala mubugalo gweTjilebeleki yakafanila lawugwa neNkwalili wePhalamente akazwisa pasi kweKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe, kuhingisiwe mabhokisi evotela seleba kweNdawulo dzimile, kakale mbhudilo yehalulo yakafanila zibisiwabanhu.

(5) Esanhu atangisa n’hingo uwe, Tjilebeleki tjakafanila tuna zwitunhozwekuzwipila negala mubugaalo nezila yakatjenesiwa muNdongoloso yebuTatu (3) mbeli kweNtongi Nkulu weNlayo kene Ntongi Nkulujaji nkulu unowuntobela mubugalo uyapo.

(6) Milili weNtunhu muPhalamente unohagwa kuti abe Tjilebeleki hatjabvumigwa kuti abeMilili weNtunhu MuPhalamente, kwakafanila kuti kuhagwe umwe unobaMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente kutebegwa zila yakatjenesiwa muNlayo weHalulo.

(7) Tjilebeleki tjingajita mubugalo hule kwezibisa Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente nenlomo kene, nekwalila Nkwalili wePhalamemente, hapa Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente gusingahangane,

(8) Tjilebeleki tjakafanila jita mubugalo—

(a)

hhubaLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente guhangana kwekutanga hule kwehalulo yebabanhu bose;

(b) hapa tjigala gumwe bugalo gwehingila tjaba kene awana n’hingo kuwumwe nhu;

(c)

hapa tjibaMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente kene tjibaNtungamili weSenethi;

(d)

hapa tjiba Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, Minisita kene Ntobedzeli waMinisita;

(e)

hapa kungatikala zwinhu zwinoota kuti asiye mubugalo gugwe, hapa wayeliMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente; kene

(f)

hapa kwatiwa tjibvumilano nembabi mbili munhatu dzenhengo dziyapo muLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kuti adusiwe mubugalo.

127 Ntebedzeli weTjilebeleki tjePhalamente

(1) Nekulibilidza hule kwehalula Tjilebeleki hule kwehalulo yebanhu bose, Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamentegwakafanila hala umwe wenhengo dzayo kuti abe Ntebedzeli weTjilebeki.

(2) Lubaka gose hapa kusina ugele mubugalo gweNtebedzeli weTjilebeleki, Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamentegwakafanila hala nhu unotola bugalo igogo nekulibilidza.

(3) Halulo yeTjilebeleki yakafanila tungamigwaneNkwalili wePhalamente elawugwa neKhomishini yeZimbabwe yeHalulo, kuhingisiwa mabhokisi evotela seleba kweNdawulo Dzimile, kakale mbhudilo yehalulo yakafanila zibisiwabanhu.

(4) Asanhu ekatangisa n’hingo, Ntebedzeli weTjilebeleki wakafanila tuna mbeli kweNtongi Nkulu kene jaji nkulu unowuntobela kuti unozwipila mun’hingo nebugalo gugwe setjenesiwa muNdongoloso yebuTatu.

(5) Ntebedzeli weTjilebeleki angajita mubugalo nezwilebela nenlomo muLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kene, hapaLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamentegusigele, akwale lukwalo gunonda kuTjilebeleki kene, hapa Tjilebeleki tjisipo, kuNkwalili wePhalamente.

(6) Ntebedzeli weTjilebeleki wakafanila siya bugalo gugwe—

(a)

hapa esitjinNhengo yeLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente;

(b) hapa atjibe Ntungamili weSenethi;

(c)

hapa atjibe Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, Minisita kene Ntobedzeli waMinisita; kene

(d) hapa tjibvumilano dzedusiwa kukwe mutulo tjitje tjatiwa nemhabi mbili munhatu dzenhengo dziyapo muPhalamente. LUBABI 5 Lubaka Gweba Mutulo Kwebamilili Bemitunhu Muphalamente 128 Tuna kweMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente

(1) Milili weNtunhu muPhalamente esanhu ekatola bugalo gugwe muPhalamente, Milili wakafanila tuna tjituno tjeMilili wentunhu muPhalamente selondesedzewa muNdongoloso Yebutatu.

(2) Tjituno tjinolebgwa mundimana (1) tjakafanila togwa mbeli kweNkwalili wePhalamente. 129 Lubaka gweba mutulo kweMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente

(1) Bugalo gweMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente gungasabe nenhu—

(a)

papaladziwa kweLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente;

(b) hapa Milili wentunhu asiya n’hingo uwe nekwala lukwalo gwesiya n’hingo egusa kuNtungamili weSenethi kene kuTjilebeleki, sekungabe kwatikala;

(c)

hapa Milili wentunhu abe Ntungamili weHango kene abeNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango;

(d)

hapa Milili wentunhu akabe Ntungamili weSenethi kene Tjilebeleki kene abe mhungo yegumwe lubabi;

(e)

hapa Milili wentunhu esatjafanila kwaligwa vota;

(f)

hapa, esapiwa lubaka gwesabapo mun’hingo neNtungamili weSenethi, Milili wentunhu akasibepo mulubabi gwaalinhengo yago kwemahhuba alimakumi mabili nelin’ompela etobelelana lubabi gugwe guba nemihangano, lubabi igogu gobvumilana, nevota kwegwamu lin’ompela lebanhu bose kuti atolegwe bugalo gugwe;

(g) hapa Milili wentunhu angawamutjila n’hingo wehingila tjaba kene abenhengo yebunga lezwenlayo, bunga linotungamigwa nehulumente, khansili wezwitjaba kene wematoropo kene sekhansili wemitunhu;

(h) hapa Milili wentunhu waka ehingila tjaba kene alinhengo kene n’hingi webunga lezwenlayo, bunga linotungamigwa neHulumente, khansili wezwitjaba kene wematoropo kene khansili wemitunhu nehhuba laanopiwa bugalo seMilili wentunhu muPhalamente, ekokon’wa siya n’hingo iwoyo mumahhuba alimakumi matatu kubva kunalelo hhuba;

(i)

hapa Milili wentunhu angasungwa—

(i) ekaba nenlandu muZimbabwe unopaladza bugalabwe, lulama kene kayikanyiwa; kene

(ii) pahhe kweZimbabwe kobe kwatikala nemuZimbabwe, kobe kulinlandu wopaladza bugalabwe, lulama kene kayikanyiwa; kakale wahhaligwa mujele kwemwedzi mitanhatu esingayenelisi kuti alipilenlandu kene alangiwe pahhe kwe jele, koga kukatikala kuti Milili wemitunhu muPhalamente ekakumbila kutinlandu usiwe ntome kene tapudza lubaka gohhaliligwa kube mimwedzi isingaswike mitanhatu kene akalangiwa eletshwe hhaliligwa.

(j)

hapa Milili wentunhu muPhalamente esingatja tubule lipa zwikwelete pasi kwenlayo unohinga muZimbabwe esenhu ekatjidziwa kene letjiwa, kene hapa Milili wentunhu wePhalamente wayeta n’hingo pasi kwenlayo nebanhu bekwelete kusagcimiwa kene siwa ntome;

(k) hapa Milili wentunhu muPhalamente ekabhuda mugubungano yedzotongewa kwehango dzakabanginisa muPhalamente, Tjilebeleki muPhalamente kene Ntungamili weSenethi unokwaligwa lukwalo guleba kuti Milili wentunhu muPhalamente atjipo mugubungano yedzotongewa kwehango.

(l)

hapa Milili wentunhu muPhalamente wakazwinginila esipo mugubungano yezwepolitiki, ebe engina mugubungano yedzonotongewa kwehango.

(m) hapa Milili wentunhu ekawangwa pasi kwenlayo unohinga muZimbabwe egwala bulubi kene nkumbulo usingasahinge zubanana; kene

(n) hapa Milili wentunhu awangwa enanlandu pasi kweNlayo weHalulo kakale ekawangwa neKhuta whulu esafanila kwalisa semvoti kene kongwa votela halulo.

(2) Milili wentunhu muPhalamente inobviwa mundimana (1)(i) wakwala tjikumbilo tjolambanlandu uwe engandilambeli ehinga adze awane hhandulo, ehinga seMilili wentunhu wePhalamente epiwa mbhayilo, pahhe kokuti Khuta yawuhhalilila kwakalindiwa hhandulo yenlandu. .

(3) Milili wentunhu wePhalamente ekaba gali wetulo welubahhe gwetjaba unojita mutulo tjaagele esenhu akatola bugalo senghalitulo welubahhe gwetjaba. LUBABI 6 Masimba Elubahhe Gwemilayo Ne Amwe Masimba 130 Masimba nen’hingo yeSenethi neyeLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente

(1) Pahhe pekwakalebwa mundongoloso yebuhanhu, mun’hingo wemasimba elubahhe gwenlayo Senethi kose neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kunamasimba wotanga, lulamisa, tebela kene laha masimba elubahhe gwenlayo.

(2) Pehhugwi kwemihingo yabo pasi kweNlayo Nkulu, Senethi neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kungahinga imwe mihingo yakwapiwa kene yakwatilikiwa nenlayo. 131 Zwisungo zwemilayo zwatiwa nePhalamente nezila yetshambidzigwa kwazo

(1) Masimba onlayo wePhalamente unohingisiwa pasi kwethaluso yezwisungo zwenlayo wePhalamente.

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente inlayo utjasusumiwa waka—

(a)

wakatugwa ukotshambidzigwa neSenethi neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente; kakale

(b)

bvumiwa nosayiniwa neNtungamili weHango; etebela kumuNlayo Nkulu.

(3) Matama otshambidzila Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente anoti “Tshambidzigwa nePhalamente neNtungamili weZimbabwe”, kene matama anondidzana nayihawa.

(4) Ndongoloso ino tobegwa muLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente nemuSenethi kwakalingwa milayo inosusuwiwa yakakwagwa muNdongoloso yeBuhanhu.

(5) Nlayo utjasusumiwa ukabvumiwa neSenethi neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente pasi kwendongoloso yebuhanhu, Ntungamili weSenethi kene Tjilebeleki kutiwe nelubaka—

(a)

ete kuleziwe kuNtungamili kuti kubvumiwe nosayiniwa, kuhanganila dzose whalo dzinohakika pasi kweNlayo Nkulu kuti kuyende neNlayo utjasusumiwa; kakale

(b)

pa tjaba luzibo gwehhuba Nlayo utjasusumiwa wakayendesewa kuNtungamili weHango.

(6) Hapa Nlayo utjasusumiwa watjipiwa Ntungamili kuti awubvume newusayina, kwakafanila, kutole mahhuba asingaswike makumi mabili nelin’ompela, kene—

(a)

awubvume kakale awusayine, ebe yeta kuti ubhudisiwe muGazethi masinyane; kene

(b) hapa ewumbona sekungati awuzotambunuka kene enakusagutshikana nekwawo, unobwiliza Nlayo utjasusumiwa kuPhalamente ehingisa Nkwalili wePhalamente, kose nelukwalo gunoleba nekwe mamo ose asingawungutshe netjikumbilo tjekuti Nlayo utjasusumiwa ulingisisiwe kakale.

(7) Hapa Nlayo utjasusumiwa wabwiliziwa kuPhalamente kutebegwa ndimana

(6)(b), Tjilebeleki tjakafanila nekulibilidza dana n’hangano weLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente, wakafanila—

(a)

lingisisa kakaleNlayo utjasusumiwa kan’ompela nekusingagutshe Ntungamili weHango; kene

(b

tshambidzilaNlayo utjasusumiwa, hule kwekayigwa kene usakayigwa, kuli mbabi mbili munhatu dzeLubahhe gweMabilili beMitunhu muPhalamente;

kakale kene kwanda nayipi zila Tjilebeleki tjakafanila thama kuti Nlayo utjasusumiwa undisiwe kuNtungamili weHango nekulibilidza kuti awumbvume kakale awutshambidzile kakale tjakafanila zibisa tjaba kuti Nlayo utjasusumiwa wakandisiwa kuNtungamili weHango wupi nsi.

(8) Hapa Nlayo utjasusumiwa wandisiwa kuNtungamili weHango kutebegwa ndimana (7) tjoba tjopetela kose kwakusingagutshe Ntungamili weHango, Ntungamili weHango wkafanila bvuma Nlayo utjasusumiwa awutshambidzile mumahhuba alimakumi mabili anahhuba lin’ompela ekotjithama kuti udusiwe patjena nekwagwa muGazethi nekulibilidza, koga hapa Ntungamili weHango etjina kusagutshikana nekweNlayo utjasusumiwa, wakafanila kuti mulubka igogo—

(a)

abvume Nlayo utjasusumiwa awutshambidzile, kusineni nesagutshikana kukwe; kene

(b)

andise Nlayo utjasusumiwa kuKhutha yeNlayo nkulu kuti kupiwaniwe mano nekwebamunlayo netambunuka kweNlayo utjasusumiwa.

(9) Hapa kutebegwa ndimana (8) Khutha yeNlayo Nkulu iti Nlayo utjasusumiwa wakatambunuka kakale umunlayo, Ntungamili weHango wakafanila wumbvuma kakale awutshambidzile tjinyolotjo kakae athame kuti udusiwe patjena nekwagwa muGazethi nekulibilidza.

(10) Hapa Nlayo utjasusumiwa upiwa Ntungamili weHango kuti awumbvume kakale awutshambidzile usina sethifikethi tjinohakika nemamo eNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kene api zwawo, Ntungamili weHango hazofanila wumbvuma newutshambidzila iwhoyo Nlayo utjasusumiwa kudza kunobepo sethifikethi itjetjo koga wakafanila thama kuti Nkwalili wePhalamente azibisiwe, nelukwalo nekulibilidza, kuti sethifikethi hatjizobepo kuhhisiwa Nlayo utjasusumiwa. 132 Tangisa hinga kweZwisungo zweMilayo zwatiwa nePhalamente Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjotangisa hinga hhuba latjinodusiwa patjena nezila yekwagwa muGazethi, kene hhuba linoba lakakwagwa muTjisungo kene tjimwe tjitshambidzilo. 133 Longewa kweZwisungo zweMilayo zwatiwa nePhalamente

(1) Hapa Ntungamili weHango atjibvuma akatshambidzila Tjisungo tjeNlayo muphalamente, Nkwalili wephalamente wakafanila ndisa, itjo zwatjo itjetjo Tjisungo tjenlayo tjabon’wa, tjikabvumiwa, tjikatshambidzigwa neNtungamili weHango neNtolo weHango yeZimbabwe kuti nditjo zwatjo ntja, kuti tjinobeyapo mubugalo gweWunokwala kakale wakabhata whalo dzeKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu, beyapo kwatjo lebululagwendongoloso dziyitjetjo Tjisungo tjeNlayo kuhhe kwekutiTjisungo tjeNlayo itjetjo tjikayigwe kutebegwa Tjisungo tjenlayo muPhalamente tjinobviwa mundimana

(2).

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayo muPhalamente tjingathama kuti nlayo, kene lubabi gwawo, udusiwe patjena hule kwepamhulugwa kakale tjingathama kuti—

(a)

2013

pakudusiwa patjena kwa nlayo wapamhulgwat, ube iwo unowamutjigwa sewakatambunuka;

(b) nlayo watambunidziwa waakafanilakuti ukwalisiwemubugalo gweWunokwala kakale wakabhata whalo dzeKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu; kakale.

(c)

whalo dzalongwa mubugalo gweWunokwala kakale wakabhata whalo dzeKhuta yeNlayo mukhutandilo lebeswa lino wamutjigwa.

(3) Bulebeswa gweTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kene gwehanduligwakwenlayo akutosendama mu mukwalisiwa kusi kwelutabi igogu. 134 Milayo Mitukununu MuTjisungo tjeNlayo muPhalamente tjipi natjipi, Phalamente ingahandamisa masimba ebakanya mamo enlayo ehingidzana kakale alipasi kweTjisungo tjeNlayo itjetjo, koga—

(a)

Masimba ePhalamente ebakanya nlayo hazofanila kobewa;

(b) mamo enlayo hazofanila tjuluka kene ganhula ndulamilo nensununguko kene dzipi nadzipi dzibolebgwa muLukwalo gweBigwa kweNdulamilo;

(c)

mamo enlayo akafanila ndidzana neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente aakabambigwa pasi kwatjo;

(d)

Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjakafanila sapatjena kunoma simba, tjimo nebupabi gwempanga wenlayo ungatiwa, nezila dzinobvumila hingisiwa nempanga wenlayo;

(e)

mamo enlayo hana samba lenlayo kuhhe kwekuti adusiwa patjena nezila yekwagwa muGazethi.

(f)

milayo itjasusumiwa yakafanila yendesewa kuLubahhe gweBamilili beMitunhu muPhalamente nekumuNlayo unopiwa Khomithi yeNlayo muPhalamente kuti ilingisisiwe. LUBABI 7 Ndisiwa Kwen’hingo Muphalamente

135 Ntungamili wePhalamente

(1) Tjilebeleki nditjo Ntungamili wePhalamente koga wakafanila hinga mihingo iye sema kwenlayo.

(2) Nema kweNlayo, Ntungamili weSenethi uNtebedzeli weNtungamili wePhalamente kakale unohinga seNtungamili hapa Tjilebeleki tjisitubule bhata nayigogo lubaka bhata n’hingo. 136 Banhu banotungamila muPhalamente

(1) Nhu unotungamila kakale elawula n’hangano weSenethi wakafanila ba—

(a)

Ntungamili weSenethi, hapa esipo, Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weSenethi; kene

(b) hapa Ntungamili weSenethi neNtebedzeli weNtungamili weSenethi, Senetha wakahaligwa n’hingo iwoyo neSenethi, koga Senetha iwoyo hazofanila ba Minisita ken Ntobedzeli waMinisita.

(2) Nhu unotungamila kakale elawula n’hangano weLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente wakafanila ba—

(a)

Tjilebeleki, apa tjisipo, Ntebedzeli weTjilebeleki; kene

(b) Hapa Tjilebeleki neNtebedzeli weTjilebeleki, Milili weNtunhu muPhalamente wakahaligwa n’hingo iwhoyo neLubahhe gwebaMilili muPhalamente, koga iwhoyo Milili weNtunhu muPhalamente azofanila ba Minista kene Ntobedzeli waMinista.

(3) Tjilebeleki, kene apa tjisipo Ntungamili weSenethi, wakafanila tungamila kakale elawula n’hangano weLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente neSenethi. 137 Bunji gwenhengo dzePhalamente gumunlayo gunota kuti igale

Senethi neLubahhe gwebaMilili bePhalamente kwakafanila longa patjena nekumuNlayo bungana gwebanhu gunobvumigwa kuti n’hangano undile mbeli kene bamwe besipo. 138 Vota nendulamo yewhiligwa muPhalamente

(1) Pahhe kwekuti Nlayo Nkulu ujalo—

(a)

yose mibhuzo inobhuzwiwa nekwetjisungo tjePhalamente (Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kene Senethi) inobhuda nevotegwa nebaMilili bemiTunhu kene Senethi bayapo;

(b) Nhu unotungamila kakale elawula n’hangano Phalamente (Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kene Senethi) hatobvumigwa vota pandebo inoba ilebgwa muPhalemente;

(c)

hapa mavoti ebaMilili bemiTunhu kene Senethi akalizana ndebo yayivotegwa inosendekewa.

(2) Batebedzeli beNtungamili weHango banamasimba egala nelebeleka, koga besingavote, muPhalamente (Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente neSenethi).

(3) MaMinisita neBatobedzeli bemaMinisita banamasimba egala nelebeleka muPhalamente (Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente neSenethi), koga besina ndulamilo yevota, muPhalamente (Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente neSenethi).

(4) Nhengo dzeKhomithi yeNlayo muPhalamente nemamwe makhomithi ePhalamente banobvumigwaa gala nelebeleka muPhalamente kutigwa nginisa kene hapa kuzuwiwa nekwendongoloso yeKhomithi yabakamilililamuphalamente, koga habana masimba ovota habesi nhengo dzePalamente.

(5) Hapa Nhengo yePhalamente, kuhhe kwaMinisita kene Ntobedzeli waMinisita, atula Nlayo utjasusumiwa muPhalamente yahali nuungo, kakale Phalamente yatshambidzila woyu Nlayo, iyeyiya Nhengo kene, isipo, imwe Nhengo yePhalamente inamasimba egala nelebeleka muwumwe n’hangano wePhalamente isiyagala kutanga kwakabhakhwa ndebo yeNlayo utjasusumiwa, ayina masimba evota mun’hangano wePhalamente imwe. 139 Ndawulo Dzimile

(1) Ndongoloso dzeLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente neSenethi zotungamigwa nemilayo inoyi Ndawulo Dzimile, inotiwa nelimwe nelimwe gubungano pakati kwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kene Senethi kene akahangana kulingwa tjalebgwa neKhomithiyeNdamulo Dzimile neMilayo.

(2) Ndawulo Dzimile dzingapa—

(a)

tshambidzigwa kweMilayo itjasusumiwa muPhalamente;

(b) halugwa nezila yehinga kwemakhomithi nepiwa nlandu wehinga;

(c)

zila masimba, bhatsho, dzibilidza kwePhalamente yakungahingisawa nayo;

(d) Bhuzwiwa kweMaMinisita nebaBtatshi beMaMinisita nebaMilili bePhalamente;

(e)

Zwibhata kwebaMilili bePhalamente;

(f)

bvumigwa kwendulamilo yetjaba kuti phethishini kuPhalamente; kakale

(g) lingwa kwemazwibhato nendongoloso muPhalamente (Lubahhe gwebaMilili be Phalamente kene Senethi).

(3) Zwiyeto nendongoloso dzePhalamente nemakhomithi ayo, selondesedzwa muNdawulo Dzimile, zwakafanila susuma butjena, dzakafanila susuma kakale kuti nhengo dzemakubungano ezwepolitiki dzibhatike muPhalamente netjaba, kakale dzakafanila benekulizana nendulwamo.

(4) Khomithi yakatiwa neNdawulo Dzimile yakafanila lakidza kwazo kwazo, ma kwePhalamente kene Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kene Senethi kunohingisa Ndawulo Dzimile idzedzo munazwepolitiki nezwebulume nebukadzi. 140 Ndebo dzinotugwa neNtungamili weHango nematama aanotumila kuPhalamente

(1) Ntungamili weHango engatubula kuti alebeleke neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kene Senethi kene mun’hangano wePhalamente.

(2) Ntungamili weHango angatumila matama kuLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kene Senethi, kakale matama iyawa akafanila bagwa neNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango kene Minisita kene nenhu unotungamila Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kene Senethi kwatumigwa matama.

(3) Ntungamili weHango enganda kumihangano yePhalamente kuti ahandule mibhuzo nekwendebo dziyabo dzalondesedzewa muNdawulo Dzimile.

(4) Ntungamili weHango wakafanila lebeleka neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente neSenethi mun’hangano un’ompela nekwetjimo tjehango kan’ompela kene kunopinda kan’ompela negole, kakale Tjilebeleki neNtungamili weSenethi bakafanila yeta zwinhu zwakafanila kuti Phalamente iwamutjile matama akajalo. 141 Swikilila Phalamente kwebanhu nebhatika kwabo muPhalamente Phalamente yakafanila—

(a)

Yeta kuti tjaba tjibhatike mundongoloso dzenlayo nedzimwe ndongoloso nemundongoloso dzose dzemabunga ayo;

(b) Be nelebeswa lekuti mhabi dzinohaka bhatika dzobudziwa nekwemilayo itjasusumiwa itjalingiwa nePhalamente, kuhhe kwekuti babudza kusingahakike; kakale

(c)

mazwibhato nezwinhu zwayinota zwibe patjena kakale ite mihangano yayo, neyemakhomithi ayo, pabanhu, nekene nhambo dzingatogwa—

(i) kuti kube nekunyalala mundongoloso dzephalamente

(ii) lawula swikilila banhu, kuhanganyisila yendesewa kwendebo, kuPhalamente nemakhomithi ayo;

(iii) kusinginisiwe tjaba, kuhanganyisila bezweyendesiwa kwendebo, mumihangano yemaKhomithi, kakale;

(iv) be nelebeswa lekuti banhu banohogwa kakale, hapa kuhakika, kulambidziwe ngina kuPhalamente kene dusiwa kwenhu muPhalamente, koga idzedzo nhambo dzakafanila benebunhu kutjaba tjakazwimila, tjinandulamo, tjinolemekedza nhu, tjinakulizana nensununguko. 142 Simba lendongoloso muPhalamente

(1) Hayika kwe imwe nhengo yeSenethi kene yeLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente, kene misiwa n’hingo kweMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente, akutohhalilila Senethi kene Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kuti ite ndongoloso dzayo.

(2) Kuti nhu wabesafanila kuti angine avote mun’hangano weSenethi kene weLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente wangina kene kuti wabhatika mundongoloso dzeSenethi kene dzeLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente akutobva kuti ndongoloso idzedzo adzitohinga.

LUBABI 8 TJIBAKA, PALADZIWA NEGALA KWEPHALAMENTE 143 Tjibaka nepaladziwa kwePhalamente

(1) Phalamente inohalugwa kuti ihinge kwemakole mahanu anotangisa kubva pahhuba lebigiwa mutulo kweNtungamili wahalugwa netangisa n’hingo kukwe selondesedzewa mundima 94(1)(a), kakale Phalamente inopaladziwa pakati kwebusiku kumuka kulihhuba lekutanga levota muhalulo dziyapo selebwa mundima ya 144.

(2) Ntungamili weHango wakafanila paladza Phalamente hapa Senethi neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente, benamihangano yakasiyana, nemavoti embabi mbili munhatu dzenhengo dzose dziyapo muSenethi neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente, yabvumilana paladza.

(3) Ntungamili weHango engapaladza Phalamente hapa Lubahhe gweBamilili beMitunhu muPhalamente gwalamba tshambidzila Nlayo utjasusumiwa unolebgwa mundima 305.

(4) Tjibvumilano tjepaladza Phalamente selondesedzewa mundimana (3)tjinga, nekumbigwa kweMilili wentunhu muPhalamente, bigiwa ntome kuti tjikolingisisiwa ne Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu.

(5) Tjikumbilo tjolingisisa paladziwa kwePhalemente yakafanila hadzisiwa neKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu weHango kusenhu kukaswika mahhuba anotendeka kwatjibudziwa Hango nekwatikala, kakale—

(a)

khuta yeNlayo Nkulu kwakafanila ilawule tjikumbilo mahhuba elimakumi mana esenhu ekaswika kumilikila tjahhadzwa; kakale

(b)

kutjaka mililigwa bhudililo yetjikumbilo inobva muKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu, papaladziwa kwePhalamente komisiwa.

144 Halulo yebanhu bose inotiwa nentha yepaladziwa kwePhalamente

(1) Hapa Phalamente isanhu ikadusa tjisungo tjepaladza selondesedzewa mundima 143(2), Ntungamili weHango wakafanila, ehingisa masimba awe, adane kakale abige mahhuba ehalulo yebanhu bose inootiwa pakati kwelubaka gwalebgwa mundima 158.

(2) Hapa—

(a)

Phalamente yatjibvumilana kuti ipalale itebela ndima 143(2);

(b)

Ntungamili weHango atjipaladza Phalamente etebela ndima 143;

(c)

Ntungamili weHango atjipaladza Phalamente hulekwetobokewa kwePhalamente kutebegwa ndima 109(4); kene

(d) Phalamente inopaladziwa hulekwetobokewa kwePhalamente kutebegwandima 109(5); Ntungamili weHango wakafanila,ehingisa masimba awe, adane kakale abige mahhuba ehalulo yebanhu bose inootiwa mahhuba elimakumi elihhanalume esanhu ekaswika hule kwekuti Phalamente yabvumilana palala kene Ntungamili weHango wapaladza Phalamente kene Phalamente yapaladziwa, kuyendelana nekungabe kutikala.

(3) Mahhuba ehalulo yebanhu bose adangwa kutebegwa ndimana (1) kene

(2) akafanila tshambidzigwa neNtungamili weHango hule kwelebesana neKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe. 145 Gala kwekutanga kwePhalamente hule kwehalulo

(1) Gala kwekutanga kwePhalamente hule kwehalulo kwakafanila tiwa nelubaka nehhuba lalebwa neNtungamili weHango, koga hhuba ilelo alizofanila kuti liswike kwatjipela mahhuba alimakumi matatu hule kwekuti Ntungamili weHango wahalugwa atole bugalo gugwe selebgwa mundima 94.

(2) Kudzakuswika halulo yeNtungamili weSenethi kene Tjilebeleki,sekungabe kulipo, n’hangano wekutanga weLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kene Senethi wakafanila tungamigwa neNkwalili wePhalamente. 146 Lubaka gwegala negwenyaluluka Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kene Senethi gozwisila lubaka gwegala kwabo nekuti gala kwabo koobe ngapani, kuhhe kwegala kwabo kwekutanga, nezwibaka zwenyalaluka, koga—

(a)

Ntungamili weHango engagadza Phalamente kene nagupi lubaka kuti kutiwe zwimwe zwinhu.

(b) A k u z o f a n i l a g a l a m a h h u b a a n o p i n d a g u l u l i n a m a k u m i alihanakadziLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente keneSenethi kusagala. 147 Sendekiwa kweMilayo itjasusumiwa, ndebo, maphethishini neyimwe mihingo pakupaladziwa kwePhalamente Mupaladziwa kwePhalamente, dzose ndongoloso dzadzisanhu dzikapela dzomisiwa, neNlayo unosusuwiwa, ndebo, phethishini ne dzimwe ndebo dzopalala.

LUBABI 9 WHOKU NAWHOKUYA KWAKALINGISANA NEPHALAMENTE

148 Sununguka nelutabilo gwePhalamente

(1) Ntungamili weSenethi, Tjilebeleki nebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente bakasununguka kuti balebe soda kwabo muPhalamente nekumakhomithi ePhalamente kakale, koga kwakafanila batobele milayo yePhalamente, abatotilikiwa nlandu, abatosungiwa kene hhaliligwa kene lipisiwa netjabaleba, tjabathama kene tjabahhisa muPhalamente kene mumakhomithi ayo.

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjinga—

(a)

(b)

(c)

bvumila kumwe sununguka, gumwe lutabilo neamwe masimba ePhalamente neNhengo dzayo nebahingi bayo; layidza zila dzosa tobegwa kwenlayo muPhalamente, hapa kwatjinya Nhengo dzePhalamente kene bamwe banhu; kakale pa ndulamilo yohandula, kuhingisiwa Tjilebeleki kene Ntungamili weSenethi, kuyendelana nendebo, pekwebanhu bawha buthuku nekwalebwa nekwabo muPhalamente; koga akuna Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjinobvumila Phalamente kene baMilili bemitunhu kene bahingi kuti belange setjimo tjenlandu, setjinyila Phalamente.

149 Ndulamilo yeyendesa zwikumbilo kuPhalamente

(1) Wose nhu nenhu ugele kumunlayo muZimbabwe unandulamilo yeyendesa zwikumbilo kuPhalamente kuti ilingisise kene ini zwatjo tjipasi kwesimba layo, kuhanganyila tjitshambidzilo, bakanya kene tambunudziwakwenlayo.

(2) Kwakafanila tjenesewa muNdawulo Dzimile, zila zwikumbilo yazwinoyendesewa nayo kuPhalamente, nenhambo inootogwa nePhalamente pakuwamutjila tjiikumbilo. 150 Panonginila Phalamente Phalamente ingathama n’hangano kuyimwe ntha isinhanga yePhalamente, hapa kulikuti injemulo yetjaba, inozwidzibilila kene kulelu kuti ihangane ipapo. 151 Khomithi lakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile

(1) Phalamente yakafanila biga khomithi inoozwibgwa seKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile inowothama n’hingo we—

(a)

tungamila bhakhwa kwePhalamente;

(b)

baka Ndawulo Dzimile;

(c)

lingisisa netambunudza zose zwakalingisana nePhalamente; ne

(d) nethama yimwe mihingo yakalingisana kene inoba yatilikwa khomithi iyeyo nenlayo nkulu kene nendawulo Dzimile kene umwe nlayo zwawo.

(2) Khomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile yakafanila hanganyila Tjilebeleki ne Ntungamili weSenethi nhengo dzeLubahhe gwebaMilili bemitunhu muPhalamente dzinotebela—

(a)

Ntobedzeli weTjilebeleki;

(b)

Ntobedzeli weNtungamili weSenethi;

(c)

Minisita wakalingisana nedzimari neamwe maMinisita mabili akabigwa neNtungamili weHango;

(d)

Ntungamili weMabhizinesi aHulumente muSenethi newemuPhalamente;

(e)

Ntungamili weGubungano linonyanyayidzana muSenethi nemuPhalamente;

(f)

banabugalo gwelinga nekwemazwibhato ebamilili mumakubungano ezwepolitiki anabamilili muSenethi nemuLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muphalamente;

(g) Ntungamili weKhansili yaboHe yeHango;

(h) banhu babili basimaMinisita kene Batobedzeli bemaMinisita, un’ompela wabo aliSenetha wakabigwa muKhomithi neNtungamili weSenethi ne umwe unoba aliMilili wentunhu muPhalamente wakabigwa neTjilebeleki;

(i)

nebamwe balihhanakadzi basimaMinisita kene Batobedzeli bemaMinisita, balibana bakahagwa neSenethi nebalibana bakahagwa neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente.

(3) Nhengo idzedzo dzakafanila bigwa kene hagwa muKhomithiyakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile nekulibilidza hule kwehangana kwekutanga kweyimwe neyimwe Phalamente, kakale bakafanila hagwa nezila inoota kuti khomithi ilakidzile ma kweSenethi neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kwakalingwa makubungano ezwepolitiki nebulume nebukadzi.

(4) Khomithiyakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile inobigwa ikahinga lubaka gwakalizana gwephalamente iyeyo.

(5) Khomithiyakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile inolawugwa neTjilebeleki kene, hapa asipo, inolawugwa neNtungamili weSenethi.

(6) Ndongoloso inootobegwa neKhomithiyakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile yakafanila tjenesewa muNdawulo Dzimile.

(7) Hapa kuba nebugalo gusigegwe muKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile kwakafanila kuti kune nenhu unohagwa kene unobigwa, kunda nekuti kunohakika ndikupi, mubugako nekulibilidza. 152 Khomithi yePhalamente yakalingisana neMilayo

(1) Nekulibilidza hule kwehangana kwekutanga kwePhalamente, Khomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile yakafanila biga khomithi inoodan’wa iyi Khomithi yeNlayo muPhalamente, ina baMilili beMitunhu muPhalamenti batatu kunda pehhugwi basiMaMinisita kene Batobedzeli beMaMinisita.

(2) Bunjinji gwebanhu bamuKhomithi yeNlayo muPhalamente bakafanila banefanilo dzehinga muZimbabwe belibamilili benlayo kuhhe belibahomanana banhu bakajalo bangabigwa mukhomithi.

(3) Khomithi yeNlayo muPhalamente yakafanila linga—

(a)

wose Nlayo utjasusumiwa, kuhhe kweNlayo Nkulu utjasusumiwa, usanhu wavotegwa kwebupelo muSenethi kene muLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente;

(b)

kene wupi Nlayo utjasusumiwa wakayigwahule kwelingwa neKhomithi, Nlayo utjasusumiwa usanhu ukavotegwa kwebupelo muSenethi kene muLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente;

(c)

yose ndebo yekwenlayo yadusiwa patjena nekwagwa muGazethi;

(d) wose nlayo utjasusumiwa watjindisiwa kuKhomithi neNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango kene Minisita, nayiko

(e)

kose kwakalingisana nenlayo kutjasusumiwa kwatjindisiwa kuKhomithi nengali tulo unamasimba ethama ikoku, kakane wakafanila kuPhalamente kene kunaNtebedzeli weNtungamili, Minisita kene nghalitulo, sema kwendebo, kene iyapo munlayo utjasusumiwa, kwakalingisana nenlayo kutjasusumiwa, hapa kwakatshambidzigwa, koba kwatjiba tjuluka Nlayo Nkulu.

(4) Hapa kwatjilingisiwa dzose ndebo dzekwenlayo Khomithi yePhalamente yakalingisana neMilayo yakafanila ndisa londesedzo kuPhalamente kene kuNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, Minisita a kene nghalitulo unowolinga kuti yakatambunuka imunlayo kene, hapa yatshambidzigwa, inoobe ilipehhugwi kwemasimba eTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(5) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kene Ndawulo Dzimile kungapa Khomithi yePhalamente yakalingisana neMilayo masimba ebhata imwe mihingo. 153 Bhadagwa kweNtungamili weSenethi, Tjilebeleki nebaMilili bemitunhu muPhalamente

(1) Mari inobhadagwa Tjilebeleki neNtungamili weSenethi—

(a)

yakafanila tshambidzigwa muTjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente kakale inobhuda muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente;

(b)

(c)

ayizofanila jitisiwa mulubaka betjigele mutjigalo; kakale kene Phalamente yatjipaladziwa bakafanila ndilambeli bebhadagwa bedza bejita muzwitulo tjigalo.

(2) Mbhayilo unopiwa baMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente wakafanila tshambidzigwa muTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente. 154 Nkwalili wePhalamente nebamwe bahingi

(1) Khomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile, kunakubvumigwa neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente neSenethi, yakafanila biga n’ghali tulo unoba Nkwalili wePhalamente. Nkwalili wePhalamente unobehinga; etebela Ndawulo Dzimile kakale etungamigwa neTjilebeleki, hhuba nehhuba ehinga muPhalamente.

(2) Nkwalili wePhalamente uno bigwa mutulo kwelubaka gungapa makole ali kutanhatu, kakale angabviliziwa mutulo kakale kwe amwe makole alikutanhatu.

(3) Nkwalili wePhalamente wakafanila jita mutulo—

(a)

hapa kubva muKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile, banhu banopinda gwamu muphalamente bebvumilana kuti Nkwalili adusiwe;

(b) hapa Nkwalili akafanila jita mutulo agala eliMilili wentunhu muPhalamente; kene

(c)

kene kuli neyimwe zila, hapa akatongoba Nkwalili kwemakole ali gumi nemabili (12).

(4) Khomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile yakafanila biga bamwe bahingi muPhalamente hapa libona kuhakika.

(5) Nkwalili wePhalamente nebamwe bahingi bePhalamente—

(a)

banobigiwa kutebegwa mihingo yadusiwa neKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile; kakale

(b)

banohingila tjaba koga besipasi kwelutabi gweBanohingila Hulumente.

DANHO 7 HALULO LUBABI 1 ZILA NENDONGOLOSO DZEHALULO 155 Zila dzeyetiwa kwezwinhu muhalulo

(1) Halulo, yakafanila bhakwa hule kwelubaka kusikulefulefu, nema referendamu, kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu wehango kwakafanila kuti—

(a)

ibe nelunyalalo, kusununguka nekuhalabvuka;

(b)

kuhingisiwa mabhokisi evotela;

(c)

vota kwebanhu bose bawhola nelizana kwemavoti;

(d)

nehalabvuka kusina kuhunguludzika kene dzimwe zila dzesasununguka mukuhalula.

(2) Hango gwakafanila tola nhambo dzakatambunuka, dzinohanganyila nhambo dzenlayo, kuti gube nelebeswa lekuti zila dzalebgwa mundimana (1) dzotebegwakakale kwazo, dzakafanila—

(a)

banelebeswa kuti banhu beZimbabwe, kubviwa buzwagwa gwakalondesedzewa Ndongoloso yeBuna; bakakwagwa mulukwalo gwebanovota;

(b) banelebeswa kuti wose nhu unabuzwagwa gweZimbabwe unandulamilo yevota muhalulo kene referendamu bana nkoba wevota, kakale yakafanila bhatsha kuti banhu bakakubadzika batubule vota;

(c)

be nelebeswa lekuti mhabi dzose dzezwetongewa kwehango nebanokakabadzana nehalulo kene banobhatika mumareferendamu banowana kose kunohakika kuti babhatike zwibuyanana.

(d) pa wose makubungano ezwepolitiki nebanobhatika munayikoko kene mamumareferendamu jidzo lezwemakhompiyutha, kakale

(e)

tjibe nelebeswa lekuti kobankanyiwa sawanana munazwepolitiki lubaka nelubaka.

156 Yetiwa kwehalulo nemareferendamu Muhalulo dzose nemareferendamu, Khomishini yezweHalulo yeZimbabwe yakafanila be nelebeswa lekuti—

(a)

zila yevota inohingisiwa, ilelu, inalebeswa, inokhona lingisisiwa, yakadzibililika kakale ipatjena;

(b)

mbhudilo yehalulo nemareferendamu yakafanila lebgwa nekulibilidza hule kwepela kwevota;

(c)

yeta ndongoloso dzakafanila—

(i) kuti kusibe negwa neamwe mazwibhato asaka lulwama mulubaka gwehalulo; kakale

(ii) be nelebeswa lekuti zwinhu zwinohingisiwa pahalulo zwakadzibililigwa.

157 Nlayo weHalulo

(1) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila bhatsha muyetiwa kwehalulo nemareferendamu anotungamigwa neNlayo nkulu iwhoyu, kwazo kwazo muna ikoku kunotebela—

(a)

ganhugwa kwemitunhu nemawadi nelubaka kutobegwa ndima 161;

(b)

kwalisiwa kwebanovota, nekunohakika kuti bakwalise vota mudzimwe ntha dzevotela;

(c)

mazwibhato emhabi dzezwepolitiki, bakamila banhu pahalulo nebamwe banobhatika muhalulo kene mareferendamu;

(d) Ndongoloso dzemigwa kwebanhu kwakalizana pahulugwa kwebanhu banootola bugalo muSenethi dzinolebgwa mundima 120(1)(a) nebugalo gwakabigigwa betjikadzi muLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente gunolebgwa mundima 124(1)(b), nendongoloso dzesiwa kwebanhu mubugalo igogo,bugalo gwakafanila togwa nebanhu—

(i) bamumhabi dzezwepolitiki dzinofanana nedzababa babe beli mubugalo igogo kakale;

(ii) bakafanila kuti babe betjilume hapa kulikuti babe belimubugalo babe belibetjilume kene babe betjikadzi hapa kulikuti babe belimubugalo bababe belibetjikadzi;

(e)

halugwa kwebanhu banomilila banhu kunababo bakakubadzika selebgwa mundima 120(1)(d);

(f)

bhakhwa kwehalulo dzemakhansili ematunhu neematoropo nemakhansili emintunhu;

(g) n’on’ona mbhudilo yehalulo.

(2) Ndongoloso dzemigwa kwebanhu kwakalizana kunolebgwa mundimana (1)

(d) dzakafanila be nelebeswa lekuti betjikadzi banomigwa kwakalizana munaSenetha anolebgwa mundima 120(1)(a).

(3) Nlayo weHalulo wakafanila yeta kuthi kube nethangiwa kwebamilili muhalulo hule kwemahhuba aligumi nekuna kwatjilebgwa kuti kunahalulo. Vota kwakafanila tiwa kwapinda mahhuba alimakumi matatu kwathangiwa bamilili.

(4) Akuzofanila be nekayigwa kunotiwa muNlayo weHalulo, kene kumilayo mitukununu yatiwa kusi kwenlayo iwhoyo, kuhhe kwekuti kwabhuzwiwa Khomishi yezweHalulo yeZimbabwe kakale mikumbulo yahhisiwa neKhomishini yalingisisiwa.

(5) Hapa kwatjilebgwa kuti koobe nehalulo, handugwa kweNlayo weHalulo kene umwe nlayo unondilana nezwehalulo akuneni nehalulo iyeyo.

LUBABI 2 LUBAKA GWEHALULO 158 Lubaka gwehalulo

(1) Halulo yebanhu bose yakafanila tiwa kudza kuti vota kusipinde—

(a)

mahhuba alimakumi matatu kusanhu kwaswika lubaka gwemakole mahanu setjenesiwa mundima 143;

(b) hapo Phalamente kwayadusa zwibvumilano zwekuti ipaladziwe setjenesiwa mundima 143(2), mahhuba elimakumi alihhanalume hule kwedusiwa kwetjibvumilano tjinonga itjetjo;

(c)

hapo Phalamente payinopaladziwa setjenesiwa mundima 109(4) kene (5) hule kwetobokiwa, mahhuba alimakumi alihhanalume kwapaladziwa.

(2) Halulo dzebanhu bose kumakhansili emitunhu dzakafanila tiwa lubaka kutiwa dzehalula Ntungamili nedzePhalamente.

(3) Halulo yepamululo muPhalamente nekuMitunhu kwakafanila itiwe kusenhu kukapinda mahhuba makumi ahanalume kumilikila lubaka gohayika nhu wakamilila mitunhu pahhe kokuti nhu wakamilila mitunhu atjipo kwatjisala mimwedzi ihanalume kuti kube nehalulo yeHango, hapo kwakajalo kolindiwa halulo yeHango. 159 Gadziwa kwebanhu muzwitulo nezila yehalulo Hapa kuhhakika nhu unobigwa ndohalugwa mubugalogwehingila tjaba gwakabumbiwa kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu, kusibugalo gunobviwa mundima 158, bakapiwa masimba othama halulo kwakafanila bathame halulo yewana nhu unogala bugalo igogu kusenhu kukapela mahhuba alimakumi alihanalume. LUBABI 3 GANHUGWA KWEMITUNHU YEHALULO 160 Bungana gwemitunhu yehalulo nemawhadi

(1) Kudze kuti kuhalugwe baMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente, Khomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe yakafanila palula Zimbabwe ibe nemitunhu izwiwulu zwibili zwinagumi.

(2) Kudze kuti kuhalugwe banohingila Hulumente wemitunhu, Khomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe kwakafanila ipalule mitunhu ibe mawhadi kundilane nebanhu banohalugwa kuti bahingile Hulumente kumitunhu. 161 Ganhugwa kwemitunhu yehalulo

(1) Kan’ompela mumakole elimakumi, muhhuba kene mulubaka gwakamisiwa neKhomishini gwakamela tebela baliwa kwebanhu, Khomishini yeHalula yeZimbabwe kwakafanila kuti iganhule mitunhu yehalulo muZimbabwe.

(2) Ganhugwa kwemitunhu yohalulo ikapela kusenhu kukaswika mimwedzi itanhatu hhuba lohalula lisenhu likaswika, ganhulo yekutanga ayitohinga muhalulo, koga ganhulo dziyapo kutjambo ganhugwa dzohinga.

(3) Miganhu yemitunhu yakafanila kuti, hapa kutikala, nelubaka gweganhula banhu bakwalisa vota banolizana nosiyana kwemitunhu muZimbabwe.

(4) Miganhu yemaWhadi kwakafanila kuti, makutikala, nelubaka gweganhula banhu bakwalisa vota banolizana nosiyana kwemawhadi mumitunhu iyapo.

(5) Hapa kuganhugwa—

(a)

miganhu yemaWhadi, Khomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe yakafanila bona kuti akuna ntha dzakapalalana dzinothama whadi in’ompela;

(b)

miganhu yemitunhu, Khomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe yakafanila bona kuti akuna mitunhu yakapalalana inothama whadi in’ompela;

(6) Muganhula Zimbabwe iba mawhadi nemitunhu, Khomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe yakafanila, ilemekedza ntha yose, yakalingisisa—

(a)

zwinobonakala zwiyapo;

(b)

zila yoyendesa ndebo muntha;;

(c)

gala kwebakakwalisa vota;

(d)

whisisana pakati kwebakakwalisa vota;

(e)

kukatikala ganhula mitunhu kwatongotikala ganhugwa, miganhulo yehalulo iyapo; kakale

(f)

kuti kunabanhu bangana;

kakale kumilidza kwawangwa, Khomishini ingaletja kwakalingiligwa kuti Mitunhu nemaWhadi kwakafanila kube nebanhu banolizana, koga akuna Mitunhu kene maWhadi akafanila iba nebanhu bakakwalisa banopinda imwe nemaphecenti amakumi mabili.

(7) Kukapela ganhugwa mawhadi nemitunhu, Khomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe kwakafanila ipe Ntungamili weHango londesedzo ina—

(a)

mazina emawhadi nemitunhu, betjenhesa miganhu;

(b)

tjilakidzo kene zwilakidzo zwemawhadi nemitunhu; kakale

(c)

gose luzibo kene kose Khomishini yayingabona kungahhingisiwa; kakale Ntungamili wakafanila yendesa londesedzo yokutanga kuPhalamente kusenhu kukapinda mahhuba anotondeka.

(8) Mumahhuba aligumi nemana hule kweyendisiwa kwe nlondesedzo yemiganhu kuPhalamente—

(a)

Ntungamili weHango angati nlondesedzo ibwiliziwe kuKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe kuti ilingisise kene yipi ndebo iyapo;

(b) Senethi kene Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kunga bvumilana kutinlondesedzo ibwiliziwe kuKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe kuti ilingisise kene yipi ndebo iyapo, kakale kutikala ikoku Ntungamili weHango wakafanila bwiliza nlondesedzo kuKhomishini kuti inolingisisiwa.

(9) Hapa nlondesedzo yemiganhu yaabwiliziwa kuKhomishini kutebegwa ndimana (8), Khomishini yeHalula yeZimbabwe yakafanila lingisisa ndebo iyeyo iyapo, koga nkumbulo nenhambo dzinotogwa neKhomishini ndiko kwebupelo.

(10) Nekulibilidza hule kwehanganya ndimana (7) na(9), Khomishini yeHalula yeZimbabwe yakafanila yendisa nlondesedzo yebupelo yemiganhu kuNtungamili weHango.

(11) Mumahhuba aligumi nemana hule kwewamutjila nlondesedzo yebupelo inobva kuKhomishini yeHalula yeZimbabwe, Ntungamili weHango wakafanila dusa patjena tjitshambidzilo nezila yekwala muGazethi mazima nemiganhu kwemawadi nekwemitunhusekwakwa bigwa nekhomishini.

(12) Hapa kuna pakunoleyana pakati kwetjenesewa kwemiganhu ewadi kene yipi kene wupi ntunhu nemepu kene dzimepu dzakatiwa neKhomishini yeHalula yeZimbabwe, tjenesewa kwemiganhu ndiko kunohinga. DANHO 8 ZWEMILAYO NEDZIKHUTA LUBABI 1 TJIMO TJEDZIKHUTA 162 Masimba edziKhuta Masimba edzikhuta anobva mubanhu beZimbabwe kakale akapiwa khuta dzinohanganyila—

(a)

Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu;

(b)

Khuta iPehhugwi;

(c)

Khuta Whulu;

(d)

Khuta yeBahingi;

(e)

Khuta yebhakhwa kwebanhu;

(f)

Khuta dzemitunhu;

(g)

Khuta dzenlayo wemabvilo;

2013

(h) nedzimwe khuta dzakabumbiwa neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kene dzakabumbiwa kutebegwa Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente. 163 Dzikhuta

(1) Khuta dzeZimbabwe dzina—

(a)

Ntongi Upehugwi, Ntebedzeli weNtongi Upehugwi ne amwe majaji eKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu;

(b)

majaji eKhuta iPehhugwi;

(c)

Ntungamili weMajaji muKhuta Whulu nebamwe majaji eKhuta iyeyo;

(d)

Ntungamili weMajaji muKhuta yebahingi ne amwe majaji eKhuta iyeyo;

(e)

Ntungamili weMajaji muKhuta yebhakhwa kwebanhu nebamwe majaji eKhuta iyeyo;

(f)

banhu banotungamila khuta dzemitunhu, khuta dzenlayo wemabvilo nedzimwe khuta dzakabumbiwa neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kene dzakabumbiwa kutebegwa Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(2) Ntongi Upehugwi ndiye ntungamili dzikhuta kakale ndiye wakalinga Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu neKhuta iPehhugwi.

(3) Ntungamili weMajaji muKhuta Whulu ndiye wakalinga khuta iyeyo.

(4) Ntungamili weMajaji muKhuta yebahingi ndiye wakalinga khuta iyeyo.

(5) Ntungamili weMajaji muKhuta yebhakhwa kwebanhu ndiye wakalinga khuta iyeyo. 164 Zwimila kwedzikhuta

(1) Khuta dzakazwimila kakale dzipasi kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu nenlayo, wabakafanila hingisa nekulizana , nekulibilidza kakale kusina kuhla, nezila isina lubihho kene kutshambidzila.

(2) Zwimila, kulizana nelemekedzeka kwedzikhuta ndiyo nhongoma yehinga kwenlayo nehulumente unozwitonga, kakale ndizo—

(a)

hakuna Hango kene lipi kene nalipi gubungano kene lutabi gwahulumente mumbihho dzose, kakale akuna nhu, unganginilila mukuhinga kwedzikhuta;

(b) hango, kuhingisiwa nhambo dzenlayo nedzimwebo nhambo, kwakafanila tjidza nedzibila dzikhuta kuti dzitubule zwimila, dzisitole lubihho dzibe pakati napakati, dzilemekedzeke, dzibepatjena dziwanike netjaba, nebona kuti khuta dzitebele milenje nezwilemela zwenlayo selondesedzewa kwazo mundimana 165.

(3) Nkumbulo weKhuta unamasimba pehhugwi kweHango nebanhu bose nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzose dzahulumente dzinobe dzilipasi kwawhoyo nlayo, kakale wakafanila tobegwa.

(4) Akuna tjimundima iyeyi tjingalingwa setjinodzibilila hinga kweTjisungo tjeNlayo muPhalamente kuhhe kwepa n’hingo nhu kuti ahinge zwakalingisana

nenlayo, koga bhakhwa kwayiwhoyo n’hingo akutotshambidzila zwimila kwen’hingi wekhuta wakalingisana nehinga kwenhabi dzenlayo kakale kusingalingile pasi zwimila kwenlayo. 165 Ndongoloso dzinotungamila hinga kwedzikhuta

(1) Pakuhingisiwa kwe Masimba edzikhuta, majaji akafanila tungamigwa nezwilemela zwinotebela—

(a)

banhu bose bakafanila tongewa nezila yakatambunuka, kusineni nemamo kene tjimo tjabo;

(b)

tongewa akuzofanila nonoka, kakale majaji akafanila bhata n’hingo nezila yakatambunuka inabubuya;

(c)

hingo wedzikhuta welondolodza ndulamilo nesununguko dzebanhu nesimba lenlayo unkulunkulu kwazo.

(2) Bahingi bedzikhuta, aliyega kene balibanjinji, bakafanila zwisapasi nelemekedza mihingo yabanohinga ngobe zwigagwamoyo netjaba kakale bakafanila gwisa kudza zwimila kwabo kudze batjengetedze bugalimoyo gwetjaba mudzikhuta.

(3) Hapa kutiwa tjisungo, n’hingi wekhuta wakafanila kuti abe akasununguka kakale kusina tjinowutshambidzila kene tjinowulawula.

(4) Bahingi bedzikhuta habazofanila—

(a)

bhatika muzwinu zwe tongewa kwehango;

(b) bamubugalo kene banhengo yegubungano lezwepolitiki;

(c)

hakila mali gubungano kene dusa mali kugubungano lezwepolitiki; kene

(d) yenda mihangano yadan’wa nemakubungano ezwetongewa kwehango.

(5) Bahingi bedzikhuta habazofanila haka kene wamutjila tjipo, mbhayilo, mari yehadzimiwa kene mbihho kunothama kuti basitonge nezila yakatambunuka kene layidza bupitipiti.

(6) Majaji edzikhuta akafanila hinga n’hingo wawo nebuswupi gupehhugwi kupinda imwe mihingo, kakale aazofanila bhatika muzwimwe zwinhu zwinotsiyetsa kene nginilila hingo wenlayo.

(7) Majaji edzikhuta akafanila tola nhambo dzedzibilila nekudza busupi neluzibo, bumihha, bunhu, kwazo kwazo azwibhate abenenkumbulo wemila nlayo wehango kene wehango dzose, 166 Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu

(1) Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu ikhuta ipehhugwi yetebela kakale—

(a)

Ntongi Nkulu weNlayo neNtebedzeli weNtongi Nkulu;

(b) nebamwe majaji elimahanu (5) be Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu;

(2) Hapa jidzo lejaji wakabhatila umwe lihakika muKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu kwelubakanyana, Ntongi Nkulu weNlayo angabiga jaji kene jaji wakaatjisiya n’hingo kuti abhate n’hingo muKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu kwelubakanyana.

(3) Milandu imuKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu—

(a)

yakalingisana tshambidzigwa kwendulamilo dzebunhu dzakazinda kene nsununguko dzakatjenesewa muDanho 4, kene kwakalingisana nehalula Ntungamili weHango kene Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, kwakafanila whiwa nemajaji ose ekhuta;

(b) kuhhe kwemilandu yalebgwa mutjikamu (a), yakafanila whiwe nemajaji eKhuta basipasi kwebatatu, koga Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente kene milayo yekhuta ingahinga mudzindebo nditjimunhambo dzetongewa kuti dziwhiwe nejaji kene majaji eKhuta.

(4) Majaji kene bakatongoba majaji akabigiwa kuti abhate n’hingo kutebegwa ndimana (2) angandilambeli ehinga semajaji eKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu hule kwepela kwebugalo gwabo, kutigwa kuti apedzise milandu yaakabehinga lubaka etjakabhata. 167 Ndawulo yeKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu

(1) Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu—

(a)

ndiyo khuta ipehhugwi munazose zwenlayo, kakale akutjina khuta ingan’o’n’ona tonga kwayo;

(b) ndiyo yakalingisana nendebo dzenlayo nkulu wehango nedzimwe ndebo dzinondila nekwenlayo nkulu wehango, kwazo kwazo kunotobegwa nekunohingisiwa kusi kwendima 131(8)(b) netjikamu 9(2) tjeNdongoloso yebuHanu;

(c)

ndiyo inoleba kuti ndebo ndeyezwenlayo nkulu wehango kene kuti ndebo inondilana nenkumbulo unohhisiwa mundebo dzenlayo nkulu wehango.

(2) Hapa kwakalingwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, Khutha yeNlayo Nkulu koga ndiyo inga—

(a)

ingapa mano nekwenlayo watugwa kuti unondilana nenlayo nkulu wehango kene, koga ingajalo koga hapa nlayo watugwa watongolingwa kutobegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu;

(b) ingawhilila sawhanana kwabepo pahalulo yeNtungamili weHango ikobhuda netjisungo tjoyenda mbeli;

(c)

ingawhilila sawhanana kwekuti nhu wakafanila kene azofanila bigwa seNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango; kene

(d) ingaleba kuti Phalamente kene Ntungamili weHango bakon’wa yeta n’hingo wabakafanila thama seleba kwenlayo nkulu wehango.

(3) Khutha yeNlayo nkulu ndiyo inotola tjisungo tjekuti Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kene mazwibhato eNtungamili weHango kene ePhalamente kondidzana neNlayo Nkulu, kakale yakafanila lingisisa milayo ibone kuti akuna imwe khutha yabvumila kumwe kusingandilane neNlayo nkulu wehango usanhu wahingisiwa.

(4) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingapa simba kuKhutha yeNlayo Nkulu kuti ite ndawulo dzekhutha.

(5) Ndawulo dzeKhutha yeNlayo nkulu wehango dzakafanila bvumila, hapa kuli zwendulamo nekene kusabvumigwa kene kwabvumigwa neKhutha yeNlayo Nkulu—

(a)

kuti kuhhisiwa ndebo dzinondilana nekweNlayo Nkulu kuKhutha yeNlayo Nkulu;

(b) zwikumbilila kunayiyo Khutha yeNlayo Nkulu kusi imwe khutha;

(c)

kuti abe nkwinya wekhutha.

168 Khuta iPehhugwi

(1) Khutha Ipehhugwi ndiyo ipehhugwi kwadzo dzose khuta dzedzindawulo kakale ina—

(a)

Ntongi Nkulu weNlayo neNtebedzeli weNtongi Nkulu weNlayo;

(b) nebamwe majaji eKhutha Ipehhugwi banopinda babili, kakale

(c)

ne amwe bo majaji abigwa seleba kwendimana (2).

(2) Hapa n’hingo we umwe jaji uhakika muKhutha Ipehhugwi kwelubaka guhomanana, Ntongi Nkulu angabiga jaji weKhutha Whulu, kene nhu wakatongobentongi weKhutha Ipehhugwi kuti ahhe ohinga kwelubaka igogo.

(3) Majaji kene banhu bakatongobe majaji abigwa kuti abhate n’hingo seleba kwendimana (2) angandila mbeli elimajaji eKhutha Ipehhugwi nekene lubaka gwawo gwebamun’hingo gwapela, etila kuti abhate ndongoloso dzakahhisiwa mbeli kwabo etjakabhata n’hingo. 169 Ndawulo yeKhuta iPehhugwi

(1) Khutha Ipehhugwi ndiyo khutha yebupelo yeyendesaa zwikumbilo yeZimbabwe, kuhhe kwendebo dzinotongwa neKhutha yeNlayo Nkulu wehango.

(2) Seleba kwendimana (1), Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingapamidzila gumwe butongi kakale tjipe simba kuKhutha Ipehhugwi.

(3) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingabvumila tonga kweKhutha Ipehhugwi kakale nayikoko ingapa Khutha Ipehhugwi simba leyeta milayo yekhutha.

(4) Milayo yekhutha ingapa nkwalili weKhutha Ipehhugwi gumwe butongi gweKhutha nemasimba mumilandu yetjaba—

(a)

kuti atonge mumilandu isalambiwa, kusimilandu inobhatika palebgwa nebhakhwa zwibuyanana kwebana;

(b) kuti alinge dzinotanga hhisiwa kene ndebo dzitjoolingisisiwa, kuhanganyisila zwikumbilo zwejidzo,koga asilinge ndebo dzinobhatika pabululwami gwenhu; koga milayo iyeyo yakafanila bvumila kuti nhu unobhatika pamilayo nezwisungo zwenkwalili kuti awane ndulamilo yekuti jaji weKhutha Ipehhugwi alingisise kakale tjisungo itjetjo, jaji iwhoyu angatjibvuma tjisungo itjetji, angatjibakanya kene atjiduse kene aduse tjimwe tjisungo tjaanobona tjakafanila. 170 Khutha Whulu Khutha Whulu ndiyo khutha ipehhugwi inozibgwa kakale ina—

(a)

Ntongi Nkulu weNlayo, Ntebedzeli weNtongi NKulu weNlayo neNtungamili weMajaji muKhutha Whulu;

(b) ne amwe majaji eKhutha Whulu bangabe babigiwa nelubaka. 171 Ndawulo yeKhuta Whulu

(1) Khuta Whulu—

(a)

inamasimba endawulo mumilandu yetjinyila tjaba nebudzudzu yose muZimbabwe;

(b) inandawulo yolingila mihingo yedzikhuta dzemitunhu nedzimwe khuta dzipasi kakale itambunhudze kwatiwa;

(c)

ingasekisa milandu yemilayo pahhe kweyinolingiwa neKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu; kakale

(d) inamasimba endawulo sekupasi kweTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(2) Tjisungo TjeNlayo tjingapa masimba ondawula muKhuta Whulu kakale nezila iyeyo masimba ihawo engayeta milayo yeKhuta.

(3) Tjisungo TjeNlayo tjingakoba Khuta Whulu ibe nembihho yohinga kwakasiyana siyana, koga dzose mbihho dzakafanila hinga pasi kwendawulo yeKhuta Whulu mumilandu yose inoleziwa muKhuta Whulu.

(4) Milayo yeKhuta inokwagwa mulukwalo gweKhuta Whulu inamasimba mumilandu yetjaba—

(a)

thama ndawulo mumilandu isawhisisiwa, kuhhe kwendawulo dzinondilana ndekula kene legwa kene lingwa kwebana;

(b) lingisisa milandu yokutanga kene inodzidza, kuhanganisila zwikumbilo zowana luzibo kuti kutiweni, koga hayi milandu inodzibilila sununguka kwenhu; koga milayo kwakafanila ipe nhu wabhakhwa nemasimba elukwalo gwenlayo awane ndulamilo yelingisisiwa nejajii weKhuta Whulu, ungabvuma, ayitambunudze kene ayibigile ntome kene ase yimwe ndawulo kene alebe kwakalulamila ipapo. 172 Khuta yeBahingi

(1) Khuta yeBahingi ikhuta inolinga ndebo dzemihingo kakale ina—

(a)

Jaji nkulu; kakale

(b) bamwe majaji eKhuta yeNlayo sebengabigwa noyenda kolubaka. [Ndimana iyeyi yakahandugwa neTjikamu 3 tjeTjisungo 10 tja2017]

(2) Khuta yebahingi inamasimba endawulo mumilandu yebahingi nemihingo sekunobva muTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(3) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingapa masimba endawulo yeKhuta yebahingi kakale netjibaka itjetjo ipe masimba othama milayo muKhuta. 173 Khuta yeTungamigwa kweMakubungano akasiyana siyana neGalisana kwebanhu

(1) Khuta yeTungamigwa kweMakubungano akasiyana siyana neGalisana kwebanhu, ikhuta inolinga nekwetungamigwa kwemakubungano akasiyana siyana negalisana kwebanhu kakale ina—

(a) Jaji nkulu ne; kakale

(b) bamwe majaji eKhuta yeTungamigwa kweMakubungano akasiyana siyana neGalisana kwebanhu bengabigwa neyenda kwelubaka. [Ndimana iyeyi yakahandugwa neTjikamu 4 tjeTjisungo 10 tja2017]

(2) Khuta yeTungamigwa kweMakubungano akasiyana siyana neGalisana kwebanhu inamasimba endawulo mumilandu yebhakhwa kwebanhu sepiwa kwayo neTjisungo Tjenlayo tjePhalamente.

(3) Tjisungo Tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjingapa Khuta yeTungamigwa kweMakubungano akasiyana siyana neGalisana kwebanhu masimba ondawula kakale nemasimba othama milayo muKhuta. 174 Dzimwe khuta nembahhe mu—

(1) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjohingisiwa mubumbiwa, ma nendawula

(a) khuta dzemitunhu, dzosekesa milandu yetjinyila tjaba nebudzudzu;

(b) dzikhuta dzenlayo wemabvilo dzinandawulo yohingisiwa kwenlayo wemabvilo;

(c)

dzimwe khuta dzipasi kweKhuta Whulu; kakale

(d) mbahhe dzozuwhisani, lebesana nedzimwe zila dzotambunhusa sawhililana.

(2) Kutobegwa ndima 188(4) yeNlayo Nkulu, mari inobhadagwa, nekumwe kungapiwa majaji eKhutha yeBahingi neKhuta yeTungamigwa kweMakubungano akasiyana siyana neGalisana kwebanhu akuzofanila jitisiwa betjahinga kene bakabhatila umwe kubvila muhhuba letangisa hinga kweTjisungo itjetji. [Ndimana iyeyi yakahandugwa neTjikamu 5 tjeTjisungo 10 tja2017]

175 Masimba edziKhuta muna zwenlayo nkulu

(1) Hapa khuta inosa ndawulo yosatambunuka kwenlayo sekweNlayo Nkulu kene tjinya kweNtungamili weHango kene Phalamente, ndawulo ayina masimba kuhhe kwekuti kubvile muKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu.

(2) Khuta inosa ndawulo yosatambunhuka kohinga kwenlayo sepasi kwendimana (1) ingapa dzibililo yelubakanyna kene kumwe kwelubakanyana mugubungano, kene misa ndongoloso, kwakalindiwa nkumbulo weKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu nekwetambunhudziwa kwenlayo kene kunohakiwa tambunhudziwa.

(3) Wose nhu unohaka whisisa engando hakisisa, kene kumbila, kuKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu kuti ilebe kene ise ndawulo nekwetambunuka kwentongo unobe watugwa kukhutha mundimana (1).

(4) Hapa ndebo inondilana nezwenlayo nkulu ingahha mundongoloso dzekhutha, nhu unotungamila khutha iyeyo angayipindisila kuKhutha yeNlayo Nkulu hapa kuhakika, kuhhe kwekuti eyibona isina mano kene tjinangwa tjayo tjili tjengodziidza.

(5) Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente kene milayo yekhutha kwakafanila linga ndawulo yahhisiwa kuKhutha yeNlayo Nkulu inondilana sesabe nelebeswa kwenlayo nkulu yatiwa kutobegwa ndimana (1) neyimwe khutha isiKhutha yeNlayo Nkulu.

(6) Pakulawula ndebo yezwenlayo nkulu mukulawula kwayo, khutha inga—

(a)

leba kuti nlayo kene mazwibhato asingandilane neNlayo Nkulu akutohinga sesandilana kwako neNlayo Nkulu;

(b) thama umwe ndawulo yakalulwama kakale yakalizana kuhanganyisila ndawulo inoganhula kunootikala hapa kwatjiyi nlayo awutohinga nenlayo unomisa tshambidzigwa kwesahinga kwenlayo kwelubaka kudza bakalingisana nayikoko bakutambunudze. 176 Masimba ebvilo eKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu, Khuta iPehhugwi neKhuta Whulu Khutha yeNlayo Nkulu, Khutha iPehhugwi neKhutha Whulu kunamasimba ebvilo edzibilila nelinga ndongoloso dzako nethama nlayo webanhu bose kene nlayo wemabvilo, kulingisisa ndulamo nekunolebgwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu.

LUBABI 2 BIGWA NELUBAKA GWEHINGA KWEBAHINGI BEDZIKHUTA

177 Fanilo dzeMajaji eKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu

(1) Nhu unofanila bigwa sejaji weKhutha yeNlayo Nkulu hapa elinhu weZimbabwe, enamakole asipasi kwe alimakumi mana kakale enaluzibo gwenlayo nkulu gunowhakala kakale, pehhugwi kwayikoko, enafanilo dzinotebela—

(a)

2013
