abe jaji wekhutha enandawulo isinamiganhu mundebo dzetjaba kene dzebudzudzu muhango inanlayo weMaRoma nemaBhunu kene weMakhuwa, kakale Tjikhuwa tjili igo lulimi gunohinga kumunlayo; kene

(b) abe nemakole asipasi kwe aligumi nekubili ehinga semilili wenlayo—

(i) muZimbabwe; kene

(ii) muhango inohingisa nlayo weMaRoma nemaBhunu kene weMakhuwa kakale Tjikhuwa tjili igo lulimi gunohinga kumunlayo; kakale enafanilo dzehinga ikoko nayigogo lubaka.

(2) Kuti abigwe sejaji weKhutha yeNlayo Nkulu nhu wakafanila kuti abe akafanila kuti agale mubugalo gwebajaji. 178 Fanilo dzeMajaji eKhuta iPehhugwi

(1) Nhu unofanila bigwa sejaji weKhutha iPehhugwi hapa elinhu weZimbabwe kakale enamakole asipasi kwe alimakumi mana kakale, pehhugwi kwayikoko—

(a)

alijaji kene akatongobejaji wekhutha enandawulo isinamiganhu mundebo dzetjaba kene dzebudzudzu muhango inanlayo weMaRoma nemaBhunu kene weMakhuwa, kakale Tjikhuwa tjili igo lulimi gunohinga kumunlayo; kene

(b) abe nemakole asipasi kwe aligumi elimilili wenlayo—

(i) muZimbabwe; kene

(ii) muhango inohingisa nlayo weMaRoma nemaBhunu kene weMakhuwa kakale Tjikhuwa tjili igo lulimi gunohinga kumunlayo; kakale enafanilo dzehinga ikoko nayigogo lubaka.

(2) Kuti abigwe sejaji weKhutha iPehhugwi nhu wakafanila kuti abe akafanila kuti agale mubugalo gwebajaji. 179 Fanilo dzeMajaji eKhuta Whulu, Khuta yeBahingi neKhuta yeTungamigwa kweMakubungano akasiyana siyana neGalisana kwebanhu

(1) Nhu unofanila bigwa sejaji weKhutha Whulu, Khutha Bhingi neKhuta yeTungamigwa kweMakubungano akasiyana siyana neGalisana kwebanhu hapa enamakole asipasi kwe alimakumi mana kakale, pehhugwi kwayikoko—

(a)

alijaji kene akatongobejaji wekhutha enandawulo isinamiganhu mundebo dzetjaba kene dzebudzudzu muhango inanlayo weMaRoma nemaBhunu kene weMakhuwa, kakale Tjikhuwa tjili igo lulimi gunohinga kumunlayo; kene

(b) kwemakole anopinda kene aligunotendeka, kundilambeli kene, akafanila hinga alimilili wenlayo—

(i) muZimbabwe;

(ii) muhango inohingisa nlayo wemaRoma nemaBhunu kakale inohingisa tjiKhuwa selulimi gumunlayo; kene

(iii) hapa enabuzwagwa gweZimbabwe, muhango inohingisa nlayo wetjiKhuwa netjiKhuwa selulimi gumunlayo.

(2) Nhu unobigwa sejaji mutulo muKhuta Whulu, Khuta yeBahingi kene Khuta yeTungamigwa kweMakubungano akasiyana siyana neGalisana kwebanhu wakafanila lizanila nelulwamila gala mutulo alijaji.

180 Bigwa kwemajaji

(1) Ntongi Nkulu, Ntobedzeli weNtongi Nkulu, Ntungamili weMajaji eKhuta Whulu ne amwe ose majaji banobigwa neNtungamili weHango seleba kwendima iyeyi.

(2) Ntongi Nkulu, Ntobedzeli weNtongi Nkulu, Ntungamili weMajaji eKhuta Whulu ne amwe ose majaji banobigwa neNtungamili weHango hule kwezuwisana neKhomishini yeDzikhutha.

(3) Pa bigwa kweNtongi Nkulu, Ntobedzeli weNtongi Nkulu, Ntungamili weMajaji eKhuta Whulu kusingandilane nekwalebgwa neKhomishini yeDzikhutha kutobegwa ndimana (2), Ntungamili weHango unotubula ta kuti Senethi izibisiwe nekulibilidza: Kutila kuti kube palenje, kwatshambidzigwa kuti kunoba kwatiwa neNtungamili weHango ndiko kumunlayo akungatjahandugwe.

(4) Hapa kungatikala kuti kubigwe Ntongi Nkulu, Ntobedzeli weNtongi Nkulu, Ntungamili weMajaji eKhuta Whulu Khomishini YeDzikhutha yakafanila—

(a)

zibisa tjaba nekwetjigalo tjiyapo;

(b) koka Ntungamili weHango netjaba kuti bahalule banhu babanohaka;

(c)

bhuzwa banhu bakathangiwa mibhuzo yekuti bangine mun’hingo;

(d)

nekwala pasi mazina ebanhu batatu babhudilila bangagadziwa mutulo;

(e)

nendisa mazina ihawo kuNtungamili weHango; kubva kuti, kutobegwa ndimana (5), Ntungamili weHango wakafanila biga un’ompela wabo mutjigalo.

(5) Hapa Ntungamili weHango ebona kuti mumazina aapiwa kutobegwa ndimana (4)(e) akuna wakafanila bigwa mutjigalo, wakafanila haka amwe mazina matatu kakale kuKhomishini yeDzikhutha, ndizwo, Ntungamili weHango wakafanila biga un’ompela wabo mutjigalo.

(6) Ntungamili weHango wakafanila thama kuti kukwagwe muGazethi kuzibisiwa tjaba nekwebigwa mutjigalo kunotikala kutobegwa ndima iyeyi.

(7) Tjigalo tjajaji nkulu weKhuta yeBahingi netjigalo tjajaji nkulu weKhuta yeTungamigwa kweMakubungano akasiyana siyana neGalisana kwebanhu tjakafanila piwa umwe jaji kene wakabhata tjigalo tjajaji, kakale ehalugwa neNtongi Nkulu hule kwezwuwisana neKhomishini yezweDzikhutha.”. [Ndimana iyeyi yakahandugwa neTjikamu 6 tjeTjisungo 10 tja2017]

181 Bigwa kwebakabhatila majaji

(1) Hapa kusina ugele mutulo tjeNtongi Nkulu kene hapa ngali tulo esingatubule bhata iwhoyo n’hingo nezila yakatambunuka, Ntebedzeli weNtongi Nkulu unoba akabhata iwhoyo n’hingo, koga hapa zose zwitulo zwisigegwe bubili gwazo kene bose bagali bezwitulo bubili gwabo besingatubule bhata iwhoyo n’hingo nezila yakatambunuka, Ntongi Nkulu unowuntobela mubugalo, ntongi nkulu unotebela weKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu unoba akabhata seNtongi Nkulu.

(2) Hapa bugalo gwe—

(a)

Ntungamili weMajaji muKhuta Whulu;

(b) Ntungamili weMajaji muKhuta yeBahingi; kene

(c)

Ntungamili weMajaji muKhuta yeTungamigwa kweMakubungano akasiyana siyana neGalisana kwebanhu:

guisigegwe kene hapa nghalitulo esingatubule bhata iwhoyo n’hingo nezila yakatambunuka, jaji nkulu panayibo bose unotebela wekhuta yakalingisana nayigogo bugalo unoba akabhata seNtungamili weMajaji. [Ndimana iyeyi yakahanndugwa neTjikamu 7 tjeTjisungo 10 tja2017]

(3) Hapa kuhakika kuti kupamidziwe umwe jaji muKhuta Whulu, muKhuta yeBahingi kene muKhuta yeTungamigwa kweMakubungano akasiyana siyana neGalisana kwebanhu kwelubaka nyana Ntungamili weHango, etebela mano aapiwa neKhomishini inolinga nezwedziKhuta, angabiga umwe wakatongoba jaji kuti abe akabhata tulo kwelubaka gusingapinde mimwedzi iligumi nemibili, kungatikala kuti awumbgwiliziwe abe akabhata kakale kweyimwe mimwedzi iligumi nemibili.

(4) Banhu bamuzwitulo bakabigwa kuti babe bakabhata nezila yatjenesewa mundimana (3) bangandilambeli behinga semajaji hule kwekuti lubaka gwegala mutulo gwapela, kutigwa kuti batubule hinga milandu yakayakabhakhwa nayibo mulubaka betjigele muzwitulo bakabhata. 182 Bigwa kweMajaji eKhuta dzemitunhu nebamwe banohinga mudzikhuta Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila hhula mikoba yebigwa kwemajaji eKhuta dzemitunhu nebamwe banohinga mudzikhuta basimajaji, koga—

(a)

majaji eKhuta dzemitunhu bakafanila bigwa neKhomishini inolinga nezwedziKhuta;

(b) banohinga mudzikhuta basimajaji eKhuta dzemitunhu kene amwe majaji akafanila bigwa hapa Khomishini inolinga nezwedziKhuta yabvuma;

(c)

bigwa muzwitulo kwakajali kwakafanila tiwa nezila ipatjena kakale kusina kuhla, nkuha, kutshambidziligwa kene kuleleka.

183 Bahingi bedziKhuta abazofanila hingila khuta dzinopinda in’ompela Kuhhe kwekuti sekwakunobviwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, nhu hangatongobigwa mutulo kuti ahingile khuta dzinopinda in’ompela. 184 Bigwa kwebahingi bedziKhuta kulakidzile tjimo tjetjaba Bigwa kwebahingi bedzikhutha kwafanila lakidza kwazo siyana netjimo tjebunhukadzi nebunhulume muZimbabwe. 185 Tuna pagadziwa mubugalo

(1) Ntongi Nkulu kene Ntebedzeli weNtongi Nkulu asanhu atangisa n’hingo, wakafanila tuna mbeli kweNtungamili weHango kene umwe nhu wapiwa ilelo simba neNtungamili weHango selondesedzewa muNdongoloso yebuTatu.

(2) Hapa jaji, usiNtongi Nkulu kene Ntebedzeli weNtongi Nkulu, etangisa n’hingo, wakafanila tuna mbeli kweNtongi Nkulu kene jaji nkulu selondesedzewa muNdongoloso Yebutatu.

(3) Zwisungo zwenlayo zwePhalamente zwinolawula bigwa kwemamejasitirithi nebahingi bayidzedzo khutha, kusimajaji, zwakafanila leba tuna kwakafanila togwa nayibabo banobigwa. 186 Lubaka gweba mubugalo kwemajaji

(1) Majaji eKhutha yeNlayo Nkulu wehango banobigwa kwemakole aligumi nekuhanu ekapinda abatobgwiliziwa mun’hingo, koga—

(a)

bakafanila siya n’hingo bakabe nemakole alimakumi alikunotondeka; kakale

(b) hapa lubaka gwema n’hingo gwaswika, bangabigwa semajaji eKhutha Ipehhugwi kene Khutha Whulu, sehala kwabo, hapa bengatubula siwa munawoyo n’hingo.

(2) Majaji eKhutha Ipehhugwi, Khutha Whulu ne amwe majaji anogala mun’hingo kubva muhhuba laabigwa nalo kudza kunoswika makole alimakumi alikunotondeka, kwaakafanila siya n’hingo.

(3) Nhu engabigiwa sejaji weKhutha ipehhugwi, Khutha Whulu nedzimwe dzikhutha kwelubaka gwabigiwa, koga hapa nhu abigiwa kujalo,esakabhatila umwe, unoma bejaji hapa aswika makole alimakumi alikunotondeka nekene lubaka gwepela kwen’hingo gusanhu gwaswika.

(4) Nekene jaji asiya n’hingo kene aswika makole alikumakumi alikunotondeka kene, hapa kuliNtongi weKhutha yeNlayo Nkulu wehango kene jaji unolebgwa mundimana (3), aswika kunopelela lubaka gweba mun’hingo kukwe, angandila mbeli elijaji kudza alinge dzimwe ndongoloso dzakahhisiwa kunayiye etjijaji.

(5) Jaji engasiya n’hingo uwe kene lini nekwala lukwalo gwesiya n’hingo egundise kuNtungamili weHango gundisiwa Khomishini inohinga nezwetonga nenlayo.

(6) Whofisi yejaji ayizofanila paladziwa etjimutulo tjebutongi. 187 Dusiwa kwemajaji mubugalo

(1) Jaji engadusiwa mutulo koga—

(a)

hapa akon’wa yeta n’hingo uwe nentha yegwala mumbili kene mun’holo;

(b) hapa ekon’wa yeta n’hingo kunobhatisa honi; kene

(c)

hapa enamazwibhato akapeyama;

kakale jaji haangadusiwe mutulo kuhhe kwekuti ata zwinhu zwinolondesedzwa mundima iyeyi.

(2) Hapa Ntungamili weHango ebona ngati dusiwa kweNtongi Nkulu mutulo tjitje kwakafanila lingisisiwa, Ntungamili wakafanila biga bunga linothama ikoku.

(3) Hapa Khomishini inolinga nekwen’hingo wedzikhuta ingapa Ntungamili weHango mano kuti dusiwa kwejajii mubugalo, kuhanganyisila Ntongi Nkulu, kwakafanila lingisisiwa, Ntungamili weHango wakafanila biga bunga linothama ikoku.

(4) Lubahhe gwabigwa setjenesewa mundima iyeyi gwakafanila ba nebanhu batatu bahagwa neNtungamili weHango, bakafanila kuti—

(a)

un’ompela abe nhu—

(i) wakatongobe jaji weKhutha Ipehhugwi kene Khutha Whulu muZimbabwe; kene

(ii) umubugalo kene wakatongobe mubugalo sejaji wekhutha isina nghanhu dzendawulo munazwetjinyila tjaba kene budzudzu muhango unanlayo unondidzana newemaRoma nemaBhunu kene Makhuwa, kakale tjiKhuwa ndigo lulimi gunohingisiwa kumunlayo.

(b) un’ompela wakafanila hagwa munlongoloso yebamilili benlayo batatu kene banopinda ipapo banamakole alikunotondeka (7) balimun’hingo kene anopinda ipapo bathangiwa nebunga, linolawugwa neTjisungo tjeNlayo TjePhalamente, tjinomila bamilili benlayo muZimbabwe.

(5) Gubungano linolebgwa mundimana (4)(b) lakafanila bakanya mazina inolebgwa mundimana iyeyo hapa Ntungamili weHango ajalo.

(6) Ntungamili weHango wakafanila halula imwe nhengo yelubahhe yabigwa kutebegwa ndima iyeyi kuti abe n’ ghalitulo welubahhe.

(7) Lubahhe gwabigwa pasi kwendimana (2) kene (3) kwakafanila bhuzwa nekwezila yodusa jaji iwhoyo mubugalo kakale, gwathama gujalo, gwakafanila londesedza tjawan’wa kuNtungamili kakale balebe kuti jaji wakafanila dusiwa kene letjiwa elimubugalo.

(8) Ntungamili weHango wakafanila hingidzana nekwalebwa nelubahhe pasi kwendimana (7).

(9) Lubahhe kwagadziwa kutebegwa ndima iyeyi gunandulamilo nemasimba semaKhomishina kutebegwa MaKhomishini eTjisungo tjeNlayo Tjobhuzwisisa [Danho 10:07], kene umwe nlayo unonginila Tjisungo itjetjo.

(10) hapa mbhuzo wodusiwa kwejaji mubugalo wandisiwa kulubahhe kutebegwa ndima iyeyi, jaji unomisiwa hinga kwakalindiwa Ntungamili weHango, ehingisa mano abva kulubahhe, unobwiliza kene udusa jaji iwhoyo mubugalo.

(11) Tjisungo Tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjingapa Khomishini iNolinga nedziKhuta kene lubahhe masimba gwabigwa pasi kwendima iyeyi kuti bakumbile umwe newumwe jaji kuti alezi lukwalo gohogwa nebalapi kubva kulutabi gwebalapi kwatigwa ikoku, kudze kubongwe kuti wakatjila mumbili kene munkumbulu. 188 Tjimo tjehinga nelubaka gweba mubugalo kwebahingi bedziKhuta

(1) Majaji akafanila wana mibhayilo, milipo nezwimwe zwinowamutjidziwa, inolebgwa neyenda kwelubaka, neKhomishini inolinga nekwedziKhuta nobvumigwa neNtungamili watjilebeleka neMinisita wakalingisana nendulamo ebvumilana neMinisita wakalingisana nedzimari.

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila zibisa tjimo tjehing kubahingi bedziKhuta kuhhe kwemajaji nefanilo yemilidziwa, siwa kudzimwe ndawo netahwa, nedzimwe ndongoloso dzotambunhudza dzingatogwa mubali, dzotiwa—

(a)

nobvumigwa neKhomishini inolinga nedzezweKhuta; kakale

(b) nezila yakatambunhuka neyatjeneswa nesahla,sahalula kene kumbuligwa kwakayipa.

(3) Mibhayilo, milipo nezwimwe zwinowamutjidziwa, yebanohingila dziKhuta ifanilo inobva muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente

(4) Mibhayilo, milipo nem’holo webanohingila dziKhuta akuzofanila ijitisiwe betjahinga. LUBABI 3 KHOMISHINI INOLINGA NEKWEN’HINGO WEDZIKHUTA 189 Bumbiwa nema kweKhomishini inolinga nekwen’hingo wedziKhuta

(1) Kuna Khomishini yakalinga nekwehinga kwedziKhuta inohanganilisila—

(a)

Tongi Nkulu;

(b) Ntebedzeli weNtongi Nkulu;

(c)

Ntungamili weMajaji muKhuta Whulu;

(d) Jaji un’ompela unobva muKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu, Khuta iPehhugwi, Khuta Whulu, Khuta yebaHingi neKhuta yeBhakhwa kweBanhu;

(e)

Milili Nkulu weNlayo;

(f)

He wedziKhuta dzeMitunhu;

(g) Nghalitulo weKhomishini yebanohingila Hulumente;

(h) baMilili benlayo batatu banohinga benamakole anotondeka behinga mihingo yegubungano yakabumbiwa pasi kweTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente linomilila baMilili benlayo muZimbabwe;

(i)

Mpepetha kene N’diyi Nkulu wenlayo bahalugwa negubungano yakamilila badiyi banjinji benlayo mumaYunivesithi kuZimbabwe kene, ma kusina gubungano yakajali, babigiwa neNtungamili;

(j)

nhu watjibo nelubaka gunoswika makole anotondeka behinga muZimbabwe beli akhawuntenti kene n’holi wehingisiwa kwemari, kakale wahalugwa negubungano, yatiwa pasi kweTjisungo TjeNlayo muPhalamente tjinomila banhu bakajali; kakale

(k) nhu unamakole anoswika makole anotondeka behinga mulutabi gwebutongi guhingisana nebanhu, wabigiwa neNtungamili.

(2) Ntongi Nkulu kene, hapa esipo, Ntebedzeli weNtongi Nkulu ndiye unotungamila mihangano yeKhomishini inolinga nekwen’hingo weDzikhutha, kakale hapa besipo bose mun’hangano kene upi banhu bayapo bakafanila hala nhu un’ompela kuti atungamile n’hangano.

(3) Nhengo dzeKhomishini inolinga nekwen’hingo weDzikhutha dzinolebgwa mutjikamu (d), (h), (i), (j) netjikamu (k) zwendimana (1) dzobigwa kan’ompela kwemakole matanhatu. 190 Mihingo yeKhomishini inolinga nekwen’hingo wedziKhuta

(1) Khomishini inolinga nekwen’hingo wedziKhutha ingapa mano kunaHulumente mundebo dzinondilana nekwedzikhutha kene dzebhakhwa kwendulamo, kakale Hulumente wakafanila whilila mano ihawo.

(2) Khomishini inolinga nekwen’hingo wedziKhutha yakafanila susuma kakale itjidze muzwimila nehinga kwakatambunuka kwedzikhutha nebhakhwa kwendulamo kunogutsha, kwakakwana kakale kunabutjena muZimbabwe, kakale inamasimba ose anohakika kuti itubule thama n’hingo iwhoyu.

(3) Khomishini inolinga n’hingo wedzikhutha, nebvumigwa naMinisita wakalingisana nezwendulamo, ingathama ndongoloso kene milayo inolawulwa zwinolebgwa nendima iyeyi.

(4) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingapa Khomishini inolinga nekwen’hingo wedziKhutha mihingo inondilana nepa mihingo, langa nemamo ehinga kwebanhu banohinga muKhutha yeNlayo Nkulu, Khutha iPehhugwi, Khutha Whulu, Khutha yeBahingi, Khuta yeTungamigwa kweMakubungano akasiyana siyana neGalisana kwebanhu nedzimwe dzikhutha. 191 Zila yakatambunuka nebutjena gwendongoloso dzeKhomishini inolinga nekwen’hingo wedzikhuta Khomishini inolinga nekwen’hingo wedziKhutha yakafanila thama mihingo yayo nezila inandulamo, yakalizana kakale inabutjena.

LUBABI 4 WHOKU NAWHOKUYA 192 Nlayo unowhotshambidzigwa Nlayo unootshambidzigwa nedzikhutha dzeZimbabwe unlayo wake uhinga nehhuba letangisa hinga kwenlayo nkulu iwhoyu, sekayigwa kwawo kunohakika. 193 Ndawulo yemilandu yebudzudzu yedziKhuta Khutha dzinotebela ndidzo dzoga dzingalawula kene dzingapiwa ndawulo yemilandu yebudzudzu—

(a)

Khutha yeNlayo Nkulu, Khutha iPehhugwi, Khutha Whulu nekhutha dzemitunhu;

(b) khutha kene lubahhe gwenlayo gwakalingisana nemilandu ipasi kwenlayo welanga, hapa ndawulo yago ihakika kudza kulangiwe kunohakiwa kuti kulangiwe. DANHO 9 ZILA DZEBHAKHWA KWETJABA NEBUNTUNGAMILI

194 Walilo dzebunhu nezila dzebhakhwa kwetjaba

(1) Bhakhwa kwetjaba mumbihho dzose dzaHulumente, kuhanganyisila makubungano nenhabi dzeHango, nemabunga anolawugwa nahulumente neamwe mabhizinesi etjaba, kwakafanila tungamigwa nemidzi yebunhu nezila dzebhakhwa kwebanhu dzinazwitonga dzinotugwa muNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, kuhanganyisila zila dzinotebela—

(a)

hinga nezila inabunhu kwakafanila susumiwa kakale kugale kutiwa;

(b) hingisiwa kwezwiwanikwa nezila inogutsha kakale inohhisa mari kwakafanila susumiwa;

(c)

bhakhwa zwibuyanana kwetjaba kwakafanila linga pfumbila kwetjaba;

(d) mihingo yakafanila tiwa nezila isina kuhalawula, nekulizana, kunogutsha kakale kusingatogwe mbihho;

(e)

kunohakiwa nebanhu kwakafanila lingwa nelubaka gunowhakala, kakale tjaba tjakafanila kulumbidziwa kuti babhatike mutiwa kwendongoloso;

(f)

banohinga bhakhwa kwetjaba bakafanila zwitjenesa kuPhlamente nekubanhu;

(g) makubungano nenhabi dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose kwakafanila hingidzana;

(h) butjena gwakafanila milidziwa nepa tjaba luzibo nelubaka, gunoswikililika kakale gwakatambunuka;

(i)

bhakhwa zwibuyanana kwezwiwanikwa zwebanhu netiwa zwendongoloso dzinopfumbidza mihingo yebanhu kudza kukudziwe tubula kwebanhu, kwakafanila tiwa kwazo;

(j)

bhakhwa kwetjaba kwakafanila linga migwa kwezwitjaba zwakasiyana zweZimbabwe;

(k) n’hingo, pfumbidza nendila mbeli muzwidiyo kwakafanila tiwa kwakalingwa tambunuka, tubula, bunangwa, tambunuka, lizana kwebetjilume nebetjikadzi kubviwa kose nebanhu bakakubadzika; kakale Hulumente wakafanila tola nhambo, dzinohanganyila nhambo dzenlayo; kuti kukudziwe bunhu nemilenje iyeyi.

(2) Bigwa muzwitulo mumbihho dzose dzahulumente, kuhanganyigwa makubungano ahulumente nenhabi dzinolawugwa nahulumente nemakampani etjaba, kwakafanila tiwa nezila yakatambunuka. 195 Makampani anolawugwa naHulumente

(1) Makampani ne amwe mabhizinesi aHulumente kene anolawugwa naHulumente akafanila, pehhugwi kwekuti atebele milayo yalebwa mundima 194(1), hinga kudze andile atubule ndila mbeli ehinga kakale atebele milayo yakatambunuka yetungamigwa kwemabhizinesi.

(2) Makampani nemabhizinesianobviwa mundimana (1) akafanila banezila dzetenga ndinabubuya, dzipatjena kakale dzatatambunuka. 196 Milandu yebanohingila tjaba nezila dzetungamila

(1) Masimba akapiwa nhu umubugalo gwehingila tjabaabugalimoyo gwetjaba gwakafanila hingisiwa nezila—

(a) inondidzana nehinga nezwinangwa zweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu;

(b) inolayidza mazwibhato ebanhu nelulwamila tjidza banhu pana kubatungamila; ne

(c)

milidza bugalabgwe mutulo tjigegwe nenhu unohingila tjaba.

(2) Banhu banohingila tjaba bakafanila zwibhata, mubutjilo, nezila isakaleyana nebutjilo gwetjaba kene isakaleyana nen’hingo, kakale basiyane nebutjilo gunothama n’hingo wabo ukalingigwa pasi.

(3) Banohingila tjaba mubugalo gweButungamili bakafanila tebela mamo eButungamili anotobela—

(a)

bunangwa nesetola lubuhho mukubakanya mikumbulo;

(b) thama n’hingo nezila inalebeswa yakatambunuka;

(c)

tjeseka kutjaba mukuhinga nemikumbulo; kakale

(d) tebela nlayo nezwipila mukuhingila tjaba. 197 Lubaka gweba mubugalo kwebatungamili benhabi dzinolawugwa naHulumente Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjinga ganhula lubaka gweba mubugalo kwebatungamili benhabi dzinolawugwa nahulumente nebatungamili bemabhizinesi ahulumente nebatungamili nemabhizinesi etjaba anolawugwa naHulumente. 198 Milayo inothama kuti kutobegwe Danho 9 Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanela hhisa nhambo dzetshambidzila zwinolebgwaneDanho ileli, kuhanganyila nhambo dze—

(a)

haka kuti banohingila tjaba baduse patjena bezibisa tjaba nhundu dzabo lubaka nelubaka;

(b) bumba zila dzezwibhata dzakafanila tebegwa nebanhu banohingila tjaba

(c)

sapatjena mamo etungamigwa kwemabhizinesi akatambunuka akafanila tobegwa nenhabi dzemabhizinesi dzinolawugwa nahulumente nemabhizinesi aHulumente kene mabhizinesi anolawugwa naHulumente;

(d) hhula mikoba yelangiwakwebanhu banotjuluka milayo inolebwa muDanho ileli kene zila dzezwibhata dzinolegwa mutjikamu (b).

DANHO 10 HINGILA HULUMENTE 199 Hingila Hulumente

(1) Kuna gubungano lin’ompela lebanhu banohingila Hulumente, ndilo lakalingisana nebhakhwa kweZimbabwe.

(2) Gubungano lebanohingila Hulumente lina banhu banohingila Hango kuhhe kwe—

(a)

nhengo dzedzibiligwa kwehango nedze amwe makubungano edzibilila angabumbiwa;

(b) majaji, mamejasitirithi nebamwe banotungamila mudzikhuta dzakabumbiwa neTjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente;

(c)

nhengo dzeKhomishini dzakabumbiwa neNlayo Nkulu iwoyu;

(d) bahingi bePhalamente; ne

(e) kene umwe nhu unabugalo kene tulo tjabagwa, neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu keneTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente, kuti asibe wegubungano lebanohingila Hulumente.

(3) Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila hhula mikoba kuti kube nekubungana, mbihho, Butungamili, nlayo, mazwibhato ne, kutebegwa ndima 203, mamo ehinga kwebanhu banohingila Hulumente, 200 Zwibhata kwebanohingila Hulumente

(1) Banhu banohingila Hulumente bakafanila zwibhata nezila inotobela Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu.

(2) Akuna nhu unohingila Hulumente wakafanila tobela ndawulo kene thama tjimu tjakaleyana nenlayo kene tjisimunlayo.

(3) Akuna nhu unohingila Hulumente, nekene ethama n’hingo uwe, unobvumigwa kuti—

(a)

atole lubihho gwelimwe gubungano lezwepolitiki;

(b) kuti hapa jidzo muzwinhu zwinondilana nekwegubungano lezwepolitiki;

(c)

atshambidzile zwinhu zwimunlayo zwinohakiwa negubungano lezwepolitiki; kene

(d) athubule ndulamilo nensununguko dzenhu,

(4) Banhu banohingila Hulumente abazofanila be bahingi kene bamilili be limwe lemakubungano ezwepolitiki.

(5) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila be nelebeswa lekuti banhu banohingila Hulumente abatobhatika munazwepolitiki. 201 Minisita wakalingisana nebanohingila Hulumente Ntungamili weHango wakafanila biga Minisita unowholinga banhu banohingila Hulumente. 202 Bumbiwa nema kweKhomishini yebanohingila Hulumente

(1) KunaKhomishini yebanohingila Hulumente ina—

(a)

nghalitulo neNtebedzeli wenghalitulo; ne

(b) bamwe banhu banobigwa neNtungamili weHango basipasi kwebabili kakale basingapinde bahanu.

(2) Banhu bamuKhomishini yebanohingila Hulumente bakafanila hagwa neluzibo gwabo kene kuti batjihinga kungapani mubhakhwa kwebanhu, tungamila kene hingila tjaba. 203 Mihingo yeKhomishini yebanohingila Hulumente

(1) Khomishini yebanohingila Hulumente inamihingo inotebela—

(a)

yebiga banhu banafanilo kakale banamatatabuko ehingila Hulumente;

(b) seleba kwendima 65(5), ibankanye kakale ilinge hinga kwebanhu, kuhanganyisila mbhayilo, mbhudilo nezwimwe zwinhu zwingapiwa banohingila baHulumente;

(c)

kuti ihingise simba layo patungamila nelanga banhu banohingila Hulumente;

(d) kuti ilingisise zwikumbilo kakale ibankanye kwakumbigwa nebahingi baHulumente kunondilana nezwiyeto zwebatungamili kene zwimwe zwisatiwa;

(e)

ihingise nhambo dzeyeta kuti kube nehinga kunobhatsha kakale kwakatambunuka, nelulwama kweN’hingo waHulumente;

(f)

ibe nelebeswa lekuti bahingi baHulumente banohinga nezila yakatambunuka isina halulo;

(g) kuti ipe mano kuNtungamili weHango naMinisita nendebo dzakalingisana neN’hingo waHulumente;

(h) susuma kubahingi baHulumente bose bunhu netjilemela kunolondesedzewa muNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu; kakale

(i)

kuti ite imwe mihingo yayinopiwa kene inolebgwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kene Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente.

(2) Khomishini yebanohingila Hulumente, nebvumigwa naMinisita wakalingisana nezwehingila Hulumente, ingathama milayo yetobegwa kwezwinhu zwalebgwa mundimana (1).

(3) Khomishiini yebanohingila Hulumente yakafanila yeta mihingo yayo itobela dzose ndongoloso dzehinga dzahhisiwa naMinisita wakalingisana nehingigwa kwaHulumente.

(4) Pa baakanyiwa kwembhayilo, mbhudilo nezwimwe zwinhu zwingapiwa banohingila Hulumente, Khomishini inolinga banohingila Hulumente yakafanila kuthama ikoku nebvumigwa neNtungamili weHango atjipiwa mano naMinisita wakalingisana nekwedzimari kakale atjilebesana naMinisita wakalingisana nekwehingigwa kwaHulumenete. 204 Bamilili behango kudzimwe hango nebamwe batumwa beZimbabwe Ntungamili weHango angabiga banhu kuti babe bamilili behango kudzimwe hango kene kuti babe bamilili eZimbabwe kudzimwe hango kene kuti bahinge mumakubungano ehango dzose kakale angabadusa mumihingo iyeyo kene nagupi lubaka.

205 Bakwalili benhabi dzaHulumente

(1) Bakwalili bakulu beNhabi banobigwa neNtungamili weHango hule kwelebesana neKhomishini yebanohingila Hulumente.

(2) Lubaka gwebamubugalo kweNkwalili Nkulu makole mahanu, kakale unobgwiliziwa mun’hingo kwe amwe makole mahanu kan’ompela koga kulingwa matatabuko awe, hinga kukwe nezwaahhisa elimun’hingo. DANHO 11 MIHINGO YEDZIBILILA LUBABI 1 WHOKU NA WHOKUYA KUYAPO 206 Dzibiligwa kwehango

(1) Zwinangwa zwedzibiligwa kwehango muZimbabwe dzakafanila lakidza zwipila kwebanhu beZimbabwe begale belizane nelununguko, lunyalalo nekutebama, sahla, nobhudilila.

(2) Dzibiligwa kwehango yeZimbabwe kwakafanila kundidzane neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu nenlayo.

(3) Kwazo kwazo, dzibiligwa kwehango kwakafanila kusundiwe nelemekedza—

(a)

ndulamilo nensununguko dzakazinda nezwitonga kunabunhu nezwilemela zwakakwagwa muNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu;

(b) nelawula kwenlayo. 207 Mihingo yedzibilila

(1) Mihingo yedzibilila kuZimbabwe inohanganyila—

(a)

Masole;

(b) mihingo yeMapholisa;

(c)

mihingo yeNyaya;

(d) Lutabi gweMajele nezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa;

(e)

negumwe lutabi gwedzibilila gwakabumbiwa neTjisungo TjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(2) Banodzibilila hango bapasi kwemasimba eNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, eNtungamili weHango neKhabhinethi kakale bangabhuzwiwa mibhuzo nePhalamente.

(3) Banohingila lutabi gwedzibilila kwakafanila lakidza siyana kwebanhu beZimbabwe. 208 Zwibhata kwebanohinga dzibilila

(1) Banohinga dzibilila bakafanila hinga betebela Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu nenlayo.

(2) Hakuna banohinga dzibilila kene nhengo dzabo, pa kuthama n’hingo wabo banga—

(a) tola lubihho munazwepolitiki;

(b) milidza zwinangwa zwelimwe gubungano lezwepolitiki kene kwalinohaka;

(c)

benga zwinangwa zwimunlayo weyimwe gubungano lezwepolitiki kene kwayinohaka; kene

(d) tjuluka ndulamilo dzakazinda kene nsununguko dzebanhu bose.

(3) Banohinga dzibilila habazofanila banhengo kene galamubugalo mugubungano lezwepolitiki kene limwe gubungano.

(4) Batjahingila dzibilila kwakafanila basihinge kene bethame kumwe kudzinabi dzetjaba kuhhe kwelubaka kumbhatsha hango. 209 Lubahhe gweDzibiligwa kweHango

(1) KunaLubahhe gweDzibiligwa kweHango guna Ntungamili weHango senghalitulo, baTebedzeli beNtungamili ne amwe MaMinisita nebanohinga dzibilila nebamwe banhu sekwakunolebgwa muTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(2) Mihingo yeLubahhe gweDzibiligwa kweHango—

(a)

inofumbidza ndongoloso yelubahhe gwedzibiligwa kweZimbabwe;

(b) ino zibisa nepa mano kuNtungamili weHango nekwendebo dzedzibilila Hango; kakale

(c)

inoyeta imwe mihingo sekwagwa kwayo muTjisungo TjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(3) baLawuli bedzibililo kwakafanila kuti bape Lubahhe gweDzibiligwa kweHango nelondesedzo dzinoleba nekwedzibililo kuZimbabwe sekunohakiwa neLubahhe gweDzibiligwa kweHango. 210 Zila dzen’un’una kwebakazwimila boga Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila tjibe nezila yehinga yakazwimila yewamutshila nohakisisa sagutsikana kwenhengo dzetjaba notjuluka nlayo kwebanohingila dzibilila, kakale lulamisa jinyo latiwa notjuluka nlayo. LUBABI 2 MASOLE

211 Masole

(1) Defence Forces of Zimbabwe inamasole anodzibilila hango, Masole emuMhepo neyimwe mihingo ingabambiwa kusi kweTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(2) Masole ndiwo woga masole amunlayo muZimbabwe.

(3) Masole akafanila lemekedza ndulamilo nensununguko dzakazinda dzebanhu bose kakale asitole lubihho gwezwepolitiki, ahingile tjaba, azwipile kutjaba, ahinge kumunlayo kakale azwise pasi kwebanotungamila banhu sebigwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu.

(4) Masole akafanila gala ehinga nezila yakafanila.

(5) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila yeta kuti kube nebankanyiwa, tjibakhwa, tungamigwa, lingiwa, langiwa nemilidziwa nejitisiwa kwebagalitulo nebamwe bahingi kakale, kutobegwa ndima 218, mamo ehinga kweMasole. 212 Mihingo yeMasole N’hingo weMasole ndewedzibilila Zimbabwe, banhu bayo, mihingo yayo yezwedzibilila nezwayinohaka nelebeswa layo nemilidzilatobegwa kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu.

213 Tumwa kweMasole

(1) Kutobegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, Ntungamili weHango koga, seNlawuli Nkulu weMasole, unamasimba—

(a)

ebvumila tumwa kweMasole; kene

(b) unamasimba eleba zila yehingisiwa kweMasole.

(2) Nemasimba eNtungamili, Masole angatumwa muZimbabwe—

(a)

edzibilila Zimbabwe;

(b) ebhatsha Mapholisa muthama kuti kube nekugadzikana nekunyalala mutjaba; kene

(c)

ebhatsha Mapholisa negumwe Butungamili gwetjaba hapa kunamhatsha kene mboli.

(3) Nemasimba eNtungamili weHango, Masole angatumwa kuhhe kweZimbabwe—

(a)

mundongoloso dzelunyalalo dzegubungano leUnited Nations Organisation kene limwe zwalo gubungano lehango dzose kene lentunhu linobe Zimbabwe ili nhengo yalo;

(b) kuti adzibilile imwe hango;

(c)

mukuhhadzisa tjibvumilano nedzimwe hango; kene

(d) pakudzibilila ludzibililo gwehango yeZimbabwe kene zwinohakiwa nehango.

(4) Hapa kunabanhu banoswika mbabi mbili munhatu dzebanhu bose bamuPhalamente mun’hangano un’ompela weSenethi neLubahhe gweMaMinisita, Phalamente ingasunga kuti tumwa kweMasole kuhhe kweZimbabwe akuzofanila tiwa.

(5) Hapo Phalamente kwayasunga kuti Masole hazofanila tumwa kuhhe kwehango, Ntungamili weHango wakafanila tola dzose nhambo dzakafanila kuti Masole asitumwe, elinga kwazo dzibililika kwebanhu beZimbabwe nezwinhu zweZimbabwe. 214 Zwitjenesa kwebutongi gwehango mukutumwa kweMasole Hapa Masole etumwa—

(a)

muZimbabwe kuti atjidze pakuhhisa kunyalala mutjaba; kene

(b) kuhhe kweZimbabwe; Ntungamili weHango wakafanila yeta kuti Phalamente izibisiwe nekulibilidza kakale kudusiwa patjena kuti neni atumwa ne—

(i) paanotumwa muZimbabwe, ntha yaanotumwa;

(ii) paanotumwa kuhhe kweZimbabwe, hango yaanotumwa. 215 Minisita wakalingisana neMasole Ntungamili weHango wakafanila biga Minisita wakalingisana neMasole. 216 Ndawulo yeMasole

(1) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhlamente tjakafanila yeta kuti—

(a)

Masole alawugwe neNlawuli un’ompela, kene

(b) umwe newumwe n’hingo weMasole, kene imwe mibili kene inopinda ipapo yakabhatshanyiwa, itiwe ilawugwa ne umwe Nlawuli.

(2) Nlawuli wose weMasole, neNlawuli wose wehinga kweMasole, unobigwa neNtungamili weHango hule kwezuwisana naMinisita wakalingisana neMasole.

(3) Nlawuli dzeMasole neNlawuli dzehinga kweMasole, dzobigwa mubugalo kwelubaka gusingapinde makole mahanu, kakale nhu hazofanila ba bugalo kwelubaka gunopinda makole ali gumi.

(4) Nhu wakatongoba mubugalo gweNlawuli wehinga kweMasole angabigwa mutulo akabaNlawuli weMasole, koga nhu wakatongoba mubugalo gwe Nlawuli weMasole hangatongobigwa mutulo tje Nlawuli wehinga kweMasole kena kuti alawule lutabi gwezwedzibilila

(5) Wose Nlawuli weMasole, wose Nlawuli wehinga kweMasole, wakafanila hingisa ndawulo iye kwakatambunuka kunondilana nemilayo yakakwagwa pasi inobva kuna Minisita wakalingisana neMasole ehinga alipasi kwemasimba eNtungamili weHango. 217 Bumbiwa nema kweKhomishini yeHinga kweMasole

(1) Kuna Khomishini yeHinga kweMasole inanghalitulo wakafanila be nghalitulo weKhomishini yebanoHingila Hulumente, nebamwe banhu basipasi kwebabili kakale basipehhugwi kwebatanhatu bakabigwa neNtungamili weHango.

(2) Bahingi beKhomishini yeHinga kweMasole bakafanila hagwa nentha yeluzibo gwabo kene hinga kwelubaka mulutabi gwebhakhwa kwewhalo, gweButungamili, gwe ngwa, kene banezwidiyo zwakafanila kene lizanila nje koga bigwa mubugalo, ne—

(a)

basipasi kwegwamu bakafanila babanhu basakatongoba Masole kene basiMasole;

(b) umwe wabo kene bamwe babo bakafanila kuti babe bakatongoba mubugalo gupehhugwi muMasole kwemakole asipasi kwemahanu. 218 Mihingo yeKhomishini yeHinga kweMasole

(1) Khomishini yeHinga kweMasole inamihingo inotebela—

(a)

pamihingo banhu banafanilo kakale banabumhihha gwebhata iyeyo mihingo muMasole;

(b) tambunudza nelawula zila dzehinga, kuhanganisila mibhayilo, milipo nem’holo, yemaMasole;

(c)

banelebeswa kuti Masole akabhakhwa zwibuyanana nemamo ebutjilo gweMasole akalulwama;

(d) banelebeswa kuti Masole anotebela ndima 208;

(e)

kudza bhatana newhisisana pakati kweMasole netjaba;

(f)

pa mano Ntungamili weHango naMinisita mundebo dzakalingisana neMasole;

(g) nethama kene upi n’hingo wapiwa Khomishini neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kene Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(2) Khomishini yeHinga kweMasole, ibvumigwa naMinisita wakalingisana neMasole, ingabakanya milayo yakalingisana nezila yehinga yalebwa mundimana (1).

(3) Pakutambunudza mibhayilo, milipo nebhatshoyeMasole, Khomishini yeHingo kweMasole yakafanila kuthama hule kwebvumigwa neNtungamili weHango mano abva kuna Minisita wakabhata lutabi gwedzimali kakle hule kwepana mano

naMinisita wakalingisana neMasole. LUBABI 3 MAPHOLISA

219 Mapholisa nemihingo yawo

(1) Kuna Mapholisa akalingisana ne—

(a)

susuma, hakisisa nedzibilila kutjinya;

(b) londolodza dzibililika kweZimbabwe;

(c)

dzibilila butjilo gwebanhu nedzibilila nelisa nhundu dzebanhu;

(d) londoloda nlayo nebunhu; kakale

(e)

milidzila Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu netshambidzila tobegwa kwenlayo kusina kuhla kene nkuha.

(2) Mapholisa akafanila thama n’hingo wabo ehingidzana ne—

(a)

banyayi bamunlayo;

(b) nenhu ungabe wabigwa kumunlayo kuti abe phorisa linobonyololola, hakisisa kene dzibilila tjinya kwakasiyana siyana; ne

(c) makubungano akatiwa nehango dzakabakidzana neZimbabwe nehango dzose kuti kupedza butjinyi.

(3) Mapholisa azofanila tola lubihho gwelimwe lemakubungano ezwepolitiki, ahingile tjaba nelebeswa, azwipile muhango, ahinge nezila yakatambunuka kakale atobele Butungamili selondesedza kweNlayo Nkulu wehango iwhoyu.

(4) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila hhulila mikoba yegubungano, mbhihho,Butungamili, milayo, mazwibhato nemilidziwa nejitisiwa kweMapholisa mun’hingo nebamwe bahingi, kutobegwa ndima 223, zila dzehinga dzeMapholisa. 220 Minisita lakalingisana neMapholisa Ntungamili weHango wakafanila biga Minisita wakalingisana neMapholisa. 221 Nlawuli nkulu weMapholisa

(1) Mapholisa anolawugwa neNlawuli Nkulu weMapholisa wabigwa neNtungamili weHango hule kwelebesana naMinisita wakalingisana neMapholisa.

(2) Nlawuli Nkulu weMapholisa unobigiwa kwelubaka gungapa makole mahanu kakale angabgwiliziwa mun’hingo kwe amwe makole mahanu kan’ompela.

(3) Nhu wakatongobe Nlawuli Nkulu weMapholisa aangabigwe kuti alawule imwe mihingo yedzibilila.

(4) Nlawuli Nkulu weMapholisa wakafanila lawula etobela ndongoloso dzahhisiwa naMinisita wakalingisana Mapholisa linotobela kunolebgwa neNtungamili weHango. 222 Bumbiwa nema kweKhomishini yehinga kweMapholisa

(1) KunaKhomishini yehinga kweMapholisa inanghalitulo, wakafanila benghalitulo weKhomishini yebanohingila Hulumente nebamwe banhu babigwa neNtungamili weHango basipasi kwebabili kakale basipehhugwi kwebatanhatu.

(2) Nhengo dzeKhomishiniyehinga kweMapholisa dzakafanila hagwa neluzibo gwadzo kene kuti dzatjihinga kungapani munazwebhakhwa kwenlayo nekunyalala, Butungamili, kene fanilo dzen’hingo dzadzinadzo kene matatabuko abanawo angata

kuti babigwe mun’hingo; kakale—

(a)

kwakafanila kuti gwamu lebanhu ibabo libe lili lebanhu basakatongo hinga seMapholisa;

(b) kwakafanila kuti umwe wabo ebe akatongo tola bugalo gupehhugwi Mubupholisa kwelubaka gungapa makole mahanu kan’ompela kene kunopinda ipapo. 223 Mihingo yeKhomishini yehinga kweMapholisa

(1) Khomishini yehinga kweMapholisa inamihingo inotebela—

(a)

pa mihingo kubanhu banafanilo kakale banamatatabuko ehinga muBupholisa;

(b) kuti ibankanye kakale ilinge yetiwa kwen’hingo, kuhanganyisila mibhayilo, milipo, m’holo nezwimwe zwingapiwa Mapholisa;

(c)

ibe nelebeswa bululwami netungamigwa kwakalulwama kweMapholisa nelingiwa kwen’hingo wawo nezila yakatambunuka kwazo;

(d) be nelebeswa lekuti Mapholisa ose anotobela ndima 208;

(e)

isusume kuti kube nekunyalala newhisisana pakati kweMapholisa nebanhu;

(f)

ipe mano kuNtungamili weHango naMinisita nendebo dzinondilana neMapholisa; kakale

(g) ithame imwe mihingo yayingapiwa kene ingalondesedzewa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kene Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(2) Khomishini yehinga kweMapholisa, nebvumigwa naMinisita lakalingisana neMapholisa, ingata zila dzeyeta ndongoloso dzalebgwa mundimana (1).

(3) Pabakanya mibhayilombuyidzilo nezwimwe zwinhu zwingapiwa Mapholisa, Khomishini inolinga hinga kweMapholisa yakafanila kuthama ikoku yabvumigwa neNtungamili weHango apiwa mano naMinisita wakalingisana nekwedzimari kakale atanga alebesana naMinisita wakalingisana neMapholisa. LUBABI 4 MIHINGO YENYAYA

224 Bumbiwa kwen’hingo wenyaya

(1) Imwe mihingo yenyaya muHango, kuhhe kwelutabi gwenyaya muMasole kene maPholisa, yakafanila bumbiwa pasi kwenlayo kene ndawulo yeNtungamili weHango kene yeKhabinethi.

(2) Imwe mihingo yenyaya muHango kwakafanila kuti isitole lubihho munazwepolitiki,hingila tjaba nelebeswa, zwipila mutjaba, hinga kwakatambunhuka nezwisa pasi kwemasimba etjaba selebakwe muNlayo Nkulu. 225 Minisita lakalingisana nebunyayi gwehango Ntungamili weHango kwakafanila abige Minisita lakalingisana nebunyayi gwehango. 226 Ndawulo kene lawula kwebunyayi gwehango

(1) Mihingo yebunyayi gwehango yakafanila kuti ibe pasi kwendawulo kene ilawugwe neNlawuli Nkulu wemiHingo yebuNyayi wakafanila bigwa neNtungamili weHango kwemakole mahanu engapamunugwa kan’ompela.

(2) nlawuli Nkulu wemiHingo yebuNyayi wakafanila hingisa ndawule iye kene lawula kukwe nezila inondidzana nekwakakwagwa mundongoloso dzinobva kuMinisita lakalingisana nemihingo yebunyayi muhango lihingisa masimba alapiwa neNtungamili weHango.

(3) Nhu watongohinga seNlawuli Nkulu mumiHingo webuNyayi hangabigwe kuti alawule imwe mihingo yedzibilila. LUBABI 5 LUTABI GWEMAJELE NEZWETAMBUNUDZIWA KWEBASUNGWA

227 Lutabi gweMajele nezweTambunudziwa kweBasunga nemihingo yago

(1) Kuna Lutabi gweMajele nezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa gwakalinga—

(a)

dzibilila tjaba kubatjinyi ndohhalilila kakale bediya mazwibhato banhu banotjinya nebamwe banohakiwa nenlayo kuti bahhaliligwe, kakale notjilisana zwibuyanana nebamwe h’apa babhuda mujele; kakale

(b) tungamigwa kwemajele nelutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa.

(2) Majele neLutabi gwezwetambunudziwa kweBangwa kwakafanila kusitole lubihho kwezwepolitiki, hingila tjaba nelebeswa, zwipila mutjaba, hinga kwakatambunhuka nezwisa pasi kwemasimba etjaba sekwakabumbiwa muNlayo Nkulu.

(3) Tjisungo TjeNlayo tjePhalemente tjakafanila hhulila mikoba yegubungano, mbihho, Butungamili, milayo, mazwibhato nemilidziwa nejitisiwa kweban’hingi mubugalo nedzimwe nhengo kakale, kutobegwa ndima 231, tjimo tjohinga tjenhengo dzeMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa. 228 Minisita wakalingisana neLutabi gweMajele nezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa Ntungamili weHango wakafanila biga Minisita wakalingisana neLutabi gweMajele nezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa. 229 Nlawuli Nkulu weMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa

(1) Lutabi gweMajele nezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa gupasi kwendawulo yeNlawuli Nkulu weLutabi gweMajele nezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa wabigwa neNtungamili weHango nolebelesana naMinisita wakalingisana neLutabi gweMajele nezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa.

(2) Nlawuli Nkulu weLutabi gweMajele nezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa unobigwa kwemakole mahanu engapapiwa kan’ompela.

(3) Nhu watongohinga seNlawuli Nkuli weLutabi gweMajele nezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa hangabigwe seNlawuli wemihingo inodzibilila.

(4) Nlawuli Nkulu weMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa wakafanila lawula ehingila pehhugwi kwekwakakwagwa naMinisita wakalingisana neMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa ehingila pekwemasimba eNtungamili weHango. 230 Bumbiwa nema kweKhomishini yeMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa

(1) Lutabi gweMajele nezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa gohanganyila nghalitulo, wakafanila abe nghalitulo weKhomishini yebanohingila Hulumente nebanhu

babili nebangaswika batanhatu bahalugwa neNtungamili weHango.

(2) Nhengo dzeKhomishini yeLutabi gweMajele nezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa dzakafanila halugwa neluzibo kene hinga kwelubaka betonga, behinga pehhugwi, behinga nodzibilila, kene nefanelo dzakatambunuka kene lulamila bigiwa mutulo, kakale—

(a)

gwamu labo lakafanila ba banhu banohinga muMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa;

(b) umwe wabo wakafanila kuti akatongogala mubugalo gupehhugwi muMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa kan’ompela kene kunopinda ipapo kunoswika makole mahanu. 231 Mihingo yeKhomishini yeMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa

(1) Khomishi yeMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa inamihingo inotebela—

(a)

kuti ipe mihingo kubanhu banafanilo kakale banamatatabuko kuti batole bugalo kene bahinge muMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa;

(b) yebakanyana nelinga mamo ehinga, kuhanganyisila nlipo, mbhayilo nedzimwe mbuyizilo, dzebanohinga muMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa;

(c)

yebe nelebeswa lekuti Majele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa kwakalulwama kakale kohinga nezila yakatambunuka nelingiwa kwago muhinga nezila inogutsha;

(d) yebe nelebeswa lekuti bahingi beMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa banotobela ndima 208;

(e)

milidzila kunyalala newhisisana pakati kweMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa nebanhu;

(f)

yepa mano kuNtungamili weHango naMinisita mundebo dzakalingisana neMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa;

(g) neyethama imwe mihingo ingabe yatilikiwa muKhomishini neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kene Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(2) Khomishini yeMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa, ibvumigwa naMinisita wakalingisana neMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa, ingathama milayo yekumwe kwalebgwa mundimana (1).

(3) Pakubakanya mari dzinobhadagwa, mibhayilo nedzimwe mbuzilo dzebahingi beMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa, Khomishini yeMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa yakafanila hinga ibvumigwa neNtungamili weHango apiwa mano naMinisita wakalingisana nezwemari hule kwebonesana kukwe naMinisita wakalingisana neMajele neLutabi gwezweTambunudziwa kweBasungwa. DANHO 12 MAKHOMISHINI AKAZWIMILA ANOBHATSHA NEKWEZWITONGA

LUBABI 1 WHOKU NAWHOKUYA

232 Makhomishini akazwimila Makhomishini anotebela ndiwo Makhomishini akazwimila—

(a)

Khomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe;

(b) Khomishini yeNdulamilo dzeBanhu yeZimbabwe;

(c)

Khomishini yeBunhukadzi neBunhulume yeZimbabwe;

(d) Khomishini yezwendisiwa kweNdebo yeZimbabwe; ne

(e)

Khomishini yeHango yeLunyalalo netilana Mazwibhatigwa.

233 Zwinangwa zweMakhomishini akazwimila Makhomishini akazwimila anazwinangwa zwinotebela pehhugwi kwazezo zwangapiwa imwe nemwe—

(a)

yemilidza netshambidzila ndulamilo dzebanhu nezwitonga;

(b) yedzibilila zwitonga nekunohakiwa nebanhu;

(c)

milidza tobegwa kwemilayo;

(d) yesusuma butjena neba nenlandu mumakubungano anohingila tjaba;

(e)

yethama kuti Hango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzahulumente, nemabunga anolawugwa nahulumente kutebele bunhu netjilemela tjezwitonga;

(f)

neba nelebeswa lekuti satobegwa kwendulamo kotambunudziwa.

234 Bahingi beMakhomishini akazwimila Makhomishini akazwimila anamasimba epa banhu mihingo kakale, etobela nlayo, elinga mamo ehinga kwabo. 235 Zwimila kweMakhomishini

(1) Makhomishini akazwimila—

(a)

akazwimilila woga kakale akuna nhu unoyi wakafanila hatungamila;

(b) akafanila zila nezila dzimuNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu; kakale

(c)

akafanila thama mihingo yawo nezila isina kuhla, nkuha kene kutshambidzila; kene zwako ananlandu wetjenese mihingo yawo nezila yakahingwa nayo kuPhalamente.

(2) Hango nemakubungano ose nenhabi dzahulumente mumatanhilo ose, kuhingisa nhambo dzenlayo nedzimwe nhambo, kwakafanila bhatsha maKhomishini akazwimila kakale kwakafanila dzibilila zwimila kwawo, satola lubihho,lebeswa nethama nezila yakwako.

(3) Akuna nhu anganginilila mukuhinga kwemaKhomishini akazwimila. 236 Bahingi bemaKhomishini akazwimila habazofanila bhatika muna zwepolitiki

(1) Bahingi bemaKhomishini akazwimila habazofanila kuti, pakuhinga kwabo—

(a)

batole lubihho gwelimwe gubungano lezwepolitiki;

(b) bhatika pakumilidzila zwinohakwa nemakubungano ezwepolitiki;

(c)

tshambidzila hinga kwakatambunuka kumunlayo kwemakubungano ezwepolitiki; kene

(d) tjuluka ndulamilo kene lusununguko gwewumwe nhu.

(2) Banhu banoba balinhengo dzegubungano lezwepolitiki pakubigwa kwabo muzwitulo zweKhomishini yakazwimila bakafanila letja banhengo dzegubungano lezwepolitiki nekulibilidza kusanhu kwapinda mahhuba alimakumi matatu babigwa muzwitulo.

(3) Hapa nhengo yeKhomishini yakazwimila—

(a)

ibanhengo yegubungano lezwepolitiki kene limwe gubungano; kene

(b) hapa yayili nhengo yegubungano lezwepolitiki kene limwe gubungano pakubigwa kwayo mutulo tjeKhomishini yakazwimila, ikokon’wa letja banhengo yegubungano lezwepolitiki kusanhu kwapinda mahhuba alimakumi matatu yabigwa mutulo; kubva ipapo inoba isitji nhengo yayiyeyo Khomishini yakazwimila. 237 Bigwa nedusiwa mubugalo kwebahingi beMakhomishini akazwimila

(1) Pakuthangiwa kwebanhu banoobigwa muzwitulo zwe imwe neyimwe Khomishini yakazwimila, Khomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile yakafanila—

(a)

zibisa tjaba nekwen’hingo iwhoyo;

(b) koka tjaba kuti tjithange banhu batjinohaka;

(c)

bhuzwa banhu bakathangiwa mibhuzo;

(d) kwala pasi mazina ebanhu babhudilila mukuthangiwabangabigwa mutulo;

(e)

nendisa mazina ihawo kuNtungamili weHango.

(2) Nhengo yeKhomishini yakazwimila ingadusiwa mubugalo hapa kuli kuti—

(a)

hayitotubula thama mihingo yebugalo gwayo nentha yesatubula kwembili kene kwenkumbulo;

(b) inokon’wa hinga;

(c)

yawanika nlandu wesaba nemazwibhato; kene

(d) hayizotjafanila bigwa mutulo tjeKhomishini iyeyo.

(3) Zila yedusiwa kwemajaji mubugalo ndiyozwayo inohingisiwa pakudusiwa kwenhengo yeKhomishini yakazwimila. LUBABI 2 KHOMISHINI YEHALULO YEZIMBABWE

238 Bumbiwa nema kweKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe

(1) Kuna Khomishini inoodan’wa iyiKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe ina—

(a)

nghalitulo wakabigwa neNtungamili hule kwezuwisana neKhomishini inolinga nekwen’hingo wedziKhuta neKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile; ne

(b) nebamwe banhu balihhanakadzi bakabigwa neNtungamili weHango ebatola mumazina ebanhu basipasi kwegumi nebabili bathangiwa neKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile.

(2) Nghalitulo weKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe wakafanila bajaji kene nhu wakatongoba jaji kene nhu wakathama zwidiyo zwinobvuma kuti angabigwa akabejaji.

(3) Hapa bigwa kwenghalitulo weKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe kwakaleyana nekunohakiwa neKhomishini inolinga nekwe n’hingo wedziKhuta, Ntungamili weHango wakafanila thama kuti Khomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimileizibisiwe tjinyolotjo.

(4) Banhu bamuKhomishini yeZimbabwe yeHalulo bakafanila be banhu beZimbabwe kakale bahagwa nentha yebe nelebeswa kukwe nelubaka gwaatjihinga nematatabuko awe mubhata ndebo dzetjaba kene bemabhizinesi bakazwimila boga.

(5) Nhengo dzeKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe dzobigwa kuti dzibe mun’hingo kwemakole matanhatu kakale dzingahinga kakale kwemakole matanhatu hapa ekutanga apela, koga akuna nhu ungabigwa kene ungahinga muKhomishini hapa akatongo benhengo kwelubaka gun’ompela kene gunopinda ipapo,tjoselele kene akatongoma, kwemakole aligumi nekubili. 239 Mihingo yeKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe Khomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe inamihingo inotobeta—

(a)

kuti ibankanyile, ithame kakale itungamile—

(i) halugwa kweNtungamili weHango nehalulo yengina muPhalamente;

(ii) halulo dzemakhansili ematunhu neematoropo nemabunga anotungamila makhansili emitunhu;

(iii) halulo dzenhengo dzeKhansili yeHango yaboHe inolondesedzwa nendima 285; ne

(iv) mareferendamu; nebe nelebeswa lekuti halulo idzedzo nemareferendamu koyetiwa nezila yakatambunuka, yakahalabvuka, nekulizana, nebutjena kakale ktobegwa nlayo;

(b) itungamile halugwa kweNtungamili weSenethi neTjilebeleki nebe nelebeswa lekuti halulo idzedzo dzotiwa nezila yakatambunuka kakale kutobegwa nlayo;

(c)

bakwalise banoovota;

(d) ikwale whalo dzebanovota nemazina ebavoti;

(e)

be nelebeswa lekuti whalo dzebanovota nemazina ebavoti kobhakhwa zwibuyanana;

(f)

kuti iganhule mitunhu, mawhadi ne imwe miganhu yehalulo;

(g) bakanya, tshambidzila neyendesa maphepha evota, bakanya yetiwa kwemabhokisi emaphepha evota, kakale ibankanye ntha dzinovotegwa;

(h) kuti bathame kakale balinge diyiwa kwebanhu nekwevota;

(i)

(j)

kuti ibige banhu banoolinga yetiwa kwehalulo nemareferendamu; kuti ibudze banhu banohingila Hulumente kene banohinga kusi kwemakhansili emitunhu zila dzehinga kudza kuti halulo nemareferendamu kunde nezila yakatambunuka, yakahabvuka, kulizane kakale kutiwe nebutjena; kakale

(k) iwamutjile kakale ilinge n ón’ona kwebanhu kakale ithame kwayinobona kwakafanila nen ónóna ikoko.

240 Safanila bigwa muKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe Pehhugwi kwebanhu balebgwa mundima 320(3), banhu banotobela abazofanila bigwa kuti bahinge muKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe—

(a)

banhu banohingila tjaba, kuhhe kwemajaji;

(b) banohingila makhansili ematunhu neematoropo nemakhansili emitunhu, ne

(c)

nhengo nebanhu banohingila mabunga ezwenlayo nemabunga anolawugwa naHulumente.

241 Khomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe yakafanila londesedza nekwehalulo nekwema referendamu Pehhugwi kwelondesedzo inohakika seleba kwendima 323, Khomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe yakafanila nekulibilidza, kakale ihingisa Minisita wakafanila, ndisa londesedzo yeyetiwa kwehalulo dzose nemareferendamuose kuPhalamente. LUBABI 3 KHOMISHINI YENDULAMILO DZEBANHU YEZIMBABWE

242 Bumbiwa nema kweKhomishini yeNdulamilo dzeBanhu yeZimbabwe

(1) KunaKhomishini yakafanila zibgwa seKhomishi yeNdulamilo dzeBanhu yeZimbabwe ina—

(a)

nghalitulo wabigwa neNtungamili weHango hule kwelebesana neKhomishini inolinga nekwe n’hingo wedziKhutha neKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile;

(b) nebamwe banhu balihhanakadzi bakabigwa neNtungamili weHango ebahala mumazina ebanhu basipasi kwegumi nekubili bakathangiwa akahhisiwa neKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile.

(2) Nghalitulo weKhomishini yeNdulamilo dzebanhu yeZimbabwe wakafanila be nhu wabe nefanilo dzehinga munazwenlayo kwemakole asipasi kwe alikunotondeka ehingila muZimbabwe.

(3) Hapa bigwa kwenghalitulo weKhomishi yeNdulamilo dzeBanhu yeZimbabwe kusingandilane neleba kweKhomishini inolinga nekwe n’hingo wedziKhutha, Ntungamili weHango wakafanila yeta kuti Khomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile izibisiwe nekulibilidza.

(4) Nhengo dzeKhomishini yeNdulamilo dzeBanhu yeZimbabwe dzakafanila haluligwa ba nelebeswa neba neluzibo newhisisa,nehinga munazwemilidza ndulamilo dzebanhu. 243 Mihingo yeKhomishini yeNdulamilo dzeBanhu yeZimbabwe

(1) Khomishini yeNdulamilo dzeBanhu yeZimbabwe inamihingo inotebela—

(a)

ye susuma zibgwa nelemekedziwa kwendulamilo nensununguko mumatanhilo ose etjaba;

(b)

yesusuma dzibiligwa, pfumbidziwa newan’wa kwendulamilo nensununguko;

(c)

lingisisa, holisisa nebona kuti ndulamilo nensununguko dzebanhu dzobhakhwa zwakanaka;

(d)

iwamutjile kakale ilinge n ón’ona kwebanhu kakale ithame kwayinobona kwakafanila nen ónóna ikoko;

(e)

dzibilila tjaba muhingisiwa kwemasimba nezila isakatambunuka nekon’wa bhata zwibuyanana banhu neHulumente nemakubungano etjaba nebamubugalo mumakubungano ihawo;

(f)

hakisisa zwibhata kweButungamili kene kwenhu, panokumbuligwa kuti ndulamilo nensununguko dzebanhu dzinolebgwa muLukwalo gweBigwa kweNdulamilo dzatjulukiwa neButungamili igogo kene nhu;

(g)

wana zila dzakatambunuka dzetambunudza, kuhanganila kuludzila sekesiwa kwebatjinyi, hapa ndulamilo kene nsununguko dzebanhu dzatjulukiwa;

(h) pa zila Nlawuli Nkulu weMaphorisa kuti alingisise milandu yebatjinyi banosolegwa tjuluka ndulamilo kenensununguko dzebanhu nezila yebudzudzu nelondesedzela Khomishini nekwawangwa mulingisisa kwatiwa;

(i)

kulumbidzila Phalamente zila dzinohinga milidza ndulamilo nensununguko dzebanhu;

(j)

thama hakuludzo mundebo dzakalingisana nendulamilo nensununguko dzebanhu negalisana kuna ndulamo; ne

(k) kumba nelinga—

(i) majele, pano hhaliligwa banhu, dihhilo yebanhu banotihha hhunguludziwa nentha dzinondilana nayikoku; ne

(ii) kunogala banhu bana bugwele gwenkumbulo kene baka kubadzika mikumbulo; kudze wan’we tjimo tjepabanogala ibabo banhu, nepa mano nekwe tjimo tjentha idzedzo kuMinisita wakalingisana nehingisiwakwenlayo wakalingisana nentha idzedzo.

(2) Nlawuli Nkulu weMapholisa wakafanila tobela kwaabudziwa neKhomishini yeNdulamilo dzeBanhu yeZimbabwe pasi kwendimana (1)(h). 244 Nlondesedzo dzinonda nedzinobva kuKhomishini yeNdulamilo dzeBanhu yeZimbabwe

(1) Khomishini yeNdulamilo dzeBanhu yeZimbabwe ingahaka nhu kene wupi, gubungano kene lipi kene gupi lutabi, kweHulumente kene kunohingila Hulumente—

(a)

zibisa Khomishini nekwenhambo dzatogwa pakumilidza ndulamilo nensununguko dzebanhu dzimulukwalo gweBigwa kweNdulamilo; ne

(b) hhisa luzibo muKhomishini gwayinohaka kudze ibankanye londesedzo inohakika yakafanilandsiwa kugubungano leHango dzakabakisana neZimbabwe kene Hango dzose Zimbabwe yayili mukati bo kutebegwa tjisungo tjendulamilo dzebanhu, zwibvumelwano zwakasiyana siyana.

(2) Pehhugwi kwelondesedzo inohakika seleba kwendima 323,Khomishini yeNdulamilo dzeBanhu yeZimbabwe inga, kakale ihingisa Minisita wakafanila, ndisa londesedzo kuPhalamente nekwe zwimo zwendulamilo nensununguko dzebanhu, dzinobe dzabongwa neKhomishini kuti dzakafanila zibisiwa Phalamente. LUBABI 4 Khomishini Yelizanyisiwa kweBanhu Betjilume neBanhu Betjikadzi yeZimbabwe

245 Bumbiwa nema kweKhomishini yeLizanyisiwa kweBanhu beTjilume neBanhu beTjikadzi yeZimbabwe

(1) KunaKhomishini inoozibgwa seKhomishini yeLizanyisiwa kweBanhu beTjilume neBanhu beTjikadzi yeZimbabwe ina—

(a)

nghalitulo wabigwa neNtungamili weHango atjizuwisana neKhomithi yeMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile; ne

(b)

dzimwe nhengo dzilihanhakadzi, dzina nhengo dzili—

(i) kunotondeka dzakabigwa neNtungamili weHango edzihala mumazina ebanhu basipasi kwegumi nekubili bakathangiwa akahhisiwa neKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile; ne

(ii) un’ompela wakathangiwa neLubahhe gwaboHe gweHango, akabigwa neNtungamili weHango.

(2) Nhengo dzeKhomishini yebunhukadzi nebunhulume yeZimbabwe dzakafanila hagwa netha yebe nelebeswa kwadzo neluzibo gwadzo newhisisa kwadzo ndebo dzebunhulume nebunhukadzi mutjaba, muna zwemilenje, zwefumwa yehango nemunazwepolitiki, kakale betjikadzi nebetjilume bakafanila migwa kwakalizana muKhomishini iyeyo. 246 Mihingo yeKhomishini yeLizanyisiwa kweBanhu beTjilume neBanhu beTjikadzi yeZimbabwe Khomishini yeBunhukadzi neBunhulume yeZimbabwe inamihingo inotebela—

(a)

yelinga ndebo dzakalingisana nelizana kwebetjilume nebetjikadzi kudza ibe nelebeswa lekuti betjikadzi banolizana nebetjilume selondesedzewa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu;

(b) yehakisisa pakungabe kwatjulukiwa ndulamilo dzinondilana nebunhukadzi nebunhulume;

(c)

yewamutjila nelingisisa n’un’unukunobva kutjaba nethama kwayinobona kwakafanila nekwen’un’unaikoko;

(d) yethama hakuludzo nekwezwinhu zwinondilana nekwe bunhulume nebunhukadzi nedzinondilana nekwewan’wa kwendulamo netjaba, nepa mano kuti kuhandugwe milayo nezwiyeto zwinohhisa luhalawulo pabukadzi nebulume;

(e)

yepa mano kumakubungano etjaba nemakubungano akazwimila kudza atubule lizanisiwa kwebetjikadzi nebetjilume;

(f)

yehhisa ndongoloso dzinobhatika kudza kube nekulizana kwebetjilume nebetjikadzi;

(g) yesusuma kuti banhu banotjuluka ndulamilo dzinondilana nebunhulume nebunhukadzi batilikiwe milandu yebudzudzu;

(h) yebakanya (nlipo) pakwatjulukiwa ndulamilo dzinondilana nebunhulume nebunhukadzi; ne

(i)

thama kose kunohakika kudza kube nekulizana kwebetjikadzi nebetjilume.

247 Nlondesedzo dzinotiwa neKhomishini yeLizanyisiwa kweBanhu beTjilume neBanhu beTjikadzi yeZimbabwe Pehhugwi kwelondesedzo yayakafanila hhisa seleba kwendima 323, Khomishini yeBunhukadzi neBunhulume yeZimbabwe ingandisa nlondesedzo kuPhalamente ihingisa Minisita wakafanila nekwe ndebo dzinondilana nebunhulume nebunhukadzi dzayinobona dzakafanila kuti dzizibisiwe Phalamente.

LUBABI 5 KHOMISHINI YEYENDESEWA KWENDEBO YEZIMBABWE 248 Bumbiwa nema kweKhomishini yeyendesewa kweNdebo yeZimbabwe

(1) Kunakhomishini yakafanila zibgwa seKhomishini yeyendesewa kweNdebo yeZimbabwe ina—

(a)

nghalitulo wabigwa neNtungamili weHango hule kwelebesana neKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile; ne

(b) bamwe banhu balihhanakadzi babigwa neNtungamili weHango ebatola mumazina ebanhu bathangiwa asipasi kwegumi nekubili ahhisiwa neKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile.

(2) Nhengo dzeKhomishini yeyendesewa kweNdebo yeZimbabwe dzakafanila haligwa benelebeswa kwadzo nematatabuko adzo munazwebhakhwa kwebanhu neluzibo gwadzo nekwendebo dzendulamilo dzebanhu nezwiyeto zwakanaka munazweyendesewa kwendebo. 249 Mihingo yeKhomishini yeyendesewa kweNdebo yeZimbabwe

(1) Khomishini yeyendesewa kweNdebo yeZimbabwe inamihingo inotebela—

(a)

yemilidza, susuma nepfumbidza lusununguko munazweyendesewa kwendebo;

(b) yesusuma nehingisa zila dzakatambunuka nebunhu muyendesewa kwendebo;

(c)

yelinga zwinodusiwa pabanhu kuti zondilana nekunohakiwa netjaba, kwazo kwazo, ibe nelebeswa lekuti kuna kulizana nesiyana siyana kwemikumbulo inomila zwitjaba zweZimbabwe zose;

(d) kulumbidza yetiwa kwezila dzehinga kwebanhu banohinga munazweyendesiwa kwendebo kakale,apo kusina zila idzedzo, dzitiwe kakale dzihingisiwe;

(e)

yewamutjila nelinga n’un’unakunobva kubanhu kakale, pakatambunuka, ithame kwakafanila kubahaki bendebo nebamwe banhu muyendesewa kwendebo kene mudusiwa kwendebo bangabe bawan’wa batjuluka nlayo kene zila dzehinga dzabakafanila tobela;

(f)

yebe nelebeswa lekuti banhu beZimbabwe banoswikilila luzibo kwakalizana kakale kwakakwana;

(g) yekulumidza hingisiwa nepfumbidziwa kwendimi dzose dzimunlayo muZimbabwe;

(h) yekulumidza hingisiwa kwethekinoloji ntshwa muyendesewa kwendebo nepandisiwa kweluzibo;

(i)

yesusuma ngwa yakalizana nesiyana siyana muyendesewa kwendebo;ne

(j)

thama hakuludzo nekwendebo dzinondilana nekwensununguko munazweyendesewa kwendebo nezwilayidza, kakale munayikoko isusume handugwa kwenlayo.

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingatuma Khomishini yeyendesewa kweNdebo yeZimbabwe kuti—

(a)

ithame hakuludzo nelingisisa—

(i) muzwiyeto kene mamo anonga asingabhate zwibuyanana lusununguko munazweyendesewa kwendebo; ne

(ii) mamo eyendisiwa kwendebo; ne

(b) tola nhambo kene hhisa zila yelanga banoyendisa ndebo betjuluka nlayo kene mazwibhato abakafanila tobela.

(3) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila lawula yendesewa kwendebo. 250 Nlondesedzo dzeKhomishini yeyeNdesewa kweNdebo yeZimbabwe Pehhugwi kwelondesedzo inohakika seleba kwendima 323, Khomishini yeyeNdesewa kweNdebo yeZimbabwe, kakale ihingisa Minisita wakafanila, ingandisa nlondesedzo kuPhalamente nekwe yendesewa kwendebo, dzinobe dzabongwa neKhomishini kuti dzakafanila zibisiwa Phalamente. LUBABI 6 KHOMISHINI YELUNYALALO NELEKEDZELANA KWETJABA

251 Bumbiwa nema kweKhomishini yeLunyalalo neLekedzelana kweTjaba

(1) Hule kwelubaka katjipinda makole, kuna Khomishini inoyi Khomishini yeLunyalalo neLekedzelana kweTjaba ina—

(a)

nghalitulo wakabigwa neNtungamili weHango hule kwezuwisana neKhomishini inolinga nezn’hingo wedziKhuta neKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile; ne

(b) bamwe banhu balihhanakadzi bakabigwa neNtungamili weHango ebatola mumazina ebanhu basipasi kwegumi nebabili bakathangiwa bekahhisiwa neKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile.

(2) Nghalitulo weKhomishini yeLunyalalo neLekedzelana kwetjaba wakafanila banhu wakathama zwidiyo zwinobvuma kakale unamakole asipasi kwegunotendekaelimilili wenlayo muZimbabwe.

(3) Hapa bigwa kwenghalitulo weKhomishini yeLunyalalo neLekedzelana kweTjaba kwakaleyana nekunohakiwa neKhomishini inolinga nezwedziKhuta, Ntungamili weHango wakafanila thama kuti Khomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimileizibisiwe tjinyolotjo.

(4) Nhengo dzeKhomishini yeLunyalalo neLekedzelana kweTjaba dzakafanila bigwa nentha yeba nelebeswa neluzibo gwabo newhisisa kwabo ndebo dzelekedzelana kwebanhu, hanginyila kwebanhu, kudzibilila sawililana, nepedza sawililana kwakulipo nemilidzila lunyalalo. 252 Mihingo yeKhomishini yeLunyalalo neLekedzelana kweTjaba Khomishini yeLunyalalo neLekedzelana kweTjaba inamihingo inotebela—

(a)

banemalebeswa kuti nlayo unohinga kuti kupele sawililana, kubenetilana mazwibhatigwa neLekedzelana;

(b) bamba ndongoloso dzekudza tilana mazwibhatigwa kwetjaba, bhatana neyendidzana muZimbabwe nezila inakunyalala yetambunudza sawililana;

(c)

thama kuti kube nekuLekedzelana mutjaba tjose nekuludzila kuti banhu balebe lebeswa nekwakatikala ntolo nebhatshapakuLekedzelana kwebanhu nekuti nlayo utole nhambo dzawo;

(d) bamba ndongoloso nemakubungano etjaba tjose ebhatsha pakulebelesana kwemakubungano ezwepolitiki, zwitjaba, makubungano ne amwe mabunga, kudza kudzibiligwe sawhisisana nesawililana kungatikala mulubaka gunohha;

(e)

bamba ndongoloso dzinoota kuti banhu bakahunguludziwa, whisiwa zogwadza nedzimwe nhambo dzedziyidziwa bawane lapiwa nebhatshiwa kuti babe mutjimo tjakalulwama;

(f)

wamutjila nelinga n’un’unakunobva kutjaba nelanga pakafanila;

(g) bumba zila dzenyaya nekulibilidzamuntha dzingaba nesawililana nesawhisisana, netola nhambo dzedzibilila dzakatambunuka;

(h) thama zwinuzwinondidzana nedzibilila sawililana nekudza kunyalala;

(i)

lebeleka netonga sawililana muzwitjaba, mumakubungano, mumabunga nemubanhu; ne

(j)

kuludzila nlayo unothama kuti kubene bhatsho, kuhanganyila newhalo, linopiwa banhu bakaba nekukubadzika nentha yesawililana, ngwele kene amwe mamo bo.

253 Nlondesedzo yeKhomishini yeLunyalalo neLekedzelana kweTjaba Pehhugwi kwelondesedzo inohakika seleba kwendima 323, Khomishini yeLunyalalo neLekedzelana kweTjaba, kakale ihingisa Minisita wakafanila, ingandisa nlondesedzo kuPhalamente nekwelungalalo neLekedzelana kwetjaba, dzinobe dzabongwa neKhomishini kuti dzakafanila zibisiwa Phalamente NDIMA 13 NHABI DZEGWISA BUPITIPITI NETJINYA LUBABI 1 KHOMISHINI YEGWISA BUPITIPITI YEZIMBABWE

254 Bumbiwa nema kweKhomishini yeGwisa Bupitipiti yeZimbabwe

(1) KunaKhomishini inoozibgwa seKhomishini yeGwisa Bupitipti yeZimbabwe ina—

(a)

nghalitulo wabigwa neNtungamili weHango hule kwezuwisana neKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile; ne

(b) bamwe banhu balihhanakadzi (8) bakabigwa neNtungamili weHango ebatola mumazina ebanhu basipasi kwegumi nebabili (12) bakathangiwa bekahhisiwa neKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile.

(2) Nhengo dzeKhomishini yeGwisa Bupitipiti yeZimbabwe dzakafanila haligwa banelebeswa kwadzo neluzibo gwadzo nelubaka gwadzatjihinga bhakhwa kene sendedzela kene hakisisa nezwebudzudzu kene kuti bakafanila bigwa koga, ne—

(a)

un’ompela wabo wakafanila banewhalo dzehinga semilili wenlayo muZimbabwe, kakale abe newhalo idzedzo kwemakole asipasi kwe alikunotendeka;

(b) un’ompela wabo wakafanila banewhalo dzehinga se akhawuntenti kene mbonyololi wetjaba muZimbabwe, kakale abe newhalo idzedzo kwemakole asipasi kwe alikunotendeka; ne

(c)

un’ompela wabo wakafanila be nhu unamakole aligumi elimun’hingo wehakisisa butjinyi.

255 Mihingo yeKhomishini yeGwisa Bupitipiti yeZimbabwe

(1) Khomishini yeGwisa Bupitipiti yeZimbabwe inamihingo inotebela—

(a)

kuti ihakisise kakale ise patjena bupitipitiguyapo mumabhizinesi etjaba kene akazwimila oga;

(b) kuti igwise bupitipiti, bubava,sahinga nezila yakatambunuka, hingisa masimba nezila isakatambunuka neamwe mazwibhato asakafanila mumabhizinesi etjaba neakazwimila oga;

(c)

kuludzila ba nelebeswa, bhakhwa zwibuyanana kwedzimari nebutjena mu mabhizinesi etjaba ne akazwimila oga;

(d) wamutjila nelinga nónóno dzinobva kubanhu netola nhambo dzakafanila dzebakanya nónóno idzedzo nezila yayinobona yakatambunuka.

(e)

yeyendesa Nlawuli Nkulu weMapholisa kuti ahakisise ndebo dzebupitipiti nekuti andise londesedzo kuKhomishini nekwabhuda pahakisisa;

(f)

kuti indise ndebo kuLubahhe gweSekesa Milandukuti kutilikiwe milandu;

(g) ikumbile jidzo kuMapholisa nedzimwe nhabi dzeHango dzinohakisisa ndebo; kakale

(h) pa mano kuHulumente nebamwe banhu nenhambo dzingatogwa kuti banhu babe nelebeswa kakale babe nenlandu nen’hingo wabo kakale idzibilile mazwibhato asakalulwama mutjaba nekuna bemabhizinesi bakazwimila.

(2) Nlawuli nkulu weMapholisa wakafanila tobela zwaanobudzwa neKhomishini yeZimbabwe yegwisa bupitipiti selondesedzewa mundimana (1)(e).

(3) Hulumente yakafanila hingisa nlayo nedzimwe zila kuti ibe nelebeswa lekuti Khomishini yeGwisa Bupitipiti yeZimbabwe inasimba lebvumila sungwa netilikiwa nlandu kwebanhu bangabe besolegwa banebupitipiti, basingahingise masimba kumunlayo neamwe mazwibhato asakalulwama anotongwa neKhomishini. 256 Hingisiwa kwe amwe matama eDanho 12 muKhomishini yeGwisa Bupitipiti yeZimbabwe Ndima 234, 235, 236 nendima 237 dzohinga Khomishini yeGwisa Bupitipiti yeZimbabwe nenhengo dzayo sekunoyi iKhomishini yakazwimila yabambiwa neDanho 12. 257 Nlondesedzo dzeKhomishini yeGwisa Bupitipiti yeZimbabwe Hule kwehhisa londesedzo yayakafanila hhisa seleba kwendima 323, Khomishini yeGwisa Bupitipiti yeZimbabwe ingandisa dzimwe londesedzo, ihingisa Minisita wakafanila, kuPhalamente dzinoleba nekwemazwibhato asakalulwama mutjaba nemunabemabhizinesi bakazwimilila ayinobona akafanila zibisiwa Phalamente. LUBABI 2 LUBAHHE GWESEKESA MILANDU

258 Bumbiwa kweLubahhe gweSekesa miLandu nemihingo yago KunaLubahhe gweSekesa Milandu kwakalinga tanga nesekesa milandu lakamilila hango kakale lidusa mihingo inohakika kene isingahakike kusekisa milandu.

259 Nsekesi Nkulu nebamwe bahingi

(1) Kuna Nsekesi Nkulu unotungamila Lubahhe gweSekesa milandu.

(2) Bugalo gweNsekesi Nkulu bugalo gwetjaba koga akunongina muLutabi gwebano Hingila Hulumente.

(3) Nsekesi Nkulu unobigwa neNtungamili nobonisiwa neKhomishini inolinga nezwedziKhuta kutebegwa ndongoloso yobigiwa kwejaji wenlayo.

(4) Nsekesi Nkulu kwakafanila abe nhu unafanilo dzokuti abigwe sejaji muKhuta iPehhugwi.

(5) Lubaka gwegala mutulo kweNsekesi Nkulu makole matanhatu kakale kungapamulugwa kwegumwe lubaka gun’ompela.

(6) Esenhu ekatola bugalo, Nsekesi Nkulu wakafanila, tuna pasi kweNtungamili kene nhu wapiwa masimba neNtungamili, tuna kwebugalo kutebegwa kwakakwagwa Ndongoloso yebuTatu.

(7) Mamo akalingisana nodusiwa kwejaji wenlayo mubugalo dzondilana nedzinodusa Nsendedzi Nkulu mutulo.

(8) Tjimo tjohinga tjeNsekesi Nkulu, kuhanganila mbhayilo, kwakafanila kulebwe muTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente, koga mbhayilo wakafanila usijitisiwe nelubaka Nsekesi Nkulu etjahinga.

(9) Mari inobhadagwa Nsekesi Nkulu inobva muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente.

(10) Tjisungo tjeNlayo wePhalemente tjakafanila tjithame bunga linopa nh’ingo banhu banombhatsha Nsekesi Nkulu muhinga, kakale tjakafanila pa—

(a)

fanilo dzebanhu ibabo;

(b) tjimo tjohinga, zwibhata nemabhato ebanhu ibabo;

(c)

muthama mihingo yabo, banhu ibabo kwakafanila bazwimile nesaleleka notobela nlayo koga nelawugwa neNsekesi Nkulu;

(d) mbihho negubungana yeLubahhe gweSekesa Milandu; kakale

(e)

zila dzohinga dzakatambunuka nemamo eLubahhe gweSekesa Milandu.

(11) Nsekesi Nkulu engabudza Nlawuli Nkulu weMapholisa kuti ahakisise kakale ambudze kose, Nsekesi Nkulu engakukumbulila kuti inlandu watikala kene ungatikala, kakale Nlawuli Nkulu weMapholisa wakafanila atebele nekwafanela tikala. 260 Zwimila kweNsekesi Nkulu

(1) Seleba kweNlayo Nkulu, Nsekesi Nkulu—

(a)

yakazwimila nesatungamigwa kene lawugwa newumwe nhu; kakale

(b) kwakafanila hinga esakaleleka nesahla, sahalula, sakumbulila kwakayipa kene benga.

(2) Nsekesi Nkulu wakafanila leba nebudza tjaba zila dzaahalula kuti unohinga tjini mundongoloso yemilandu. 261 Zwibhata kwebahingi beLubahhe gweSekesa Milandu

(1) Nsekesi Nkulu nebanohinga muLubahhe gweSekesa Milandu bakafanila hinga betebela kumuNlayo Nkulu nenlayo.

(2) Akuna n’hingi weLubahhe gweSekesa Milandu wakafanila, kuti hapaa ehinga—

(a)

atole lubihho muna zwepolitiki;

(b) anginge njemulo dzelimwe gubungano lezwepolitiki;

(c)

benga zwinangwa zwimunlayo zweyimwe gubungano lezwepolitiki kene baka; kene

(d) tjuluka ndulamilo dzakazinda kene nsununguko dzebanhu bose.

(3) Banohingila Lubahhe gweSekesa Milandu abazofanila kuti babe nhengo kene bahingi begubungano lezwepolitiki kene limwe gubungano.

(4) Tjisungo TjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingathama dzimwe zila dzinobhatsha bahingi beLubahhe gweSekesa Milandukuti basilelekele mugubungano lezwepolitiki. 262 Nsekesi Nkulu wakafanila palondesedzo kuPhalamente gole negole Nsekesi Nkulu wakafanila yendesa nlondesedzo yegole negole yemihingo nekwatiwa ndeLubahhe gweSekesa Milandu kuPhalamente, ehingisa Minisita wakafanila, nlondesedzo inondisiwa kusanhu kukapinda mimwedzi mitanhatu mugole linotebela gole lekunolebwa mulondesedzo. 263 Amwe masimba esekesa milandu Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingapa masimba osekesa nlandu mubanhu basiLubahhe gweSekesa Milandu, koga masimba ihhawo akuzofanila aganhule kene agwise masimba eLubahhe gweSekesa Milandu kutebela lubabi igogu. DANHO 14 HULUMENTE WEMATUNHU NEWEMITUNHU

TJIWALILO Saku kuyemugwa kuti:

(a) kulondolodzewe bhatana kweTjaba muZimbabwe nedzibilila kose sabhatana nepaladzana;

(b)

banhu bose netjaba tjose tjeZimbabwe tjibhatike muhulumente; nekuti

(c)

kube nekobewa kwezwiwanikwa zwehango kwakalizana nebhatikana kwebanhu muhala kwakafanila tiwa kutanga mupfumbidza mitunhu yabo;

kwakafanila kube nekobewa kwemasimba nemihingo kutila teledza mbihho dzahulumente weZimbabwe. LUBABI 1 TJIWALILO 264 Andamisiwa kwemasimba aHulumente nemihingo iye

(1) Hapa kwakalulama, masimba ahulumente nemihingo yakafanila andamisiwa mumakhansili ematunhu nematoropo nehulumente wemitunhu inaluzibo gothama mihingo iyeyo nezila yakatambunuka ndeyina bhudililo.

(2) Zwinangwa zwe andamisa masimba ahulumente nemihingo mumakhansili ematanhu nematoropo nehulumente wemitunhu zoti—

(a)

pa masimba banhu otungamila hulumente wemitunhu nemilidza bhatikana mumasimba eButungamili gweHango nethama zwisungo zwakalingisana nabo;

(b) kulumidzila zwitonga, tikala, butjena, zwisa nlandu nelebeswa muhulumente yeZimbabwe yose;

(c)

kulondosedza nesusuma lunyalalo, bhatana kwehango nesapalalana kweZimbabwe;

(d)

milidza ndulamilo dzetjaba kuti batonge zwakalingisana nabo nendisala mbeli pfumbiwa kwabo;

(e)

iba nelebeswa lolizanisa kobewa kwezwiwanikwa zwemitunhu nezwehango; ne

(f)

kobewa kwemihingo nezwiwanikwa zwinobva muhulumente wehango kudze kuti kubumbiwe khwama tjemari tjakasima tjemakhansili ematanhu nematoropo wose nemitunhu.

265 Zila dzehinga kwaHulumente weMatunhu neweMitunhu

(1) Makhansili ematunhu nematoropo nehulumente wemitunhu yakafanila, mkati kwemiganhulo yawo—

(a)

bakanya kuti kube netungamila kunandulamokwakatambunuka ndoba nebhudilo, tjena, zwisa nlandu nebhatana kwegubungano;

(b) hinga mihingo inolebgwa neNlayo Nkulu kene Tjisungo TjeNlayo tjePhalamente koga;

(c)

hinga nezila isinganginilile muhango, mihingo kene bunhu begubungano yedzimwe mbihho dzahulumente;

(d) whililana nebamwe, kwazo kwazo—

(i) budza bamwe, nekumbila mano mubamwe, mundebo dzakalingisana nabo;

(ii) hanganilana nehingisana mumihingo yabo;

(e)

londosedza lunyalalo, bhatana kwehango nesapalalana kweZimbabwe;

(f)

dzibilila pfuma yetjaba; kakale

(g) ba nelebeswa nemiligwa kwakalizana kwebanhu mumitunhu yabanamasimba.

(2) Dzose nhengo dzehulumente wemitunhu dzakafanila halugwa nebanhu bakakwalisa vota mumitunhu iyeyo kunahulumente wemitunhu wakabumbiwa.

(3) Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila hhula mikoba nezila dzehingidzana pakati kwehulumente, makhansili ezwitjaba nemakhinsili ematoropo nemakhansili emitunhu. 266 Mazwibhato ebahingi baHulumente wematunhu newemitunhu

(1) Bahingi bemakhansili ematunhu nemakhinsili ematoropo nemakhansili emitunhu bakafanila tobelaNlayo Nkulu neyimwe milayo.

(2) Akuna n’hingi wemakhansili ematunhu nemakhinsili ematoropo nemakhansili emitunhu, mukuhinga kukwe, anga—

(a) batole lubihho gwelimwe gubungano lezwepolitiki;

(b) bhatika pakumilidzila zwinohakwa nemakubungano ezwepolitiki;

(c)

tshambidzila hinga kwakatambunuka kumunlayo kwemakubungano ezwepolitiki; kene

(d) thubula ndulamilo kene nsununguko dzemwe nemwe nhu.

(3) Bahingi bemakhansili ematunhu nemakhinsili ematoropo nemakhansili emitunhu habazofanila kuti babe mubugalokene gwalipi gubungano lezwe politiki.

(4) Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila bakanya mamo anothama kuti bahingi bemakhansili ematunhu nemakhinsili ematoropo nemakhansili emitunhu basibhatike munazwepolitiki. LUBABI 2 MATUNHU NEMAKHANSILI EMATUNHU NEEMATOROPO

267 Matunhu nemitunhu yeZimbabwe

(1) Matunhu Zimbabwe yakapalalanyiwa ikaba iwo hawa—

(a)

Doropo lakaBulawayo;

(b) Doropo leHarare;

(c)

Dunhu leManicaland;

(d) Dunhu leMashonaland Central;

(e)

Dunhu leMashonaland East;

(f)

Dunhu leMashonaland West;

(g) Dunhu leMasvingo;

(h) Dunhu leMatabeleland North;

(i)

Dunhu leMatabeleland South;

(j)

Dunhu leMidlands;

tjinanghano dzakatjenesewa muTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente—

(a)

tjakafanila bumba nlayo weganhugwa kwematunhu etiwa mitunhu; kakale

(b) tjakafanila bumba nlayo wepaladza nethama butshwa minghanhu yematunhu nemitunhu; hule nezuwisana neKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe nebanhu bematunhu nemitunhu iyeyo. 268 Makhansili eMatunhu

(1) Limwe nelimwe dunhu lina khansili yalo, kuhhe kwematoropo, makhansili ematunhu ana—

(a)

nghalitulo wekhansili, wakahagwa kutebegwa ndima 272;

(b)

masenetha akahagwa mumatunhu ihawo;

(c)

boSenetha baliboHe babili bakahagwa mumatunhu kutebegwa zwinolebwa nendima 120(1)(b);

(d)

ntungamili ne Ntebedzeli wentungamili we Lubahhe gwaboHe gwehango yose, hapa ntha yabo ilimudanho ilelo;

(e)

bose baMilili bemiTunhu uPhalamente banamitunhu yabo imuzwitjaba izwezo;

(f)

banhu betjikadzi bakahagwa sebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente kutebegwa ndima 124(1)(b) kumitunhu iyeyi;

(g)

mameyara nenghalitulo, kene naapi mazina abonadan’wa nawo, dzematoropo kene dzemitunhu mumatunhu ihawo;

(h) nebanhu baligumi bakahagwa nemigwa kwakalizana kunobviwa mundimana (3).

(2) Nhu ufanilo dzehagwa kuti angine mukhansili yedunhu kutebegwa zwinolebwa nendimana (1)(h) hapa efanila hagwa kuti abeMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente.

(3) Halulo dzemakhansili ezwitjaba dzakafanila yetiwa nezila inotobela Nlayo weHalulo, wakafanila be nelebeswa lekuti banhu balebgwa mundimana (1)(h) banohagwa kuhingisiwa ndongoloso yepartnlongoloso yemigwa kwakalizana—

(a)

kunda nevotegwa kwebamilili bemakubungano ezwepolitiki mudunhu labakamilila muhalulo yeBamilili beMitunhu muPhalamente; ne

(b) mazina akwagwe etobelelana, lenhu wetjilume litobegwa ndelenhu wetjikadzi, mazina ose etanga ne ebanhu betjikadzi.

(4) Bugalo gwenhengo yekhansili yematunhu gunolebgwa mu—

(a)

tjikamu (b), (c), (e) kene (f) tjendimana (1) gobe gusinanhu hapa nhengo iyeyo ingasiya bugalo gwayo muPhalamente;

(b) tjikamu (g) tjendimana (1) goba gusitjigegwe hapa nhengo ingaletja baMeyara kene nghalitulo wekhansili yematunhu mutjaba gunolebgwa;

(c)

tjikamu (h) tjendimana (1) tjobe tjisisina nhu hapa kungatikala kunolebgwa mundima 129, sekunoyi nhengo yayili Milili weMitunhu muPhalamente.

269 Makhansili eMatoropo

(1) Mumatoropo ose kunakhansili yedoropo ina—

(a)

sekaBulawayo, kuna Meyara weDoropo lakaBulawayo ali iye Nghalitulo weKhansili yeDoropo lakaBulawayo;

(b) sekuHarare—

(i) Meyara weDoropo leHarare, ali iye Nghalitulo weKhansili yeDoropo leHarare,

(ii) neMeyara kene nghalitulo wekhansili yematoropo iyebubili bukulu, mutjaba itjetjo, unobe aliNtebedzeli wenghalitulo weKhansili yeDoropo leHarare;

(c)

Bamilili bemitunhu muPhalamente bose banamitunhu yabo ikusi kwedoropo ilelo;

(d) Bamilili bemitunhu muPhalamente betjikadzi bahalugwa seleba kwendima 124(1)(b) mudoropo ilelo;

(e)

Masenetha ahalugwa mudoropo ilelo; ne

(f)

maMeyara neBatobedzeli bemaMeyara neghalitulo kene Batobedzeli bebagalitulo, nedan’wa kwabo, emakhansili ose mudoropo ilelo.

(2) Bugalo gwenhu umukhansili gunolebgwa mu—

(a)

tjikamu (a) kene (b) tjendimana (1) gobe gusigegwe hapa nhu iwhoyo esitjiMeyara, Ntobedzeli waMeyara kene nghalidulo, sekunobe kuliyapo;

(b) tjikamu (c), (d) kene (e) tjendimana (1) gobe gusitjina nhu hapa nhu engabva mubugalo gugwe muPhalamente.

(c)

tjikamu (f) tjendimana (1) gobe gusitjina nhu hapa nhu wabe elimubugalo angaletja kuba Meyara, Ntobedzeli waMeyara, nghalitulo kene Ntebedzeli wenghalitulo, sekunobe kuliyapo, gwekhansili edoropo ilelo.

270 Mihingo emakhansili ematunhu neematoropo

(1) Khansili yematunhu kene yedoropo yakalingisana nepfumbila kwetjaba munazwegalisana nemunazwefumwa, kuhanganyisila—

(a)

yeta nehingisa ndongoloso dzepfumbidza dunhu mugalisana kwebanhu nepfumwa milili;

(b) tungamila nehingisa ndongoloso dzaHulumente mudunhu;

(c)

haka nehingisa nhambo dzelondolodzewa, pfumbidziwa nebhakhwa kwezwiwan’wa mudunhu;

(d) susuma kumbiwa nebanhu mutjaba, neyeta zwinhu zwinookubhudililisa ikoku;

(e)

linga hingisiwa kwezwiwan’wa mudunhu lawo; ne

(f)

yeta imwe mihingo, kuhanganyisila mihingo yezwenlayo, ingabe yatilikiwa munayiyo ne kene kusi kweTjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente.

(2) Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila linga bumbiwa, zwibakwa nebahingi bemakhansili ematunhu ne ematoropo, ilinge kakale kose nezila yabanohinga nayo.

(3) Banhu banohingila makhansili ematunhu kene matoropo bananlandu, bose, kene umwe nemwe, kubagali betjaba tjabo naHulumente weHango mumihingo yabo. 271 MaKhomithi emakhansili ematunhu Kudze bahinge kwakatambunuka, Makhansili ematunhu nematoropo enga bumba, makhomithi koga imwe neyimwe khomithi yakafanila tungamigwa nenhengo yalebwa mundima 268(1)(h) kene 269(1)(h), kundilana nekuti nikupi. 272 Nghalitulo dzemakhansili ematunhu

(1) Kwakunogagwa kwekutanga kwatjipela halulo yebanhu bose, khansili yematunhu yakafanila halula nghalitulo mumazina ebanhu bengaba babili benafanilo abva mu—

(a)

gubungano lezwepolitiki lawana banhu banjinji kupinda amwe banomilila mitunhu muPhalamente mudanho ilelo; kene

(b)

pa kusinagubungano lezwepolitiki selebwa kutjikama (a), gubungano lezwepolitiki yawana mavoti manjinji kupinda amwe mudunhu muhalulo yebanhu bose yebaMilili baMitunhu muPhalamente.

(2) Nhu unafanilo yohalugwa senghalitulo wekhansili yedunhu kene enafanilo yohalugwa senhengo yeSenethi.

(3) Bugalo gwenghalitulo wekhansili yedunhu ndekwebugalo yetjaba koga ayitoba pasi kwelutabi gweBanohingila Hulumente.

(4) Esanhu ekatanga hinga, nghalitulo wekhansili yedunhu wakafanila tuna kunkwalili wekhansili yetjaba elayidza zwipila muhingo sekumuNdongoloso yebuTatu.

(5) Nghalitulo wekhansili yedunhu angasiya n’hingo ndobika siya kukwe n’hingo mukhansili yedunhu.

(6) Nghalitulo wekhansili yedunhu wakafanila bhuda mubugalo—

(a)

nehhuba khansili yedunhu kwayinohangana kwekutanga kwatjipela halulo yebanhu bose;

(b) pa ekadusiwa kuti abe nhengo yekhansili yedunhu;

(c)

hapa kukaba netjibvumilano tjodusa nghalitulo wemakhansili etjaba mubugalo nemanhengo ekhansili yedunhu asipasi kwembabi mbili munhatu dzenhengo zdemakhansili wose; kene

(d) pa edusiwa mubugalo neLubahhe gunolebwa mundimana (7).

(7) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila thama kuti kubumbiwe Lubahhe gwakazwimila kuti guhinge mihingo yodusa banghalitulo bemakhansili ematunhu mubugalo, koga dusiwa kwabo kwakafanila kube muzwibaka zwinoti—

(a)

kongwa thama mihingo yebugalo gwabo nen’tha yobugwele gwen’holo kene khubazeka;

(b) sahinga nenzila yakatambunuka;

(c)

tjinya kwazo;

(d)

sungigwa nlandu whosaba nelebeswa, bupombgwe kene hingisa masimba ohinga zwakayipa; kene

(e)

tjuluka nlayo mwaza, kuhanganila nlayo wemitunhu.

(8) Nghalitulo wekhansili yetjaba katosiya bugalo kuhhe kwezwibaka zwimundima iyeyi. 273 Whoku nawhokuya kwakalingisana nemakhansili ematunhu neematoropo

(1) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila thama, kunondilana neDanho ileli, mubumbiwa nehinga kwemakhansili ematunhu neematoropo kakale, kwakalingisiwa—

(a)

ndongoloso dzemakhansila ematunhu neematoropo;
