(b) mihingo yenghalitulo dzemakhansili ematunhu neematoropo;

(c)

tjimo tjohinga tjenhengo dzemakhansili ematunhu neematoropo; kakale

(d) bigwa, tjimo tjehinga nedusiwa kwebahingi mumakhansili ematunhu neematoropo.

(2) Nlayo weHalulo wakafanila thama, kundilana neDanho ileli, pa kuhalugwa kwebanhu muzwitulo zwenhengo dzemakhansili ematunhu sekumundima 268(1)(f) nemubugalo gwenghalitulo dzemakhansili ematunhu, kuhalugwa kwebanhu begadziwa muzwitulo kwakafanila tiwa—

(a)

nebanhu bamugubungano lezwepolitiki lin’ompela nebakabenabugalo; kakale

(b) kuhhe kwenghalitulo, nenhu unabunhulume nebunhukadzi bunoyendelana newasiya. LUBABI 3 HULUMENTE WEMITUNHU

274 Hulumente wemitunhu mumatoropo

(1) Kuna hulumente wemitunhu mumatoropo unomila netungamila zwakalingisana nebanhu bakumatoropo muhango yose yeZimbabwe.

(2) Hulumente wemitunhu mumatoropo unotungamigwa nemakhansili anohanganila makhasila ahalugwa nebanhu bakakwalisa vota mumatoropo ihawoo

kakale elingwa nemameyara ahalugwa kene nghalitulo, kunda nekuti ndilipi zina linohingisawa.

(3) Mabunga akasiyana ehulumente wemitunhu engabumbiwa etigwa ntha dzakasiyana mumatoropo, kakale ntha dzakasiyana mutoropo dzimbili kene dzinjinji dzingatungamigwa nebunga lin’ompela lehulumente wemitunhu.

(4) Fanilo nendongoloso dzohalula banhu banobviwa mundinyana (2) dzakafanila lebgwa muNlayo weHalulo.

(5) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingapa Meyara kene nghalitulo wehulumente wemitunhu kumatoropo masimba eButungamili, koga meyara kene nghalitulo upi nawupi wapiwa masimba iyawo wakafanila kuti ahalugwe patjena nebanhu bakakwalisa vota muntha iyeyo kwakabumbiwa hulumente wemitunhu. 275 Hulumente wemitunhu yekumakanyi

(1) Kuna hulumente wemitunhu yakanyi, yakabumbiwa kutebegwa ndima iyeyi, kumila netungamila zwakalingisana nebanhu baakanyi.

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila thama kuti—

(a)

kubumbiwe hulumente wemitunhu yakanyi;

(b) kuhalugwe, nebakakwalisa vota bentha dzakanyi, kwemakhansili kuti atungamile zwakalingisana nehulumente wemitunhu selebgwa mutjikamu

(a);

(c)

kuhalugwe nghalitulo, bedangwa kene nalipi zina, kuti batungamile makhansili selebgwa mutjikamu (b); ne

(d) fanilo dzenhengo dzekhansili dzalebgwa mutjikamu (b).

(3) Mabunga akasiyana ehulumente wemitunhu engabumbiwa etigwa ntha dzakanyi dzakasiyana, kakale ntha dzakasiyana dzakanyi mbili kene dzinjinji dzingatungamigwa nahulumente wemitunhu un’ompela. 276 Mihingo yabo hulumente bemitunhu

(1) Kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kene tjipi, hulumente wemitunhu unandulamilo yetungamila, nezila iye, zwakalingisana nebanhu bemitunhu muntha iyeyo yabumbigwa iwhoyo hulumente wemitunhu, kakale unamasimba wose wothama kwakafanila tiwa.

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingapa hulumente wemitunhu mihingo, kuhanganila—

(a) masimba othama milayo yemitunhu, ndongoloso kene mipanga yetungamila kwakatambunuka muntha idzedzo dzabumbigwa hulumente wemitunhu.

(b) masimba ohaka mari mubanhu nemiketisokakale bewane zila dzowana mari inothama kuti bahinge mihingo yabo nekwakalingiligwa kuti bakuthame. 277 Halulo yabohulumente bemitunhu

(1) Halulo yemakhansila ehulumente yemitunhu yakafanila kuti itiwe—

(a)

hapa kulihalulo yemameyara nemakhansila, kondidzana nehalulo yebamilili bemitunhu muPhalamente neNtungamili weHango;

(b) hapo kuna halulo, koga kusihalulo yebanhu bose, kuhakiwa halula nhu un’ompela kene banjinji, nekulibilidza kwatjilingisisiwa kwatjihhulika bugalo.

(2) Halulo yemameyara nenghalitulo dzehulumente wemitunhu, kusi mameyara kene nghalitulo dzaapiwa masimba eButungamili selebgwa kutebegwa ndima 274(5), yakafanila tiwa pakugala kwekutanga kwemakhansili kwatjipinda halulo yebanhu bose.

(3) Kuhhe kwekuti sekwakunolebgwa mundimana (2) kene Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente, mameyara, nghalitulo nemakhansila ehulumente wemitunhu banotola bugalo nehhuba lehanhalume kwatjibikiwa mbhudilo yehalulo yebanhu bose makhansila kwaakahagwa. 278 Lubaka gweba mutulo kwebamilili bemitunhu muhulumente wemitunhu

(1) Bugalo gwameyara, nghalitulo kene khansila wehulumente wemitunhu gohaya nhu selebgwa mundima129, sekungati wayelimilili wemitunhu muPhalamente, kunotondeka kuTjilebeleki kene Ntungamili weSenethimundima 129(1)(k) kohingisiwa sekunotondeka Minisita wakalingisana nehulumente wemitunhu.

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila bakanya lubahhe gwakazwimila gunohinga dusa mameyara, nghalitulo nemakhansela mubugalo, koga ikoku dusiwa mubugalo kotikala hakulikuti—

(a)

habatotubula hinga nentha yesatubula kwembili kene kwenkumbulo;

(b) hatoziba n’hingo;

(c)

habana mazwibhato;

(d) bawanika nlandu wesaba nelebeswa, bupitipiti kene hingisa bugalo nezila isakatambunuka; kene

(e)

kene tjuluka nlayo mwaza, kose nenlayo wetjaba.

(3) Meyara, nghalitulo kene khansela wentunhu hatojita mutulo kuhhe kwekuti kutebegwa ndima iyeyi. 279 Ndongoloso dzahulumente wemitunhu Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila bakanya ndongoloso dzinotebegwa nemakhansili emitunhu. DANHO 15 BATUNGAMILI BEMABVILO

280 Butungamili gwemabvilo

(1) Banhu banozwisapasi kwegubungano, tjimo ne n’hingo we baTungamili wemabvilo pasi kwenlayo wemabvilo.

(2) Ntungamili wemabvilo wakafanila bhata mihingo yebuHe yakalingisana nemilenje, bunhu nemabvilo, 281 Bunhu gwakafanila tobegwa nebatungamili bemabvilo

(1) Batungamili bemabvilo bakafanila—

(a)

hinga betebela Nlayo Nkulu neyimwe milayo yeZimbabwe;

(b) tebela milenje yakalingisana neButungamili gwemabvilo neyihingisa kutila kuti gubungano lebatungamili bemabvilo lilemekedzewe neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu; kakale

(c)

bhata zwitjaba zwabo nekwakalizana.

(2) Batungamili bemabvilo habazofanila—

(a)

banhengo dzemakubungano ezwepolitiki kene bhatika munazwepolitiki;

(b) thama sebanhu banalubihho;

(c)

bhatika pakumilidzila zwinohakwa nemakubungano ezwepolitiki; kene

(d) tjuluka ndulamilo kene lusununguko gwewumwe nhu. 282 Mihingo yebatungamili bemabvilo

(1) Batungamili bemabvilo banamihingo inotebela muhango dzabanotungamila—

(a)

kudza nemilidza tjibanhu tjezwitjaba, kwazo kwazo, nekudza bunhu mumiha.

(b) tola nhambo dzedzibilila milenje, tjibanhu, mabvilo netshinde lemabvilo ezwitjaba zwabo, kose nentha dzinoyila;

(c)

bhatsha kuti hango ipfumbile;

(d) kutebegwa Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente, bona kuti hango inohingisiwa zwakanaka nedzibilila zose zwehango;

(e)

wangula sawililana pakati kwebanhu kutjaba kutebegwa nlayo wetjibanhu;

(f)

nethama imwe mihingo yabanobvumigwa kene yabanotilikiwa neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(2) Kuhhe kwekumuTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente, batungamili bemabvilo banasimba, ndawulo netungamila hango yose kene dzimwe ntha dzakabapiwa kuti batungamile, nebanhu bamuhango izedzo kene bamuntha idzedzo.

(3) Mukuhinga kwabo, batungamili bemabvilo habato lawugwa kene tungamigwa nenhu kene nesimba, kuhhe sekwakunolebgwa muTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(4) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila bakanya mamo anolisa zwiyeto ne mazwibhato zwebatungamili betjibanhu. 283 Bigwa nedusiwa kwebatungamili bemabvilo Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila bakanya mamo, anoyendelana nemilenje iyapo nayigogo lubaka, tjibanhu nezwiyeto zwezwitjaba izwezo ezwinotebela—

(a)

bigwa, misiwa, tola bugalo nedusiwa kwebatungamili bemabvilo;

(b) bambiwa nemisidzigwa kwebuhe;

(c)

nelulwamisiwa kwesawhanana nendebo dzebigwa, misiwa n’hingo, togwa kwebugalo nedusiwa mubugalo kwebatungamili bemabvilo; koga—

(i)

bigwa, dusiwa nemisiwa n’hingo kwaboHe kwakafanila tiwa neNtungamili weHango apiwa mano nelubahhe gwaboHe gwezwitjaba guhingisa Khansili yaboHe yehango naMinisita wakalingisana nebatungamili bemabvilo kutobegwa bunhu nembvilo dzetjaba itjetjo;

(ii) sawhanana mundebo dzebigwa, misiwa n’hingo nedusiwa kwebatungamili bemabvilo kwakafanila bankanyiwa neNtungamili weHango apiwa mano nelubahhe gwaboHe bezwitjaba ihingisa Minisita wakalingisana nebatungamili bemabvilo;

(iii) tjisungo tjakafanila hhisa nhambo dzeyeta lebeswa lekuti ndebo dzose dzolingiwa nekulizana kusingalingwe zwepolitiki;

(iv) tjisungo itjetji tjakafanila hhisa nhambo dzelondolodza bulebeswa gwemabunga ezwemabvilo nezwimila kwawo esingalinge zwepolitiki. 284 Mari dzinobhadagwa batungamili bemabvilo

(1) Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila kuti tjithame kuti mbhayilo nembuyizilo dzebatungamili bemabvilo dzidusiwe nebvumigwa neNtungamili weHango apiwa mano naMinisita wakalingisana nedzimari hule kwelebesana naMinisita wakalingisana nebatungamili bemabvilo.

(2) Mbhayilo wentungamili wemabvilo wakafanila lipiwa kakale udusiwe muKwama tjeMari yeHango.

(3) Mbhayilo wentungamili wemabvilo awuzofanila jitisiwa etjimun’hingo iwhoyo. 285 Lubahhe gwaboHe gwehango yose nembahhe dzaboHe mumatunhu

(1) KunaLubahhe gwaboHe gweHango yose gwakabumbiwa kutobegwa Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente, kuti gumile boHe bose muZimbabwe.

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayotjePhalamente tjakafanila tila matunhu ose, kuhhe kwematoropo, lubahhe gwaboHe gwezwitjaba gunaboHe bose mutjaba itjetjo.

(3) Sekungakhona tikala boHe bamuzwitjaba zose bakafanila migwa kwakalizana muLubahhe gwaboHe gweHango yose.

(4) Halulo dzeNtungamili weHango, Ntebedzeli weNtungamili nenhengo dzeLubahhe gwaboHe gweHango yose dzakafanila tiwa neKhomishini yeZimbabwe yehalulo.

(5) Mbahhe dzaboHe dzose muzwitjaba dzakafanila halula, dzitobela Nlayo weHalulo, boHe manomila tjaba muSenethi selondesedzewa mundima 120(1)(b).

(6) Ntungamili neNtebedzeli weNtungamili weLubahhe gwaboHe gwehango yose banohalugwa kuti bahinge kwelubaka gungapa makole alimahanu kakale bangahalugwa kakale kuti bahinge kwe amwe makole mahanu, koga nhu watongohinga kwelubaka gwemakole mahanu kabili aliNtebedzeli weNtungamili angahalugwa kuti abe Ntungamili.

(7) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila yeta kuti kube ne—

(a)

halulo dzaboHe banongina muLubahhe gwaboHe gwehango yose, nefanilo sesafanila kwebanhu banoohalugwa;

(b) tuna kunotiwa hapa nhu engina mun’hingo kwakafanila tiwa nebanhu bamuLubahhe gwaboHe gwehango yose neLubahhe gwaboHe muzwitjaba;

(c)

lubaka gweba mutulo kwenhengo dzeLubahhe gwaboHe gwehango yose;

(d) mbhayilo,pentjeni nedzimwe mbuyizilo dzeNtungamili, Ntebedzeli weNtungamili nenhengo kweLubahhe gwaboHe gwehango yose;

(e)

ndongoloso dzitobegwe mumihangano yeLubahhe gwaboHe gwehango yose nelubahhe gwaboHe muzwitjaba;ne

(f) bumbiwa kwebunga lebakwalili beLubahhe gwaboHe gwehango yose nembahhe dzaboHe muzwitjaba.

286 Mihingo yeLubahhe gwaboHe gwehango yose nembahhe dzaboHe mumatunhu

(1) Lubahhe gwaboHe gweHango yose, kakale mudunhu lago, lubahhe gwaboHe mumatunhu gunamihingo inotobela—

(a)

dzibilila, isusume kakale ipfumbidze milenje nembvilo dzeZimbabwe.

(b) mila mikumbulo yebatungamili bemabvilo nesimisa bunhu netjimo tjenhabi dzemabvilo;

(c)

dzibilila, susumisa nemilidza zwido zwetungamili bemabvilo;

(d) whilila n’un’unakwatiwakubatungamili bemabvilo nemigwa kwabayapo;

(e)

sapatjena netobegwa nezila yakatambunuka neyinabunhu zwibhata kwebatungamili bemabvilo nepfumbila Butungamili gwabo;

(f)

yebhatsha pedzasandilana pakati nekwakalingana nebatungamili bemabvilo;

(g) thama mihingo inobviwa kene inotilikiwa neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila kuti—

(a)

Lubahhe gwaboHe gweHango nembahhe dzabo He mumatunhu banotubula thama mihingo dzakazwimila nezila yakatambunuka; kakale

(b) banhu banohinga pasi kweLubahhe gwaboHe gwehango neMbahhe dzaboHe mumatunhu banohinga nezila yakatambunuka neyisangahalule. 287 Khomithi yakalingisana neLebeswa neBululwami Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila bhatsha mubumbiwa, bunhengo nendongoloso yeKhomithi yakalingisana neLebeswa neBululwami yaboHe, kuti bathame kunotebela—

(a)

pa pfimbisa netobegwa kwelebeswa nebululwami mumazwibhato ebatungamili bemabvilo;

(b) pa lulamisa sawhililana pakati kwebatungamili bemabvilo;

(c)

pa whilila notambunudza n’un’unakweebatungamili bemabvilo. DANHO 16 HANGO YELIMA NEPFUWA

288 Tjenesewa kweDanho 16

MuDanho ileli— “hango yelima nepfuwa” inatjeneso yayapiwa nendima 72. 289 Zila dzinotungamila ndongoloso yehango yelima nepfuwa Kutila lulamisa zila dzisina ndulamo nelizana muba mweni wemavu dzakaleziwa nebatungamili gohha, kakale nolezi kobewa kwemavu nekuti mawangwa ehango aswikiligwe nebanhu bose beZimbabwe kusahalugwana, ndongoloso dzemavu olima nopfuwa dzakafanila dzitungamigwe nezwilemela zwinotebela—

(a)

mavu tjiwangwa tjikulukulu tjinobumba pfuma inozibwa yebubvilo yebanhu bose beZimbabwe;

(b) pasi kwendima 72, wose nhu unabuzwagwa muZimbabwe una ndulamilo yowana, bhata, gala, hingisa, pindisa, tengesa kene letja ba nemavu olima nopfuwa kusinga lingwe ganda lonhu kene mbala;

(c)

piwa nekobewa kwemavu olima nopfuwa kwakafanila kusilelekele ntome nelizana, kwakalingiwa lizanisa bunhulume nebunhukadzi nesiyana nekunoyemugwa nezwitjaba;

(d) zila dzolubaka gohingisa mavu dzakafanila milidza pfumidzigwa nesa mari nebanhu mumavu olima nopfuwa;

(e)

hingisiwa kwe mavu o;ima nopfuwa kwakafanila milidza bapo kwezodliwa, wongeka zwibuyanana nezodliwa zwakatambunuka nethama mihingo, kudzibiligwa nebhakhwa zwakanaka kwehango kutigwa zwizwagwa zwinoha;

(f)

akuna nhu engatolegwa ndulamilo yiye kamwaza yohingisa nogala mumavu olima nopfuwa.

290 Ndilambeli kwendulamilo dzeHango muhango yelima nepfuwa

(1) Hango yose yelima nepfuwayaka—

(a)

siwa muNdongoloso 7 yeNlayo Nkulu wakapinda; kene

(b) kusenhu kukaswika hhuba lokutanga, kukawangwa pasi kwendima 16B(2)

(a)(ii) kene muNlayo Nkulu wakapinda; kundila mbeli kuhakisisiwa neHango.

(2) Sandilana pakati kwekumwe nekumwe kumkati—

(a)

mutjizibiso tjakakwagwa muNdongoloso 7 yeNlayo Nkulu wakapinda; kene

(b) tjizibiso tjinoleba nekwehango yelima nepfuwa kwatjidusiwa patjena kutebegwa ndima 16B(2)(a)(ii) kene (iii) muNlayo Nkulu nkulukugwi; nelukwalo gunolakidza mweni gunobviwa kene gwakafanila bviwa, kakale nesatambunuka kuyapo mutjizibiso tjakajalo, akutotjinyidzila n’hingo wendimana (1) kene paladza kuti mweni wehango yelima nepfuma inobviwandeyaHulumente wehango kutebegwa ndimana iyeyo. 291 Ndilambeli kwendulamilo dzebagali behango yelima nepfuwa Seleba kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, nhu wabe ehingisa kene egala kene akafanila hingisa kene gala hango yelima nepfuwa nentha yetjibvumilano neHango kusanhu kukapinda nsi, unondila mbeli enandulamilo yehingisa kene gala muhango iyeyo pahhuba letangisa hinga kene hule kwalo, sekunondilana netjibvumilano itjetjo. 292 Mamo ebvumigwa hingisahango yelima nepfuwa Butungamili gweHango gwakafanila tola nhambo dzakafanila, kuhanganyisila nhambo dzenlayo, kuti gupe mamo nelubaka gwehingisa hango yelima nepfuwa kunhu wose unogala kene unahango yelima nepfuwa. 293 Bigwa ntome kweminda yelima nepfuwa kunotiwa naHulumente

(1) Hulumente angabiga ntome minda yelima nepfuwa qakatheka kwayo, nepa umwe nhu kuti ibe iye kene nehhisa tjibvumilano kene imwe ndulamilo yegala kene hingisa, koga sendekiwa ikoko kwakafanila tiwa kutobegwa zila dzinolebgwa mundima 289.

(2) Hulumente aangape minda yelima nepfuwa inopinda in’ompela kunhu un’ompela kene banhu baano galanabo.

(3) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila longa zila dzesendekiwa nepiwa kwehango yelima nepfuwa neHango, kakale nlayo iwhoyo wakafanila ndilana nekunolebgwa mundima 289. 294 Njendiso yehango yelima nepfuwa nebeni kene bagali Kutobegwa mighanhu inohhisiwa nenlayo, nhu unahango yelima nepfuwa kene unogala muhango yelima nepfuwa unandulamilo yepa umwe nhu,yeyibhatisa,yegadza banhu banolipa kene yelayidza ndulamilo iye muhango iyeyo. 295 Lipigwa togwa kwehango yelima nepfuwa yakabe yapiwa nhu hapa yotogwa

(1) Wose nhu unogala muZimbabwe unahango iye yelima nepfuwa yakatogwa naHulumente kusanhu kwapinda nsi unandulamilo yewana nlipo wehango iyeyo nepfumbidziwa kwakabe kulipo itogwa unobva kunaHulumente.

(2) Wose nhu unahango iye yelima nepfuwa yakatogwa naHulumente kusanhu kwapinda nsi kakale unandulamilo dzidze dzenhundu mulubaka igogo dzakatshambidzigwa kene dzakadzibiligwa netjibvumilano tjatiwa naHulumente weZimbabwe nahulumente weyimwe hango, unandulamilo yewana nlipo unobva kunaHulumente wehango elipigwa hango iyeyo nepfumbidziwa kungabe kwatiwa muanyiyo sekunolebgwa netjibvumilano itjetjo.

(3) Wose nhu, kusi nhu unobviwa mundimana (1) kene (2), unahango iye yelima nepfuwa yakatogwa naHulumente kusanhu kwapinda nsi unandulamilo yelipiwa naHulumente elipigwa pfumbidziwa kwakabe kulipo muhango mulubaka gwetogwa kwayo.

(4) Nlipo wakafanila lipiwa seleba kwendimana (1), (2) na(3) wakafanila lingisisiwa kakale ulipiwe kutobegwa Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente. 296 Bumbiwa nema kweKhomishini yeminda yeZimbabwe

(1) KunaKhomishini yakafanila zibgwa seKhomishini yeminda yeZimbabwe ina—

(a)

nghalitulo neNtebedzeli weNghalitulo; ne

(b) bamwe banhu basipasi kwebabili kakale basingapinde kunotondeka; babigwa neNtungamili weHango.

(2) Nhengo dzeKhomishini yeHango yeZimbabwe dzakafanila—

(a)

hagwa nentha yebe nelebeswa kwadzo nematatabuko mu, neluzibo newhisisa, kunotiwa pakubhakhwa kwehango neButungamili; ne

(b) layidza siyana kwebanhu beZimbabwe, kwazo kwazo kunohakiwa nezwitjaba nelizana kwebetjilume nebetjikadzi.

(3) Ndima 237 inohingisiwa pakunolebgwa nedusiwa mubugalo kwenhengo dzekhomishini yeHango yeZimbabwe sekunoyi iKhomishini yakazwimila yakabambiwa naDanho 12.

297 Mihingo yeKhomishini yeminda yeZimbabwe

(1) Khomishini yeminda yeZimbabwe inamihingo inotebela—

(a)

yebenelebeswa leba nenlandu, lizana nebutjena mubhakhwa nelingwa kwehango yelima nepfuwa inolawugwa naHulumente;

(b) yethama ndongoloso dzelinga hango yelima nepfuwa lubaka nelubaka;

(c)

yepa mano kunaHulumente nekwe—

(i) togwa kwehango yakasendekewa kuti ihingisiwe netjaba;

(ii) swikilila newana negala muhango yelima nepfuwa, kwazo kwazo— A. dusiwa kwehalawugwa kose kungabepo, kwazo kwazo halawugwa pabunhulume nebunhukadzi; B. tshambidzigwa kwenlayo ubvumila kuti nhu kene n’ha ungaba nehango ingapani yawungahingisa lima kene pfuwa,

(iii) zila hango yayinohingisiwa nayo nebungapani gwehango yelima nepfuwa nefumwa yayo;

(iv) tjenesewa kwezila nendulamilo dzewana kene pa hango;

(v) zila nemamo ehingisiwa negagwa kwehango; ne

(vi) nlipo wakalizanila unodusiwa kumunlayo kupfumbidziwa hango yelima nepfuwa yatogwa nezila inunlayo;

(vii) kobewa netogwa kwe hango yelima nepfuwa;

(d) hakisisa nesekisa n’un’una nesawililana nentha yelingwa, bhakhwa nekobewa kwehango yelima nepfuwa.

(2) Khomishini yeminda yeZimbabwe, ibvumigwa naMinista wakalingisana nekwehango, ingabakanya milayo yakalingisana nezila yehinga yalebwa mundimana

(1).

(3) Khomishini yeminda yeZimbabwe yakafanila hinga itebela ndongoloso yakakwagwa inobva kuMinisita wakalingisana nekwehango.

(4) Mukuhinga, Khomishini yeHango yeZimbabwe yakafanila kuti itungamigwe zwilemela zwakakwagwa mundima 289.

(5) Hulumente nemakubungano ose ahulumente nenhabi dzahulumente mumbihho dzose, nezila dzimunlayo nedzimwe nhambo bo, yakafanila bhatsha Khomishini yeminda yeZimbabwe kakale yakafanila dzibilila zwimila, satola lubihho, bamulebeswa nejidzo.

(6) Hulumente wakafanila tola nhambo, nekumunlayo neamwe mamo akatambunuka, etila kuti—

(a)

Khomishini yeminda yeZimbabwe inotubula hinga zwakanaka nekuzwimila; kakale

(b) bahingi beKhomishini yeminda yeZimbabwe banohinga nekutjenjela, kusina lubihho.

DANHO 17 ZWEMARI LUBABI 1 BHAKHWA NELINGWA KWEZWEDZIMARI

298 Zila dzebhakhwa nelingwa kwedzimari dzeTjaba

(1) Zila dzinotebela dzakafanila tungamila kose bhakhwa nelingwa kwedzimali muZimbabwe—

(a)

kwakafanila kuti kube nelebeswa nebutjena mukubhakhwa kwemali;

(b) mali dzetjaba dzakafanila hinga pfumbidza tjaba, kwazo kwazo ne—

(i) ntolo weketisakwakafanila kobewa kwakalizana;

(ii) mari yawan’wa mutjaba yakafanila kobewa nekulizana pakati kwe hulumente netjaba nembihho dzahulumente;

(iii) nendleko dzakafanila badzepfumbidza tjaba tjeZimbabwe, kwazo kwazo nendleko dzakalingisana nentha nenjudzi akatjambidzilika;

(c)

mbudilo dzinowan’wa pakuhingisiwa kwezwiwan’wa kwakafanila kobewa nekulizana pakati kwetjaba tjinotjila ngwenuwali netjaba tjinootjila mulubaka gunohha;

(d) mari dzetjaba dzakafanila hingisiwa nezila ipatjena, inakulingisisa, inabufumi netambunuka;

(e)

bamubugalo gwebhakhwa nelingwa kwemari bananlandu wethama n’hingo nezila yakatambunuka, nelondesedzo yehinga kwemari ibe patjena; ne

(f)

hadzimiwa kwetjaba nezila dzose dzeyendesiwa kwemari dzipetela kwelete tjehango kwakafanila tikala nezila ipatjena kakale kutigwa bhatsha Zimbabwe.

(2) Akuna miketiso inodan’wa kuhhe kweyatjenesewa nesimba leNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu keneTjisungo njeNlayo tjePhalamente. 299 Lingwa kwemari dzeHango nehingisiwa kwadzo nePhalamente

(1) Phalmente yakafanila tobelela nelinga hingisiwa kwemari nehango nemaKhomishini wose nemakubungano nenhambi dzahulumente mumbihho dzose, kose nenhabi dzakabambiwa nenlayo, nhabi dzinolawugwa nahulumente, makhansili ezwitjaba neebatoropo neemitunhu, kutila kuti—

(a)

yose mari inozibwa hinga kwayo;

(b) mari dzose dzahingisiwa nezila yakatambunuka; kakale

(c)

mighanu nemamo ehingisiwa kwedzimari nekobiwa kwadzo atobegwa.

(2) Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila hhisa mamo ehinga kwePhalamente mulinga netungamila hingisiwa kwedzimari kunolebgwa mundimana

(1). 300 Miganhu yehadzimiwa kwemari naHulumente, zikwelete zwetjaba negadzabgwe kwaHulumente

(1) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila sa miganhu mu—

(a)

hadzima kwaHulumente;

(b) zwikwelete zwehango; ne

(c)

zwikwelete nemilandu yakafanila lipiwa neHango, kakale miganhu iyeyo ayizofanila tiwa Phalamente isabvuma.

(2) Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila hhisa ndawulo nemamo akafanila hingisiwa neHulumente mukweletisa banhu mari.

(3) Mumahhuba alimakumi matanhatu (60) hule kwekuti Hulumente athama tjibvumilano kene agadzabgwe, Minisita wakalingisana nezwemari wakafanila thama kuti tjibvumilano itjetjo tjikwagwe muGazethi.

(4) Minisita wakalingisana nezwemari wakafanila—

(a)

kuti ayendise londesedzo kuPhalamente yehinga—

(i) kwemari dzawan’wa naHulumente weHango dzinohadzimiwa;

(ii) nekwemari dzinohadzimiwa dzatshambidzigwa naHulumente weHango;

(b) mulubaka gun’ompela hapa kuhhisiwa londesedzo dzemari dzehango nehingisiwa kuPhalamente selebgwa mundima 305, kuhhisiwe kuPhalamente londesedzo yekwelete tjehango tjehango yeZimbabwe. 301 Kobewa kwemari pakati kwembihho dzaHulumente wematunhu naHulumente wemitunhu

(1) Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila yeta kuti—

(a)

kobewa kwedzimari pakati kwemakhansili ematunhu nemakhansili ematoropo nemakhansili emitunhu kwakalizana; ne

(b) kobegwa kwematunhu nemakhansili emitunhu, ne amwe mamo angatobegwa pakobewa.

(2) Tjisungo tjinolebgwa mundimana (1) tjakafanila linga, kuhanganyila zwimwe zwinhu—

(a)

zwinohakiwa nehango;

(b) zwakafanila tiwa kulingwa kwelete tjehango nezwimwe zwinhu zwakafanila tiwa nehango;

(c)

zwakafanila nezwinohakiwa nehulumente, kwakalingwa zwinangwa zwiyapo;

(d) zwinonga yetiwa kwemihingo inohakika kwazo, kuhanganyila zwidiyo nezwelapiwa, vula, mimwahha, hingigwa kwetjaba nemagetsi kuntha dzinolingigwa pasi;

(e)

matatabuko ebhata nehingisa mari zwibuyanana emakhansili ematunhu neematoropo nemakhansili emitunhu;

(f)

pfumbidzwa nezwimwe zwinohakiwa nemakhansili ematunhu neematoropo nemakhansili emitunhu; ne

(g) salizana munazwefumwa mukati kwematunhu neku amwe matunhu.

(3) Kwakafanila ndisiwa mari isipasi kwe kuhanu mukhulu kwemari yehango yawan’wa negole kumakhansili ematunhu neemitunhu munalelo gole.

LUBABI 2 KWAMA TJEMARI YAHULUMENTE

302 Kwama tjeMari yaHulumente Kuna Kwama tjeMari yaHulumente lipigwa mari dzose, miketiso nemari dzahadzimiwa nadzo dzose mari dzaHulumente, nekene dzibva poni, kuhhe kwekuti Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente—

(a)

tjihaka kene tjibvumila kuti mari dzilipigwe kutjimwe kwama tjahhuligwa ikoko; kene

(b) tjingabvumila kuti Butungamili gwawamutjila mari guyitole, kene imwe yakhona, kudza kuti ihingisiwe pan’hingo wago. 303 Dusiwa kwemari muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente

(1) Akuna mari inobvumigwa dusiwa muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente kuhhe kwekuti inohingisiwa zwinhu zwabvumigwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kene neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(2) Mari yadusiwa muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente yakafanila lipiwa iwhoyu nhu yega inokolodewa.

(3) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila leba zila ye—

(a)

dusiwa kwemari kwakafanila tiwa muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente neyimwe mari yethitjaba; kakale

(b) mari imuKwama tjeMari yaHulumente neyimwe mari inodziniwa ikohingisiwa nezila yakatambunuka. 304 Ndipo dzeKwama tjeMari yaHulumente

(1) Dzose ndipo dzezwikoloto dzinobva muHango dzakafanila ndipiwa muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente.

(2) Mari dzinohingisiwa mukubunganya nelinga Kwama tjeMari yaHulumente inobumba ndipo yokutanga yeKwama tjeMari yaHulumente.

(3) Nentha yekumundimana (1)— “ndipo dzezwikoloto” kohanganyila mari yatjizwala, ndipo yemari inowa, lipiwa kwezwikoloto nehingisiwa kwemari kwakalingisana nehaka mari yobolekewa mudzibiligwa kweKwama tjeMari yaHulumente nelulamisa nepedza koloto tjatiwa nemari yebolekewa.

LUBABI 3 MBVUMO YEHINGISIWA KWEMARI YEKWAMA TJEMARI YAHULUMENTE

305 Hingisiwa kwakatambunuka kwemari dzeKwama tjeMari yaHulumente

(1) Gole negole Minisita wakalingisana nedzimari wakafanila hhisa londesedzo yemari inoleba kuti kokumbuligwa kuti Hulumente ungawamutjila mari ingapani nekuti Hulumente unowhohingisa mari ingapani mugole linohha kuLubahhe gweBamilili bemitunhu muPhalamente.

(2) Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente gwakafanila zibisiwa nekwemari inokumbuligwa kuti inoowamutjigwa neyinokumbuligwa kuti inoohingisiwa naHulumente seleba kwendimana (1) nehhuba lehangana kweLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kusanhu kwaswika kene kusanhu kwapinda mahhuba elimakumi matatu kwatangisa gole ilelo, koga hapa Phalamente yapaladziwa kakale kusingatubulike kuti kuzibisiwe Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente nekwadzedzo mari dzinokumbuligwa munayigogo lubaka,kobe kwatjifanila kuti Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente guzibisiwe mumahhuba alimakumi matatu hule kwehangana kwago kwekutanga hule kwepaladziwa kwePhalamente.

(3) Kumbuligwa kwemari dzinoowamutjigwa nedzinoohingisiwa kwakapalalana kwezwinotebela kwakafanila hhisiwa—

(a) imwe nemwe Khomishini yakabumbiwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu;

(b) bugalo gweMbonyololi Nkulu;

(c)

lutabi gweTilika Nlandu gweHango;

(d)

lubahhe gwaboHe; ne

(e) kene lipi gubungano akalebgwa muTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(4) Hapa Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente gwabvuma mari dzinokumbiligwa kuti dzoohingisiwa mugole ilelo, kuhhe kwemari dzinoohingisiwa dzinoobhuda muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente seleba kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kene Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente, Minisita wakalingisana nedzimari wakafanila yeta kuti Nlayo utjasusumiwa unowhozibgwa seNlayo utjasusumiwa weHingisiwa kwedzimari unowhondisiwa kuLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente, kakale Nlayo utjasusumiwa iwhoyo wakafanila—

(a)

thama kuti kube nemari inowhobhuda muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente kuti ihinge zwabvumigwaniwa kuti izwihinge; kakale

(b) hingisa mari munazwatjenesiwa mulondesedzo yekumbuligwa kwehingisiwa kwemari, muzwibvumilano zwakasiyana siyana muhingisiwa kwemari kwakasiyana siyana kwabvumigwaniwa.

(5) Hapa mari yadusigwa umwe n’hingo kutobegwa Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjeHingisiwa kwemari isingalizanile kene hapa kuhakika mari inoohingisiwa mun’hingo usadusigwa mari, Minisita wakalingisana nedzimari wakafanila yeta kuti kube nelondesedzo yemari inopamhidzila inowhondisiwa kuLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente, kakale hapa Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente gungayibvuma londesedzo iyeyo, Minisita iwhoyo wakafanila thama umwe Nlayo utjasusumiwa weHingisiwa kwedzimari unopamhidzila undisiwe kuLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente unowhothama kuti mari inohakika idusiwe muKwama tjeMari yaHulumete. 306 Mbvumo yehingisiwa kwemari isanhu yahingisiwa

(1) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingabvumila Ntungamili weHango kuti hapa masimba edusiwa kwemari muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente kuti ihinge mihingo yabe isingabon’we kene mihingo yabe isinamari yayabigigwa kutobegwa nlayo kene upi, koga—

(a)

Tjisungo tjeNlayo atjizofanila bvumila kuti kudusiwe mari inopinda yakalizanila kun’ompela negwamu mukhulu mumari yose yakalebgwa muTjisungo tjeNlayo tjeHingisiwa kwakatambunuka kwemari;

(b) mari dzose dzadusiwa kutobegwa Tjisungo tjeNlayo itjetjo dzakafanila hanganyiwa nedzimwe mari dzinolebgwa munlondesedzo dzemari dzinokumbuligwa kuti dzoohingisiwa dzipamhidzila kuLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente nekulibilidza kakale, hapa Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente gungabvuma nlondesedzo idzedzo, mari iyeyo yakafanila dusiwa muKwamatjeMari yaHulumente kutobegwa tjimwe Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjeHingisiwa kwedzimari.

(2) Hapa Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjeHingisiwa kwedzimari tjegole ilelo tjisanhu tjikatangisa hinga pakutanga kwegole ilelo, Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingabvumila Ntungamili weHango kuti abvumile dusiwa kwemari muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente kudza ihingisiwe payinohakika kuti kutiwe mihingo yaHulumente kwemimwedzi mina yekutanga yegole ilelo, koga—

(a)

Tjisungo tjeNlayo itjetjo atjizokafanila bvumila dusiwa kwemari inopinda kun’ompela mukutatu kwemari dzakabe dzahingisiwa mugole lapinda;

(b) mari yadusiwa kutebegwa Tjisungo tjeNlayo itjetjo yakafanila lebgwa muTjisungo tjeNlayo tjeHingisiwa kwedzimari tjegole ilelo, muzwibvumilano zwakasiyana nehingisiwa kwemari kwakasiyana.

(3) Hapa Phalamente ingapaladziwa kusanhu kukahhisiwa mari dzakakwana kuti kutiwe mihingo yaHulumente, Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingabvumila Ntungamili weHango kuti abvumile dusiwa kwemari muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente kudza kutiwe kunohakika mumihingo iyeyo kudza kube nemimwedzi mitatu hule kwekuti Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente gugale kwekutanga hule kwepaladziwa kwePhalamente, koga mari yose yadusiwa kutobegwa Tjisungo tjeNlayo itjetjo yakafanila lebgwa muTjisungo tjeNlayo tjeHingisiwa kwedzimari tjegole ilelo, muzwibvumilano zwakasiyana nehingisiwa kwemari kwakasiyana. 307 Hingisiwa kwemari kusabvumigwa

(1) Hapa kungawanika kuti mari injisa yahingisiwa kutiwa tjinhu tjisabvumigwaniwa seleba kweLubabi igogu, kene kuti mari iyeyo yahingisiwa n’hingo wabe usabigigwa mari muLubabi igogu, Minisita wakalingisana nedzimari wakafanila hhisa Nlayo utjasusumiwa muLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente ekumbila mazwibhatigwa neHingisiwa kwemari kusabvumigwa ikoko.

(2) Nlayo utjasusumiwa unobviwa mundimana (1) wakafanila hhisiwa muLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente nekulibilidza kakale muamwe mamo kusipinde mahhuba alimakumi matanhatu hule kwekuti kwalingwa kuti mari yahingisiwa kusi munlayo kungapani. LUBABI 4 LONDOLODZEWA KWEMARI NENHUNDU DZETJABA

308 Mihingo yebalondolodzi bemari nenhundu dzetjaba

(1) Mundima iyeyi— “mari dzetjaba” kohanganyisila yose mari yaHulumente kene yakabhakhwa naHulumente kene gubungano lipi kene lutabi gwahulumente, kuhanganyisila mbihho dzahulumente mumatunhu nemumitunhu, mabunga ezwenlayo nemabunga anolawugwa nahulumente; “nhundu dzetjaba” kobviwa nhundu dzose dzaHulumente kene dzakabhakhwa naHulumente kenegubungano lipi kene lutabi gwahulumente, kuhanganyisila mbihho dzahulumente mumatunhu nemumitunhu, mabunga ezwenlayo nemabunga anolawugwa nahulumente;

(2) Inlandu wenhu wose wakalingisana nehingisiwa kwemari dzetjaba kuti adzilondolodze kakale abe nelebeswa lekuti dzohingisiwa mumihingo imunlayo kakale mubungana gunobvumigwa kumunlayo.

(3) Inlandu wenhu wose unolondolodza kene unolawula nhundu dzetjaba kuti adzilondolodze kakale abe nelebeswa lekuti adzitolahika, adzitobulayiwa, adzitokayikanyiwa,kene adzitohingisiwa nezila isakalulwama isimunlayo.

(4) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila thama kuti kubon’we nekulibilidza tjulukiwa kwendimana (2) nendimana (3) netjemegwa nelangiwa kwebanhu bangabe batjuluka ndimana idzedzi kakale, hapo kunohakika,kutogwe mari kene nhundudzisahingisiwa nezila yakatambunuka. LUBABI 5 MBONYOLOLI NKULU

309 Mbonyololi Nkulu nemihingo iye

(1) Kwakafanila kuti kube neMbonyololi-Nkulu, unabugalo gwehingila tjaba koga esipakati kwebanoHingila Hulumente.

(2) Mihingo yeMbonyololi Nkulu ndiyeyi—

(a)

kuti abonyolole whalo dzedzimari, ndongoloso dzedzimari nebhakhwa kwedzimari munhabi dzose, makubungano nenhabi dzahulumente, makhansili ose ematunhu neematoropo nemakhansili emitunhu;

(b) hapa akumbigwa naHulumente, abonyolole whalo dzemari dzebunga lenlayo kene lipi kene bunga linolawugwa nahulumente;

(c)

hapa ndawulo yekuti kutogwe nhambo dzebakanya sabhakhwa zwibuyanana kwemari nenhundu dzetjaba nesalondolodzewa kwadzo zwibuyanana

(d) thama kene upi n’hingo waapiwa kene waatilikiwa kutebegwa Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente.

(3) Banohingila tjaba bakafanila tobela ndawulo dzinobva kuMbonyololi Nkulu kutebegwa ndimana (2) (c). 310 Bigwa kweMbonyololi Nkulu

(1) Mbonyololi Nkulu unobigwa neNtungamili weHango ebvumigwa nePhalamente.

(2) Mbonyololi Nkulu wakafanila banhuweZimbabwe wakahagwa nentha yeba nelebeswa kukwe, kakale alinhu unafanilo dzekuti watjihinga sembonyololi kwemakole asipasi kwe aligumi.

(3) Lubaka gwebamutulo kweMbonyololi Nkulu aguzofanila pinda makole alikutanhatu kakale nhu hazofanila bigwa seMbonyololi Nkulu hule kwebamutulo kan’ompela kene kanjinji, kutebelelana kene kusingatebelelane, kwemakole aligumi nemabili.

(4) Esanhu agala mubugalo, Mbonyololi Nkulu wakafanila, tuna, zwituno zwema mulebeswa etebela zila yetuna setjenisiwa kwayo muNdongoloso yebuTatu, mbeli kweNtungamili weHango kene umwe nhu watumwa neNtungamili weHango.

311 Zwimila kweMbonyololi Nkulu Mukuhinga kukwe Mbonyololi Nkulu wakazwimila koga unohinga elipasi kwenlayo. 312 Mbhayilo weMbonyololi Nkulu

(1) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila yeta kuti mbhayilo nezwinhu zwinowamutjidziwa Mbonyololi Nkulu kuna kubankanyiwe nebvumigwa neNtungamili weHango hule kwemano abva kuMinisita wakalingisana nezwedzimari.

(2) Mbhayilo weMbonyololi Nkului wakafanila bhuda muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente kakale awuzofanila jitisiwa mulubaka elimubugalo. 313 Dusiwa kweMbonyololi Nkulu mubugalo

(1) Mbonyololi Nkulu angadusiwa mubugalo koga hapa kuli kuti—

(a)

hatotubula hinga nentha yekon’wa kwembili kene kwenkumbulo;

(b) unokon’wa thama n’hingo nezila yakalingiligwa; kene

(c)

haana mazwibhato.

(2) Hapa Minista wakalingisana nekwedzimari, ebvumilana nekhomithi yephalamente yakalingisaka newhalo dzedzimari dzetjaba, unzibisa Ntungamili weHango kuti ndebo yedusiwa kweMbonyololi Nkulu mubugalo kohakika kuti ilingisisiwe, Ntungamili weHango wakafanila biga lubahhe gwenlayo gunoolingisisa ndebo iyeyo.

(3) Lubahhe gwenlayo gwabigwa kutebegwa ndimana (2) gwakafanila banebanhu basipasi kwebatatu babigwa neNtungamili weHango, pakati kwabo—

(a)

umwe wabo wakafanila benhu wakatongohinga sejaji; kakale

(b) umwe wabo wakafanilabe ahagwa mugubungano lebanhu basipasi kwebatatu bathangiwa nelutabi kene gubungano lakabambiwa nenlayo kuti limile babonyololi muZimbabwe.

(4) Lutabi kene gubungano linobviwa mundimana (3)(b) lakafanila thanga banhu banobviwa mundimana iyeya hapa kwayi lithame lijalo neNtungamili weHango.

(5) Lubahhe gwenlayo gwahagwa kutebegwa ndimana (2) gwakafanila lingisisa ndebo yedusiwa kweMbonyololi Nkulu mubugalo kakale, gwatjithama iwhoyo n’hingo, gwakafanila yendesa kuNtungamili weHango londesedzo yegwalawana nepa mano kuti Mbonyololi Nkulu adusiwe kene asidusiwe, kakale Ntungamili weHango wakafanila tobela tjalebgwa nelubahhe, haguti adusiwe Ntungamili weHango wakafanila wundusa etobelantolo wehango.

(6) Lubahhe gwenlayo gwakabigwa kutebegwa ndimana (2) gunandulamilo nemasimba anofanana neemakhomishina apasi kwemaKhomishini yeTjisungo tjeNlayo tjeHakisisa [Danho 10:07], kene wupi nlayo unomisa tjetjo Tjusungo tjeNlayo. 314 Bahingi beMbonyololi Nkulu Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila bamba mamo ebigwa kwebunga lenginisa banhu mumihingo banoobhatsha Mbonyololi Nkulu mukuhinga kukwe, kakale tjakafanila—

(a)

bamba mamo efanilo dzebanhu ibabo;

(b) bamba mamo ehinga, lulwama nezwibhata kwebanhu ibabo;

(c)

2013

bamba mamo ezwimila, satola lubihho nebamulebeswa kwebanhu ibabo, ne

(d) ma, hinga zwibuyanana nedzibililika kwebugalo gweMbonyololi Nkulu LUBABI 6 WHOKU NAWHOKUYA

315 Tenga kwaHulumente nezwimwe zwibvumilano zwaHulumente

(1) Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila hhisa ndongoloso tjakafanila tobegwa patengewa kwezwinhu zwinohakika muhango naHulumente nenhabi dzose nemabunga ose aHulumente mumatanhilo ose, kudza kuti tengewa ikoku kutiwe nebutjena, nekulizana, nelebeswa, nehingisa mari zwibuyanana kakale nezila dzakafanela.

(2) Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila yeta kuti kube nendebeleko nehinga kwezwibvumilano zwaHulumente zwinotebela—

(a)

zwibvumilano zwebhatana;

(b) zwibvumilano zwebakwa nehinga kwezwibakwa nezwinhu zwinohingisiwa nebanhu; ne

(c)

zwibvumilano zwendulamilodzemabgwe anotshiwa mumavu nedzimwe ndulamilo; kudza kuti kube nebhudilana patjena, lebeswa, galikabgwe nehingisa mari zwibuyanana kakale nezila inajidzo.

316 Bhakhwa kwenhabi dzakabambiwa nenlayo Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila yeta kuti nhabi dzakabambiwa nenlayo dzihinge nezila yakatambunuka kakale, kwazo tjakafanila ba nelebeswa kuti batungamili benhabi idzedzo banohinga kwelubaka gufupi kakale bengabgwiliziwa mun’hingo kunda nekuti banohinga nezila inohanhisa kene. 317 Bhenki gulu leZimbabwe

(1) Kuna bhenki gulu leHango, lakafanila zibgwa liyi Reserve Bank of Zimbabwe, linazwinangwa izwezwi—

(a)

kuti lilawule gala nehinga kwedzimari;

(b) kuti lidzibilile mari yeZimabwe lilinga kula kwetjaba munazwefumwa kunolizana;

(c)

kuti libumbe kakale lihingise ndongoloso dzehingisiwa kwedzimari.

(2) Tjisungo tjenlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila yeta kuti kube netjimo tjehinga nema kweBhenki Gulu leZimbabwe kakale tjilipe imwe mihingo. DANHO 18 WHOKU NAWHOKUYA NEKUMWE KUNOPAMIDZILA LUBABI 1 WHOKU NAWHOKUYA KWAKALINGISANA NEMAKHOMISHINI

318 Hingisiwa kweLubabi 1 Lubabi igogu goleba nekwe maKhomishini abumbiwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu nekwebanhu bose banohinga mumaKhomishini ihawa.

319 Makhomishini akafanila benhabi dzakabambiwa nenlayo Makhomishini mabunga amunlayo anohinga endila mbeli kusingapele kakale anotubula sungisa kene sungiwa nemazina awo. 320 Bunhengo gwemaKhomishini nemamo ehinga kwenhengo

(1) Kuhhe kwekuti kwatjenesewa muNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, nhengo yose yeKhomishini inobigwa kwelubaka gungapa makole mahanu kakale inotubula bgwilila mun’hingo kwe amwe makole alimahanu kan’ompela koga.

(2) Nhengo dzeMakhomishini, kusi—

(a)

maKhomishini akazwimila;

(b) Khomishini inolinga nezwedziKhutha;

(c)

Khomishini yeZimbabwe yeGwisa Bupitipiti; ne

(d) Khomishini yeHango yeZimbabwe; dzohinga neda kweNtungamili weHango.

(3) Kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, Bamilili bemitunhu muPhalamente nebanhu banohingila makhansili ematunhu, ematoropo, makhansili emitunhu nemabunga anolawugwa naHulumente habazofanila bigwa senhengo dzemaKhomishini.

(4) Hapa Khomishini inanghalitulo neNtebedzeli wenghalitulo, bakafanila siyana umwe abe wetjikadzi umwe abe wetjilume.

(5) Dzisanhu dzikatangisa n’hingo, nhengo dzemaKhomishini dzakafanila tuna mbeli kweNtungamili weHango kene nhu wabigwa neNtungamili weHango kuti dzozwipila mun’hingo iwhoyo selondesedzewa muNdongoloso yeButatu.

(6) Nhengo dzeMakhomishini dzakafanila piwa mbhayilo, milipo nekumwe kwadzingawamutjidziwa sekwakungamisiwa ne kene pasi kweTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente, kakale mbhayilo hawuzofanila jitisiwa mulubaka dzilimubugalo.

(7) Mbhayilo nemilipo yenhengo dzemaKhomishini kobhuda muKwama tjeMari yaHulumente. 321 Mihingo nendongoloso dzemaKhomishini

(1) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingasa imwe mihingo muKhomishini kakale tjinga lawula zila Khomishini yayinohinga nayo, kwakalingwa kuti zwimila kene hinga kwakatambunuka kweKhomishini akutonginiligwa.

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjinolebgwa mundimana (1) tjingabvumila Khomishini kuti ikobe mihingo yayo, koga Khomishini hayizofanila koba masimba obiga banhu kuzwitulo, kene lebe kwafanila tikala kene pa mano, bugalo bakabumbiwa pasi kweNlayo Nkulu.

(3) Kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, tjisungo kene tjipi tjeKhomishini tjohaka kuti kube nebvumilana kwenhengo njinji dzeKhomishini dzinobe dzilipo kutogwa tjisungo itjetjo.

(4) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingapa ndongoloso dzafanila tobegwa neKhomishini, kakale kukatikala kuti adzipo Khomishini ingathama dzayo ndongoloso, koga dzose ndongoloso dzakafanila dzibe nelebeswa nekudza butjena muhinga nemumihingo yeKhomishini.

322 Piwa mari kwemaKhomishini Phalamente yakafanila ba nelebeswa kuti Makhomishini anopiwa mari dzakalizanila kuti ahinge mihingo yawo nezila inogutsha. 323 MaKhomishini akafanila londesedzela Phalamente nekwen’hingo wawo gole negole

(1) Imwe neyimwe Khomishini yakafanila ndisa kuPhalamente, ihingisa Minista wakafanila, londesedzo yegole lose inotjenesa hinga nemihingo yayo yose, londesedzo iyeyi yakafanila ndisiwa mwedzi waNdabhatani wegole linotebela usanhu ukapela.

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingahaka Khomishini kuti ihhise dzimwe nlondesedzo pehhugwi kwe londesedzo yegole lose inolebgwa mundimana (1), kakale tjingatjenesa zila dzehhisa nlondesedzo idzedzo. LUBABI 2 WHOKU NAWHOKUYA

324 Tiwa kwefanilo dzeNlayo Nkulu nekulonga moyo Fanilo dzose dzeNlayo nkulu dzakafanila tiwa zwibuyanana kakale nekulibilidza. 325 Piwa mari kwenhabi dzeNlayo Nkulu nedzimwe nhabi

(1) Hulumente wakafanila ba nelebeswa lekuti mari yakalizanila inopiwa—

(a)

maKhomishini nedzimwe nhabi dzakabumbiwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, kudze kuhinge mihingo yako nezila inogutsha;

(b) Phalamente, kudza itubule hangana nemakhomithi ayo hapa kuhakika; ne

(c)

dzimwe dzose nhabi dzeHango naHulumente, kudza dzitubule thama mihingo yadzo kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu.

(2) MaKhomishini nedzimwe nhabi dzabumbiwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kwakafanila piwa mikoba yekuti kutubule lebelesana nekhomithi yephalamente nekwemari yakunoopiwa gole negole. 326 Nlayo wemabvilo wehango dzose

(1) Nlayo wemabvilo wehango dzose lubabi gwenlayo weZimbabwe, kuhhe kwekuti usingandidzane neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kene neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(2) Hapa kutjenesiwa nlayo, khuta dzose nembahhe dzose dzenlayo dzakafanila tola tjeneso inowhakala yenlayo inondilana nenlayo wemabvilo wehango dzose unohinga muZimbabwe, kusiya imwe tjeneso isingandidzane nenlayo iwhoyo. 327 Zwibvumilano zwehango dzose

(1) Mundima iyeyi— “Gubungano lehango dzose” kobviwa gubungano linabunhengo gweHango mbili kene dzinopinda mbili dzakazwimila kene linaHango mbili kene dzinopinda mbili dzakazwimila dzakamigwa; “tjibvumilano tjehango dzose” kobviwa tjibvumilano, ndongoloso pakati kweButungamili gwedzimwe hango gun’ompela kene gunopinda ipapo kene mahulumente edzimwe hango kene makubungano ehango dzose.

(2) Tjibvumilano tjehango dzose tjatshambidzigwa kene tjadusiwa neNtungamili weHango kene nembvumo yeNtungamili weHango—

(a)

atjitosunga Zimbabwe tjidza tjibvumiwa nePhalamente; kakale

(b) atjitoba lubabi gwenlayo weZimbabwe kuhhe kwekuti tjanginisiwa munlayo neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente.

(3) Tjibvumilano tjisitjehango dzose koga—

(a)

tjatshambidzigwa kene tjadusiwa neNtungamili weHango kene nembvumo yeNtungamili weHango negubungano lin’ompela kene manjinji edzimwe hango kene mabunga; kakale

(b) tjipa Zimbabwe nlandu munazwemari;atjitosunga Zimbabwe kuhhe kwekuti tjabvumiwa nePhalamente.

(4) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjingathama kuti ndimana (2) na (3)—

(a)

dzisihingisiwe mutjibvumilano tjehango dzose kene tjipi zwatjo kene tjimwe tjibvumilano kene zwimwe zwibvumilano zwinondilana nayikoko; kene

(b) d z i h i n g i s i w e k o g a d z i h a n d u g w a m u n d e b o d z i n o n d i l a n a nekwezwibvumilano nedzimwe hango kene zwimwe zwibvumilano zwadzo.

(5) Phalamente ingasunga kuti tjibvumilano tjehango dzose kene tjimwe zwatjo tjibvumilano atjizofanila bvumiwa seleba kwendimana (2), koga tjisungo tjakajalo atjitohinga muzwibvumilano zwinohaka kuti hinga kwadzo kube ne—

(a)

dusiwa kene hingisiwa kwemari dzimuKwama tjeMari yaHulumente; kene

(b) handugwa kwenlayo weZimbabwe.

(6) Hapakutjenesiwa nlayo, dzose khutha nembahhedzenlayo dzosekwakafanila hingisa zila dzetjenesa dzinobhatika dzinondilana netjibvumilano tjehango dzose, kene tjimwe tjibvumilano tjinosunga Zimbabwe, kusi imwe zila isingandilane netjibvumilano itjetjo. 328 Bakanyiwa butshwa kweNlayo Nkulu

(1) Mundima iyeyi— “Nlayo utjasusumiwa weNlayo Nkulu” kobviwa Nlayo utjasusumiwa unohaka tambunudza Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu; “lizanyiwa kwelubaka gwetungamila” kobviwa mamo eNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu anoganhulalubaka gwebamubugalo gwehingila tjaba.

(2) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjinotambunudza Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu tjakafanila kuthama ikoku nezila dzipatjena.

(3) Nlayo utjasusumiwa weNlayo Nkulu awungandisiwe kuSenethi kene kuLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente seleba kwendima 131 kuhhe kwekuti Tjilebeleki tjaleba mamo eNlayo iwhoyo muGazethi mahhuba asipasi kwe alimakumi alihhanalume usanhu ukadusiwa.

(4) Phalamente yakafanila koka banhu nekulibilidza kuti balebe kwabanokumbula nekweNlayo iwhoyo wahhisiwa mumihangano yebanhu bose nemuwhalo dzingahhisiwa hule kwekuti Tjilebeleki tjaleba nekwe Nlayo utjasusumiwa weNlayo Nkulu seleba kwendimana (3), kakale yakafanila thama mihangano kakale ithame kuti banhu batubule hhisa mikumbulo yabo.

(5) Nlayo utjasusumiwa weNlayo Nkulu wakafanila bvumigwaniwa, pakubagwa kwawo kwebupelo muLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente neSenethi, nevota kwenhengo dzingaba mbabi mbili munhatu dzenhengo dziyapo muSenethi nemuLubahhe gwebaMalili beMitunhu muPhalamente.

(6) Hapo Nlayo utjasusumiwa weNlayo Nkulu pawunohaka tambunudza kunolebgwa neDanho 4 kene Danho 16—

(a)

mumimwedzi mitatu wabvumigwaniwa neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente neSenethi seleba kwendimana (5), wakafanila ndisiwa kureferendamu yehango; kakale

(b) hapa ungabvumiwa nebavoti banjinji banovota mureferendamu,Tjilebeleki tjeLubahhe gwebaMalili beMitunhu muPhalamente tjakafanila thama kuti undisiwe kuNtungamili weHango nekulibilidza,wakafanila wubvuma kakale awutshambidzile.

(7) Kusingadzibiligwe amwe mamo endima iyeyi, kayigwa kwe mamo elubaka gwebamubugalo, kunobakutigwa kuti kupamidzigwe lubaka gweba mubugalo kwenhu, akutohinga kunhu wakatongoba mubugalo igogo, kene mubugalo gunolizana nayigogo, kusanhu kukatiwa kayigwa ikoko.

(8) Ndimana(6) na (7) adzizofanila kayigwa muNlayo utjasusumiwa weNlayo Nkulu iwo zwawo kene kayigwa kwendimana idzedzo bubili gwadzo hakungandisiwe kubanhu kuli mureferendamu iyo zwayo.

(9) Ndima iyeyi ingakayigwa koga kutebegwa ndongoloso dzinolebgwa mundimana (3), (4), (5) na (6), sekunoyi ndima iyeyi imuDanho 4.

(10) Hapa Nlayo utjasusumiwa weNlayo Nkulu undisiwa kuNtungamili weHango kuti ubvumiwe kakale utshambidzigwe, wakafanila kuti ubene—

(a)

sethifikheti tjinobva kuTjilebeleki tjinoleba kuti pakuvotegwa kwebupelo muPhalamente Nlayo utjasusumiwa wakavotegwa nebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamenti basipasi kwembabi mbili munhatu;

(b) nesethifikheti tjinobva kuNtungamili weSenethi tjinoleba kuti pakuvotegwa kwebupelo muSenethi Nlayo utjasusumiwa wakavotegwa naboSenetha muPhalamenti basipasi kwembabi mbili munhatu. 329 Tangisa hinga kweNlayo Nkulu, nezila dzefuluka kunlayo nkulukugwi Ndongoloso yebuTanhatu inobva tangisa hinga kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, lahiwa kweNlayo Nkulu wawuhinga neletja hingisa nlayo wawuhinga kuwamutjigwa ndawulo yenlayo ntshwayakabambiwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu. LUBABI 3 TJENESO

330 Hingisiwa kweLubabi 3 Igogu lubabi gohinga pakutjenesiwa kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kuhhe kwekuti zwakakwagwa zwibva kumwe. 331 Zila dzakasiyana siyana dzetjenesiwa kweNlayo Nkulu Ndima 46 inohinga, nehandugwa kungabapo, pakutjenesiwa kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kuhhe kweDanho 4.

332 Tjeneso MuNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu “Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente” kobviwa—

(a)

tjitshambidzilo tjabvumigwaniwa nePhalamente tjikonobvumiwa kakale tjitshambidzigwe neNtungamili weHango kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu; kene

(b) tjitjambidzilo tjakatjihinga muZimbabwe seTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kusanhu kwaswika nsi wetangisa hinga, kose ne—

(i) kene tjipi tjitshambidzilo tjinohanganyigwa mulukwalo gwenlayo weZimbabwe gwakahanduligwa gwakatiwa mugole la1996 kutebegwa simba leTjisungo tjeNlayo tjeKubunganya neHandulila Nlayo [Danho 1:03]; ne

(ii) kene tjipi tjitshambidzilo, tjakatjulikiwa mulukwalo gwakahanduligwa gunobviwa mutjikamwana (i), tjakandilambeli tjihinga kusineni nekuti tjakatjulukiwa; ne

(iii) kene tjipi tjitshambidzilo tjakatshambidzigwa nePhalamente yeZimbabwe hule kwekuti lukwalo gwakahanduligwa gunobviwa mutjikamwana (i) kusanhu kwaswika nsi wetangisa hinga; “zila yetiwa kwen’hingo” inohanganyila kene tjipi tjisungo, tjiyeto kene tjuluka kwenhu unohingila tjaba kene nhu unothama n’hingo iwhoyo ehingila tjaba, nekon’wa kene lamba kwenhu whoyo kuti aswike patjisungo tjakajalo kene thama tjiyeto tjakajalo; “tambunudza” kohanganyila siyana, handula, pamidza, dzima kene tola; “haluloyapakati kwelubaka” kobviwa halulo yehhalila bugalo gusitjina nhu muPhalamente kene muButungamili gwetjaba; “He” kobviwa He sekunolebgwa muDanho 15; “Hingila Hulumente” kobviwa sekunolebgwa nendima 199; “Khomishini” kobviwa” Khomishini yakabambiwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu; “Khomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile” kobviwa khomithi inazina ilelo yakabumbiwa kutebegwa ndima 151; “Hango yetjaba” kobviwa hango yakasiyiwa ntome kutebegwa Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kakale yakabhakhwa netjabatjinotungamigwa naHe kutebegwa nlayo wemabvilo; “Nlayo utjasusumiwa weNlayo Nkulu” kobviwa Nlayo utjasusumiwa, hapa watshambidzigwa, unoba utambunudzadzimwe ntha dzeNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu; “Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu” kobviwa Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu yakabumbiwa nendima 162(a); “ndebo yezwenlayo nkulu” kobviwa ndebo inamatama anohanganyila tjenesewa, dzibiligwa kene tshambidzigwa kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu; “nlayo wemabvilo” kobviwa nlayo wemabvilo welubabi gwetjaba kene tjaba tjebanhu beZimbabwe; “nlayo wetjemela” kobviwa nlayo wakakwagwa pasi unolawula hinga zwibuyanana kwebanhu banohinga mumabunga anotobela nlayo; “bunga linotobela nlayo” kobviwa—

(a) masole emuvula, masole kene masole emumhepo;

(b) Mapholisa;

(c) mihingo yemajele neyelutabi gwetambunudza; kene

(d) limwe bunga lakabambiwa kuti lihingile tjaba neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kakale lakatshambidzigwa neTjisungo itjetjo kuti ibunga linotobela nlayo; “nsi wetangisa hinga” kobviwa nsi wetangisa hinga kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kwakapelela selondesedzewa mutjikamu 3(2) tjeNdongoloso yebuTanhatu; “Nlayo weHalulo” kobviwa Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjinolawula halulo selebgwa muNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu; “gole lehinga kwemari” kobviwa lubaka gwemimwedzi iligumi nekubili (12) gunopela nsi wa 31 munaZwita; “n’hingo” unohanganyisila masimba nenlandu; “Gazethi” kobviwa Gazethi imunlayo yaHulumente kakale inohanganyisila kumwe kunopamidzila iyeyo Gazethi; “halulo yebanhu bose” kobviwa halulo yebanhu bose—

(a) kuhalugwa Ntungamili weHango, baTebedzeli beNtungamili weHango neBamilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente;

(b) kuhalugwa nhengo dzeButungamili gwemakhansili emitunhu; “Hulumente” kobviwa Hulumente weZimbabwe; “bunga linolawugwa naHulumente” kobviwa bunga lezwemabhizinesi linohinga lilawugwa neHango kene nenhu wakamila Hango, nekene enagobe bo munalelo bunga nekene esina; “Lubahhe” kuhhe kwekuti gumwe gwasiwa munlayo, kobviwa Senethi kenePhalamente; “Khomishini yakazwimila” kobviwa Khomishini inolebgwa mundima 232; “jaji” kobviwa ntongi weKhutha yeNlayo Nkulu , Khutha Ipehhugwi, Khutha Whulu, Khutha yeBahingi kene Khutha yeTungamigwa kwebanhu; “Khomishini inoLinga nekweHinga kwedziKhutha” kobviwa Khomishini inodan’wa iyago yakabumbiwa nendima 189; “nlayo” kobviwa—

(a) kunolebgwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kene Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente;

(b) zila yehinga imunlayo; kene

(c) kene upi nlayo usakakwagwa pasi koga unohinga muZimbabwe, kuhanganyisila nlayo wemabvilo; kakale mabala anoti “kumunlayo”, “nezila imunlayo”, “zila inotobela nlayo” nelinoti “kutobegwa nlayo” akafanila whisisiwa zwibuyanana; “nhu unohinga nekwenlayo” kobviwa nhu unambvumo yehinga n’hingo wezwenlayo muZimbabwe; “khansili yemitunhu” kobviwa khansili inolebgwa muLubabi 3 gweDanho 14; “nhengo”, hapa kulebgwa nekwe—

(a) Khomishini kene limwe bunga lakabumbiwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, kohanganyisila Nghalitulo neNtebedzeli wenghalitulo;

(b) bunga lezwenlayo, khansili yedunhu kene yedoropo kene khansili yentunhu, kobviwa nhu wabigiwa kene wahalugwa kuti atungamile khansili iyeyo, lubahhe kene gumwe Butungamili—

(i) gunobe gulibunga lezwenlayo, khansili yedunhu kene yedoropo kene khansili yentunhu; kene

(ii)

lakalinga nekwebhakhwa kwezwebunga lezwenlayo ilelo, khansili yedunhu kene yedoropo kene khansili yentunhu;

“Milili weNtunhu muPhalamente” kobviwa Senetha kene Nhengo yeLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente; “khansili yedoropo” kobviwa khansili yakabumbiwa nendima 269 ibumbigwa dunhu ledoropo; “dunhu ledoropo” kobviwa Dunhu leDoropo lakaBulawayo kene Dunhu leDoropo leHarare, kunda nekuti kunobviwa ndikupi; “Minisita” kohanganyisila nhu unothama mihingo waMinista, sedan’wa kukwe; “nlayo wehango” kobviwa Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kene mpanga wenlayo watiwa kutebegwa Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente; “tuna” kohanganyisila bvuma; “nlandu” kobva tjuluka nlayokwebudzudzu; “lubaka gwembatsha yetjaba” kobva lubaka hapa kuhinga tjalebva kuti kuna mbatsha yetjaba muhango kutebegwa ndima 113; “nhu” kobva wakazwimila kene banhu banjinji, kene bakahangana kene bakazwimila; “Ntungamili weHango” kobviwa Ntungamili weHango yeZimbabwe; “Ntungamili weSenethi” kobviwa Ntungamili weSenethi wakahagwa kutebegwa ndima 122; “khansili yedunhu” kobviwa khansili yakabumbiwa nendima 268 yedunhu kuhhe kwedunhu ledoropo; “bugalo gwetjaba” kobviwa bugalo gunolipiwa gwehungila Hulumente; “n’hingi wetjaba” kobviwa nhu unohinga kene wakabhata bugalo mutjaba; “n’hingo wedzibilila” kobviwa n’hingo wedzibilila unolebwa mundima 207; “Milili waboHe muSenethi” kobviwa He wakahagwa kuti abenhengo yeSenethi kutebegwa ndima 120(1) (b) kene (c); “gala”kobviwa lubaka hapa Senethi kene Lubahhe gwebaMilili bemitunhu muPhalamente begala tjoselele, kuhanganila gose lubaka Senethi kene Phalamente, kwatikala, kuti bamukhomithi; “hhuba logala”kobviwa limwe lelimwe hhuba muviki lakahalugwa neNdawulo Dzimile yePhalamente kene Senethi, kuyendelana nekuti kotikalani, kube hhuba logala, hangana kene kusahanganiwa Phalamente inohangana nehhuba ilelo; “Tjilebeleki” kobviwa Tjilebeleki tjePhalamente tjakahagwa kutebegwa kwendima 126; “Ndawulo Dzimile”, kwakalingwa kumwe nekumwe kungatiwa ne—

(a) Senethi, inobviwa Ndawulo Dzimile dzeSenethi;

(b) Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente, kobviwa Ndawulo Dzimile dzeLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente;

(c) Senethi neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kwakahangana, kobviwa Ndawulo Dzimile dzakahangana; dzakabumbiwa kutebegwa ndima 139; “nhabi dzakabambiwa nenlayo” kobviwa—

(a) Khomishini yakabumbiwa ne Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu; kene

(b) gubungano lakabumbiwa ne kene kutobegwa Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kutigwa zwibaka zwakasiyana zwinotjenesewa

muTjisungo tjeNlayo itjetjo, linabunhengogwebanhu babigwa neNtungamili weHango, Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, Minista, Ntebedzeli waMinista, limwe gubungano lakabambiwa nenlayo kene neKhomishini yakabumbiwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu. “mipanga yenlayo” kobviwa imwe neyimwe mipanga inamasimba enlayo yatiwa neNtungamili weHango, Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, Minista kene umwenhu kene nhu unamasimba pasi kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kene Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente; “miketiso” unohanganila; “ntungamili wemabvilo” kobviwa nhu wabigwa kutebegwa kunolebgwa mundima 283; “Zimbabwe” kobviwa Hango yeZimbabwe inozwitonga; “Khomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe” kobviwa Khomishini yezina ilelo yakabumbiwa nendima 238; “Khomishini yeNdulamilo dzeBanhu yeZimbabwe” kobva Khomishini yezina ilelo yakabumbiwa nendima 242. 333 Panolebgwa nekwemaTanho, ndima, nekumwe Kumwe nekumwe kunoleba muNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, kusina fanilo, nekwe—

(a)

Danho, ndima kene Ndongoloso, kwakafanila lingwa sekunoleba nekweDanho kene ndima kene Ndongoloso yeNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu;

(b) ndimana, inoolingwa seyinobva kunolebgwa mundimana yendima iyeyo inoleba kunobviwa;

(c)

tjikamu, tjoolingwa setjinobva kunolebgwa mutjikamu tjeNdongoloso,ndima, ndimana kene tjeneso iyeyo inoleba kunobviwa;

(d) tjikamwana , tjoolingwa setjinobva kunolebgwa mutjikamwana tjetjikamu kene tjikamwanaitjetjo tjinoleba kunobviwa. 334 Matama anobva kun’ompela akafanila hanganisila ne anobva kunjinji, ne anobva kunjinji akafanila hanganisila ne anobva kun’ompela MuNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, matama anobva kun’ompela anohanganyila anobva kunjinji kakale matama anobva kunjinji anohanganyila anobva kun’ompela. 335 Mathebuli nemiholo yendebo Mathebuli nemiholo yendebo mumaTanho, Mbabi, ndima ne amwe mamo eNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu atobumba lubabi gweNlayo Nkulu kakale kwakasiwa sekunotondeka koga. 336 Panolebgwa nekwelubaka

(1) MuNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, hapa kulebgwa nekwelubaka kubagwa mahhuba—

(a)

kutangisa kene kubva muhhuba lalebgwa, ilelohhuba lalebgwa alitobagwa bo mulubaka igogo;

(b) kunoma kene kunoswika muhhuba lalebgwa, ilelohhuba lalebgwa lobagwa mulubaka igogo;

(2) Kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, hapa kulikuti lubaka gwethama tjinhu kene n’hingo sekwakunolebgwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu gupela kene guwila muhhuba

leNgibelo, hhuba leTshipi kene nsi wetjaba, lubaka gondisiwa mbeli kakale tjinhu kene n’hingo iwhoyo kungatiwa muhhuba linotebela lisi hhuba leNgibelo, hhuba leTshipi kene nsi wetjaba.

(3) MuNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu hapa kuyi lubaka gungapa mwedzi koba kubviwa mwedzi wekhalenda, kakale lubaka gungapa mimwedzi goba gubva pa nsi wetangisa kwelubaka igogo kunoswika hhuba lemwedzi kwakunopelela lubaka igogo.

(4) MuNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu hapa kubviwa lubaka kusingalizanisiwe negole koba kubviwa lubaka gungapa mimwedzi iligumi nemibili. 337 Tondeko yebamubugalo gwetjaba Hapa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu uleba nekwenhu ugele mubugalo kwelubaka gwakatjenesewa, koba kubviwa kose nenhu ungaba akabhata kene unothama mihingo yebugalo igogo kumunlayo. 338 Panolebgwa nekwePhalamente Hapa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu uhaka kuti londesedzo kene gumwe lukwalo kundisiwe kuPhalamente,londesedzo iyeyo kene lukwalo igogo kwakafanila kuti kundisiwe kuSenethi nekuLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente. 339 Mano nebhuzwa

(1) Hapa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu uhaka kuti kene nhu upi kene gupi Butungamilikuhingise mano anobva kuwumwe nhu,nhu kene Butungamili kwakafanila—

(a)

zibisa whoya nhu, nelukwalo, kuti ini tjakunohaka thama kakale kupe nhu iwhoya luzibo gwakalizana kutila kuti iwhoya nhu awhisise tjimo nembudilo netjinohaka tiwa; kakale

(b) kupe nhu iwhoya lubaka gwakalizanila kuti ahhise mano; kakale nhu kene Butungamili kwakafanila tobelela mano ahhisiwa nenhu iwhoyo.

(2) Hapa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu uhaka kuti kene nhu upi kene gupi Butungamili kuzuwisane newumwe nhu, kene kuti kuthame umwe n’hingo hule kwezuwisana newumwe nhu, Butungamili igogo kene nhu kwakafanila—

(a)

zibisa iwhoya nhu, nelukwalo, tjakunohaka thama kakale kupe iwhoya nhu luzibo gwakalizanila kutila kuti iwhoya nhu awhisise tjimo nembudilo yetjinohaka tiwa;

(b) kupe iwhoya nhulubaka gwakalizanila kuti abakanye mano kene matama nekunohaka tiwa;

(c)

nelingisisa mano kene matama kunoba kubva kunhu iwhoya nekwe tjinohaka tiwa;

koga nhu kene Butungamili igogo hakutosundidzigwa kuti kutebele mano kene matama abva kunhu iwhoya. 340 Bigwa kwebanhu

(1) Kuhhe kwekuti kwalebgwa neyimwe zila muNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, simba linoba litobela Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu libiga nhu mubugalo lohanganyila simba zwalo—

(a)

lebgwiliza nhu mubugalo igogo;

(b) lemilidza nhu egala mubugalo igogo kene leyehhisa nhu kuti egale bugalo igogo;

(c)

2013

lebiga nhu kuti abe akabhata bugalo igogo;

(d) lebiga nhu mubugalo igogo gutjigegwe nenhu unoba asipo akapiwa mahhuba kuti asibepo kutila kuti kubakanyiwe igogo bugalo;

(e)

letambunudza nesiyanyisa mamo en’hingo mubugalo igogo, kuhanganyila nembhayilo nelubaka gweba mubugalo nezwaakafanila wamutjidziwa pakupela kwebugalo; kakale

(f)

kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, lemisa kene dusa nhu mubugalo.

(2) Hapa kwatikala kuti kube nebanhu babili kene banopinda babili bamubugalo gun’ompela nentha yekuti mweni wago wanonyaluluka kene unobakanyila siya bugalo, nhu wakabigwa kwebupelo ndiye unozibgwa senghali wayigogo bugalo.

(3) Kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakafanila bamba zila dzebigwa keBatobedzeli benhu umubugalo kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kakale tjakafanila bamba mamo nezila dzehinga kwabo.

(4) Hapo nhu ebigwa ebaTebedzeli wenhu umubugalo kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, Ntebedzeli iwhoyoangathama yose mihingo yebugalo igogo hapa nghalibugalo esingatubule thama mihingo iyeyo.

(5) Kutebegwa zila dzeNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu dzingaganhula lubaka kene bungana gwebamubugalo kwenhu, nhu wasiya bugalo gwakabambiwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kungatikala kuti, hapa enafanilo,liziwe mubugalo kene ahagwe kakale. 341 Siya n’hingo

(1) Nhu wabigwa kene wahagwa kuti agale mubugalo gwakabambiwaneNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu angasiya bugalo igogo hule kwekwalila nhu wakawumbiga kene wakawun’hala kuti agale mubugalo igogo lukwalo gwehaka siya n’hingo, koga hapa kulikuti—

(a)

uNtungamili weHango, lukwalo gwakafanila kwaligwa Tjilebeleki;

(b) uNtungamili weSenethi kene Ntebedzeli uwe, lukwalo gwakafanila kwaligwa Nkwalili wePhalamente kene gubaligwe Senethi;

(c)

Tjilebeleki kene Ntebedzeli weTjilebeleki tjeLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente, lukwalo gwakafanila kwaligwa Nkwalili wePhalamente kene gubaligwe Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente;

(d) iSenetha, lukwalo gwakafanila kwaligwa Ntungamili weSenethi;

(e)

uMilili weNtunhu muPhalamente, lukwalo gwakafanila kwaligwa Tjilebeleki;

(f)

nhengo leMakhansili ezwiTjaba, lukwalo gwakafanila kwaligwa n’nghalitulo wekhansili;

(g) nhengo yekhansili yedoropo, lukwalo gwakafanila kwaligwa meyara wedunhu;

(h) nhengo yahulumente wemitunhu, lukwalo gwakafanila kwaligwa ntungamili wekhansili;

(2) siya kwenhu bugalo gunolebgwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kotangisa nsi kene lubaka gwakakwagwa mulukwalo gwesiya bugalo kene, hapa kusina nsi kene lubaka gwakakwagwa mulukwalo, hapa lukwalo guwamutjigwa nenhu wakwaligwa kene wumwe wakapiwa masimba eguwamutjila.

342 Tiwa kwemihingo nekumwe

(1) Simba, nlayo kene ndulamilo inobvumigwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu ingahingisiwa, kakale n’hingo unohakiwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu wakafanila tiwa, nelubakagwawakafanila tiwa.

(2) Wose makubungano akabambiwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu anamasimba wose ekuti athame zwinangwa zwawo zwibhudilile nehinga mihingo yawo.

(3) Hapa simba, nlayo kene ndulamilo inobvumigwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, amwe masimba kene ndulamilo dzinohakila kuti n’hingo ubhudilile dzobvumigwa bo. 343 Hapa nhu esingalingwe sewumubugalo gwehingila tjaba Nentha yehingisiwa kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, banhu abatoyi bagele mubugalo gwetjaba ngobe bewana pentjeni, gwamu lembhayilo, kene umwe zwawo mbhayilo nentha yin’hingo wabakatongo tila tjaba. 344 Lizanyo imunlayo nembudilo yesagagwa kwebugalo munhabi dzenlayo nkulu

(1) Lutabi gwakabumbiwa neNlayo nkulu iwhoyu kene gwakabumbiwa kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu gungahinga nekene guna bugalo gwenhengo gusigegwe, hapa kulikuti nhengo dzelutabi igogo dzinobvuma kene dzinothama iwhoyo n’hingo dzibe mulizanyo imunlayo inohakika kuti lutabi gubhate n’hingo.

(2) Kuhhe kwekuti Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kene nlayo unotungamila hinga kwelutabi igogo uba nedzimwe zila, gwamu lenhengo dzelutabi lakabumbiwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyo kenelakabumbiwa kutobegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu loba bungana gunohakika kuti lutabi gubhate n’hingo.

(3) Hapa kuyi mavoti muNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu —

(a)

egwamu lenhengo dzelutabigunabunhengo gusingake gukapalugwa gukaba nembabi mbili dzinolizana;

(b) embabi mbili munhatu dzenhengo dzelutabigunabunhengo gusingake gukapalugwa gukaba nembabi nhatu dzinolizana; kene

(c)

embabi nhatu muna dzenhengodzelutabigunabunhengo gusingake gukapalugwa gukaba nembabi dzilina dzinolizana;

kwakafanila whisisiwa kuti kobviwa bungana gwemavoti aguzofanila bapasi kwebungana gwakapelela gunopinda gunotebela gwamu lin’ompela, mbabi mbili munhatu kene mbabi nhatu muna, kunda nekuti nditjipi tjimo, tjebunhengo gwelutabi.

(4) Hapa kuyi bunhengo gose gwePhalamente koba kubviwa bungana gose gwebanhu bose bakahagwa sebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente nayigogo lubaka. 345 Sayendedzana kwezwakakwagwa muNlayo Nkulu Hapa kungatikala kuti kunasayendedzana munazwakakwagwa muNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, kowhiligwa kwakakwagwa neTjikhuwa.

NDONGOLOSO YEKUTANGA (Ndima 4) Fulegi yeHango, Lumbo gweHango, Lutjwayo gweHango neNtolo weHango LUBABI1 FULEGI YEHANGO

LUBABI 2 LUMBO GWEHANGO 1.

Milidzani fulegi yedu yeZimbabwe Yakazwagwa nengwa yelununguko (Nelopa) Nelopa njinji lebagwi bedu Tilidzibilile muzwita zose; Ayipiwe mahha hango yeZimbabwe.

2.

Lingani Zimbabwe Hango yakanaka Nematombo nenjizi dzakanakisisa. (Ngayine) Vula ngayine minda ipembele Bahingi balalame banhu bose bagute. Ayipiwe mahha hango yeZimbabwe.

3.

Ndzimu yengemadzani Hango yeZimbabwe Hango yabobatategulu bedu tose (Kubvila) Kubva Zambezi kunoti Limpopo. Nebatungamili babenendulamo,

Ayipiwe mahha hango yeZimbabwe.

LUBABI 2 LUMBO GWEHANGO (NJIMBO)

LUBABI 3 LUTJAYO GWEHANGO

LUBABI 4 NTOLO WEHANGO NDONGOLOSO YEBUBILI (Ndima 87) PaNoMa NdulaMilo MulubaKa gwEMbatsha YEtjaba Tjenesiwa kweNdogoloso yeKutanga 1. MuNdongoloso iyeyi— “nhu wakahhaliligwa” kobviwa nhu wakahhaliligwa kutebegwa nlayo wemboli unothama kuti kube nesungwa kwakadzibilika; “nlayo wembhatsha” kobviwa nlayo wakakwagwa unobvumila kuti kube nenhambo dzinotogwa kuti kutambunudziwe kene tjipi tjimo tjinoba tjitikala mulubaka gwembhatsha yetjaba; “ndulamilo dzebanhu dzakazinda kene nsununguko” kobviwa ndulamilo kene nsununguko dzakatjenesewa muLubabi 2 gwaDanho 4; “lubahhe gwenlayo gwelingisisa ndebo” kobviwa lubahhe gwalebgwa mutjikamu 3(1). Panoma nghanu dzendulamilo dzebanhu dzakazinda kene nsununguko 2. (1) Nlayo wembhatsha ungaganhula ndulamilo dzebanhu dzakazinda kene nsununguko, koga nezila isingake ikapinda nhambo dzinolebgwa mundima 87.

(2) Hapa kungalebgwa kuti kwabenetjimo tjembhatsha yetjaba muyimwe ntha yeZimbabwe kutebegwa ndima 113, nlayo wembhatsha awungaganhule ndulamilo dzebanhu dzakazinda kene nsununguko dzimuNdongoloso iyeyi kene munayipi ntha yeZimbabwe. LubahhegweNlayo gweLingisisa Ndebo dzeBanhu bakahhaliligwa 3. (1) Kene wupi nlayo wembhatsha unobvumila hhaliligwa kwakadzibiligwa wakafanila bamba lubahhe gwenlayo gwelingisisa ndebo dzebanhu bakahhaliligwa.

(2) Lubahhe gwenlayo gwelingisisa ndebo gwakafanila bigwa neNtungamili weHango hule kwepiwa mano neKhomishini inolinga nezwedziKhuta nehule kwezuwisana neKhomithi yakalingisana neMilayo neNdawulo Dzimile.

(3) Lubahhe gwenlayo gwelingisisa ndebo gwakafanila bane—

(a)

nghalitulo, ijaji kene wakatongoba jaji;

(b)

nebamwe babili, umwe wabo—

(i) ijaji kene wakatongoba jaji kene unafanilo dzinobvumakuti abigwe sejaji;

(ii) wabe jaji wekhuta dzemitunhu muZimbabwe kwemakole asipasi kwegunotendeka; kene

(iii) abe newhalo dzinobvuma kuti ahinge munazwenlayo muZimbabwe, wabenadzo kwelubaka gungapa makole asipasi kwegunotendeka. Ndulamilo dzebanhu bakahhaliligwa

4\. (1) Bose banhubakahhaliligwa—

(a)

bakafanila zibisiwa netjinyolotjo, kusanhu kwapinda mahhuba aligunotendeka, kutibahhaliligwani;

(b)

bakafanila bvumigwa nekulibilidza—

(i) bezwilipila, hala nezuwisana ntome nebanohinga zwenlayo; kene

(ii) hapa beda,bazuwisane ntome nenhu unohinga zwenlayo wabapiwa naHulumente unolipiwa naHulumente;

kakale bakafanila zibisiwa netjinyilotjo nekwendulamilo dzabo kutebegwatjikamu itjetji; kakale

(c)

bakafanila bhakhwa nezila inabunhu kakale inamazwibhato nekwenzinda yetjilemekedzo sebanhu.

(2) Hapa itjetji tjikamutjihaka kuti nhu wakahhaliligwa apiwe luzibo—

(a) luzibo igogo gwakafanila bamululimi gunowhisisiwa nenhu wakahhaliligwa; kakale

(b) hapa nhu wakahhaliligwa esingatubule bala kene kwala, gose luzibo gwakakwagwa gwakafanila tjenesewa nezila inowhakala kakale inogutsha iyenhu wakahhaliligwa. Lingisisiwa kwemilandu yebanhu bakahhaliligwa 5. (1) Wose nlandu wenhu wakahhaliligwa wakafanila yendesewa kulubahhe gwelingisisa kusanhu kwapimda mahhuba eligumi ahhaliligwa kakale lubahhhe gwakafanila zibisiwa zina lenhu wakahhaliligwa iwhoyo, paakahhaliligwa nekuti wahhalililigwani.

(2) Wose nlandu wenhu wakahhaliligwa wakafanila kuti ube uyendesewa kulubahhe gwenlayo gwelingisisa ndebo tjoselele hakuswika mahhuba ali makumi matatu hule kwensi nlandu wawakalingisisiwa nawo nelubahhe igogo, kene mulubaka gufutshwanana hapa lubahhe igogo gujalo.

(3) Lubahhe gwenlayo gwelingisisa ndebo gwakafanila lingisisa yose milandu yahhisiwa nekulibilidza.

(4) Wose maseko anotiwa nelubahhe gwelingisisa, banhu bakahhaliligwa banamilandu inolingisisiwa bakafanila zwitulila matama abo kene, hapa beda zwabo—

(a) bahingise banohinga zwenlayo babapiwa naHulumente belipiwa naHulumente; kene

(b) bahingise banohinga zwenlayo babazwihalila kakale bezwilipila.

(5) Tjinobviwa ne tjikamwana (1) nekwe mahhuba aligumi tjopetela kunobva lubaka gwehhaliligwa guhomanana gunoswika mahhuba aligumi, kulebgwa nekwe nhu wakahhaliligwa unodusiwa mumahhuba aligumi hule kwehhaliligwa kukwe kwekutanga ekotjihhaliligwa kakale mumahhuba aligumi hule kwedusiwa ikoko. Mano anobva kulubahhegwenlayo gwelingisisa ndebo 6. Hule kwelingisisa nalandu wenhu wakahhaliligwa, lubahhe gwenlayo gwelingisisa ndebo gwakafanila kwala pasi mano ekuti nhu iwhoyo andile mbeli akahhaliligwa kene adusiwe agunopa nlawuli wakati uwhoyo nhu ahhaliligwe, kakale iwhoyo nlawuli wakafanila tobela mano awapiwa nelubahhe gwenlayo gwelingisisa ndebo. Nhu wahhaliligwa akadusiwa aangahhaliligwe nenlandu iwo zwawo. 7. (1) Nhu wakahhaliligwa watjidusiwa nentha yelondesedzo yemano elubahhe gwenlayo gwelingisisa ndebo inoleba kuti akuna tjinobhatika tjinota kuti ahhaliligwe azofanila hhaliligwa kakale nentha yenladu wakafanana newekwekutanga.

(2) Kutigwa tjikamu itjetji, nhu unoyi wahhalililigwa nentha yekunofanana nayikoko kwaakahhaliligwa kutanga kuhhe kwekuti bunga lenlayo linolingisisa lati, sebona kwalo kuna amwe mabaka matshwa angata kuti nhu ahhaliligwe. Susuma swikilila dzikhutha kwebakahhaliligwa 8. Ndongoloso iyeyi ayizofanila whisisiwa sekunoyi inoganhula ndulamilo dzenhu wakahhaliligwa kuti atshindike mukhutha kuti hhaliligwa kukwe kumunlayokene hayi, nekene nlandu uwe wandisiwa kulubahhe gwenlayo gwelingisisa ndeboiye kakale.

NDONGOLOSO YEBUTATU (Ndima 94, 104, 114, 122, 123, 126, 127, 128, 185, 259, 272, 310 na 320) zwituNo NEbvuMa

Tjeneso: 1. Nhu ahakafanila tuna nezila inotebela, anagakuta nezila yaanoda kene ekagadzabwe koga ehingisa mabala anotebela. 2. Hapa nhu wakafanila tuna, nezila inotebela akahingisa matama awe anota kuti tjituno kene bvuma ikoko kusitjahinge. tuNa KENE bvuMa KwEzwiPila

Imi . . . . . . . . . . . . . . ndotuna [kene ndobvuma nemoyo wangu wose] kuti ndoozwipila kakale ndoohingila Zimbabwe nelebeswa kakale nditobela milayo yeZimbabwe. Ndizo Ndzimu munditjidze.[Kotjulukiwa hapa kuli bvuma]

Tuna ikoku kene bvuma ikoku kwakafanila tiwa mbeli kwenhu walebgwa munlayo wakafanila. tuNa KENE bvuMa KwEbugalo

Imi . . . . . . . . . . . . . ndotuna [kene ndobvuma nemoyo wangu wose] kuti ndoohingila Zimbabwe zwibuyanana kakale nelebeswa mubugalo gwe. Ndizo Ndzimu munditjidze. [Kotjulukiwa hapa kuli bvuma] Tuna ikoku kene bvuma ikoku kwakafanila tiwa mbeli kwenhu walebgwa munlayo wakafanila. tuNa KENE bvuMa KwENtuNgaMili wEhaNgo NEbat EbEdzEli bENtuNgaMili wEhaNgo

Imi . . . . . . . . . . . . ndotuna [kene ndobvuma nemoyo wangu wose] kuti seNtungamili [kene seNtebedzeli weNtungamili] weZimbabwe ndoozwipila kuhango yeZimbabwe kakale ndootobela, ndimilidze kakale ndimile Nlayo Nkulu nayo yose imwe milayo yeZimbabwe, kakale— •

kuti ndoobvumila kose kunomilidza, kakale ndilambe kose kungakubadza, Zimbabwe;

•

kuti ndoodzibilila kakale ndimilidze ndulamilo dzebanhu beZimbabwe;

•

kuti ndoothama mihingo yangu nemasimba angu ose neluzibo gwangu nematatabuko angu kakale nelebeswa lebunhu gwangu; kakale

•

kuti ndoozwipila mululwama kweZimbabwe nebanhu bayo.

Ndizo Ndzimu munditjidze. [Kotjulukiwa hapa kuli bvuma] Tuna ikoku kene bvuma ikoku kwakafanila tiwa mbeli kweNtongi Nkulukene, hapaesipo, mbeli kwe umwe jaji unabugalo gunotobela gugwe uyapo. tuNa KENE bvuMa KwaMiNisita

Imi . . . . . . . . . . . ndotuna [kene ndobvuma nemoyo wangu wose] kuti ndoozwipilakuhango yeZimbabwe, nendili mubugalo gwaMinisita, ndootjengetedza Nlayo Nkulu neyimwe yose milayo yeZimbabwe; kuti ndoopaNtungamili weZimbabwe mano angu ndakasununguka kakale nezila yandinoba ndibona yakatambunuka nayo gose lubaka hapa kuhakika kuti ndijalo, kudze kubenetungamigwa nebhakhwa zwibuyanana kwendebo dzetjaba tjeZimbabwe; kuti andingaduse, patjena mulubahhe kene netjantome tjimwe netjimwe tjakasumbika tjinozuwhisana muKhabhinethi kene tjipi tjakasumbikwa tjandatilikiwa mukuhinga kwangu saMinisita; nekuti nemazwibhato ose ndoohinga n’hingo webugalo gwangu ndakazwipila netubula kwangu kose. Ndizo Ndzimu munditjidze. [Kotjulukiwa hapa kuli bvuma] Tuna ikoku kene bvuma ikoku kwakafanila tiwa mbeli kweNtungamili weHango kene, hapa esipo,mbeli kweNtongi Nkulu kene jaji nkulu kupinda bose uyapo. tuNa KENE bvuMa KwENtobEdzEli waMiNisita

Imi . . . . . . . . . . ndotuna [kene ndobvuma nemoyo wangu wose] kuti ndoozwipila kuhango yeZimbabwe, nendili mubugalo gweNtobedzeli waMinisita,ndootjengetedza Nlayo Nkulu neyimwe yose milayo yeZimbabwe; kuti ndoopa Ntungamili weZimbabwe mano angu ndakasununguka kakale nezila yandinoba ndibona yakatambunuka nayo gose lubaka hapa kuhakika kuti ndijalo, kudze kubenetungamigwa nebhakhwa zwibuyanana kwendebo dzetjaba tjeZimbabwe;

kuti andingaduse, patjena mulubahhe kene netjantome tjimwe netjimwe tjakasumbika tjozuwhisana muLubahhe gwemaMinisita kene tjipi tjiphiri tjandatilikiwa mukuhinga kwangu seNtobedzeli waMinisita; nekuti nemazwibhato ose ndoohinga n’hingo webugalo gwangu ndakazwipila netubula kwangu kose. Ndizo Ndzimu munditjidze. [Kotjulukiwahapa kuli bvuma] Tuna ikoku kene bvuma ikoku kwakafanila tiwa mbeli kweNtungamili weHango kene, hapa esipo,mbeli kweNtongi Nkulu kene jaji nkulu kupinda bose uyapo. tuNa KENE bvuMa KwEMilili wENtuNhu MuPhalaMENt E

Imi . . . . . . . . . . ndotuna [kene ndobvuma nemoyo wangu wose] kuti ndoozwipila kuhango yeZimbabwe, kuti ndootjengetedza Nlayo Nkulu neyimwe yose milayo yeZimbabwe, nekuti ndoothama mihingo yangu ndiliSenetha/Milili weNtunhu muPhalamente [kunda nekuti ndikupi kwakatambunuka] ndakazwipilanetubula kwangu kose. Ndizo Ndzimu munditjidze. [Kotjulukiwahapa kuli bvuma] Tuna ikoku kene bvuma ikoku kwakafanila tiwa mbeli kweNkwalili wePhalamente. t u Na KENE bvu Ma KwEbahiNgi bEdziKhuta

Imi . . . . . . . . . . . . ndotuna [kene ndobvuma nemoyo wangu wose] kuti ndoozwipila kuhango yeZimbabwe nendili mubugalo gwe. Ndootjengetedza nedzibililaNlayo Nkulu kakale ndoohingisa ndulamo mubanhu bose nekunofanana ndisina kuhla, halula kene tshambidzila nditebela Nlayo Nkulu nenlayo. Ndizo Ndzimu munditjidze. [Kotjulukiwahapa kuli bvuma] Hapa nhu unotuna tjitunho itjetji kene unobvuma ejali alintongi, kwakafanila kuti kutiwe mbeli kweNtongi Nkulu kene Ntongi nkulu kupinda bose uyapo. Mudzimwe ntha dzose kwakafanila tikala mbeli kwenhu walebgwa munlayo wakafanila. tuNa KENE bvuMa KwENhENgo dzEMaKhaNsili EMatuNhu/MaKhaNsili EMatoroPo

Imi . . . . . . . . . . . ndotuna [kene ndobvuma nemoyo wangu wose] kuti ndoozwipilakuhango yeZimbabwe, nendili mubugalo gweNhengo yekhansiliyematunhu [kene yematoropo] yeDunhu le, [zina leDunhu] ndootjengetedza Nlayo Nkulu neyimwe yose milayo yose yeZimbabwe; kuti ndoopa mano angu kuNghalitulo [kene Meyara] we[zina ledanho] ndakasununguka kakale nezila yandinoba ndibona yakatambunuka nayo gose lubaka hapa kuhakika kuti ndijalo, kudze kubenetungamigwa nebhakhwa zwibuyanana kwendebo dzetjaba tjeDanho; kuti andingaduse, patjena mulubahhe kene netjantome tjimwe netjimwe tjakasumbika tjozuwhisana mukhansili yematunhu [kene yematoropo] kene tjipi tjiphiri tjandatilikiwa mukuhingakwangu ndilinhengo yayeyo khansili; nekuti nemazwibhato ose ndoohinga n’hingo webugalo gwangu ndakazwipila netubula kwangu kose. Ndizo Ndzimu munditjidze. [Kotjulukiwahapa kuli bvuma] Tuna ikoku kene bvuma ikoku kwakafanila tikala mbeli kwenhu walebgwa munlayowakafanila.

NDONGOLOSO YEBUNA (Ndima 92, 121, 124, 125 na 158) FaNilo DzEbavoti Fanilo dzekwalisa semvoti 1. (1) kutebegwa tjikamwana (2) netjikamu (2), nhu unafanilo dzekwagwa semvoti mulukwalo gwebavoti bentunhu hapa—

(a)

enamakole ali kene anopinda gumi nehhanakadzi; kakale

(b) alinhu weZimbabwe.

(2) Nlayo weHalulo ungapamidza zwinohakika zwakalingisana negala pakukwalisa kwebavoti kutila kuti bavoti bakwagwe muwhalo dzevota dzakatambunuka, koga izwezo zwinoba zwihakika zwakafanila yendidzana neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, kwazo kwazo nendima 67. Safanila kwalisa semvoti 2. Nhu hazofanila kwagwa semvoti—

(a)

hapa ahhaliligwa elingwele wenkumbulo kene wakakubadzika

(b)

munkumbulo kutebegwa Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente tjakalingisana netjila munkumbulo;

(c)

hapakulikuti wawanika nendawulo yekhutaesingatubule thamazwakalingisana nebutjilo gugwe, kwelubaka ndawulo iyeyo ihinga; kene

(d)

hapa awanika enanlandu kutebegwa Nlayo weHalulo kakale khuta whulu yati hazotjafanila kwagwa semvoti kene hazotjafanila vota, kwelubaka igogo gwaayi hazofanila, koga lubaka igogo haguzofanila pinda makole mahanu. NDONGOLOSO YEBUHANU (Ndima 130 na 1310)

zila YEbhaKhwa KwEMilaYo itjasusuMiwa NEdziMwE NdEbo MuPhalaMENtE

MA KWEZWIKAMU LUBABI 1

tjiwalilo tjEMilaYo itjasusuMiwa, whalo NEMaPhEthishiNi

Tjikamu 1. Tjeneso muNdongoloso yeBuhanu. 2. Kwabva Milayo itjasusumiwa 3. Nhengodzingatjimbidzisayendila mbeli kweMilayo itjasusumiwa neWhalo 4. Phalamente hayineni ne Nlayo wezwedzimari utjasusumiwa kenewhalo dzezwemari kene zwikumbilo kuhhe hule kwewana mano anobva kuNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, Minisita kene Ntobedzeli waMinisita. LUBABI 2 zila dzaKaliNgisaNa NEMilaYo itjasusuMuwa

5\. 6. 7.

Ndisiwa kwemilayo itjasusumiwa pakati kweLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente neSenethi. Sabvumilana pakati kweLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente neSenethi. Milayo yezwedzimari itjasusumiwa.

LUBABI 3

NloNdEsEdzo dzEKhoMithi YEPhalaMENtE YaKaliNgisaNa NEMilaYo 8. 9.

Nlondesedzo dzeKhomithi yeNlayo yePhalamente yakalingisananekweMilayo itjasusumiwa. Nlondesedzo dzeKhomithi yeNlayo yePhalamente yakalingisananekwemipanga yemilayo. LUBABI 1

Tjiwalilo Mumilayo Itjasusumiwa Nemaphethishini Tjeneso muNdongoloso yeBuhanu 1. Mundongoloso iyeyi— “Nlayo wezwedzimari utjasusumiwa” kobviwa nlayo utjasusumiwa unothama mamo—

(a) etshambidzigwa, milidziwa kene jitisiwa kwemiketisounotjidza Hango;

(b) ehingisiwa kwedzimari dzibva mukwama tjemali yahulumente kene dzimwe mari dzinolawugwa nahulumente, kene tshambidzigwa, milidziwa kene jitisiwa kwenlipo mukwama tjemari yahulumente kene dzimwe mari dzinolawugwa nahulumente;

(c) ehanganya kene lipiwa kwekwelete tjeHango;

(d) ebvuma kon’wa lipisamiketisooweHango, kene

(e) ebvuma hingisiwa kwemari naHulumente kusabvumigwa. Kwabva Milayo itjasusumiwa 2. (1) Nlayo utjasusumiwa kene wupi ungatangisila kuLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu Muphalamente.

(2) Nlayo u t j a s u s u m i w a kene w u p i , k u s i Nlayo wezwedzimari utjasusumiwa ungatangisila kuSenethi. Nhengo dzingatjimbidzisa yendila mbeli kweMilayo itjasusumiwa neWhalo 3. Kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu neNdawulo dzimile—

(a)

Senetha kene wupi angahhisa kene wupi zwawo Nlayo utjasusumiwa muSenethi kene tula whalo kudze kuzuwisaniwe nekwadzo muSenethi kene hhisa kene tjipi tjikumbilomuSenethi;
