(b) Milili weNtunhu muPhalamentekene wupi angahhisa kene wupi zwawo Nlayo utjasusumiwa muLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente kene tula whalokudze kuzuwisaniwe nekwadzo muLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente kene hhisa kene tjipi tjikumbilo muLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente;

(c)

Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango kene wupi, Minisita kene Ntobedzeli waMinisita angahhisa kene wupi zwawo Nlayo utjasusumiwa kene tula whalo kudze kuzuwisaniwe nekwadzo kene hhisa kene tjipi tjikumbilo kuSenethi kene kuLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente.

(d)

Nhengo kene yipi yahhisa Nlayo utjasusumiwa muLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente kene muSenethi, yakafanila kuti hapa Nlayo utjasusumiwa iwhoyo wabvumigwaniwa muLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente kene muSenethi, awundise kuLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente kene kuSenethi.

Phalamente hayineni ne Nlayo wezwedzimari utjasusumiwa kenewhalo dzezwemari kene zwikumbilo kuhhe hule kwewana mano anobva ku Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, Minisita kene Ntobedzeli waMinisita 4. (1) Kuhhe kwemano anobva kuNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, Minisita kene NtobedzeliwaMinisita, Lubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente kene kuSenethi hakuzofanila—

(a)

yendila mbeli nekwe Nlayo utjasusumiwa, kuhanganyila kayigwa kweNlayo utjasusumiwa, unolin’gwa seNlayo wezwedzimali utjasusumiwa neNtungamili weSenethi kene Tjilebeleki, sekungabe kulipo;

(b) yendila mbeli nekwe whalo kene dzipi, kuhanganyila kayigwa kwewhalo, kunolingika kuNtungamili weSenethi kene Tjilebeleki, sekunohakika mamo akafanila tigwa zwinotebela—

(i) etshambidzigwa, milidziwa kene jitisiwa kwemiketisounotjidza Hango;

(ii) ehingisiwa kwedzimari dzibva mukwama tjemari yahulumente kene dzimwe mari dzinolawugwa nahulumente, kene tshambidzigwa, milidziwa kene jitisiwa kwenlipo mukwama tjemari yahulumente kene dzimwe mari dzinolawugwa nahulumente;

(iii) ehanganya kene lipiwa kwekwelete tjeHango;

(iv) ebvuma kon’wa bhadalamiketisowakafanila bhadagwa neHulumente; kene

(v) ebvuma hingisiwa kwedzimari neHulumente kusimunlayo; kene

(c)

ingawamutjila tjikumbilo, sebona kweNtungamili weSenethi kene Tjilebeleki, sekungabe kuliyapo, tjinobe tjikumbila kuti kutjidziwe nekumwe kwalebgwa mutjikamwana (b)

(2) Tjikamwana (1) atjitohinga kuNlayo utjasusumiwa watugwa, zwikumbilo, bakanya kwahhisiwa kene tjikumbilo tjahhisiwa neNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, Minisita kene Ntobedzeli waMinisita. LUBABI 2 NdoNgoloso YEMilaYo itjasusuMiwa Ndisiwa kwemilayo itjasusumiwa pakati kweLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente neSenethi 5. (1) Nlayo utjasusumiwa wakabva mugumwe Lubahhe gwePhalamente kakale wadusiwa nayigogo Lubahhe wakafanila ndisiwa kugumwe Lubahhe nekulibilidza, kakale hhuba leyendesiwa kwawo lakafanila kwagwa mulukwalo gweLubahhe igogo gunowuyendesa.

(2) Nlayo utjasusumiwa wayendesiwa kugumwe Lubahhe gwePhalamente wakafanila dusiwa muLubahhe igogo nekulibilidza, kakale Lubahhe igogo gungawulamba Nlayo iwhoyo kene guwubvume gwawubakanya kene gusawubakanya.

(3) Nlayo utjasusumiwa unoti, watjiyendisewa kuLubahhe gwePhalamente seleba kwetjikamu itjetji, unoba unobvumiwa neLubahhe igogo wakayigwa wakafanila bgwiliziwa kuLubahhe gwawakatangisilaunakukayigwa kwabvumigwa kumunlayo neNkwalili wePhalamente, kakale Lubahhe gwePhalamente gunobgwiliziwa nlayo iwhoyu gungalamba, gubvume kene gukayileipapo kwakayigwa.

(4) Hapa, hule kwekuti Nlayo utjasusumiwa wabgwiliziwa kuLubahhe gwakawuthama selondesedzewa mutjikamwana (3), bankanyiwa kwatiwa kuna iwo negumwe Lubahhe kolambiwa kene kubankanyiwe neLubahhe gwakawuthama, gumwe

Lubahhe gungatshigila tambunudziwa, guduse bakanya ikoko kene gubvumilane nebankanyiwa ikoko. Sabvumilana pakati kweMbahhe dzePhalamente 6. (1) Seleba kwetjikamu itjetji, hapa—

(a)

Senethi neLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente kusabvumilana mukayigwa kwakafanila tiwa muNlayo utjasusumiwa watangisila kuLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente mumahhuba alimakumi alihhanalume hule kwekuti Nlayo utjasusumiwa iwhoyo wazibisiwa Senethi;

(b) Senethi neLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente kusabvumilana mukayigwa kwakafanila tiwa muNlayo utjasusumiwa wakatangisila kuSenethi mumahhuba alimakumi alihhanalume hule kwekuti Nlayo utjasusumiwa wabgwiliziwa kuSenethi; kene

(c)

Nlayo utjasusumiwa wakatangisilakuLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente walambiwa kene usadusiwa neSenethi mumahhuba alimakumi alihhanalume hule kwekuti Nlayo utjasusumiwa iwhoyo wazibisiwa kuSenethi;

(d) Nlayo utjasusumiwa iwhoyo ungandisiwa kuNtungamili weHango kuti awubvume kakale atshambidzile nezila yawadusiwa nayo neLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente, kuhhe kwehandulo nikinini dzinohakika nekupinda kwelubaka nekumwe kayigwa kungabe kwabvumigwaniwa neSenethi neLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente.

(2) Hapa, sebona kweTjilebeleki, Nlayo utjasusumiwa—

(a)

wakatangisilakuLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente; kakale

(b)

wazibisiwakuLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamentemumahhuba alimakumi alihhanalume hule kwekuti Nlayo utjasusumiwa wantolo wakatangisila kuSenethi wazibisiwa Senethi; unazwimwe zwinhu zwinondilana nayidzedzo dzimuNlayo utjasusumiwa wantolo, kuhhe kwehandulo nikinini dzinohakika nekupinda kwelubaka, tjikamwana (1) tjohinga muNlayo utjasusumiwa iwhoyo sekunoyi mahhuba alimakumi alihhanalume anolebgwa muzwikamwana (a) na (c) zwetjikamwana itjetjo abe elimahhuba alihhanakadzi kugegwe mihangano.

(3) Nlayo utjasusumiwa unolebgwa mutjikamwana (1) kene (2) awungandisiwe kuNtungamili weHango kuti awubvume atshambidzile kuhhe kwekuti, hule kwemahhuba alimakumi alihhanalume kene lubaka gwemahhuba alihhanakadzi gwalebgwa munayitjetjo tjikamwana, Lubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamentegwasunga kuti Nlayo utjasusumiwa iwhoyo undisiwe kuNtungamili weHango kuti awubvume.

(4) Nlayo utjasusumiwa unondisiwa kuNtungamili weHango kuti awubvume kakale atshambidzile kutobegwa tjikamu itjetji wakafanila pelekedzewa nelukwalo gunobva kuTjilebeleki gunoleba kuti Nlayo utjasusumiwa iwhoyo ndiwo unolebgwa netjikamu itjetji kakale nezwinohakiwa netjikamu itjetji zwahhisiwa.

(5) Nlayo utjasusumiwa wayendesiwa kuNtungamili weHango seleba kwetjikamu itjetji unoyi watjidusiwa nePhalamente kumunlayo nezila yawandisiwa nayo kuNtungamili weHango.

(6) Kutigwa tjikamu itjetji—

(a)

Nlayo utjasusumiwa watangisila kuLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente unokumbuligwa kuti watjihhisiwa kuSenethi nehhuba

legala hule kwensi weyendesiwa kwawo kuSenethi, sekwagwa muwhalo ndebo yeLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente sekumutjikamu 5(1);

(b) Nlayo utjasusumiwa watangisila kuSenethi unokumbuligwa kuti wakabgwiliziwa kuSenethi nehhuba legala kutjambopinda nsi wawunobgwiliziwa nawo kwekutanga kuSenethi kutebegwa tjikamu 5(3).

(7) Tjitjamu itjetji atjitohinga muMilayo itjasusumiwa yeNlayo Nkulu kene Milayo yezwedziMari itjasusumiwa. Milayo yezwedzimari itjasusumiwa 7. (1) Senethi ayinamasimba etambunudza Nlayo utjasusumiwa weMari koga ingapa mano kuLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente kuti guwukayile.

(2) Nkwalili wePhalamente wakafanila tshambidzila kose kayila kwatiwa nentha yehhisa mano kweSenethi muNlayo utjasusumiwa wedziMari kakale wakafanila yendesa kayigwa kwatjitshambidzigwa kuLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente.

(3) Lubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamentegwakafanila lingisisa kose kayigwa kwahhisiwa pasi kwetjikamu itjetji kakale gukulonge muNlayo utjasusumiwa wedziMari iwhoyo.

(4) Hapa Senethi isangabvume Nlayo utjasusumiwa wedziMari pakati kwemahhuba egala elihhanakadzi atanga bagwa kutangisa kuhhuba letugwa kweNlayo utjasusumiwa muSenethi, Lubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamentegungasunga kuti Nlayo utjasusumiwa undisiwe kuNtungamili weHango, Nlayo utjasusumiwa ungatjindisiwa kuNtungamili weHango nezila yawabvumigwaniwa nayo neLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente.

(5) Nlayo wedziMari utjasusumiwa watjihhisiwa kuNtungamili weHnago unotshigila tjisungo tjipasi kwetjikamwana (4)—

(a)

unolingwa senlayo wakabvumiwa nePhalamente nezila yawakabvumigwaniwa nayoneLubahhe gwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente;kakale

(b) hapa undisiwa kuNtungamili weHango kuti awubvume kakale awutshambidzile, wakafanila pelekedzewa nelukwalo gunobva kuTjilebeleki guleba kuti Nlayo utjasusumiwa wabvumigwaniwa seleba kwetjikamu itjetji. LUBABI 3 NloNdEsEdzo dzEKhoMithi YENlaYo YEPhalaMENtE Nlondesedzo dzeKhomithi yeNlayo yePhalamente nekweMilayo itjasusumiwa 8. (1) Pasi kwetjikamu itjetji, Lubahhe kene gupi agungatshambidzile Nlayo utjasusumiwa kuhhe kwekuti Khomithi yeNlayo yePhalamente yahhisa londesedzo kuLubahhe igogo.

(2) Tjikamwana (1) atjitohinga mu—

(a)

Nlayo utjasusumiwa weNlayo Nkulu; kene

(b) Ku umwe zwayo Nlayo utjasusumiwa watjihhisigwa londesedzo inobva kuKhomithi yeNlayo yePhalamente kuhhe kwekuti Nlayo utjasusumiwa iwhoyo wakayigwa hule kwehhisiwa kwelondesedzo.

(3) Hapa Khomithi yeNlayo yePhalamente isalondesedza nekweNlayo utjatjasusumiwa pakati kwelubaka gwatjenesewa muNdawulo Dzimile, kene pakati

kwelubaka gwapamidzigwa gwapiwa kutebegwa Ndawulo Dzimile, kokumbuligwa kuti Khomithiinokumbula kuti akuna mamo eNlayo utjasusumiwa, hapa watshambidzigwa, anooleyana neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, neLubahhe gwakalingisana nayikoko gungandila mbeli neNlayo utjasusumiwa iwhoyo sekunoyiKhomithi yalondesedza kumuzila.

(4) Kwakalingwazwikamwana (5) na (6), HapaKhomithi yeNlayo yePhalamente ingalondesedza kuti mamo eNlayo utjasusumiwa, hapa watshambidzigwa, anooleyana neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, Lubahhe gwePhalamente gwakalingisana nayikoko gwakafanila lingisisa londesedzo iyeyo kakale, hapa Lubahhe igogo gusunga kuti mamo ihawio angaleyana neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, Lubahhe igogo aguzofanila bvumilana neNlayo utjasusumiwa unamamo ihawo.

(5) Hapa Lubahhe gwePhalamente gulingisisa londesedzo yeKhomithi yeNlayo wePhalamente inolbviwa mutjikamwana (4) nekwe Nlayo utjasusumiwa wakahhisiwa neNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango, Minisita kene Ntebedzeli weMinisita, nhu wakahhisa Nlayo utjasusumiwa iwhoyo wakafanila piwa lubaka kuti ahandule nekwelondesedzo iyeyo.

(6) Hapa Lubahhe gwePhalamente gungasunga gutebela tjikamwana (4) kuti mamo eNlayo utjasusumiwa, hapa engatshambigwa, anooleyana neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, NtebedzeliweNtungamiliweHango kene Minisita engakwalila kuKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu mumahhuba agumi nekuna hule kwedusiwa kwetjisungo kuti kutshambidzigwe kuti mamo ihawo, hapa engatshambidzigwa, engayendedzana neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, kakale hapa Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu ingatshambidzila nayeyo zila Lubahhe gwePhalamete gwakalingisana nayikoko gungandila mbeli gulingisise kakale gubvume Nlayo utjasusumiwa unamamo ihawo. Nlondesedzo dzeKhomithi yeNlayo yePhalamente nekwemipanga yemilayo 9. (1) Senethi kene Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kusanhu kukawamutjila londesedzo yeKhomithi yePhalamente yekuti mamo empanga wenlayo haatoyendedzana neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kene tjimwe Tjisungo tjayo, Khomithi inga dusa londesedo iyeyo hapa Khomithi yagutshikana kuti mamo ihawo adusiwa kene akayigwa nezila inoodusa leyana ikoko.

(2) Hapa, hule kwelingisisa londesedzo yeKhomithi yeNlayo yePhalamente yekuti mamo empanga wenlayo anoleyana neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, Senethi kene Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kungasunga kuti mamo ihawo haatoleyana neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, Nkwalili wePhalamente wakafanila yendesa londesedzo nekwetjisungo itjetjo kuButungamili gwakatshambidzila mpanga iwhoyo, kakale Butungamili igogo gwakafanila, mumahhuba alimakumi mabili nekun’ompela hule kwezibisiwa nayikoko,kuti—

(a)

gukwalile kuKhutha yeNlayo Nkulu gukumbila kuti izibise tjaba kuti mpanga wenlayo iwhoyo unoyendedzana neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu; kene

(b) gulahe mpanga wenlayo iwhoyo.

(3) Hapa Butungamili gwakalingisana netshambidzigwa kwempanga wenlayo gungakwalila Khutha yeNlayo Nkulu gukumbila kuti izibise tjaba sekunolebgwa netjikamwana (2)(a), mpanga wenlayo iwhoyo unoma hingisiwa kutjakamiligwa tjisungo tjeKhutha.

(4) Hapa, hule kwelingisisa londesedzoyeKhomithi yeNlayoyePhalamenteyekuti mamo empanga wenlayo apehhugwi kwemasimba eTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente, Senethi kene Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente kungasunga kuti mamo ihawo apehhugwi kwemasimba—

(a)

mamo ihawo haatjahingisiwa; kakale

(b) Nkwalili wePhalamente wakafanila dusila patjena nekwala muGazethi nekulibilidza, ezibisa tjaba nekwetjiswungo nembudilo yatjo.

NDONGOLOSO YEBUTANHATU (Ndima 329 ne332)

taNgisa hiNga

KwENlaYo NKulu iwhoYu, NEzila dzEfuluKa KuNlaYo NKuluKugwi MA KWE ZWIKAMU LUBABI 1 KwEKutaNga

Tjikamu 1. Tangisa hinga kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu. 2. Ma hinga kweNlayo Nkulu wawuhinga. LUBABI 2 Tangisa Hinga Kwenlayo Nkulu Nema Hinga Kwenlayo Nkulukugwi Wawuhinga 3. Tangisa hinga kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu 4. Ma hinga kweNlayo Nkulu wawuhinga. 5. 6. 7. 8.

LUBABI 3 HALULO DZE KUTANGA Londolodzewa kwemiganhu yematunhu neyehalulo iyapo. Kwalisiwa kwebavoti. N’un’una nekwembhudilo yehalulo yeNtungamili weHango. Nlayo weHalulo. LUBABI 4

Zwinobigwa Nezila Dzefuluka Kunlayo Nkulukugwi 9. Ngina Kwe Umwe Hulumente Kwabhuda Umwe. 10. Ndila Mbeli Kwemilayo Yagala Iliyapo. 11. Tjenesiwa Kwetshambidzilo Dziyapo. 12. Ndawulo Dzimile Dzephalamente 13. Nghalibugalo Dziyapo. 14. Mamo Ehagwa Nelubaka Gweba Mubugalo Kwentungamili Wehango Wekutanga Nebigwa Kwebatebedzeli Bentungamili Wehango. 15. Ndilambeli Kwedzimwe Nghalo Dzebutungamili. 16. Ndzibilili Wetjaba. 17. Dusiwa Neyendesewa Kuyimwe Ntha Kwemari Dzimukwama Tjemari Yahulumente. 18. Dzikhuta Nendongoloso Dzenlayo. 19. Mamo Nekwensekesi Nkulu. 20. Ndulamilo Dzepiwa Pentjeni.

LUBABI 1 Kwekutanga Tjeneso muNdongoloso yebuTanhatu

1\. MuNdongoloso iyeyi, kuhhe kusingandilane nekunobviwa— “nsi wetangisa hinga” kobviwa hhuba letangisa hinga kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kutebegwa tjikamu 3(2); “tjitshambidzilo tjiyapo” kobviwa nlayo wakakwagwa wakawuhingisiwa muZimbabwe kusanhu kwaswika nsi wetangisa hinga, seTjisungo

tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kene sempanga wenlayo; “nlayo uyapo’’ kobviwa tjitshambidzilo tjiyapokene umwe nlayo, kene weni zwawo, wakawuhingisiwa muZimbabwe kusanhu kwaswika nsi wetangisa hinga; “halulo dzekutanga” kobviwa—

(a) halulo yekutanga yebugalo gweNtungamili weHango kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu;

(b) halulo yebanhu bose yekutanga kuhagwa baMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu; ne

(c) halulo dzekutanga dzenhabi dzinotungamila makhansili ematunhu ne ematoropo nema khansili emitunhu; dzinotiwa kwapinda hhuba ledusa patjena; “Nlayo Nkulu wawuhinga” kobviwa Nalyo Nkulu weZimbabwe wakatangisa hinga nsi wa18 Kungulu, gole la1980, sekwawaka kayigwa; “hhuba ledusa patjena” kobviwa hhuba Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, kene nlayo unowutshambidzila, lawuno dusigwa patjena nezila yekwagwa mu Gazethi kutebegwa ndima 51(5) yeNlayo Nkulu wawuhinga. Mbhudilo yeNdongoloso lebutanhatu

2\. Ndongoloso iyeyi inondilambeli ihinga, pehhugwi kwe amwe ose mamo eNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, kudza kunoswika nhambo dzesandilana. LUBABI 2

Tangisa Hinga Kwenlayo nKulu iwHoyu nema Hinga Kwenlayo nKulu wawuHinga

Tangisa hinga kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu

3\. (1) Ndongoloso iyeyi, kose ne—

(a) Danho 3, linoleba nekwe buzwagwa;

(b) Danho 4, kuli tshambidzigwa kweNdulamilo;

(c) Danho 5, linoleba nekwe halulo negala bugalo kweNtungamili weHango;

(d) Danho 6, linoleba nekwe hagwa kwebaMilili bemiTunhu muPhalamente nedan’wa kwePhalamente hule kwehalulo yebanhu bose nebvuma Zwisungo zweNlayo zwePhalamente neNtungamili weHango;

(e) Danho 7, linoleba nekwehalulo, kuhhe kwendima 158, 160 ne 161;

(f) Danho 8, linoleba nekwendawulo nemasimba eKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu wehango;

(g) Danho 9, linoleba nekwezila dzebhakhwakwetjaba neButungamili;

(h) ndima 208, inoleba nekwezwibhata kwebanohinga zwedzibilila hango;

(i) Danho 12, muyendidzananekweKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe; ne

(j) Danho 14, linoleba nekwe hulumente wematunhu nehulumente wemitunhu; kotangisa hinga nehhuba ledusa patjena.

(2) Kuhhe kwekuti kwalebgwa neyimwe zila muNdongoloso iyeyi, kose kumwe kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyukwasala kotangisa hinga hhuba Ntungamili weHango wakahagwa muhalulo dzekutanga egala mubugalo.

(3) Pakati kwehhuba ledusiwa patjena nekweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu nensi wetangisa hinga, mamo eNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu anotjenesiwa muzwikamwana (a) kunoti zwetjikamwana (1) anokulila mamo anolizana nawo eNlayo Nkulu wawuhinga.

Ma hinga kweNlayo Nkulu wawuhinga 4. Kutebegwa Ndongoloso iyeyi, Nlayo Nkulu wawuhinga unoma hinga kubvila munsi wetangisa hinga. LUBABI 3 Halulo Dzekutanga Londolodzewa kwemiganhu yematunhu neyehalulo iyapo 5. Miganhu yematunhu, yemitunhu neyemawadi sema kwayokusanhu kukaswika hhuba ledusiwa patjena ndiyo inohinga muhalulo dzekutanga. Kwalisiwa kwebavoti 6. (1) Kene wupi nhu wakakwagwa kumunlayo semvoti muwhalo dzebavoti kusanhu kwaswika hhuba ledusisiwa patjena unandulamilo yekuti agale akakwagwa kuti avote muhalulo dzekutanga.

(2) Muhalulo dzekutanga, Nkwalili-Nkulu weBavoti ndiye wakalingisananekwala banhu banovota newhalo dzebavoti etungamigwa neKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe.

(3) Nkwalili-Nkulu weBavoti, etungamigwa neKhomishini yeHalulo yeZimbabwe, wakafanila thama n’hingo wakahalugwa wekwagwa kwebavoti nelingisisa whalo dzebavoti kwemahhuba angasipasi kwealimakumi matatu hule kwehhuba ledusiwa patjena. Bulemo gunyendedzana nehalulo yekutanga yeNtungamili weHango 7. N’un’una kungabeyapo kwebulebeswa gwembhudilo yehalulo yeNtungamili weHango kwakafanila whiligwa kulingiwe kutobegwa ndima 93 yeNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu. Nlayo weHalulo 8. Halulo dzekutanga dzakafanila tiwa kutobegwa Nlayo weHalulo kundilana neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu. LUBABI 4 ZWINOBIGWA NEZILA DZEFULUKA KUNLAYO NKULUKUGWI Ngina kwe umwe Hulumente kwabhuda umwe

9\. Hulumente wakabumbiwa kutobegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu ndiye unongina mubugalo gwaHulumente weZimbabwe wayehinga. Ndila mbeli kwemilayo yagala iliyapo 10. Kutebegwa Ndongoloso iyeyi, milayo yose yagala iliyapo inondila mbeli ihinga koga yakafanila whisisiwa nezila inobvumilana neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu. Tjenesiwa kwetshambidzilo dziyapo 11. (1) Kuhhe kwekuti kusingandilane nekunobviwa, pamwe nepamwe mutshambidzilo iyapo panolebgwa nekwe—

(a) Ntungamili weHango kwakafanila kuti kuwhisisiwe sekunolebgwa nekweNtungamili weHango unotobela Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu.

(b) Phalamente kwakafanila kuti kuwhisisiwe sekunolebgwa nekwe—

(i) Senethi, hapa kulebgwa nekwen’hingo wakafanila tiwa neSenethi yoga itobela Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu;

(ii) Lubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente, hapa kulebgwa nekwe wumwe n’hingo usiwhoyu unolebgwa mutjikamwana (i) kene tshambidzigwa kwenlayo;

(c) LubahhegwePhalamente kwakafanila kuti kuwhisisiwe sekunolebgwa nekweLubahhe gwePhalamente;

(d) Hingila Tjaba kwakafanila kuti kuwhisisiwe sekunolebgwa nekweHingila Hulumente

(e) Khomishini yeHingila Tjaba kwakafanila kuti kuwhisisiwe sekunolebgwa nekweKhomishini yeHingila Hulumente;

(f) Lutabi gweHinga kweMajele kwakafanila kuti kuwhisisiwe sekunolebgwa nekweLutabi gweHinga kweMajele nezweTambunudza;

(g) Khomishini yeHinga kweMajele kwakafanila kuti kuwhisisiwe sekunolebgwa nekweKhomishini yeLutabi gweHinga kweMajele nezweTambunudza;

(h) Nlawuli weMajele kwakafanila kuti kuwhisisiwe sekunolebgwa nekweNlawuli Nkulu weMajele nezweTambunudza;

(i) Nlawuli wedzimari neMbonyololi Nkulu kwakafanila kuti kuwhisisiwe sekunolebgwa nekweMbonyololi Nkulu;

(j) Milili Nkulu wenlayo, kwakalingiwa ndongoloso dzebudzudzu, kwakafanilakuti kuwhisisiwe sekunolebgwa nekweNsekesi Nkulu.

(2) Hapa Nlayo Nkulu upa masimba nhu kene kuButungamili kuti kutshambidzile nlayo mundebo kene yipi zwayo, nendebo iyeyo yakalebgwa mutshambidzilo iyapo yakatiwa newumwe nhu kene gumwe Butungamili, tshambidzilo iyapo iyeyo inamasimba sekunoyi yakatiwa nenhu kene gumwe Butungamili kunamasimbaeyithama kutebela Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu. Ndawulo Dzimile dzePhalamente 12. Ndawulo Dzimile dzakadzihinga kusanhu kukaswika nsi wetangisa hinga dzondila mbeli dzihinga sendawulo dzimile dzeSenethi neLubahhe gwebaMilili beMitunhu muPhalamente dzidza dzimisiwa nedzimwe kene dzikayigwa kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu. Nghalibugalo dziyapo 13. Kene wupi nhu, kusanhu kwaswika nsi wetangisa hinga, wakaba elimubugalo kene wakatongobhata bugalo gwehingila tjaba kutebegwa kweNlayo Nkulu wawuhinga unondila mbeli eligelemubugalo igogo kene akabhata bugalo igogo, kene kene bugalo gunolizana nayigogo kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, ehinga nezila dzinofanana kudza kunoswika lubaka kunopela lubaka gugwe gwebamubugalo kutebegwa mamo en’hingo ihawo kene edza esiya bugalo, ebhuda kene edusiwamubugalo kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu kene mamo ehinga ihawo, sekwakungabe kuliyapo. Mamo akatjenisiwa ehalulo nelubaka gwebamutulo kweNtungamili weHango wekutanga nebigwa kwebatebedzeli beNtungamili weHango 3. (1) Kusingadzibiligwe ndima 92, muhalulo yekutanga nehalulo imwe neyimwe yeNtungamili weHango mumakole aligumi kwatjipinda halulo yekutanga, bakamilila halugwa sebaNtungamili beHango habatothanga banhu kutebegwa ndima iyeyiya kuti bahalugwe sebaTebedzeli beNtungamili weHango.

(2) Nekulibilidza nhu wahagwa kuti abeNtungamili muhalulo yatiwa inobviwa mutjikamwana (1) wakafanila biga baTebedzeli beNtungamili weHango basingapinde babili, banohingidzana naye.

(3) Kunotikala kuti—

(a)

Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango un’ompela unobigwa kutebegwa tjikamwana (2), nhu iwhoyo uNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango wekutanga nentha yeNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu.

(b) Batebedzeli beNtungamili weHango babili banobigwa kutebegwa tjikamwana (2), Ntungamili weHango neyenda kwelubaka engathanga umwe wabo kuti awumile hapa esipo muZimbabwe kene esingatubule thama mihingo iyenentha yegwala kene kene kumwe kungata akasatubula.

(4) kusingadzibiligwe ndima 101 koga kutebegwa tjikamwana (5) na (6), hapa nhu wakahalugwa seNtungamili weHango muhalulo keneyipi inolebgwa mutjikamwana

(1) engafa, engasiya kene engadusiwa mubugalo—

(a) Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango kene, hapa kunaBatebedzeli beNtungamili weHango babili, Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango wathangwa kubupelo kuti amile sekwakunolebgwa mutjikamwana (3)

(b), unomila Ntungamili weHango kudza kunoswika lubaka gwegala mubugalo kweNtungamili weHango untshwa sekwakunolebgwa mutjikamwana (5); kakale

(b)

bugalo gweNtungamili weHango gwakafanila gagwa nenhu wathangiwa mugubungano lezwepolitiki lakaba lakamiligwa neNtungamili weHango mulubaka gwehalulo.

(5) Gubungano lezwepolitiki lakapiwa masimba ethanga nhu sekwakunolebgwa mutjikamwana (4)(b) lakafanila zibisa Tjilebeleki nekwezina lenhu wathangiwa mumahhuba alimakumi alihhanalume hule kwesaba nenhu ugele mubugalo gweNtungamili weHango, kubva ipapo nhu iwhoyo wathangiwa unotola bugalo seNtungamili weHango hule kwetuna tjituno tjeNtungamili weHango kutebegwa ndima 94, wakafanila tuna muma awa alimakumi mana nehhanakadzi hule kwezibisiwakweTjilebeleki nekwezina lile.

(6) Hapa nhu wakahagwa seNtungamili weHango muhalulo inobviwa mutjikamwana (1) kakale esakatongo mila gubungano lezwepolitiki muhalulo atjinyika, asiya kene adusiwa mubugalo, Ntebedzeli weNtungamili weHango kene, hapa kuna baTebedzeli beNtungamili weHango babili, Ntebedzeli wakathangiwa kwebupelo kuti abhate kutebegwa tjikamwana (3)(b), unotola bugalo seNtungamili weHango. Ndilambeli kwedzimwe nghalo dzeButungamili 15. Kusingadzibiligwe tjinolebgwa neNlayo Nkulu wawuhinga, nghalo dzinotebela dzakabe dzilipo nehhuba ledusiwa patjena kutebegwa Ndongoloso 8 dzeNlayo Nkulu wawuhinga, dzina mazina hawa—

(a)

Ntungamili weHango neNtebedzeli weNtungamili weHango;

(b)

Minisita unotungamila Hango neNtebedzeli waMinisita unotungamila Hango;

(c)

Minisita neNtobedzeli waMinisita;

Dzondilambeli dzilipo kudza kunoswika nsi wetangisa hinga hapo Ntungamili weHango wekutanga egala mubugalo kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, nebanhu bakabeligele idzedzo nghalo banondila mbeli beligele mudzili. Ndzibilili weTjaba 16. (1) Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjeNdzibilili weTjaba [Danho 10;18] tjoma hinga.

(2) imwe neyimwe ndebo yakayihingwa neNdzibilili weTjaba kusanhu kwaswika nsi wetangisa hinga yakafanila ndisiwa kuKhomishini yeNdulamilo dzeBanhu yeZimbabwe kuti inopedzisiwa. Dusiwa neyendesewa kuyimwe ntha kwemari dzimuKwama tjemari yaHulumente 17. Kusanhu kwaswika nsi wetangisa hinga, Mari dzakadzilimuKwama tjeMari yaHulumente tjakabumbiwa neNlayo Nkulu wawuhinga dzoba Kwama tjeMari yaHulumente tjabumbiwa neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu

Dzikhuta nendongoloso dzenlayo 18. (1) Mutjikamu itjetji— “nlandu wetjuluka nlayo nkulu utjalingisisiwa” kobviwa—

(a)

tjikumbilo, lukwalo kene kunoba kubviwa kunoba kuleyana nelukwalo gweTshambidzigwa kweNdulamilo dziyapo muNlayo Nkulu wawuhinga kunoba kulebgwa nekwako; kene

(b) kene yipi ndebo yenlandu wetjuluka nlayo nkulu, sekwakunolebgwa mundima 332 yeNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu; Kakale inobe yakamilila lingisisiwa neKhutha Ipehhugwi yeZimbabwe imunlayo kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu wawuhinga, kusanhu kwaswika nsi wedusigwa patjena.

(2) Kusingadzibiligwe ndima 166, kwemakole alikunotendeka hule kwehhuba ledusigwa patjena, Khuta yeNlayo Nkulu ina—

(a)

Ntongi Nkulu neNtebedzeli weNtongi Nkulu;

(b) ne amwe majaji alikunotendeka eKhuta iPehhugwi; bakafanila gala panhupan’ompela bewhilila nlandu wetjuluka nlayo nkulu.

(3) Kukaba nebugalo gusigegwe muKhuta iPehhugwi mumakole alikunotendeka

ekutanga hule kwensi wedusiwa patjena gwakafanila gagwa newumwe jaji kene jaji wakapamhidzila majaji kene jaji wakabhata weKhuta iPehhugwi, sekungabe kuliyapo: Hapa kungatikala kuti kube netjigalo tjeNtobedzeli weNtongi Nkulu nelubaka igogu tjigalo itjetjo tjakafanila gagwa kutobegwa ndima 180(2) ne(3).

(4) Kudza kuba netjimwe tjimo tjinotiwa neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kene kutebegwa Tjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente—

(a)

milayo ingabumbiwa kutebegwaTjisungo tjeNlayo tjeKhuta iPehhugwi [Danho 7:13] kuti ilawule hinga kweKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu;

(b) milayo yeKhuta iPehhugwi inolawula, netambunudza kwakalizanila, hinga kweKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu muku tonga kene yipi ndebo isipo mumilayo yakabumbiwa kutebegwa tjikamwana (a); koga milayo yakajalo, nezila yayinolawula nayo hinga kweKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu, yakafanila kuti ibe indidzana nendima 85 naDanho 8.

(5) Khuta Ipehhugwi yezimbabwe, Khuta Whulu yeZimbabwe, Khuta yeBahingi neKhuta yeBhakhwa kweBanhu, sebumbiwa kwadzo kusanhu kwaswika nsi wetangisa hinga, dzoba munlayo dzizibgwa seKhuta Ipehhugwi yeZimbabwe, Khuta Whulu yeZimbabwe, Khuta yeBahingi neKhuta yeBhakhwa kweBanhu kutebegwa Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, kakale kene tjipi tjisungo tjekhuta idzedzo tjakahhisiwa kusanhu kwasika nsi wetangisa hinga tjondila mbeli tjihinga zwatjo.

(6) Wose nhu, kusanhu kwasikwa nsi wetangisa hinga, waka etungamila Khuta

yeBahingi kene Khuta yeBhakhwa kweBanhu unoba jaji weKhuta yeBahingi kene Khuta yeBhakhwa kweBanhu, kunda nekuti ndewayipi khuta, kutebegwa iwo zwawo mamo ehinga anohingisiwa munawhoyo nsi kumajaji eKhuta Whulu, lubaka gwaaka hinga eliNtungamili weKhuta yeBahingi kene Khuta yeBhakhwa kwebanhu gubon’we sen’hingo wejaji weKhuta Whulu.

(7) Khuta dzemitunhu, khuta dzemabvilo nedzimwe khuta dzakabumbiwa neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjePhalamente kusanhu kwaswika hhuba ledusiwa patjena dzondila mbeli dzihinga kene kwapinda hhuba ilelo sekunoyi dzakabumbiwa neTjisungo tjeNlayo tjinobviwa mundima 174, kakale zwisungo zwakatiwa nekhuta idzedzo kusanhu kwaswika hhuba ledusiwa patjenazondila mbeli zwihinga.

(8) Kene wupi nlandu wetjuluka nlayo nkulu utjalingisisiwa—

(a)

wekakabadzana kwebanhu kusawhiwa kusanhu kwaswika nsi wedusiwa patjena waswika kwakafanila yendisiwa kuKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu sekwakunolebwa mutjikamwana (2);

(b) hapa kakabadzana kwebanhu kwawhiwa kunoswika nsi wedusiwa patjena kwakafanila pedzewa neKhuta iPehhugwi kuhhe kwekuti bose banhu benlandu iwhoyo bebvuma kuti nlandu uyendisiwe kuKhuta yeNlayo Nkulu wehango sekwakunolebwa mutjikamwana (2), hapa kwajalo Khuta iPehhugwi yakafanila yendisa nlandu iwhoyo kukhuta.

(9) Yose milandu, kuhhe kwemilandu yetjuluka nlayo nkulu isanhu yapela, yakaitjalingisisiwa kene nayipi khuta kusanhu kwaswika nsi wetangisa hinga ingandilambeli mukhuta iyeyokeneyimwe khuta inolizana nayiyeyo seleba kweNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu, kunda nekuti khuta yakalizanila ikoko ndiyipi, sekunoyi Nlayo Nkulu iwhoyu wakawehinga lubaka kutangisa lingwakwemilandu iyeyo, koga—

(a)

zila inootobegwa mumilandu iyeyo yakafanila bazila yakayihinga mumilandu iyeyo kusanhu kwaswika nsi wetangisa hinga; kakale

(b) zila inobviwa mutjikamwana (a) inohinga mumilandu iyeyonekene isingandilane nekunolebwa muDanho 4 leNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu.

(10) Nentha yetjikamwana (9)—

(a)

nlandu webudzudzu unoyi watangisa hapa nhu watilikiwa nlandu ebvuma nlandu;

(b) nlandu wesawhisisana pakati kwebanhu unoyi watangisa hapa kwandisiwa samanisikukhuta kene hapa nhu aswungisiwa, kunda nekuti kwatikala ndikupi. Mamo akalingisana neNsekesi nkulu 19. (1) Kene tjipi tjisungo tjakatiwa kene zila yakatogwa neMilili Nkulu wenlayo kene newakabhatila Milili Nkulu wenlayo kusanhu kwaswika nsi wetangisa hinga nekwendongoloso dzebudzudzu dzolingwa, nayilelo hhuba kene kwapinda ilelo hhuba, setjakatiwa kene seyakatogwa neNsekesi Nkulu wenlayo kene newakabhatila Nsekesi Nkulu wenlayo.

(2) Nhu waka elimubugalo seliMilili Nkulu wenlayo kusanhu kwaswika nsi wetangisa hinga unondilambeli elimubugalo seNsekesi Nkulunayilelo hhuba kenekwapinda ilelo hhuba. Ndulamilo dzewana pentjeni 20. Ndulamilo yewana pentjini yaka iliyapo kusanhu kwaswika nsi wetangisa hinga kakale yakayakadzibiligwa neNlayo Nkulu wawuhinga inondilambeli iliyapo kakale inodzibiligwa kunonga kwekutanga neNlayo Nkulu iwhoyu.
